### [1] [Vision-Grounded Semantic Learning and Alignment for Scalable Multilingual Text-Based Person Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15328)
*Xinyu Zhou,Fan Li,Shasha Chen,Min Cao,Ahtamjan Ahmat,Qiaoming Zhu,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个大规模多语言行人中心预训练数据集MultiPedes（4种语言、1000万图文对），并提出ViSeLA框架，通过语义感知多语言学习与视觉锚定的跨模态对齐，摆脱英语枢轴偏差，在多语言与英语TBPR数据集上均取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言文本行人检索（TBPR）具现实意义，但现有研究几乎只做英文检索。唯一的多语言TBPR工作存在两大缺陷：(1) 缺乏大规模多语言预训练数据集；(2) 模型架构次优——共享编码器忽略语言异质性，且以英语为枢轴做跨模态对齐，引入英语中心偏差。

Method: (1) 数据：构建MultiPedes，经多语言质量感知的数据构建流水线生成1000万图文对、覆盖4种语言。(2) 框架ViSeLA：① 语义感知多语言学习模块，在MoE架构基础上将语言无关语义路由至专用专家，强化多语言特征学习；② 视觉锚定跨模态对齐模块，用语义探针捕捉跨模态细粒度语义，实现不依赖枢轴语言的直接对齐。

Result: 在多语言TBPR数据集与纯英文TBPR数据集上均开展了大量实验，验证了ViSeLA的有效性。

Conclusion: MultiPedes与ViSeLA为多语言行人检索提供了数据与架构层面的双重解决方案，消除了英语中心偏差；数据集与代码已开源（https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/ViSeLA）。

Abstract: Multilingual Text-Based Person Retrieval (TBPR) aims to retrieve individuals with texts in multiple languages. Despite its practical importance, it has been overlooked by existing research, which mainly focuses on English-only retrieval. The only prior attempt at multilingual TBPR suffers from (1) the absence of a large-scale, multilingual pretraining dataset and (2) a suboptimal model architecture for multilingual text encoding and cross-modal alignment, which employs a shared encoder that overlooks linguistic heterogeneity and relies on English as a pivot for cross-modal alignment, introducing English-centric bias. For this, we contribute MultiPedes, the first large-scale, pedestrian-centric multilingual pretraining dataset. It contains 10 million image-text pairs across four languages, constructed by a multilingual quality-aware data construction pipeline. We also propose ViSeLA (Vision-grounded Semantic Learning and Alignment), a novel framework featuring: (1) a semantic-aware multilingual learning module that extends the mixture-of-experts architecture by routing language-invariant semantics to specialized experts for improved multilingual feature learning; and (2) a vision-grounded cross-modal alignment module that employs a semantic probe to capture fine-grained semantics across modalities, enabling direct cross-modal alignment without relying on a pivot language. Extensive experiments on both multilingual and English-only TBPR datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ViSeLA. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/ViSeLA

</details>


### [2] [The Neighbour's Label: Opposite Subgroup Effects of Same-Dialogue Exposure in Next-Act Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15194)
*Ali Amini,Masoumeh Chapariniya,Rytis Maskeliunas,Robertas Damaševičius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究基于窗口的"下一话轮行为"评测中的标签泄漏（相邻前驱行暴露），发现泄漏会使模型在"延续"子组获益、"转换"子组受损，而单一聚合精度掩盖了这一反向效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窗口式下一话轮行为评测本意是衡量跨对话泛化，但重叠窗口与相似度检索可能把测试对话中已标注的样本暴露给模型。最邻近的暴露样本恰是目标的前一条已标注行为：目标延续它时其标签正确，目标切换行为时其标签错误，导致"抄袭该样本"的模型在两类子组上一得一失，被整体平均精度所掩盖；作者希望受控地刻画这种暴露。

Method: 在 SGD、STAR、AMI 三个官方划分干净的语料上受控地人为引入暴露：(1) 将前驱行随机加入训练，检验词面分类器的延续/转换分数，并做剔除相邻行的消融；(2) 用 TF-IDF 检索示例进行上下文学习，测试两个 7B 指令微调模型在 8 个"模型×语料×采样"组合上的表现；(3) 不重训模型，比较两种诊断性设置（如排除测试对话）与自然权重精度。

Result: 加入前驱行使词面分类器在三个语料上延续分数上升、转换分数下降，剔除相邻行可消除大部分增益；两个 7B 模型在全部 8 个实验组合中呈现同样的反向模式（部分单格置信区间含零，一次复现仅部分成立）；自然权重精度仅变化 -0.05 至 +0.04；暴露频率依赖检索流程；排除测试对话在 SGD 上并未带来可检测的精度下降。

Conclusion: 窗口式下一行为评测的聚合精度会掩盖暴露带来的方向相反的子组效应，应分延续/转换子组报告并审计检索管线中的暴露频率；不过所有暴露均为人为诱导，未对已发表系统重新评估，且排除测试对话未必降低精度，故对真实系统的实际影响仍待验证。

Abstract: Window-based next-act evaluation often aims to measure generalisation across dialogues, yet overlapping windows and similarity retrieval can expose labelled examples from the test dialogue. The nearest such example is the target's labelled predecessor, whose act is correct when the target continues it and wrong when the target changes act; a model that copies it can gain and lose in two subgroups that one aggregate averages. We induce this exposure under controlled designs on SGD, STAR and AMI, whose official splits are clean. Randomly including the predecessor's row in training raises a lexical classifier's continuation scores and lowers its transition scores in all three corpora, and purging the adjacent rows removes most of the gain. With TF-IDF-retrieved demonstrations, two 7B instruction-tuned models show the same opposite pattern in all eight model-corpus-sample arms (some single-cell intervals include zero; one replication is partial), while natural-weight accuracy changes by only -0.05 to +0.04. Exposure frequency depends on the retrieval pipeline. We evaluate two diagnostic comparisons without model retraining and find that excluding the test dialogue does not detectably reduce accuracy on SGD. All exposure is induced; no published system was re-evaluated.

</details>


### [3] [Do the Details Help the Whole? One-Way Transfer from Person Actions to Group Intent in VLMs on JRDB-Social](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16581)
*Chengguang Gan,Zhixi Cai,Yunhao Liang,Hanjun Wei,QingHao Zhang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在JRDB-Social上系统检验"个人动作细节与群体意图整体联合训练互相促进"的假设，发现单向收益：先写动作再写意图可将意图macro-F1从30.1提升到31.0（先写意图达31.2），而动作F1基本无损；但无任何含意图训练的分支能提升动作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会场景数据集同时在"个体在做什么（动作）"与"群体为何聚集（意图）"两个层级标注，领域内普遍假设两级同时训练能互相促进，但该假设缺乏对视觉语言模型的严格验证。

Method: 以Qwen3.5 4B为主模型，在JRDB-Social上设计8个训练分支×3个随机种子进行微调；核心分支在Qwen3.5 9B与Qwen3-VL 4B上复现；采用测试前固定的判定规则评估各分支。辅以强制前缀测试、遮挡、成员掩码、线性探针与组件嫁接等机制分析。

Result: 动作→意图的书写顺序使意图macro-F1由30.1升至31.0，意图→动作为31.2；动作F1与仅动作模型相差不超过0.2点。选定配方下没有任何含意图训练的分支改善动作。收益依赖正确且图像落地的动作：空动作列表或借用同意图他组动作会降低意图F1。机制分析表明意图依赖已书写的动作、更依赖人物信息并部分存在于视觉塔中；将动作作为输入而非监督目标会同时损害两个层级。

Conclusion: "细节与整体联合训练互利"的假设在VLM上不成立，实际为单向迁移：群体意图受益于精确的个体动作描述，反之不成立；且收益以动作正确性与图像落地性为前提。

Abstract: Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Writing each member's actions before the intent raises intent macro-F1 from 30.1 to 31.0, and writing the intent first reaches 31.2, while action F1 stays within 0.2 points of an action-only model. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. The gain requires correct, image-grounded actions: empty action lists or actions borrowed from another group with the same intent lower intent F1. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.

</details>


### [4] [Fine-Grained Generative Hallucination Detection and Correction for Aerial Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15809)
*Debolena Basak,Somsubhra De,Maunendra Sankar Desarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、无参考的无人机航拍图像描述幻觉检测与纠正框架，基于InternVL3与Qwen2.5-VL实现低开销检测，并通过精炼模块纠正幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在航拍图像描述中易产生幻觉，且因视角变化大、目标小、类别不平衡和视觉实体模糊而加剧；现有方法依赖GPT-4等昂贵外部大模型且航拍域表现差，或将其简化为刚性二分类，忽略模糊样本的固有不确定性。

Method: 轻量、无参考的幻觉检测与纠正框架：利用SOTA MLLM（InternVL3、Qwen2.5-VL）以最小计算开销检测自由形式航拍描述中的幻觉内容，再由专用精炼模块纠正被检测的幻觉，提升原始描述的事实一致性。

Result: 在Labelled Illusion Dataset (LID)上的大量实验表明，该框架显著降低幻觉率，同时适用于资源受限环境下的实时部署。

Conclusion: 所提框架在航拍图像描述幻觉检测与纠正上兼顾效果与效率，具备实时应用可行性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Aerial imagery captured by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) plays a vital role in applications such as surveillance, disaster response, and large-scale environmental monitoring. Automatic generation of textual descriptions for such imagery is essential for reducing manual inspection overhead and enabling better downstream decision-making. However, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), despite their impressive captioning capabilities, are prone to hallucination, producing descriptions that are inconsistent with the visual input. This problem is significantly amplified in aerial imagery due to its unique challenges such as extreme viewpoint variation, small objects, class imbalance, and ambiguous visual entities. Existing hallucination detection approaches either rely on computationally expensive large external models such as GPT-4, which perform poorly on aerial domains, or treat the problem as a rigid binary classification task, ignoring the inherent uncertainty in ambiguous cases. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight, reference-free framework for hallucination detection and correction in free-form aerial image captioning. Our method leverages state-of-the-art MLLMs, specifically InternVL3 and Qwen2.5-VL, to detect hallucinated content in generated captions with minimal computational overhead. Detected hallucinations are corrected through a dedicated refinement module that improves the factual consistency of the original captions. Extensive experiments on the Labelled Illusion Dataset (LID) demonstrate that our framework significantly reduces hallucination rates while remaining practical for real-time deployment in resource-constrained environments. Code is available at: https://github.com/Debolena7/Hal_Detect.

</details>


### [5] [Pothole Risk Assessment with Geometrically Grounded Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16061)
*Abdalmalek Aburaddaha,Zaid A. El Shair,Ali Al-Bustami,Samir A. Rawashdeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出五阶段模块化框架，将透视变换、零样本SAM分割与几何测量和VLM推理结合，对已检测坑洞做离线严重度评估与自然语言预警生成；严重度分级被视为视觉类别而非深度测量，几何接地显著改变了预警标签并被盲评者偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有坑洞检测器只回答"哪里有坑洞"，不回答"有多严重、该怎么办"；单张RGB图像判断严重度本身是病态问题，而无视觉接地的VLM评估往往过于保守。因此需要一个可规模化的离线严重度评估与预警生成方案。

Method: 五阶段模块化流水线：透视变换→零样本SAM分割→几何特征提取→Gemini 2.5 Pro严重度分类与复合风险打分→自然语言预警生成。评估对象为1,119个行车记录仪场景中的3,092个坑洞；对比独立参考包括免预测的三标注者小组和独立RGB-D数据集实测深度，并做六臂提示消融与盲评研究。

Result: 与三标注者小组的严重度一致性弱（κ=0.139，95% CI [0.00, 0.28]，n=101），远低于标注者彼此间一致性（κ=0.605）；与RGB-D实测深度也弱（κ=0.098），但预测类别能保持实测深度的序关系。相对仅图像基线，几何接地使26.7%场景退出高紧急层、关键层判定增加109%（Stuart-Maxwell p<10⁻¹⁵）；盲评中三位评分者以3.5:1偏好接地预警。六臂提示消融显示混合提示的规则遵循主要来自逐坑洞测量而非评分细则或示例。成本约0.105美元/场景。

Conclusion: 该方法适合大规模离线基础设施评估，但其严重度类别是感知性视觉范畴而非深度测量；预警中的机动建议仅为研究性咨询输出，不构成经验证的车辆驾驶指导。

Abstract: Potholes impose billions of dollars in annual vehicle damage, yet detectors report only where potholes are, not how severe they are or what to do. Judging severity from a single RGB image is ill-posed, and ungrounded vision-language model (VLM) assessments tend to be over-conservative. We present a five-stage modular framework for the offline assessment of already-detected potholes that combines geometric measurements with VLM reasoning: perspective transformation, zero-shot SAM segmentation, geometric feature extraction, Gemini 2.5 Pro severity classification with composite risk scoring, and natural-language warning generation. We evaluate it on 3,092 potholes in 1,119 dashcam scenes against independent references. Severity agreement with a prediction-free three-annotator panel is weak (Cohen's $κ= 0.139$, 95% CI [0.00, 0.28]; $n = 101$) and far below the annotators' agreement with one another ($κ= 0.605$); against physically measured depth on an independent RGB-D dataset, it is also weak ($κ= 0.098$), although the predicted classes preserve the order of measured depths. The severity classes are therefore perceived visual categories, not depth measurements. Geometric grounding does change the warnings: relative to an image-only baseline, it moves 26.7% of scenes out of the high-urgency tier and raises critical-tier assignments by 109% (Stuart-Maxwell $p < 10^{-15}$), and in a blind study, three raters preferred the grounded warnings 3.5:1. A six-arm prompt ablation attributes the hybrid prompt's rule adherence to the per-pothole measurements rather than to the rubric or worked examples. At USD 0.105 per scene, it suits large-scale offline infrastructure assessment; its maneuver suggestions are advisory research outputs, not validated vehicle guidance.

</details>


### [6] [Cat Royale Comprehensive Dataset: A Dataset for Pose Detection, Segmentation, Animal Action Recognition and Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15601)
*Zane K. J. Hartley,Joseph Ho,Alan Chamberlain,Michael P. Pound,Steve Benford*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出猫科动物综合多模态数据集CRCD，基于500+小时视频，涵盖实例分割、姿态、时序动作定位与视频-文本描述标注，并给出四项基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物相关计算机视觉数据集在CV与动物行为学中日益受关注，多任务视觉理解对动物行为与福利研究意义重大；但现有数据缺乏将多种标注类型统一关联的多模态资源。

Method: 构建CRCD数据集，源自Cat Royale艺术与研究项目的500余小时视频；包含2万张实例分割掩码图像、5000张多主体姿态坐标图像、800+带动作类别视频片段及800+带详细文本描述片段；公开数据集与自研标注工具，并设立实例分割、姿态检测、时序动作定位、视频-文本检索基准。

Result: 形成首个将多种标注类型关联的多模态猫科数据集，并为四项CV任务提供基准评测，支持单任务及组合式复杂多模态任务。

Conclusion: CRCD为动物行为理解与福利应用提供统一多模态基础，公开数据与工具以支持持续扩展。

Abstract: Animal focused computer vision datasets are of a growing interest in the fields of both Computer Vision and Animal behaviour. The ability to make sense of complex scenes via a variety of Computer Vision tasks has substantial implications for behavioural understanding and welfare. The perfection of these tasks will help lead to better health and emotional outcomes for wild and domestic animals. In this work we present the Cat Royale Comprehensive Dataset (CRCD), a large, comprehensive, multi-modal dataset for feline understanding, based on a huge database of over 500 hours of video captured during the Cat Royale artwork and research project. Our dataset contains annotations for a range of computer vision problems including 20,000 images with instance segmentation masks, 5000 images with multi-subject pose coordinates, over 800 video clips with action classes, and over 800 clips with detailed text description. This is the first such dataset to link this wide range of annotation types together for multi-modal use. We release this entire dataset, and our custom annotation tools will also be made available, in order to allow for ongoing expansion. We also provide a series of benchmarks for the tasks of Instance Segmentation, Pose detection, Temporal Action Localisation, and finally video-text retrieval. The totality of this multi-modal dataset is the data needed to solve both these individual computer vision tasks as well as enabling more complex multi-modal tasks making use of a combination of annotations.

</details>


### [7] [FoveaDet: Software-Hardware Co-Design of Foveated CNN-Transformer for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15579)
*Deepak Mewada,Apporva Saraf,Ashwin Lele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出软硬件协同设计框架FoveaDet，借鉴生物中央凹视觉，在ViT中动态识别显著区域并裁剪无关patch，配合定制RISC-V核，实现边缘端高效实时目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在功耗受限的边缘平台上部署实时目标检测具有挑战性，处理全分辨率帧在计算上常存在冗余；受生物中央凹视觉（依据视觉显著性分配可变分辨率）启发，寻求降低冗余计算的方案。

Method: 软件侧：动态识别显著区域以预测注视点（fixation points），在ViT中激进裁剪无关背景patch，并通过优化的插值与patch选择算法缓解串行patch生成带来的减速。硬件侧：设计带专用指令的定制RISC-V核，加速中央凹处理流水线，消除硬件瓶颈。

Result: 在标准GPU上评估，浮点运算量减少65.4%–92.2%，推理加速4.76倍，相比稠密基线mAP下降可忽略。

Conclusion: FoveaDet提供了可扩展、高效的架构，可在严苛受限的移动平台上赋能高级视觉处理。

Abstract: Deploying real-time object detection on power-constrained edge platforms is challenging as processing full-resolution frames is often computationally redundant. Inspired by biological foveation which assigns variable resolution based on visual saliency, we propose a novel software-hardware co-design framework called FoveaDet. On the software side, FoveaDet dynamically identifies salient regions to predict fixation points, followed by aggressive patch pruning in Vision Transformers (ViT) to discard irrelevant background data. To mitigate slowdowns from sequential patch generation, we implement optimized interpolation and patch selection algorithms. Evaluated on standard GPUs, this foveated approach reduces floating-point operations by 65.4% to 92.2% and accelerates inference by 4.76x, while suffering a negligible mAP drop compared to dense baselines. To eliminate hardware bottlenecks, we complement the algorithm with a custom RISC-V core featuring specialized instructions to accelerate the foveated processing pipeline. FoveaDet provides a scalable, efficient architecture capable of unlocking advanced vision processing on severely constrained mobile platforms.

</details>


### [8] [RipBench: A Unified Benchmark for Multi-Level Rip Current Detection, Classification and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15574)
*Andrei Dumitriu,Aakash Ralhan,Florin Miron,Florin Tatui,Radu Tudor Ionescu,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RipBench统一基准，在同一数据与标准划分下评测离岸流检测的六类视觉任务（分类、水平/旋转框检测、实例/语义/全景分割），揭示粗粒度识别与精细空间理解之间的显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离岸流是海滩溺亡的主要诱因，但因其形态无定形、与背景相似、视角与环境差异大而难以检测；现有研究在数据集、指标、模型与方法上彼此割裂，导致对模型性能缺乏完整认识。

Method: 构建统一基准RipBench：采集304段视频（303,491帧，其中51,011帧完成全部六任务标注），覆盖多样海岸线；采用标准化划分，对分类、轴对齐与旋转目标检测、实例/语义/全景分割进行受控评测；以F2分数为主指标强调召回；辅以视频级置信区间、空间标签盲法标注者一致性研究、按视角与视觉特征的 stratified 分析，以及视频视图与时间基线参考运行。

Result: 粗粒度识别与精细空间理解间存在明显性能鸿沟：帧级分类F2=0.93，最佳检测器为0.72，最佳实例分割器为0.66；且模型排名随任务变化而改变。

Conclusion: RipBench及每任务多个基线已公开（https://RipBench.ai），为海滩安全场景下的真实世界多任务视觉研究提供统一评测平台，推动该方向进展。

Abstract: Rip currents are a serious and often under-addressed threat to beach safety and the leading cause of coastal drownings worldwide. They are difficult to detect due to their amorphous structure, similarity to the background, and large variability in viewpoints and environments. Despite their dangers and growing interest in automated detection, existing research remains fragmented across datasets, metrics, models, and approaches, leading to an incomplete understanding of model performance. We introduce RipBench, a unified benchmark that enables controlled evaluation of rip current detection spanning multiple tasks, including classification, axis-aligned and oriented object detection, and instance, semantic, and panoptic segmentation, on the same data with standardized splits. This allows direct comparison of model performance on all levels of visual abstraction. Across $304$ videos ($303{,}491$ frames, $51{,}011$ of them annotated for all six tasks) collected from diverse coastlines, our results expose a clear performance gap between coarse recognition and precise spatial understanding: frame-level classification reaches $F_2 = 0.93$, while the best detector and the best instance segmenter reach $0.72$ and $0.66$, with model rankings that change from task to task. Video-level confidence intervals, a blind inter-annotator study of the spatial labels, stratified analyses by viewpoint and visual signature, and video views with temporal baselines (single-seed, short-schedule reference runs) accompany the benchmark. All tasks are supported with carefully curated annotations and evaluated using standard metrics, with a focus on the $F_2$ score to emphasize recall in this safety-critical setting. RipBench, along with multiple baselines per task, is publicly available at https://RipBench.ai to support progress in real-world, multi-task vision for beach safety.

</details>


### [9] [VibePrevisBench: Evaluating Agentic Coding for 4D Scene Generation in Blender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15789)
*Yuzhe Chen,Muxi Diao,Lele Yang,Wenbin Xu,Xuyang Liu,Qile Su,Changyi Liu,Jiankang Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Haoyu Zhang,Hongyi Fu,Jianxiong Wang,Tianke Zhang,Haonan Fan,Yingxin Li,Bingyi Li,Xu Wang,Xuanyu Zheng,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Wentao Zhang,Jiale Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VibePrevisBench 是一个面向 4D 场景重建的基准：由编码智能体依据任务简报构建可执行的 4D 场景并渲染灰度代理视频，含 242 个任务（脚本、照片、续写、参考视频四类输入）。评估从规则化 Scene Check（九维度比对隐藏真值）与 VLM 的 Screen Check（判断事实是否在代理视频中可辨识）双维度打分。对 11 个前沿智能体的分析揭示三点：多模态理解（尤以空间与相机运动）是主要区分因素；各智能体普遍掌握基础运镜与调度，但在高难度请求上失败；智能体虽会自检渲染结果，却无法据此纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控的真实世界视频生成模型可依赖渲染的灰度代理视频（graybox proxy）来锁定布局、运动与相机，而编码智能体可通过在 Blender 中重建 4D 场景来生成此类视频。然而，现有工作缺乏能同时检验这类代理视频及其背后长时长、多镜头场景的基准，因此需要建立统一的 4D 场景重建评测标准。

Method: 构建 VibePrevisBench：每个任务以简报表述为输入，智能体需产出可执行的 4D 场景代码并渲染代理视频；数据集含 242 个任务，覆盖脚本、照片、续写、参考视频四类输入。评测采用双维度：规则化 Scene Check 重新运行代码并导出 4D 场景，与隐藏的真值事实在九个维度上比对；VLM-based Screen Check 由 VLM 裁判判定通过的事实能否在代理视频中被识别。此外分析 11 个前沿编码智能体的评测结果与执行轨迹。

Result: 对 11 个前沿编码智能体的结果显示三点关键观察：(1) 多模态理解能力区分各智能体表现，尤以空间理解与相机运动最为显著；(2) 所有智能体已基本掌握基础运镜与场景调度，但均在高难度请求上失败；(3) 智能体虽会检查自身渲染结果，却无法据此修正错误。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体在 4D 场景重建上仍需三方面改进：提升对空间与相机的视觉理解能力；针对高难度请求引入显式校验机制；对渲染结果与简报进行逐项核对验证。

Abstract: Controllable real-world video generation models can condition on a rendered graybox proxy video, which fixes layout, motion and camera. Coding agents can build such videos by reconstructing a 4D scene in Blender, yet no benchmark checks both these videos and the long, multi-shot scenes behind them. We introduce VibePrevisBench, a benchmark for 4D scene reconstruction: an agent turns a brief into an executable 4D scene and renders its proxy video. Its 242 tasks span four input kinds, including scripts, photos, continuations, and reference videos. Each delivery is scored along two evaluation dimensions. Rule-based Scene Check re-runs the code and compares the exported 4D scene with hidden ground truth facts in nine dimensions. VLM-based Screen Check asks a VLM judge whether the facts that pass can be recognized in the proxy video. We analyze the evaluation results and trajectories of 11 frontier coding agents and identify three key observations. First, multimodal understanding separates the agents, especially in understanding space and camera motion. Second, all agents seem to already master basic camera work and staging, and fail at challenging requests. Third, agents inspect their own renders but fail to correct their errors. Agents thus need better visual understanding of space and camera, explicit checks for challenging requests, and item-by-item verification of renders against the brief.

</details>


### [10] [AIFR: Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery for Diffusion-Based Makeup and Anime Portraits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15764)
*Linyan Dai,Xinwei Zhang,Yaxin Xiao,Mengyu Sun,Xinwei Liu,Renyang Liu,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应身份特征恢复框架 AIFR，在冻结的人脸识别模型单一中间层插入轻量模块，预测样本级校正强度与空间自适应残差，无需重编码画廊即可恢复原始空间嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成的妆造与人像动画改变外观，导致基于身份的内容筛查中人脸识别性能下降。现有方案或需重新编码画廊（适配编码器），或恢复输入图像不完整、扭曲身份细节。

Method: AIFR 在冻结 FR 模型的单个中间层插入轻量恢复模块，预测样本级校正强度与空间自适应残差更新；训练使用融合身份目标引导在原 FR 空间恢复嵌入，并用身份偏移得分约束校正强度、限制不必要的改变；推理单次前向、无需参考图。

Result: 仅 1.22M 可训练参数，在 FAR=10⁻⁴ 下将平均 TAR 从 ArcFace 的 66.1% 提升至 90.2%；在未见妆造风格、风格化强度和生成器上均有提升；恢复嵌入可直接驱动 Arc2Face 提升身份保持。

Conclusion: 在冻结识别器内部校正身份特征是一种高效、免重编码的方案，且恢复嵌入与嵌入条件生成下游任务兼容。

Abstract: Diffusion-based makeup and anime portraits are increasingly popular in online content creation (e.g., virtual makeup editing and anime avatar generation), but their altered appearances can hinder face recognition (FR) in identity-based content screening. Existing methods mainly adapt FR encoders or restore input images. The former require costly gallery re-encoding, while the latter may be incomplete or distort identity-related facial details. To address these limitations, we aim to correct identity features within a frozen FR model to recover its output embeddings in its original space. Appearance changes vary in strength and affect facial regions differently. To this end, we propose Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery (AIFR), a framework that inserts a lightweight recovery module at a single intermediate layer to predict sample-level correction strength and spatially adaptive residual updates. Module training uses fused identity targets to guide embedding recovery in the original FR space and an identity-shift score to regulate correction strength and limit unnecessary changes. At inference, AIFR processes a single portrait in one forward pass without reference images, producing recovered embeddings that can be directly matched with existing gallery templates without re-encoding. With only 1.22M trainable parameters, AIFR improves the average TAR at FAR=$10^{-4}$ from 66.1\% to 90.2\% over the original ArcFace, and further experiments show improvements across unseen makeup styles, stylization strengths, and generators. Beyond recognition, the recovered embeddings can directly condition a pretrained Arc2Face model to generate faces with improved identity preservation, showing compatibility with embedding-conditioned downstream generation.

</details>


### [11] [CGOcc: Learning Cross-Gaussian Dependencies for 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15692)
*Zirui Huang,Jiongle He,Yunfan Zeng,Yuanzhi Lu,Guangye Li,Boyan Tang,Weite SU,Pedro V. Sander,Amy Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CGOcc：显式建模跨高斯依赖的多相机3D语义占据预测框架，用对角加低秩表示与类条件投影实现高效读出，在nuScenes上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于高斯的语义占据预测中，重叠高斯基元会产生类别预测歧义，其联合解读对体素分类至关重要。现有方法采用空间加权混合，忽略了跨基元依赖的显式建模，限制了读出阶段利用类别相关关系证据的能力。

Method: 提出监督式多相机框架CGOcc。融合外观与几何观测以精化高斯状态；以“对角+低秩”形式编码跨高斯依赖；通过类条件投影提取对角与带符号的跨高斯统计量，避免构建稠密依赖矩阵；读出阶段利用这些统计量精化占据类别概率，同时保持学习参考的占据质量与空闲概率。

Result: 在nuScenes上达到SOTA；在高斯重叠程度高的区域增益更显著，边界附近预测改善；消融实验验证了协方差感知预测的有效性及其与几何交互的协同作用。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨高斯依赖并以低秩高效实现，可有效缓解重叠基元的类别歧义，提升占据预测精度，且与几何信息交互具有协同增益。

Abstract: In Gaussian-based semantic occupancy prediction, overlapping primitives can provide ambiguous class predictions, making their joint interpretation important for voxel classification. However, current methods using spatially weighted mixtures neglect explicit modeling of cross-primitive dependencies, limiting the readout's ability to utilize category-specific relational evidence. Hence, we introduce CGOcc, a supervised multi-camera framework that explicitly models Cross-Gaussian dependencies for 3D semantic Occupancy prediction. CGOcc integrates appearance and geometric observations to refine Gaussian states and encodes cross-Gaussian dependencies in a diagonal-plus-low-rank representation. To incorporate these dependencies into voxel classification, CGOcc uses a class-conditional projection to extract diagonal and signed cross-Gaussian statistics without constructing a dense dependency matrix. The readout then refines occupied-class probabilities using these statistics while preserving a learned reference's occupied mass and free probability. Extensive experiments on nuScenes demonstrate CGOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance, with larger gains at high Gaussian overlap and improved predictions near boundaries. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of covariance-aware prediction and support its synergy with geometric interaction.

</details>


### [12] [MemInStream: Memorizing or Merely Recognizing? Rethinking Streaming Video Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15640)
*Zhenqi He,Dunyuan Xu,Yang Bo,Kien T. Pham,Yanghao Wang,Shuhan Li,Xintong Guo,Yuyang He,Fang Liu,Huankang Guan,Jingyan Chen,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MemInStream，一个专门评测流式视频理解中记忆能力的基准，采用证据可控的验证协议将"记忆失败"与"视觉理解失败"解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需持续处理视觉流，记忆的保留与检索随历史增长愈发关键；但现有基准仅用问答准确率衡量记忆并不可靠——答错可能源于视觉理解失败而非遗忘，答对可能依赖语言先验或近期观测而无需历史记忆。

Method: 构建 MemInStream 基准并提出证据可控验证协议：为每个问题标注时间局部化的证据区间，并做两项验证——（1）证据可解性：模型在仅给定标注证据时能答对；（2）记忆必要性：模型仅凭近期观测、证据后观测或纯文本输入无法解决。据此保留问题以消除记忆无关的捷径。基准按四大类别组织：情景检索、状态跟踪与更新、多证据整合、上下文记忆。

Result: 形成覆盖多样视频场景、经双重验证过滤的问题集合，可系统性评测四类记忆能力；通过剔除可被捷径解决的问题，降低记忆无关因素对评测的干扰。

Conclusion: MemInStream 为流式视频理解中的记忆能力提供了全面且可靠的系统化评测基准，有助于区分记忆局限与理解失败。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding aims to continuously process incoming visual observations, making the retention and retrieval of relevant information increasingly important as the video history grows. Although existing benchmarks include memory-related questions, question-answering accuracy alone does not reliably measure memory capabilities. Incorrect predictions may arise from failures in visual understanding rather than forgetting, whereas correct predictions may rely on linguistic priors or recent observations without requiring historical memory. To address these ambiguities, we introduce MemInStream, a benchmark dedicated to evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding. We develop an evidence-controlled validation protocol that associates each question with temporally localized evidence intervals. The protocol validates evidence solvability and memory necessity by retaining questions that models can answer from the annotated evidence but cannot resolve using recent observations, post-evidence observations, or textual input alone. This helps disentangle memory limitations from understanding failures while reducing memory-independent shortcuts. MemInStream provides a systematic evaluation of memory capabilities through four major categories, covering Episodic Retrieval, State Tracking \& Updating, Multi-Evidence Integration, and Contextual Memory across diverse video scenarios. Overall, \textsc{MemInStream} provides a comprehensive and reliable benchmark for systematically evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [13] [DiFiT: Distribution-Fidelity-guided Visual Token Pruning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15069)
*Jingyuan Zhang,Nihao Niu,Xinyang Lv,Beibei Han,Xiaoyun Wang,Yanning Hou,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiFiT，以全token响应分布为参考学习视觉token剪枝，在保持任务精度的同时降低预测漂移与采样失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉token剪枝虽降低多模态大模型推理成本，但贪心答案正确且不变时，替代token概率仍会改变；平均预测漂移随压缩率增大，且漂移与置信度-决策不一致、采样失败相关。仅以答案准确率评价剪枝不足。

Method: DiFiT在共享参考前缀下比较全量与剪枝响应分布；双视图蒸馏在全词表上匹配概率，答案监督保障任务质量；学习式打分器+可微Top-K选择保留的视觉证据；多预算掩码目标使单一共享排序支持不同保留率，模型冻结。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，10%保留率、四任务贪心正确对照下，采样导致的负翻转较FastV相对减少30.3%；30%保留率时在MME上保持基线98.74%的性能。

Conclusion: 以分布保真度为剪枝目标可同时缓解预测漂移与采样不稳定，且单一排序即可适配多保留率，为剪枝评价提供超越准确率的视角。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference cost of multimodal large language models, but answer accuracy only partly reflects its effect. Pruning can change the probabilities of alternative response tokens even when greedy answers remain correct and unchanged. We compare full and pruned response distributions under shared reference prefixes. Average predictive drift grows with compression on fixed correctly answered samples. Separate analyses associate larger drift with more confidence-decision disagreements and sampling failures. We propose DiFiT (Distribution-Fidelity-guided Token pruning) to learn discrete selection from reference answers and full-token response distributions. Dual-View Distillation matches probabilities across the vocabulary, while answer supervision supports task quality. A learned scorer and differentiable Top-$K$ learn which visual evidence to retain. A multi-budget mask objective supports different retention ratios through one shared ranking over a frozen model. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, DiFiT reduces sampling-induced negative flips by 30.3% relative to FastV at 10% retention on four-task greedy-correct controls. It preserves 98.74% of baseline performance on MME at 30% retention.

</details>


### [14] [Estimating Image Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15044)
*Nisan Chhetri,Christopher G. Healey,Sina Baghbanijam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动估计图像"感知创造性"的框架：先预测图像在新颖性(novelty)、惊讶性(surprise)和唤醒度(arousal)三个心理创造性维度上的强度，再将三者组合为整体创造性评分；在Amazon Mechanical Turk上收集人类评分训练监督学习模型，估计得分与人类评分在统计上等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像创造性看似高度主观，但心理学研究表明创造性可建立在若干基本维度（新颖性、惊讶性、唤醒度）之上。深入理解图像创造性对研究与实际应用均有价值；若能建模人类如何用这些维度评估图像创造性，则可自动预测图像的人类创造性评分。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 用监督机器学习模型分别预测输入图像在新颖性、惊讶性、唤醒度三个维度上的强度；(2) 再用一个机器学习模型以预测出的维度强度为输入，输出整体创造性评分。训练数据来自Amazon Mechanical Turk上受控的人类被试实验，采集三个维度的强度标注与整体创造性评分。

Result: 实验结果表明，所估计的创造性评分与人类对测试图像的创造性评分在统计上无显著差异（统计等价）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：(1) 识别并验证用于估计图像创造性的三个心理学维度；(2) 自动估计输入图像新颖性、惊讶性、唤醒度强度的监督学习模型；(3) 由三维度强度预测创造性评分的监督学习模型；(4) 验证该方法准确性、优势与局限的实验方法。研究为图像创造性的自动评估提供了可解释的维度化路径。

Abstract: What makes an image creative? Although this may seem like a highly subjective decision, research in psychology suggests that creativity may be based on a set of fundamental creativity dimensions such as novelty, surprise, and arousal. A deeper understanding of image creativity can benefit both research and practical applications. Exploring how human viewers use creativity dimensions to evaluate image creativity may allow us to predict a target image's human-rated creativity automatically. We present a novel framework to estimate the perceived creativity of an image by: (1) predicting the strengths of an image's novelty, surprise, and arousal creativity dimensions, and (2) combining the creativity dimension strengths to estimate an image creativity score. Controlled human-subject experiments were conducted on Amazon Mechanical Turk to collect data to train supervised machine learning models to estimate an image's strengths on the three creativity dimensions. A final machine learning model takes the predicted dimension strengths as input to produce an estimated image creativity score. Results showed that our estimated creativity scores are statistically equivalent to human ratings of creativity for the images we tested. The novel contributions of our research are: (1) identification and validation of three psychological creativity dimensions used to estimate image creativity; (2) supervised machine learning models to automatically estimate novelty, surprise, and arousal strengths for an input image; (3) a supervised machine learning model to predict a creativity score from novelty, surprise, and arousal strengths for an input image, and (4) experimental methods to confirm the accuracy, advantages, and limitations of our approach.

</details>


### [15] [Federated Class Incremental Learning: A Pseudo Feature Based Approach Without Exemplars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15025)
*Min Kyoon Yoo,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FCLPF方法，用由原型生成的伪特征缓解联邦类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘，无需存储旧数据或使用GAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习通常假设数据预先固定，但现实中数据持续到达，导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: 基于原型生成伪特征以替代旧数据回放；避免依赖历史数据和GAN等重型生成模型，从而降低通信开销并提升效率。

Result: CIFAR-100上平均准确率51.87%、平均遗忘9.62%，优于基线47.72%/20.46%；TinyImageNet上37.56%/3.14%，优于基线27.69%/24.46%。

Conclusion: 原型驱动的伪特征是一种轻量高效、显著优于现有基线的联邦类增量学习抗遗忘方案。

Abstract: Federated learning often assumes that data is fixed in advance, which is unrealistic in many real-world scenarios where new data continuously arrives, causing catastrophic forgetting. To address this challenge, we propose FCLPF (Federated Class Incremental Learning with Pseudo Features), a method that uses pseudo features generated from prototypes to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. Our approach reduces communication costs and improves efficiency by eliminating the need for past data and avoiding computationally heavy models like GANs. Experimental results on CIFAR-100 show that FCLPF achieves an average accuracy of 51.87% and an average forgetting of 9.62%, significantly outperforming existing baselines with an average accuracy of 47.72% and forgetting of 20.46%. On TinyImageNet, FCLPF achieves 37.56% accuracy and 3.14% forgetting, compared to the baselines' 27.69% accuracy and 24.46% forgetting, demonstrating the superior performance of FCLPF.

</details>


### [16] [Validation of a Facial-Video Digital Hydration Biomarker Score for Tracking Hydration State Transitions in a Real-World Fasting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14993)
*Yudara Kularathne,D. M. J. Prathapa,Thanveer Ahamed,Janindu Athukorala,Thisara Perera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面部视频生活方式数字生物标志物评分可追踪水合状态转换方向：斋戒前后评分由36.6升至70.6（+33.9，d=2.03），98.1%受试者呈上升，体重与血清渗透压亚组提供支持性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水合状态影响生理与日常功能，但日常场景下的重复、实用测量手段仍有限，亟需非侵入、可重复的真实世界监测方法。

Method: 以斋戒作为长时间液体禁断模型，在三个验证层面评估面部视频数字生物标志物评分对"禁食（饮水前）—补液（饮水后）"转换的追踪能力：主队列104人/208段视频；嵌套体重亚组20人；嵌套血清渗透压亚组10人；并探索性比较水与电解质补液差异。

Result: 主队列评分均值由36.6升至70.6（变化+33.9，95% CI [+30.7, +37.1]，p<0.001，Cohen's d=2.03），102/104（98.1%）呈上升；体重亚组饮水后增加+0.74 kg（p<0.001），证实液体摄入；渗透压亚组下降-5.1 mOsm/kg（p<0.001），饮水后均在正常临床范围；水与电解质补液短期评分变化无统计学差异（p=0.247）。

Conclusion: 本研究不构成脱水的临床诊断检测，但支持该评分作为追踪真实世界水合状态变化方向的生活方式数字生物标志物。

Abstract: Hydration influences physiology and daily function, yet repeated practical measurement in everyday settings remains limited. We present initial real-world evidence that a facial-video lifestyle digital biomarker score can track the direction of hydration state transitions. Using Ramadan fasting as a model of prolonged fluid abstinence, we assessed whether the score tracks the transition between the fasting (pre-drink) state and the rehydrated (post-drink) state across three validation layers. In the primary cohort (104 participants, 208 videos), the score increased from a mean of 36.6 in the fasting (pre-drink) state to 70.6 in the rehydrated (post-drink) state (change = +33.9, 95% CI [+30.7, +37.1], p < 0.001, Cohen's d = 2.03), with 102 of 104 participants (98.1%) showing an upward shift. In a nested body-weight subgroup (20 participants), body weight increased by +0.74 kg after fluid intake (p < 0.001), confirming fluid uptake and providing supportive physical anchoring. In a nested serum osmolality subgroup (10 participants), osmolality decreased by -5.1 mOsm/kg (p < 0.001), with all post-drink values within the normal clinical range, providing exploratory biological support. An exploratory secondary comparison of water versus electrolyte rehydration showed no statistically significant difference in short-term score change (p = 0.247). This study does not establish a clinical diagnostic test for dehydration. Rather, these findings support the score as a lifestyle digital biomarker for tracking the direction of hydration state change in real-world settings.

</details>


### [17] [RSLiveBench-UAV: A Continually Evolving Vision-Language Benchmark for Aerial Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14953)
*Yue Zhou,Jiawei Liu,Jue Chen,Ziyang Gong,Haojia Wei,Jingxiang Li,Xue Jiang,Zhihang Zhong,Pai Peng,Xue Yang,Yansheng Li,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RSLiveBench-UAV —— 一个由持续采集的真实无人机影像驱动的、可持续更新的视觉语言基准，用于评测航空空间智能（ASI），并通过实体中心化的题目生产流程与确定性评分对 30 个模型进行系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空空间智能要求模型在变化视角与尺度下理解对象及空间关系；随着模型迭代，评测需要"新鲜的物理观测"，但新造空中空间问题必须反复建立实体身份、几何参考与跨视角对应关系，代价高昂，因此需要一个可周期性更新、可追溯的基准。

Method: 构建 RSLiveBench-UAV：更新协议仅纳入上一版本发布之后拍摄的影像并延迟答案公开；采用实体中心化生产流程，借助几何辅助关联将同一物理车辆（如车辆）的重复观测链接起来，复用人工核验属性，并结合视角相关标注与空间参考生成可追溯问题；全部任务采用确定性评分，不使用模型裁判。

Result: 在十个场景、35,491 条问题记录、覆盖 2D 感知、3D 感知、跨视角感知与推理共 17 项任务上评测 30 个通用、遥感与具身模型：18 个受测具身模型在 Overall 指标上均优于 3 个遥感对照模型，但最强具身模型为 43.29，远低于 GPT-6-Astra 的 59.88；细粒度车辆识别仍具挑战，性能随高度与相机俯仰角变化；通过回顾性场景组比较与滚动窗口重放刻画场景更新下分数模式的一致性。

Conclusion: RSLiveBench-UAV 将新鲜物理观测、实体中心化答案生产与系统化模型评测相衔接，以支持航空空间智能的持续评估；基准将于 https://rslivebench.space 发布。

Abstract: Aerial spatial intelligence (ASI) requires models to understand objects and spatial relationships across changing viewpoints and scales. Evaluating these abilities as models evolve calls for fresh physical observations, yet producing new aerial spatial questions requires repeatedly establishing entity identities, geometric references, and cross-view correspondences. We introduce \textbf{RSLiveBench-UAV}, a continually evolving vision-language benchmark driven by recurring acquisition of real UAV imagery. Its update protocol admits images captured after the preceding release and delays answer disclosure. An entity-centered production workflow uses geometry-assisted association to link repeated observations of physical vehicles, reuse human-verified attributes, and combine them with view-specific annotations and spatial references for traceable question generation. All tasks use deterministic scoring without model judges. We evaluate 30 general-purpose, remote-sensing, and embodied models on 35,491 question records from ten scenes, covering 17 tasks across 2D perception, 3D perception, cross-view perception, and reasoning. All 18 tested embodied models outperform the three selected remote-sensing comparators in Overall, although the strongest embodied model scores 43.29 versus 59.88 for GPT-6-Astra. Fine-grained vehicle recognition remains challenging, and performance varies across heights and camera pitches. Retrospective scene-group comparisons and rolling-window replay characterize score-pattern consistency under scene renewal. RSLiveBench-UAV connects fresh physical observations, entity-centered answer production, and systematic model evaluation to support continual assessment of ASI. Benchmark will release at https://rslivebench.space

</details>


### [18] [Hint-Informed Self-Distillation for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14931)
*Kunze Jiang,Yuchen Wang,Kang Li,Qixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hint-Informed Self-Distillation (HISD)，利用目标消歧证据做token级信用分配，仅调整正优势幅度，提升推理分割性能并大幅压缩推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理分割需在视觉相似物体中定位查询所指目标，而轨迹级奖励无法直接指出哪些token提供了相关视觉证据，缺乏细粒度信用分配。

Method: 离线用外部模型基于训练图像、查询与真值标注生成Segment Hints（涵盖目标身份、判别性属性、易混淆物体与锚定路径）；由hint-conditioned EMA教师对学生轨迹打分；在token支持集上计算教师-学生证据比，仅按比例调整正的分组相对优势幅度，非正优势保持不变；推理时学生无需提示与教师。

Result: 统一训练协议下，ReasonSeg测试集gIoU与cIoU较DPAD复现分别提升3.00与3.55个百分点；在ReasonSeg五个划分及RefCOCO系列的五个划分上分割指标均优于基线，有效推理片段平均token数减少80.17%–91.83%。

Conclusion: token级证据引导的自蒸馏可同时提升推理分割精度与推理简洁性，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation requires identifying the target referred to by a query among visually similar objects, yet trajectory-level rewards cannot directly indicate which tokens provide relevant visual evidence. We propose Hint-Informed Self-Distillation (HISD), which uses target-disambiguation evidence for token-level credit assignment. An external model generates Segment Hints offline from training images, queries, and ground-truth target annotations, organizing target identity, discriminative attributes, confusable objects, and anchoring paths. A hint-conditioned exponential moving average teacher scores the student's own trajectories, and HISD computes the teacher-to-student evidence ratio over a token support set to adjust only the magnitudes of positive group-relative advantages, leaving nonpositive advantages unchanged. This design encourages concise reasoning while retaining the information needed for target disambiguation, and the student requires neither hints nor a teacher at inference. Under a unified training protocol, HISD improves ReasonSeg test gIoU and cIoU over our reproduction of DPAD by 3.00 and 3.55 percentage points, respectively. Across the five evaluated splits of ReasonSeg and the RefCOCO family, HISD achieves higher segmentation metrics than this baseline and reduces the average token count of valid reasoning spans by 80.17 to 91.83 percent.

</details>


### [19] [In-orbit demonstration of object-based change detection applied to the maritime domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14857)
*Cristopher Castro Traba,Tom Hendrix,Amaury Perrocheau,Francesco Pasanisi,Finian Rogers,Aubrey Dunne,David Rijlaarsdam,Roberto Del Prete,Nicolas Longépé,Gabriele Meoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在CogniSAT-6任务上完成了星载目标级变化检测流水线的在轨验证，用于低时延海上监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为下一代Insight-as-a-Service与"提示-引导"架构提供实时星上自主情报生成能力基础。

Method: 三阶段流程：地面优化双次成像调度、星上AI实时船舶分割、多时相匹配；匹配采用匈牙利算法最优分配结合RANSAC空间与尺寸滤波剔除异常值。

Result: 在11种海上场景在轨验证中表现稳健，仅考虑船舶尺寸与位置时召回率与精确率均超过0.8，并可仅凭几何一致性跟踪合作目标与"暗"船。

Conclusion: 星上自主生成并响应实时情报的能力得到验证，为实时海上监测架构奠定基础。

Abstract: This article presents the successful in-orbit validation of an object-based change detection pipeline for low latency maritime monitoring onboard the CogniSAT-6 mission. We propose and validate a lightweight, three-phase operational sequence: optimized ground-based dual-acquisition scheduling, real-time onboard AI-based vessel segmentation, and multi-temporal matching. Upon acquiring consecutive images over a targeted Area of Interest (AoI), the onboard system correlates vessel detections using a hybrid matching approach that integrates the Hungarian algorithm for optimal assignment with RANSAC-driven spatial and size filters for outlier rejection. A series of in-orbit validations demonstrated robust performance across 11 diverse maritime scenarios, achieving valid recall and precision values (considering only vessel size and location) exceeding 0.8. The validation demonstrated the ability of the system to track both cooperative targets and uncooperative "dark" vessels strictly through geometric consistency. This capacity to autonomously generate and act upon real-time intelligence establishes a foundation for next-generation Insight-as-a-Service (IaaS) and tipping-and-cueing architectures.

</details>


### [20] [Sophon: A Procedural Diagnostic for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14838)
*J. Zach Gazak,Ryan Swindle,Justin Fletcher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出诊断基准 Sophon，揭示开源 VLM 在剥离先验后空间几何崩溃至随机水平，并将失败定位为中层"路由瓶颈"而非能力缺失，LoRA 微调可修复至前沿水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重 VLM 在 BLINK Spatial、What's Up CLEVR 等基准上得分 85–94%，但在去除物体、场景、纹理先验的简单几何问题上却退化至随机水平，说明现有基准可能被先验"污染"，其真实空间推理能力被高估，且失败机制尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 构建并开源 Sophon：程序化生成配对彩色点云刺激，覆盖三种坐标系（1D 线性、2D 笛卡尔、2D 极坐标）与四类问题（二分类、类别、比较、定量）。2) 在四种开源架构（LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-OneVision、Qwen2.5-VL、Idefics3）上与前沿模型及人类对比。3) 用线性探针分别探测 ViT 与 LLM 表示中是否保留空间信息。4) 用纯文本对照（点云表示为坐标集合）检验语言塔推理回路。5) 逐层注意力归因定位视觉整合层。6) 在视觉塔（ViT workspace/reader）、投影器、语言塔（LLM workspace/reader）上做单组件与分组微调以定位机制。

Result: 开源架构准确率聚集在 47–52%（随机水平），前沿模型（Opus 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-5.4）达 77–83%，人类 84–94%。ViT 与 LLM 线性探针分别以 83–85%、80–86% 恢复空间信息；纯文本对照较视觉刺激提升 +40 pp；注意力归因将视觉整合定位至语言塔中层。读出阶段模型已抬高正确答案集 token 的权重，但在此集合内随机选择。LoRA 微调后 7B 级 VLM 达到前沿模型的聚合准确率。

Conclusion: 开源 VLM 的空间失败并非能力缺口，而是中层"workspace"中的路由瓶颈：空间信号在 ViT/LLM 表示中可被探针解码，却未被正确路由至读出头；针对该回路的有目标微调即可修复，使 7B 模型达到前沿水平。

Abstract: Open-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) score 85-94% on spatial benchmarks (BLINK Spatial, What's Up CLEVR) yet collapse to chance on simple geometric questions when object, scene, and texture priors are stripped away. We introduce and open-source Sophon, a procedurally generated diagnostic of paired colored point-cloud stimuli across three coordinate systems (1D linear, 2D Cartesian, 2D polar) and four question types (binary, categorical, comparative, quantitative). Across four open architectures (LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-OneVision, Qwen2.5-VL, Idefics3), accuracy clusters at 47-52% while frontier models (Opus 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT-5.4) clear 77-83% and humans reach 84-94%. We use linear probes and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to trace the open-weight failures. Probes of the vision encoder (ViT) and language decoder (LLM) recover Sophon spatial information at 83-85% and 80-86% accuracy, respectively, and the language tower's reasoning circuit answers text-only controls (point clouds as sets of coordinates) at +40 pp over visual stimuli. Language tower per-layer attention attribution localizes visual integration to mid-stack layers. At readout, models elevate the correct answer-set tokens but select among them randomly. We localize the mechanism by tuning individual and grouped components across every architecture's stack, including the vision tower (ViT workspace and reader), the projector, and the language tower (LLM workspace and reader). These experiments demonstrate that this mid-stack workspace holds circuitry capable of organizing the spatial signal so that the frozen reader can decode it. LoRA fine-tuning adjusts that circuitry such that 7B-class VLMs answer Sophon spatial reasoning questions at frontier aggregate accuracy. This work suggests that these models do not have a capability gap for spatial reasoning but suffer from a routing bottleneck that targeted fine-tuning repairs.

</details>


### [21] [Reading the Unreadable: Text-Aware Image Super-Resolution Needs Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15449)
*Jaeseong Lee,Jinwoo Kim,Jinho Jeong,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文质疑TAISR是否本质上是纯视觉复原问题，指出严重退化的文本需依赖上下文与先验推理才能识别。作者提出ReasonText基准与GTTCA指标诊断出12个SR模型在"需推理文本"上的系统性短板，并提出免训练插件RTC（借助MLLM生成图像描述再送入文本条件SR模型），在6个模型上一致提升，且表明推理应成为TAISR的核心设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAISR方法将任务视为视觉复原问题，但严重退化的文本无法仅凭局部视觉证据读取，人类实际依赖图像上下文、逻辑模式与先验知识进行推理。因此作者提出根本性问题：TAISR是否需要推理？需要工具来验证并量化这一盲点。

Method: 提出两个诊断工具：(1) ReasonText——人工构建的基准，将需推理文本与可局部复原文本分离；(2) GTTCA（Ground-Truth Text-Crop Accuracy）——基于识别的指标，规避文本检测误差，直接衡量文本复原质量。基于诊断结论提出RTC（Reasoning Transfer via Captioning）：免训练即插即用方法，用外部MLLM推理退化文本并生成caption，再输入文本条件SR模型。

Result: 12个近期SR模型在需推理文本上均出现一致且显著的性能下降，揭示系统性盲点；文本条件SR模型在给定GT文本时复原显著更优；MLLM可借推理读取低分辨率退化文本。RTC在全部6个被评估的文本条件SR模型上均带来一致提升，尤其在需推理文本上；且带RTC的真实场景SR模型甚至超过带RTC的TAISR模型。

Conclusion: TAISR并非纯视觉复原问题，推理是其必要且当前被忽视的维度。将MLLM的推理能力以caption形式迁移给文本条件SR模型可有效弥补盲点，推理应成为未来TAISR的核心设计轴。

Abstract: Text-aware image super-resolution (TAISR) aims to recover high-resolution images from low-resolution inputs while preserving textual content. Existing methods treat this as a visual restoration problem, yet severely degraded text often cannot be read from local visual evidence alone. Humans instead read it by reasoning over image context, logical patterns, and prior knowledge. This raises a fundamental question: Does TAISR need reasoning? To answer this, we introduce two diagnostic tools. The first, ReasonText, is a human-curated benchmark that separates reasoning-required text from locally recoverable text. The second, Ground-Truth Text-Crop Accuracy (GTTCA), is a recognition-based metric that measures text restoration quality without text detection errors. Evaluating twelve recent super-resolution (SR) models, we find a consistent and substantial performance drop on reasoning-required text, revealing a systematic blind spot. Two observations point to a remedy. One is that text-conditioned SR models restore text substantially better when given ground-truth text. The other is that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can read degraded text in low-resolution images through reasoning. We therefore propose Reasoning Transfer via Captioning (RTC), a training-free plug-in that uses an external MLLM to infer the degraded text and feeds the resulting caption to a text-conditioned SR model. RTC consistently improves all six text-conditioned SR models we evaluate, especially on reasoning-required text. Surprisingly, a real-world SR model with RTC can even outperform the TAISR model with RTC, suggesting that reasoning should be a core design axis for future TAISR.

</details>


### [22] [Semantic Consistency of Vision Tokens: A Vision-Centric Perspective on Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15448)
*Nikola Đukić,Tim Lebailly,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出语义引导训练（SGT），通过在MLLM训练目标中加入语义不一致最小化，使语言对齐的视觉编码器同时提供语义一致的patch表示，在视觉中心任务上达到SOTA且保持文本任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言编码器擅长语言对齐，自监督编码器擅长高质量patch表示。MLLM多采用前者，但视觉中心稠密任务可能更需后者。作者想探究自监督模型能否助力MLLM的视觉中心任务，并揭示MLLM对视觉token语义一致性的依赖。

Method: 定义patch token的语义（不）一致性，划分语义一致token（SCT）与语义不一致token（SIT）；通过从MLLM输入中移除SCT或SIT进行探针实验，发现移除SCT性能下降更大，说明MLLM依赖语义一致性。据此提出SGT：将传统语言建模与语义不一致最小化相结合，用两个损失项联合信号推动编码器输出语义一致token，同时保持语言对齐。

Result: SGT在视觉中心基准上优于所有基线及使用专用视觉编码器的MLLM，同时在文本中心基准上保持强表现，在各任务平均上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 应从MLLM视角统一结合语言对齐与语义一致性，而非独立设计编码器，为更好的MLLM视觉编码器设计提供方向。

Abstract: In computer vision, vision-language and self-supervised encoders are two unique paradigms characterized by global language alignment and high-quality vision-only patch representations, respectively. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often use the former because of language alignment, despite the recent rise in vision-centric tasks resembling traditional dense vision tasks that would likely benefit from the high-quality patch representations, raising the question whether self-supervised models could aid MLLMs in vision-centric tasks. To investigate this, we define semantic (in)consistency of patch tokens from language-aligned encoders and identify two sets of tokens, semantically consistent (SCTs) and semantically inconsistent (SITs). Through systematic probing via the removal of SCTs or SITs from the input of modern MLLMs, we show higher reliance on SCTs than SITs, as removal of the former induces larger performance drops. This demonstrates that MLLMs benefit from semantic consistency of vision tokens. With this in mind, we devise a simple yet effective extension of the MLLM training objective that combines traditional language modeling with semantic guidance via minimization of semantic inconsistency. This strategy, named Semantically-Guided Training (SGT), combines the signals of both loss terms to push the vision encoder towards providing semantically consistent tokens, while preserving language alignment. Extensive evaluation shows that SGT outperforms all baselines and MLLMs with specialized vision encoders on vision-centric benchmarks while keeping strong scores on text-centric benchmarks, thus setting state of the art on average across all tasks. Our results highlight the importance of combining language alignment and semantic consistency through the lens of MLLMs rather than independently from them and pave the way towards the design of better MLLM vision encoders.

</details>


### [23] [CARE-Drive: Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15360)
*Xiao Ma,Yijun Chen,Zhiquan Hu,Jicheng Zhao,Yuming Li,Chuang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE-Drive，将轨迹精修分解为位置专家（有界度量航点残差修正）与朝向专家（以修正后路径为条件、由相邻角度增量重建朝向），在冻结骨干下经监督初始化与强化微调交替优化，在NAVISIM v1上取得88.8 PDMS，较未精修基轨迹提升2.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA自动驾驶模型的轨迹精修（候选精修、残差去噪、token校正）多为对整条轨迹的统一精修，忽视了不同物理量及其相互作用的差异，建模不够直观且简化空间有限。

Method: 将轨迹精修分解为航点残差修正与“位置条件化的朝向生成”；骨干通过模仿学习与未来token辅助任务输出预测表征和基轨迹；位置专家预测有界度量尺度航点残差，朝向专家在修正路径条件下由相邻角度增量重建车辆朝向；骨干冻结，两个专家经监督初始化与强化微调交替优化。

Result: 在NAVISIM v1上达到88.8 PDMS，较同一骨干生成的未精修基轨迹提升2.4点；消融实验验证了专业化专家与分阶段训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 以清晰的物理专业化简化精修建模，同时在预测与优化阶段保持位置—朝向的条件耦合，是一种有效且可解释的自车轨迹精修范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models bring multimodal understanding and world knowledge to end-to-end autonomous driving. To further improve the quality of predicted trajectories, existing studies introduce candidate refinement, residual denoising, and token correction. However, these methods typically focus on unified refinement of the entire trajectory, paying limited attention to the distinct physical quantities and their interactions. We further investigate physical specialization and coordination in trajectory refinement, centering on position and heading for simpler and more intuitive correction modeling. To this end, we propose CARE-Drive (Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories), which decomposes trajectory refinement into waypoint residual correction and position-conditioned heading generation. Through imitation learning and a future-token auxiliary task, the backbone provides predictive representations and a base trajectory. The position expert predicts bounded metric waypoint residuals, while the heading expert reconstructs vehicle orientation from adjacent angular increments conditioned on the corrected path. With the backbone frozen, the two experts are optimized alternately through supervised initialization and reinforcement fine-tuning. This design simplifies refinement through clear physical specialization while preserving conditional coupling during prediction and optimization. On NAVSIM v1, CARE-Drive achieves 88.8 PDMS, improving by 2.4 points over the unrefined base trajectories generated by the same backbone. Ablation studies further support the effectiveness of specialized experts and staged training.

</details>


### [24] [Bridging Update Coordination and Flatness in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15165)
*Chongwei He,Shichen Huang,Jiayun Li,Deyuan Lin,Wangze Jiang,Shengjia Li,Xu Wang,Xiaofan Li,Zhi-Ri Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一联邦学习框架，将更新协调与平坦性优化解耦桥接，客户端沿同一轨迹分别累积下降信号与平坦性修正，服务端以独立状态协调后融合为全局更新，实验表明组合方案普遍优于单独更新协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的数据异构会导致客户端更新冲突并损害泛化；更新协调可减少更新间干扰，平坦性优化可降低参数扰动敏感性以提升泛化，但现有联合设计多绑定特定算法，难以灵活组合两类机制。

Method: 构建统一框架：客户端在同一本地轨迹上分别累积下降信号与平坦性修正；服务端以独立状态分别协调两组信号，再将输出融合为全局更新；通过轨迹信号与模型更新间的显式转换，使不同协调策略可与兼容的本地或全局引导平坦性机制配合，且不改变推理架构。

Result: 所评估的组合方案在平均精度与最差客户端精度上普遍优于仅使用更新协调的基线，验证了框架组合互补优化机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了更新协调与平坦性优化的模块化解耦与灵活组合，可作为通用插件式方案提升异构联邦学习的泛化性能与客户端公平性。

Abstract: Federated learning combines complementary knowledge across clients to alleviate local data limitations without sharing raw samples. However, data heterogeneity can cause conflicting updates and hinder generalization. Update coordination reduces interference among client updates, while flatness optimization reduces sensitivity to parameter perturbations to improve generalization. Existing joint designs largely target specific algorithms, limiting flexible combinations of these approaches. To address this problem, we propose a unified federated learning framework that bridges update coordination and flatness optimization. Clients separately accumulate descent signals and flatness corrections along the same local trajectory. The server coordinates both groups with independent states before fusing their outputs into a global update. Explicit conversion between trajectory signals and model updates enables different coordination strategies to work with compatible local or globally guided flatness mechanisms without changing the inference architecture. Experiments show that the evaluated combinations generally improve average and worst-client accuracy over update coordination alone, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in combining these complementary optimization mechanisms.

</details>


### [25] [PCLSegNet: Anatomy-Aware Deep Learning Framework for MRI-based Pancreatic Cyst Segmentation and Size Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15278)
*Hanyi Zhang,Tongyu Wang,Manuel Debic,Markus Wennmann,Johannes Haubold,Tamara Schikowski,Wilfried Reichardt,Fabian Bamberg,Christopher L. Schlett,Steffen Ringhof,Thoralf Niendorf,Tobias Pischon,Michael Leitzmann,Carsten Oliver Schmidt,Mark Oliver Wielpütz,Thomas Kroencke,Josua A. Decker,Stefan N. Willich,Thomas Keil,Rafael Mikolajczyk,Miriam Klauß,Hans-Ulrich Kauczor,Philipp Mayer,Bettina K. Budai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解剖感知两阶段深度学习框架，在腹部MRI上自动分割胰腺囊性病变（PCLs），性能优于TotalSegmentator与nnU-Net等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCLs早期检测与随访至关重要，其中黏液性囊性肿瘤（MCNs）和导管内乳头状黏液性肿瘤存在较高恶变风险。现有AI研究多依赖CT，而MRI软组织对比度更优、更适合PCLs评估，因此需要针对MRI的自动化分割方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用公开的胰腺分割模型定位胰腺；第二阶段提出解剖感知的PCLSegNet，引入解剖先验引导胰腺与PCLs分割；针对类别不平衡设计类特定误差加权（CSEW）损失函数；并通过解剖约束进行后处理。

Result: 胰腺分割Dice显著高于TotalSegmentator等多器官分割模型；PCLs分割的归一化表面Dice（NSD）与召回率优于nnU-Net等先进模型，并明显降低假阴性率。

Conclusion: 该解剖感知两阶段框架有效提升MRI上胰腺与PCLs的自动分割性能，有助于PCLs的自动早期检测与无创影像学评估。

Abstract: Early detection and follow-up of pancreatic cystic lesions (PCLs) are crucial, as some of their subtypes, such as mucinous cystic neoplasms (MCNs) and intraductal papillary mucinous neoplasms, carry a significant risk of malignant transformation. Although most AI studies investigating PCLs rely on computed tomography (CT) scans, magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) offers superior soft tissue contrast, making it highly suitable for PCLs assessment. In this work, we propose an anatomy-aware two-stage deep learning framework for automated PCLs segmentation on abdominal MRI scans. First, a publicly available pancreas segmentation model is used to localize the pancreas. Then, we introduce the anatomyaware PCLSegNet, which incorporates anatomical priors as guidance for pancreas and PCLs segmentation. To address the class imbalance problem, we develop a Class-Specific ErrorWeighted (CSEW) loss function. Postprocessing is applied through anatomical constraints.Our proposed method outperforms multi-organ segmentation models such as TotalSegmentator, achieving substantially higher Dice scores for pancreas segmentation. Moreover, it also achieves a superior normalized surface Dice (NSD) and recall for PCLs segmentationcompared to other state-of-the-art models like nnU-Net, while also notably reducing the false negative rate, facilitating the automatic early detection and the noninvasive imaging-based assessment of PCLs.

</details>


### [26] [Where is My Mug? Training-Free Framework for Personalized Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15216)
*Quang Qui-Vinh Nguyen,Hung Nguyen,Rang Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有个性化图像分割方法的高分是单实例基准造成的假象；真实多实例场景下瓶颈在召回而非掩码质量，为此提出免训练模块化系统及 top-K Sinkhorn 匹配与多实例基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化图像分割需在多个视觉相似实例中区分出与参考对象匹配的目标。已有方法报告的强指标主要来自单实例基准，在存在同类相似物的真实场景中，point-prompt 方法常无法把正确实例作为提示生成，正确实例从未被提示召回，这属于召回瓶颈而非掩码质量问题。

Method: 提出免训练、模块化流水线：先用现成的类无关检测器生成高召回目标候选；再用基础模型特征（如 BLIP-2）将每个候选与参考进行匹配；最后用 SAM 对选中框进行掩码精修。匹配环节提出 top-K Sinkhorn：一种仅依据最自信匹配 patch 打分候选的 patch 级距离，通过裁剪 Sinkhorn 对偶势，以低于 1/100 的计算代价获得部分最优传输的鲁棒性，可置于逐候选循环内。

Result: 在新建的真实多实例基准数据集以及 PerSeg、PerMIS、DAVIS 上，该方法相较 PerSAM、P²SAM、PDM 提升最高约 48 mIoU，同时运行时间相当且参数量更少。

Conclusion: 建立了面向真实个性化分割的强可部署免训练基线，并指出多实例召回是 PIS 的关键评测与设计维度。

Abstract: \textbf{Personalized Image Segmentation (PIS)} aims to segment the specific object in a target image that matches a user-provided reference. Unlike category-based segmentation or few-shot segmentation, PIS must discriminate between multiple visually similar instances, a requirement central to deployed applications such as augmented reality, personalized content editing, robotics manipulation, inventory tracking, and fine-grained visual retrieval. We show that the strong numbers reported by existing PIS methods are largely an artifact of single-instance benchmarks: when evaluated on realistic scenes with several same-category look-alikes, prior point-prompt methods collapse, because the correct instance is frequently never surfaced as a prompt. We trace this to a recall bottleneck rather than a mask-quality one, and address it with a training-free, modular system. The pipeline first generates high-recall object proposals using an off-the-shelf class-agnostic detector, then matches each proposal to the reference using foundation-model features (e.g., BLIP-2), and finally refines the selected box with SAM. For robust matching we propose \textbf{top-$K$ Sinkhorn}, a patch-level distance that scores each candidate only on its most confidently matched patches; trimming the Sinkhorn dual potentials recovers the robustness of partial optimal transport at more than $100\times$ lower cost, keeping it inside the per-proposal candidate loop. To evaluate this regime, we introduce \textbf{\datasetname}, a realistic multi-instance benchmark. Across \datasetname, PerSeg, PerMIS, and DAVIS, our system surpasses PerSAM, $\mathrm{P}^2\mathrm{SAM}$, and PDM by up to $\sim\!48$ mIoU while matching their runtime and using fewer parameters, establishing a strong, deployable training-free baseline for real-world personalized segmentation.

</details>


### [27] [Split-and-scale Latent 3D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16126)
*Dorian Chan,Muhammed Kocabas,Xiaoming Zhao,Oncel Tuzel,Jen-Hao Rick Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 split-and-scale 方法：利用三维场景的坐标连续函数表示，在推理时将预训练 tokenizer 反复应用于任意小的子区域，从而在不增加训练成本的前提下大幅扩展隐表示容量并提升重建细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 隐表示受限于 latent 容量，难以捕捉复杂物体的精细细节；训练时扩展容量受显存限制、代价高昂，而推理时简单增加 latent 数量又无法有效利用新增容量。

Method: 关键观察是将 3D 场景表示为 3D 坐标的连续函数后，单个预训练 tokenizer 可在推理阶段应用于场景的任意小空间子区域，把同等 latent 容量分配给更小的空间范围。作者通过大量消融实验分析计算量、显存、latent 容量与重建质量之间的权衡。

Result: 该推理时扩展方法的重建质量可与超出单卡显存限制的更大模型竞争；并在此基础上训练了自回归 3D 生成器，其性能与当前最优基线相当。

Conclusion: split-and-scale 以可承受的训练成本实现了推理时的有效容量扩展，兼顾高质量重建与生成建模，为 3D 形状建模提供了一条可扩展路径。

Abstract: Latent 3D representations have significantly expanded the capabilities of modern shape modeling, enabling compact encoding of 3D scenes and supporting powerful 3D generative models. Despite this progress, current representations struggle to capture the fine details of complex objects, fundamentally limited by the capacity of their latents. However, scaling latent capacity at training time is computationally expensive and often infeasible under memory limits, and naively increasing the number of latents at inference fails to make effective use of the added capacity. In this paper, we address both challenges with \textit{split-and-scale}, a method that effectively expands latent capacity at inference while keeping training cost tractable. Our key observation is that by representing 3D scenes as continuous functions of 3D coordinates, a single pretrained tokenizer can be applied at inference to arbitrarily small sub-regions of a scene, allocating the same latent capacity to a smaller spatial extent and thus capturing far higher detail. We validate this insight by conducting extensive ablations to study the trade-offs between compute, memory, latent capacity, and reconstruction quality, demonstrating our inference-time scaling method achieves quality competitive with much larger models that exceed single-GPU memory limits. We further demonstrate that split-and-scale supports high-quality generative modeling, training an autoregressive 3D generator that performs competitively with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [28] [WorldDraft: If Code Builds the World, Can Video Models Just Render It?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16217)
*Zhiyu Hou,Wen-Jie Shu,Harold Haodong Chen,Weilin Ruan,Chenfei Liao,Liuzhou Zhang,Disen Lan,Xiaogang Xu,Qi Chen,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WorldDraft 基准（432 个严格配对样本，覆盖 48 个可执行 4D 世界族、6 类动态挑战），系统评测视频模型作为"生成式渲染器"的能力，并提出即插即用的 WorldDraft-Primer 视觉提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态编码智能体将 4D 世界构建与视觉生成解耦，使视频模型从"世界生成器"转变为"生成式渲染器"。核心问题在于：若世界由代码构建，视频模型能否仅可靠地渲染它？可靠渲染依赖两项互补标准——世界保持性（遵守既定 4D 运动学与相机路径）与视觉实现性（随时间持续绑定文本指定的目标属性），但二者是否可兼得尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建 WorldDraft 基准：432 个严格配对的测试用例，覆盖 48 个可执行 4D 世界族与 6 大动态挑战类别；采用 3×3 因子设计，在相同动态条件下正交交叉"草案粒度"（抽象代理→预演 previz）与"外观对齐度"（中性→冲突），并对 12 个代表性模型进行广泛评测。此外提出 WorldDraft-Primer，一种可插拔的视觉提示策略，作用于冻结的视频渲染器。

Result: 实验发现三点：(I) 世界保持性与视觉实现性之间存在尖锐的操作性张力；(II) 更丰富的草案脚手架能锚定结构，却反常地损害外观保真度；(III) 冲突源线索引发严重源泄漏（最高 64%），暴露跨模态解耦的脆弱性。WorldDraft-Primer 在冻结渲染器上对两项指标均有一致提升。

Conclusion: WorldDraft 与 WorldDraft-Primer 共同为程序化 4D 仿真与视频生成的衔接奠定基础，推动物理落地的生成式渲染研究。

Abstract: Multimodal coding agents decouple 4D world construction from visual generation, repurposing video models from world generators toward generative renderers. This division of labor raises a fundamental question: if code builds the world, can video models just render it? A reliable generative renderer hinges on two complementary criteria: (i) world preservation, retaining prescribed 4D kinematics and camera paths; and (ii) visual realization, persistently binding text-specified target attributes over time. To evaluate this capability, we introduce WorldDraft, a benchmark of $432$ strictly paired test cases across $48$ executable 4D world families spanning six dynamic challenge categories. Under a $3 \times 3$ factorial design, WorldDraft orthogonally crosses draft granularity (abstract proxy to previs) and appearance alignment (neutral to conflicting) under identical dynamics. Extensive evaluations across $12$ representative models reveal that: (I) world preservation and visual realization remain in stark operational tension; (II) richer draft scaffolding anchors structure but paradoxically impairs appearance fidelity; and (III) conflicting source cues trigger severe source leakage (up to $64\%$), exposing fragile cross-modal disentanglement. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose WorldDraft-Primer, a plug-and-play visual prompting strategy that consistently improves both objectives across frozen video renderers. Together, WorldDraft and WorldDraft-Primer lay a foundation for bridging programmatic 4D simulation with video generation, advancing physically grounded generative rendering.

</details>


### [29] [Topology-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting for Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16012)
*Leonardo Vanni,Stefano Gasperini,Federico Tombari,Chiara Plizzari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向自我中心视频拓扑变化（分裂、融合、打开、关闭）的4D重建基准TopoEgo，以及拓扑感知重建方法Topo-4DGS，通过部件级独立刚体变换与时变高斯激活/失活机制解决4DGS在拓扑变化下的伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目自我中心视频中的动态场景重建对具身智能至关重要，但现有4D Gaussian Splatting依赖对固定表示的形变，无法处理切割、装配、开合等引起拓扑变化（分裂、融合、表面显现）的日常交互，导致严重视觉伪影。

Method: Topo-4DGS：为交互的各部件分配独立的刚体变换以联合建模部件级运动；同时建模时变表面支撑，随表面出现、消失或分离动态激活/失活高斯，从而突破固定表示形变的限制。并构建首个自我中心视频拓扑变化基准TopoEgo。

Result: 大量实验表明Topo-4DGS在各类拓扑变化场景下显著优于现有最先进4DGS方法，尤其在事件发生后的帧上优势明显。

Conclusion: 指出拓扑变化是4DGS的关键失效模式，提出基准与拓扑感知方法，验证部件级运动建模与高斯动态增删的有效性；代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes and objects from monocular egocentric video is crucial for embodied AI, yet existing 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) approaches struggle with everyday interactions involving topological changes, such as cutting, assembly, opening, or closing. Since 4DGS methods rely on deforming a fixed representation, events that split, merge, or reveal surfaces cause severe visual artifacts. To address this challenge, we introduce TopoEgo, the first benchmark dedicated to topological changes in egocentric video (covering fission, fusion, open, and close), alongside Topo-4DGS, a topology-aware reconstruction method. Topo4DGS jointly models part-level motion and time-varying surface support by assigning independent rigid transformations to interacting parts and dynamically activating or deactivating Gaussians as surfaces appear, disappear, or separate. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Topo-4DGS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 4DGS approaches across topological changes, especially on post-event frames. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [30] [DEDCA: Test-Time Adaptation for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16307)
*Hao Tan,Qimin Zhang,Lihuang Fang,Kebing Jin,Jinghui Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DEDCA测试时自适应框架，通过CLIP语义证据与低层痕迹证据的双流融合及证据间分歧作为无标签自适应信号，实现泛化AI生成图像检测，在GenImage达92.0%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中测试样本常来自未见生成器、偏离源训练分布；现有检测器依赖单一证据（语义表征或低层生成痕迹），对应线索失效时脆弱；且适配新生成器通常需目标标签或重训练，代价高、不实用。

Method: 构建双证据检测器：CLIP语义证据 + 由shuffle熵与残差统计提取的痕迹证据；引入可靠性感知门控进行样本级自适应融合；以两路证据间的分歧作为无标签自适应信号，驱动测试时自适应，无需目标标签，也避免盲信单一预测流。

Result: 在GenImage基准上优于SOTA AI生成图像检测器与测试时自适应方法，平均准确率92.0%。

Conclusion: 双证据融合与分歧约束的自适应机制可有效应对分布偏移，为无标签、免重训练的泛化AI生成图像检测提供可行方案。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors face significant challenges when deployed in real-world environments, particularly when test samples are produced by unseen generators that deviate from the source training distribution. Existing detectors often rely on a single type of evidence, such as semantic representations or low-level generation traces, making them vulnerable when the corresponding cue becomes unreliable. Moreover, adapting detectors to newly emerging generators usually requires target labels or retraining, which is costly and impractical. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Evidence Disagreement-Constrained Adaptation (DEDCA), a test-time adaptation framework for generalized AI-generated image detection. DEDCA constructs a dual-evidence detector by combining CLIP-based semantic evidence with trace evidence extracted from shuffled entropy and residual statistics, and introduces a reliability-aware gate to perform sample-specific adaptive fusion. Our key idea is to exploit the disagreement between the two evidence streams as an unlabeled adaptation signal, enabling the detector to adjust to target-domain shifts without relying on target labels or blindly trusting a single prediction stream. Experiments show that DEDCA achieves superior performance on the GenImage benchmark by outperforming state-of-the-art AI-generated image detectors and test-time adaptation methods with 92.0\% average accuracy.

</details>


### [31] [FlowIR: Depth-driven Rectified Flow for Resolving Thermal Ambiguity in Infrared Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16269)
*Xingyue Zhu,Qinyun Mei,Zhexi Min,Yang Zou,Xingyuan Li,Zhiying Jiang,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FlowIR，一种深度驱动的校正流（rectified flow）框架，通过注入 3D 几何线索解决低分辨率红外图像中多热源混叠的恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率红外图像中多个热辐射源常塌缩为模糊、重叠的响应，热源难以区分；现有超分方法仅依赖模糊的 2D 证据"幻觉"结构，导致热漂移、边界粘连，在红外退化下恢复不可靠。

Method: 1) 伪 3D 深度提升（pseudo-3D depth lifting）与深度引导的 3D 卷积，用于解耦重叠前景目标；2) 簇级帧间注意力（cluster-wise inter-attention）抑制跨热源特征污染；3) 热-几何一致性损失（Thermal-Geometry Consistency Loss）在精化阶段约束几何对齐；4) 以轻量级校正流为骨干，实现少步生成。

Result: 仅需少数采样步即可恢复红外图像，输出边界清晰分离、抗退化且几何保真的高分辨率结果。

Conclusion: 将 3D 几何先验引入生成式红外超分可有效缓解热歧义，兼顾效率与几何保真度。

Abstract: Distinguishing different heat sources in low-resolution infrared (IR) images is inherently difficult, as multiple thermal emitters often collapse into ambiguous and overlapping responses. Existing super-resolution methods hallucinate structures from such ambiguous 2D evidence, leading to thermal drift, boundary merging, and unreliable restoration under infrared degradations. We propose FlowIR, a depth-driven rectified flow framework that resolves thermal ambiguity by injecting 3D geometric cues into the generation process. Pseudo-3D depth lifting and depth-guided 3D convolutions disentangle overlapping foreground objects, while cluster-wise inter-attention suppresses cross-source feature contamination. A Thermal-Geometry Consistency Loss further enforces geometric alignment during refinement. Built upon a lightweight rectified flow backbone, FlowIR restores IR images in only a few steps, producing sharply separated, degradation-robust, and geometrically faithful high-resolution results.

</details>


### [32] [ATune: Unsupervised Photo Retouching Through Image-Editor Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16236)
*Divyanshu Goyal,Gabriel Ruiz,Amir Meisami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ATune：仅用10M未标注照片自构造"损坏-修复"图像对，学习13个可量化属性（亮度、对比度、清晰度等）的目标值，再以声明式搜索驱动现成编辑器滑块完成修图；54M参数、零专家编辑训练，其修图在人类评测中与前沿大模型持平，并在FiveK/PPR10K上与有监督专用模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 照片修图模型依赖"原图-专家修图"成对数据，而专家编辑成本高，两大公开数据集FiveK与PPR10K合计仅16,161张原图，规模受限；相比之下，高质量照片本身极易获取（商业图库、Pixabay、Unsplash等拥有数百万张）。因此希望摆脱对专家编辑对的依赖，直接从大量优质照片中学习修图。

Method: 1）从优质照片出发，模拟相机缺陷或随意编辑对每张图施加"损坏"，构造同一照片的差版本/好版本对，从而获得大规模弱监督信号；2）ATune学习13个可测量属性应如何变化以提升画质；3）编辑采用声明式表述：模型只输出各属性的目标值，再由简单搜索调整开箱即用编辑器（RawTherapee、darktable）的滑块直至达到目标；4）输出为人类可读可改的滑块取值列表，同一模型可驱动任意支持这些操作的编辑器；5）在10M商业图库照片上训练，FiveK、PPR10K、SPAQ上测试，并以9个质量评判器及157名人类受试者（9,253次二选一）评估。

Result: 与前沿模型在同一编辑器下修同一照片比较，人类更偏好ATune而非GPT-6 Astra（54.3%），与Claude Opus 5基本持平（50.5%）；与在本数据集专家编辑上有监督训练的专用模型比较（FiveK上的NeurOP、PPR10K上的AdaInt），人类选择ATune编辑的比例为46.3%。

Conclusion: ATune（仅54M参数、未使用任何专家编辑）在设置编辑器滑块这一任务上已可比肩前沿大模型，并在有监督专用修图模型的"主场"上表现接近；表明"自构造损坏对+属性声明式编辑+搜索驱动现成编辑器"是替代昂贵专家编辑数据的可行路线，且编辑结果透明、可解释、可人工微调。

Abstract: Photo retouching models are trained on pairs of original and retouched photographs, usually edited by experts. Expert edits are costly, so the two main public sets, FiveK and PPR10K, hold only 16,161 originals. Good photographs on their own are far easier to find: commercial stock collections and free ones such as Pixabay and Unsplash hold millions. We learn retouching from them alone: we damage each photograph the way a camera or a careless edit would, which gives a worse and a better version of the same photograph. From these pairs, our model, ATune, learns how 13 measured attributes, such as brightness, contrast and sharpness, should change to raise a photograph's quality. The edit is declarative: ATune sets target values for the attributes, and a simple search adjusts the sliders of an out-of-the-box photo editor until the photograph reaches them. Every edit is a list of slider values a person can read and adjust. The same model can drive any editor that makes those changes; we test RawTherapee and darktable. We train on 10M photographs from a commercial stock collection and test on FiveK, PPR10K and SPAQ. Nine quality judges score every edit, and in a user study 157 human raters made 9,253 choices between our edit and another method's edit of the same photograph. When Claude Opus 5 and GPT-6 Astra edit the same photographs in the same editor, raters prefer our edits to GPT-6 Astra's (54.3%) and rate them even with Claude's (50.5%). We also compare with supervised retouchers on their home ground: NeurOP on FiveK and AdaInt on PPR10K, each trained on that set's own expert edits. Raters chose our edits in 46.3% of those comparisons. ATune, a 54M-parameter model trained without a single expert edit, now matches frontier models at setting an editor's sliders.

</details>


### [33] [Disentangled Embedding Optimization for Diffusion-Based Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16224)
*Hongcheng Li,Yucan Zhou,Yibin Wang,Xiaoyan Gu,Bo Li,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦嵌入优化（DEO）框架，用可训练嵌入替代聚类原型作为扩散模型输入，以提升数据集蒸馏样本的多样性与代表性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的数据集蒸馏方法采用类内聚类并以簇原型作为扩散输入，但原始样本在同一类别中近似均匀分布，导致簇边界附近样本纠缠、簇原型相互靠近、簇间可分性下降；扩散模型因此接收到过于相似的隐输入，生成的合成数据多样性受限。

Method: DEO以可训练嵌入替换静态聚类：优化嵌入以匹配原始数据分布的高斯统计量（均值与协方差），并设计多样性正则项促使嵌入之间相互分离，从而增强类内变异性。

Result: 大量实验表明，DEO生成的蒸馏数据比现有基于聚类的扩散方法更多样、更具代表性，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能并具备更优的泛化性。

Conclusion: 将聚类原型替换为统计量对齐且多样正则化的可训练嵌入，是缓解扩散式数据集蒸馏多样性瓶颈的有效且简洁的方案。

Abstract: Dataset distillation aims to compress large-scale datasets into compact synthetic subsets that enable efficient model training with comparable performance to the original data. Recent advances show that diffusion models are highly effective for this task due to their strong generative capability. Existing diffusion-based approaches typically perform class-wise clustering and use the obtained cluster prototypes as diffusion inputs to generate distilled samples. However, the uniformly distributed original samples in a category inevitably lead to entanglement near cluster boundaries, pulling the cluster prototypes closer and reducing the inter-cluster separation. Consequently, the diffusion model receives overly similar latent inputs, resulting in synthetic data with limited diversity. To address this issue, we propose Disentangled Embedding Optimization (DEO), a simple yet effective framework that replaces static clustering with trainable embeddings. These embeddings are optimized to match the Gaussian statistics (i.e., mean and covariance) of the original data distribution, while a diversity regularizer is designed to encourage separation among these embeddings to improve the intra-class variability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed DEO can generate more diversified and representative distilled data than existing cluster-based diffusion methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and superior generalization across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [34] [Honest Fusion Evaluation for Cross-Domain Deepfake Detection: Leakage Audits and Tail Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14856)
*Luke Collins,Chang-Tsun Li,Duc Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在22个数据集的冻结专家测试台上，路由与特征融合相对校准全局平均的增益主要是评测泄漏造成的假象；实际应全局校准融合、按完整数据集族留出并测量尾部表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域视频深伪检测普遍采用冻结基础模型专家组合（校准分数平均、逐视频路由、拼接特征学习头），文献常称路由与特征融合优于简单平均。作者质疑这些增益的真实性，旨在通过严格的评测协议检验其是否源于评测伪影（泄漏），并厘清各类组合器的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建22数据集冻结专家测试台；提出"角点定理"：因AUC仅依赖分数排序，分数空间路由的最优上界为读取测试标签选专家的oracle，不可实现且不用于给真实路由与校准全局平均排序，故路由的否定依赖实证。特征融合读取原始特征，位于定理之外，采用实证审计。设计2×2过度留出与评测粒度实验，对比泄漏式留出（留出一数据集时同族共享身份/真实样本泄漏，高估路由；留出单一操纵时该数据集真实样本残留训练，高估特征融合）与诚实的整族留出。以显著性计数（12/12 vs 0/12）定位+2.6pp特征融合"胜利"仅来自折叠方式；并考察尾部困难视频/族随专家增加的表现（全专家共误15.2%→2.3%）及InvB全局校准融合在worst-family AUC、选择性风险AURC、低假阳召回上的表现。

Result: 没有任何被测路由器优于校准全局平均；特征融合的+2.6pp增益在诚实整族留出折叠下完全消失（泄漏折叠12/12显著，诚实折叠0/12显著）。但融合在尾部仍有效：随专家增加，全专家均误判的视频比例由15.2%降至2.3%；全局校准融合InvB在worst-family AUC、AURC及低假阳召回上稳定优于最佳单专家，且跨协议稳健。

Conclusion: 路由与特征融合的报告增益大多为评测泄漏伪影。实践建议：全局校准并融合、按完整数据集族留出、测量尾部性能。在排序指标下的分数空间路由中，诚实评测与专家多样性比组合器复杂度更重要。
