Abstract: Cross-domain video deepfake detection increasingly uses frozen foundation-model experts, combined by calibrated score averaging, a per-video router picking one expert, or a learned head over concatenated features. Expressive routing and feature fusion are often reported to beat plain averaging. Across a 22-dataset frozen-expert testbed, we find those gains are largely evaluation artifacts. A corner theorem locates the gap routing aims to close: because AUC depends only on score ordering, the best any score-space router can do is an oracle reading the test label to pick each video's expert, a ceiling no realisable, label-blind router reaches. The theorem does not order real routers against a calibrated global average; the case against routing is empirical, and no router we test beats that average. Feature fusion reads raw features, falls outside the theorem, and is audited empirically. Reported gains trace to leakage: holding out one dataset leaks siblings sharing identities and reals (over-crediting routing), while holding out one manipulation keeps that dataset's reals in training (over-crediting feature fusion). A $2\times 2$ over fold and evaluation granularity pins the $+2.6$pp feature-fusion "win" to the fold alone: 12/12 significant under leaky folds vs. 0/12 under honest family-held-out folds, where both gains vanish. Fusion still pays off in the tail of hard videos and families: as experts are added, videos every expert misclassifies drop from 15.2% to 2.3%, and global calibrated fusion (InvB) beats the best single expert on worst-family AUC, selective risk (AURC), and low-false-positive recall, stably across protocols. Practitioners should calibrate and combine globally, hold out whole dataset families, and measure tail performance. For score-space routing under ranking metrics, honest evaluation and expert diversity matter more than combiner complexity.

</details>


### [35] [Paired Geometric Supervision for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14851)
*Luke Collins,Chang-Tsun Li,Duc Thanh Nguyen,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OrthoPairGTL 几何训练框架，基于部分冻结 DINOv3 骨干，通过正交配对分解、超球正则与集合级一致性目标训练 PEFT 适配器，在 17 个跨域数据集的统一预处理下重新评估六个迁移学习检测器，GSD-LoRA 以 92.82% 平均准确率匹配或超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域深度伪造检测器报告的性能差距可能源于各自的预处理与流程调优，而非方法本身；需要统一评测口径，并设计更稳定的参数高效训练策略以提升跨域泛化。

Method: 以约 300M 参数的部分冻结 DINOv3 为骨干；(1) 用作者发布权重、单一预处理流程在 17 个跨域数据集（2018–2025）上复评六个检测器（Forensics Adapter、Effort、三个 GenD 变体、FakeSTormer）；(2) 建模真实-伪造配对样本间的变换，用正交配对分解、超球正则及 GSD-LoRA 变体的集合级一致性目标训练 PEFT 适配器。

Result: GSD-LoRA 峰值后验证退化比同参数量的朴素 LoRA 浅 4.2 倍，训练期时间平均验证 AUC 高 1.82 pp；17 数据集平均 92.82% 对比最强基线 91.58%（配对 Wilcoxon p=0.015，13/17 单数据集胜出），在 DSv1.1、DSv2 等重演类基准上增益最大。严格参数预算下仅调 LayerNorm（≈0.03% 骨干参数）最稳定，均值 91.97%。

Conclusion: 统一评测口径下，几何正则化的 PEFT 训练可稳定超越现有迁移学习检测器并提升跨域泛化，且极低参数量的 LayerNorm 调优已具竞争力。

Abstract: We present OrthoPairGTL, a geometric training framework for cross-domain deepfake detection, built on a partially frozen DINOv3 backbone ($\sim$300M parameters), and make three contributions: (1) We re-evaluate six recent transfer-learning detectors (Forensics Adapter, Effort, three GenD variants and FakeSTormer) using the authors' released checkpoints under a single preprocessing pipeline across 17 cross-domain datasets (2018-2025), so that the reported gaps reflect method rather than per-method pipeline tuning. (2) We model the transformation between paired real and fake samples, training Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) adapters with an orthogonal pair decomposition, hyperspherical regularisation, and (in the GSD-LoRA variant) a set-level coherence objective. (3) Under LoRA, this regularisation gives a more stable optimisation profile: GSD-LoRA's post-peak validation degradation is $4.2\times$ shallower than plain LoRA at the same parameter footprint, and the time-averaged validation AUC across training is 1.82 pp higher. On the 17 datasets, GSD-LoRA matches or exceeds every re-evaluated baseline (92.82% vs. 91.58% for the strongest; paired Wilcoxon signed-rank $p=0.015$, 13/17 per-dataset wins), with the largest gains on modern reenactment-heavy benchmarks (DSv1.1, DSv2). Under strict parameter budgets, LayerNorm-only tuning ($\approx$0.03% of the backbone) is the most stable configuration and matches the strongest baseline with a mean of 91.97%.

</details>


### [36] [From Classification to Generation: Vision-Language Models for Roman Imperial Coin Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14821)
*Pedro Lourenco,Ognjen Arandjelović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文把罗马帝国钱币的著录任务重构为图像到文本生成，基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct为四项子任务（正反面铭文转写、正反面图像描述）分别训练LoRA适配器，在四项任务上均优于未适配基座模型与GPT-4.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统钱币著录需人工读铭文、辨图像并匹配标准目录；已有工作将各环节分别建模为预定义标签集上的分类任务，割裂了著录的整体生成属性，且受限于封闭标签空间。

Method: 将钱币描述形式化为图像到文本生成，微调开源视觉语言模型Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct完成四项任务；把约3万条拍卖记录转换为结构化图文对，流程包括正反面切分、铭文与描述的抽取与规范化、正面描述按胸像类型分类体系标准化；每项任务在单张20GB GPU上训练独立低秩适配器（LoRA），并自定义度量以覆盖钱币描述的关键要素。

Result: 四项任务上适配后模型均优于未适配基座模型和GPT-4.1；铭文转写的归一化编辑距离相对GPT-4.1降低逾40%；对训练数据中未出现的铭文，提升幅度较小但仍然存在。

Conclusion: 将钱币著录统一为图像到文本生成任务、配合任务专用LoRA适配器，是低成本且有效的方案，并具备对未见铭文的有限泛化能力。

Abstract: Cataloguing a Roman Imperial coin involves reading its legends and identifying its imagery in order to match it to an entry in the standard reference catalogue. Previous work has automated individual parts of this process, such as identifying the issuer or recognizing reverse motifs, by formulating each part as a classification task over a predefined set of labels. We instead formulate coin description as image-to-text generation and adapt an open-weight vision-language model (VLM), Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, to four tasks: transcribing the obverse and reverse legends, and describing the obverse and reverse imagery. To obtain training data, we convert approximately 30,000 auction records into structured image-text pairs. We separate the two sides of each coin, extract and normalize legends and descriptions, and standardize obverse descriptions against a bust-type taxonomy. We then train a separate low-rank adapter for each task on a single 20GB GPU, and evaluate each task with metrics that we define to capture the key components of numismatic descriptions. The adapted models outperform both the unadapted base model and GPT-4.1 on all four tasks, reducing the normalized edit distance of legend transcriptions by over 40% relative to GPT-4.1. The improvement is smaller, but still present, on legends absent from the training data.

</details>


### [37] [Fast Slice-Wise Deep Learning Motion Correction for Spinal Cord Functional MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14677)
*Thomas Dagonneau,Sandrine Bédard,Nontharat Tucksinapinunchai,Uten Yarach,Loïc Le Folgoc,Pietro Gori,Julien Cohen-Adad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级CNN逐切片回归面内平移以对齐脊髓fMRI轴向切片，两次无标签训练分别校正大幅与残余运动，在3T/7T数据上全面优于SCT，单次扫描约10秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 呼吸与整体运动使脊髓在fMRI时间序列各体积间发生位移；现有SCT方法每条时间序列需数分钟，而通用脑部工具整卷配准，但脊髓主要为逐切片运动。

Method: 轻量级卷积神经网络回归将每个轴向切片对齐到参考的面向平移；两个模型在无运动标签条件下训练，先校正大幅运动、再校正残余运动。

Result: 在3T（120例）与7T（59例）数据集上，所有指标均优于SCT（tSNR +10~+25%，DVARS -20~-38%），位移超一个体素的切片比例<0.1%；与逐切片AFNI变体相当，优于逐切片FSL FLIRT；单次扫描约10秒，较SCT快约30倍。

Conclusion: 逐切片深度学习方法可高效、有效地校正脊髓fMRI运动，已集成于SCT v7.4并开源。

Abstract: Respiratory and bulk motion displace the spinal cord between the volumes of a functional MRI time series. Current methods such as the Spinal Cord Toolbox (SCT) take several minutes per time series, and general-purpose brain tools register whole volumes, whereas the cord moves mainly slice by slice. We propose a lightweight convolutional neural network that regresses the in-plane translation aligning each axial slice to a reference. Two models, trained without motion labels, correct large and then residual motion. On a 3T (120 scans) and a 7T (59 scans) dataset, the proposed method improves on SCT on every metric (tSNR +10 to +25%, DVARS -20 to -38%), leaves almost no slice displaced by more than one voxel (<0.1%), performs on par with a slice-wise AFNI variant and better than slice-wise FSL FLIRT, and runs in about 10 s per scan, about 30 times faster than SCT. The code is available at https://github.com/ivadomed/moco-dl and the method is available in SCT v7.4.

</details>


### [38] [FreeDeshadow: Shadow Removal for User-Specified Objects With Multimodal Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14608)
*Zhiliang Wu,Jihao Gu,Kun Li,Hanyu Xuan,Hehe Fan,Meng Wang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对象感知可控去影任务与FreeDeshadow框架，支持视觉/文本提示仅去除指定物体的投射阴影，并构建大规模数据集PCSRD+。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去影方法要么全局去影，要么依赖精确阴影掩码引导修复，无法在实际应用中仅选择性地去除用户指定物体所投射的阴影。

Method: 定义"对象感知可控去影"任务；提出FreeDeshadow统一框架，由对象感知生成器（解析提示、生成无影候选）与阴影感知局部精修器（建立对象-阴影对应并将改动约束在目标阴影区域）组成；支持视觉提示（点、线、对象掩码）与文本提示；构建PCSRD+数据集（154,571组四元组，1024×1024，六类提示）。

Result: 在视觉与文本提示下均能准确去除目标物体阴影并有效保留非目标区域；在去影性能与可控性上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过对象感知的可控去影范式与统一的视觉/文本提示框架，缓解了对象-阴影空间分离造成的对应难题，实现了选择性、可控的阴影去除。

Abstract: Existing shadow removal methods either perform global shadow removal or rely on precise shadow masks to guide restoration. Such paradigms limit their ability to selectively remove the shadow cast by a user-specified object in practical applications. To this end, we introduce an object-aware controllable shadow removal task, which allows users to specify a target object through a simple prompt and removes only its cast shadow while preserving other content. However, object-shadow asymmetry makes it challenging to establish accurate correspondence between the specified object and its spatially separated cast shadow and to confine restoration to the target shadow region. To address these challenges, we propose FreeDeshadow, a unified controllable shadow removal framework that supports both visual and textual prompts. It consists of an object-aware generator and a shadow-aware local refiner. The former interprets the user prompt and produces a high-quality shadow-free proposal, while the latter establishes the correspondence between the object and its cast shadow and constrains the generated changes to the target shadow region. To support training and evaluation, we further construct PCSRD+, a large-scale dataset containing 154,571 quadruples at 1024$\times$1024 resolution and covering six types of user prompts. The visual prompts include a dot, a line, and an object mask, while the textual prompts describe the target object using attributes, spatial relations, or states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FreeDeshadow accurately removes the shadow of the target object under both visual and textual prompts while effectively preserving non-target regions. It also outperforms existing methods in shadow removal performance and controllability. Project Page: https://wzlwlm13.github.io/FreeDeshadow

</details>


### [39] [Reframing Video Inverse Tone Mapping as Blind Posterior Inference via Latent Luminance Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15146)
*Hanwool Sul,Chang-Hoon Jeong,Kisung Shin,Bomseok Park,Donghoon Cha,Hansl von Kwon,DoHyung Ku,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将SDR→HDR逆色调映射(ITM)重构为"未知算子下的后验推断"，识别并量化了现有生成式方法系统性存在的"亮度偏差(luminance error)"，提出两项修正：在视频扩散潜空间上以固定仿射泛函无解码地读出对数亮度，并沿该坐标施加非对称损失以选取后验的expectile（仅在裁剪导致亮度无约束处生效）；同时用显式色调算子族合成训练SDR，使模型对观测不可见信息进行边缘化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HDR显示已普及，但档案素材、广播内容及生成式视频模型输出多为SDR，其中生成视频完全没有HDR母版。SDR→HDR需反演一个从未被记录的色调映射，且裁剪会破坏辐亮度，属盲逆问题。现有基于生成式视频先验的方法虽能填补裁剪区域，但重建结果系统性偏移：在所有被测试的公开方法中，饱和内容偏亮或偏暗，且偏移方向随输入背后的色调算子而变化。

Method: 1) 将该现象命名为亮度偏差，同时测量其幅值与符号；2) 将ITM重新表述为未知算子下的后验推断，定位误差来源并给出两项校正；3) 用视频扩散潜空间上的固定仿射泛函直接读取对数亮度，无需解码；4) 在该坐标上使用非对称损失选取后验expectile，且仅作用于裁剪致亮度无约束区域；5) 通过显式色调算子族合成训练用SDR数据，使模型对观测无法揭示的算子不确定性做边缘化。

Result: 按此训练后，亮度偏差在不同色调算子间更平坦、平均值小于任何公开方法；完整系统在三个基准上均取得最佳感知质量。

Conclusion: 将盲逆色调映射显式建模为未知算子下的后验推断，并针对亮度坐标做非对称后验统计与算子边缘化训练，可有效消除生成式SDR→HDR的系统性亮度偏差，同时提升跨算子鲁棒性与感知质量。

Abstract: High-dynamic-range (HDR) displays are now common, but most video content, such as archival footage, broadcast material, and the output of generative video models, exists only in standard dynamic range (SDR). The need is sharpest for generated video, which has no HDR master at all and is graded alongside live-action and visual-effects material finished in HDR. Converting SDR to HDR means undoing a tone-mapping operation that was never recorded, and clipping destroys the radiance wherever it acts, so HDR recovery is a blind inverse problem. Methods built on generative video priors fill clipped regions with plausible structure, but their reconstructions are systematically biased: in every public method we test, saturated content leans too bright or too dark, and the direction of the lean moves with the tone operator behind the input. We call this failure the luminance error, measure both its magnitude and its sign, and make it the focus of this work. Reframing ITM as posterior inference under an unknown operator locates the failure in two places and yields two corrections. A fixed affine functional of the video-diffusion latent reads log-luminance without decoding; on that coordinate an asymmetric loss selects an expectile of the posterior, applied only where clipping leaves luminance unconstrained. We also synthesize training SDR through an explicit family of tone operators, so the model marginalizes over what the observation cannot show. Trained this way, our luminance error stays flatter across operators and smaller on average than any public method, and the full system achieves the best perceptual quality on all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [40] [Real Pairs Are Not Enough: Physically Anchored Supervision for Volumetric CT Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15514)
*Haoran Liu,Zipei Yan,Mingzhe Liu,Jizhou Li,Guibin Zan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIN（物理锚定隐式网络）+ Real-Pair Dual-Anchor 学习：将配准物理对监督解耦为 HR 锚（谱重放教细节）与 LR 锚（低通投影约束），并与精确合成监督共享一个体素隐式模型，避免体素级过平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体数据 CT 超分辨常依赖合成 LR-HR 对或配准的物理对训练。合成对缺乏真实采集效应；配准物理对保留跨扫描噪声、伪影与微结构差异，导致直接体素回归甚至简单合成-真实混合都会过平滑细微多孔结构。

Method: 提出 Physically-anchored Implicit Network (PIN) 与 Real-Pair Dual-Anchor 学习：HR 锚通过 pair-conditioned spectral replay 从配准 HR 传递细结构；LR 锚通过 affine-calibrated low-pass projection 用真实 LR 约束预测；同时在单一共享体隐式模型中保留精确合成监督。推理仅需真实 LR。

Result: 在真实 CT 的 30 组外部基线配置上：全容积 LPIPS 最低；相对各指标最强三个基线，平均对称表面距离降低 42.3–49.5%，骨体积分数误差降低 52.2–53.4%；被抹除的参考孔隙体积分数由 37.3–72.9% 降至 10.4%。

Conclusion: 通过将物理对监督拆分为互补的 HR/LR 双锚并统一隐式表示，PIN 在不要求重建逐体素匹配配准 HR 的前提下同时利用真实与合成数据，显著改善真实 CT 的结构保真与孔隙保留；代码与模型将公开。

Abstract: Volumetric CT super-resolution (SR) models are commonly trained on synthetic or physically acquired pairs of low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) volumes. However, synthetic pairs miss real acquisition effects, whereas registered physical pairs retain inter-scan noise, artifact, and microstructure differences---causing direct voxelwise regression, and even naive synthetic-real mixing, to oversmooth fine porous structures. We introduce the Physically-anchored Implicit Network (PIN) with Real-Pair Dual-Anchor learning, which decouples physical-pair supervision into two complementary roles. The HR anchor teaches fine structure from registered HR through pair-conditioned spectral replay, while the LR anchor constrains the prediction from acquired LR via affine-calibrated low-pass projection. Together with exact synthetic supervision in one shared volumetric implicit model, PIN learns from both physical scans without forcing the acquired-LR reconstruction to match registered HR voxel by voxel. Inference requires only acquired LR. Across 30 external baseline configurations on real CT, PIN achieves the lowest full-volume LPIPS, reduces average symmetric surface distance by 42.3-49.5% and bone-volume-fraction error by 52.2-53.4% relative to the three strongest baselines for each metric, and lowers the erased reference-pore volume fraction from 37.3-72.9% to 10.4%. Code, pretrained models, and evaluation scripts will be publicly available.

</details>


### [41] [Keep Images in Sight: Visual Position Re-Anchoring for Long-Context MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15100)
*Juntao Xu,Qichai Zhao,Rui Sun,Zhouyang Jiang,Shiwei Wu,Zhi Li,Kaisheng Ma,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现长上下文多模态中图像与问题的相对位置直接影响模型对图像细节的可靠访问，并提出免训练方法VPR：在问题处理前将缓存视觉键的旋转相位向短上下文参考位置偏移，从而在不额外前向、不提取注意力图的条件下恢复视觉访问能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文多模态场景下，随着对话与文档累积，模型需要回溯早期图像，但"保留图像"并不等于"能可靠访问其细节"。作者通过改变上下文长度、在图像周围重新分配固定文本、以及在不加文本的情况下改变位置，确认了图像与问题之间的位置关系是影响视觉访问的直接因素，而现有方法未针对这一位置因素进行处理。

Method: 提出Visual Position Re-Anchoring（VPR），一种免训练方法：在问题处理前，将KV缓存中视觉键的旋转位置相位（rotary phases）向短上下文参考位置平移，直接作用于已有KV缓存，保持标准注意力计算不变，无需提取注意力图或增加模型前向次数；并设计静态与连续两种补偿调度，默认采用静态调度。

Result: 在10个checkpoint与10个基准上，静态VPR在100个模型-任务分数中提升了97个；进一步分析显示，在6个checkpoint上模型对包含答案证据区域的注意力增强；在固定旋转频率下运行时开销极小。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过在缓存内部进行位置调整，可以高效维持长上下文多模态模型的视觉访问能力，为免训练、低开销的视觉信息利用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Long-context multimodal intelligence requires revisiting earlier images as conversations and documents accumulate, yet retaining an image does not guarantee reliable access to its details. By varying context length, redistributing a fixed text passage around the image, and changing positions without adding text, we identify the positional relation between images and questions as a direct influence on visual access. To address this gap, we propose Visual Position Re-Anchoring (VPR), a training-free method that shifts cached visual-key rotary phases toward a short-context reference before question processing. It operates directly on the existing KV cache, preserving standard attention computation without extracting attention maps or adding model forward passes. We develop static and continuous compensation schedules, using the static schedule by default. Across ten checkpoints and ten benchmarks, static VPR improves 97 of 100 model--task scores. Further analysis shows greater attention to regions containing answer evidence across six checkpoints, with minimal runtime overhead under fixed rotary frequencies. These findings demonstrate an efficient way to sustain visual access through positional adjustment within the cache.

</details>


### [42] [SAVE: Less is More in Agentic Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15079)
*Tymoteusz Wilk,Sergey Prokudin,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对现有agentic视频推理框架做受控评测，发现多数复杂框架相比原生视频推理无稳定增益且成本更高；提出极简基线Active Exploration Baseline及改进版SAVE，在6个骨干-基准设置中5个超越既有方法，并指出agentic探索的优势主要在长视频与时间集中证据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic视频推理方法在骨干模型、预处理流程和计算预算上各不相同，导致无法判断哪些组件真正带来性能提升、成本几何，以及agentic推理是否优于原生视频推理。为此作者开展带明确token与金钱成本核算的受控评测。

Method: 在两个强骨干（Gemini 3 Flash、Gemma 4）上对多种近期agentic视频推理系统进行受控对比，并核算token与金钱成本；提出刻意极简的Active Exploration Baseline，再对其进行针对性扩展得到SAVE（Simple Agentic Video Explorer）。

Result: 多数现有agentic框架未能稳定优于原生视频推理，且常增加计算成本；极简基线可与复杂agent竞争；SAVE在六个骨干-基准设置中的五个优于所有被评测的既有agentic方法。agentic探索的优势在密集处理会过度稀疏化答案相关事件时增大，尤其是长视频与时间集中的证据。

Conclusion: agentic视频推理应偏向简单框架而非日益复杂的流水线，并主要聚焦长视频场景——选择性证据获取相较原生推理优势最明显。

Abstract: Agentic video-reasoning frameworks iteratively explore a video, choosing what to observe based on the query and evidence gathered so far. Existing methods are typically evaluated with different backbones, preprocessing pipelines, and computational budgets, making it difficult to determine which components actually drive performance, how much they cost, and whether agentic inference is preferable to native video reasoning in the first place. We conduct a controlled evaluation of a diverse set of recent agentic video-reasoning systems across two strong contemporary backbones, with explicit token and monetary-cost accounting. Across Gemini 3 Flash and Gemma 4, most existing agentic frameworks fail to consistently improve over native video inference and often increase computational cost. We investigate two primary questions: (1) How much agentic framework complexity is necessary for effective video reasoning? (2) When, if at all, is agentic video reasoning preferable to just using the backbone directly? To answer the first question, we introduce a deliberately minimal Active Exploration Baseline and show that it remains competitive with substantially more elaborate agents. We then propose targeted extensions to this baseline, yielding SAVE (Simple Agentic Video Explorer). SAVE outperforms all evaluated prior agentic methods in five of six backbone-benchmark settings. Turning to the second question, our results indicate that the advantage of agentic exploration grows when dense processing would otherwise represent answer-relevant events too sparsely, particularly for long videos and temporally concentrated evidence. Overall, our results suggest that agentic video reasoning should favor simple frameworks over increasingly elaborate pipelines and focus primarily on long-form video, where selective evidence acquisition offers the clearest advantage over native inference.

</details>


### [43] [DuoJEPA: Cross-Modal Translation Between Unaligned Self-Supervised Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15968)
*Mahmut S. Gokmen,Mitchell A. Klusty,Evan W. Damron,Caroline N. Leach,Samuel E. Armstrong,V. K. Cody Bumgardner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuoJEPA通过"翻译层+目标空间对比监督"实现跨模态检索与分类，无需让两个编码器自身嵌入空间直接对齐：去掉残差的翻译层下配对余弦检验检测不到对齐，但跨模态性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常"从配对模态学习"被等同于"对齐模态"：训练两个编码器使配对样本的嵌入在共享空间中重合。作者质疑跨模态检索与分类是否必须依赖两个编码器自身空间的直接对齐。

Method: 每个模态一个编码器，用自监督LeJEPA目标训练；每个编码器后接单层Transformer，将其嵌入翻译到另一编码器空间，仅在该目标空间内计算对比损失。两个自身空间之间无任何直接比较项，但编码器通过翻译目标联合优化。比较带残差与无残差(skipless)翻译器两种设置。

Result: 残差连接决定跨模态压力被吸收的位置：带残差时两编码器直接对齐；无残差时配对余弦检验在最终模型中检测不到对齐，消融中至多弱对齐，而跨模态性能相当。学到的类别token翻译可近似为源空间的单一缩放旋转。CheXpert Plus胸片-报告：零样本AUC 86.6、跨模态检索78.5，对比同编码器与数据的LeVLJEPA重实现60.0与64.2。Sentinel-2光学与Sentinel-1雷达：冻结编码器在Copernicus-Bench线性探针复现协议下处于已发表Sentinel基础模型范围内，翻译器在4000候选中检索到另一传感器配对图像的比率为77–79%，patch级扩展可迁移场景内相似性结构。

Conclusion: 跨模态检索与分类可在两个编码器自身嵌入空间不直接对齐的情况下获得，对齐并非跨模态能力的必要条件；翻译器残差结构控制对齐是否发生。

Abstract: Learning from paired modalities is commonly equated with aligning them: two encoders are trained until the embeddings of a pair coincide in a shared space. We study whether cross-modal retrieval and classification can instead be obtained while the two encoders' own embedding spaces remain directly unaligned. DuoJEPA trains one encoder per modality with the self-supervised LeJEPA objective and appends to each encoder a single transformer layer that translates its embedding into the other encoder's space, supervised by a contrastive objective computed entirely within that target space. No loss term compares the two own spaces directly, although the encoders are optimised jointly through the translation objective. In our experiments, the translator's residual connections determine where the cross-modal pressure is absorbed: with a residual translator the two encoders become directly aligned, whereas with a skipless (residual-free) translator a paired-cosine test detects no alignment in the final models, and at most a weak one in the ablations, at comparable cross-modal performance. The learned class-token translation is well approximated by a single scaled rotation of the source space. We evaluate DuoJEPA on two modality pairs that differ in nearly every respect. On chest radiographs and reports from CheXpert Plus, it attains a zero-shot AUC of 86.6 and a cross-modal retrieval score of 78.5, compared with 60.0 and 64.2 for our reimplementation of LeVLJEPA with the same encoders and data. On Sentinel-2 optical and Sentinel-1 radar imagery, the frozen encoders fall within the range of published Sentinel foundation models under our reproduction of the Copernicus-Bench linear-probe protocol, the translator retrieves the paired image of the other sensor among 4,000 candidates in 77-79% of cases, and a patch-level extension transfers the within-image similarity structure of the scene.

</details>


### [44] [Contrastive Bootstrap Representation Learning for Label-Efficient Agricultural Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15610)
*Hazem Ahmed,Ali Hamdi,Ammar Mohammed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CBRL（对比+自举表征学习），在ViT-L/16与10%下游标签下于四个农业基准取得最高macro-F1点估计，但未验证统计显著性与协同性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业图像分类中专家标注是主要瓶颈：采集图像易、获取可靠标签难。自监督学习可利用无标签图像预训练可迁移表征以降低标签依赖；对比学习与自举（bootstrap）两种训练信号各有优势但彼此独立，因此作者希望在严重下游标签稀缺条件下，实证检验将二者统一于同一表征路径的联合形式。

Method: 提出对比自举表征学习（CBRL）：在同一在线投影空间中联合优化 SimCLR 式 NT-Xent 对比损失与双向 BYOL 式 EMA 目标预测；骨干为 ViT-L/16，下游仅使用10%标签进行迁移评估。评测包括四个主要农业测试基准，外加一个33张图像的 New Plant Diseases 探索性评估；报告单随机种子点估计，并与随机初始化仅标签基线及对比方法比较。

Result: 在四个主要基准上CBRL的macro-F1点估计最高。DeepWeeds 上达79.77%，比随机初始化/仅标签基线高32.29个百分点，比次高对比点估计高15.78个百分点；PlantVillage 上达98.14%，分别高10.43和0.43个百分点。PlantDoc 上所有方法均未达到实用性能。33图探索性评估仅作参考。

Conclusion: 该联合形式在标签受限迁移中是有前景的候选方案；但单种子点估计不足以确立客观协同效应、统计优越性或跨随机运行的鲁棒性，需多种子与显著性检验进一步验证。

Abstract: Expert annotation remains a major bottleneck in agricultural image classification, where collecting images is often easier than obtaining reliable labels from domain specialists. Self-supervised learning can reduce this dependence by pretraining transferable representations on unlabelled imagery. Contrastive and bootstrap methods provide two established but distinct training signals, motivating an empirical study of a formulation that applies both signals within one representation path under severe downstream label scarcity. We present Contrastive Bootstrap Representation Learning (CBRL), which jointly optimises SimCLR-style NT-Xent contrastive learning and bidirectional BYOL-style exponential-moving-average target prediction within a shared online projection space. Using a ViT-L/16 backbone and 10% of the downstream labels, we report single-seed point estimates on four primary agricultural test benchmarks plus an exploratory 33-image New Plant Diseases evaluation. CBRL records the highest macro-F1 point estimate on the four primary benchmarks. On DeepWeeds, its 79.77% test macro-F1 is 32.29 points above the random-initialisation, label-only baseline and 15.78 points above the next-highest comparison point estimate; on PlantVillage, its 98.14% macro-F1 is 10.43 and 0.43 points higher, respectively. On PlantDoc, however, all methods fail to reach practically useful performance. These results identify the evaluated joint formulation as a promising candidate for label-limited transfer, but they do not establish objective synergy, statistical superiority, or robustness across stochastic runs.

</details>


### [45] [Saying No by Asking Yes: Affirmative Decomposition and Evidence Reflection for Frozen CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15047)
*Aashish Pokhrel,Tejas Anvekar,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Negation-Aware Evidence Scoring (NAES)，通过将否定描述拆解为肯定查询并用否定感知证据组合打分，显著提升冻结视觉语言模型在含否定描述下的检索与匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉语言模型（如CLIP）在匹配含否定概念的caption时存在明显缺陷：查询"a kitchen with no toaster"反而偏向包含toaster的图像；但模型对肯定描述的匹配能力显著更强。作者希望利用这种"肯定强、否定弱"的不对称性来改善否定感知排序。

Method: 1) NegSplit：轻量文本分解器，将描述拆为被否定的span与剩余肯定上下文；2) NAES：将否定描述改写为肯定查询并组合证据——若存在肯定剩余内容，则匹配该内容并对被否定对象施加基于存在性的校正；若无肯定内容，则查询被否定对象的存在性并在参考点附近反射证据；多选题场景还用竞争描述对证据进行中心化与对齐。评估覆盖LAION-2B CLIP与OpenAI CLIP，并扩展至SigLIP系列与更大CLIP编码器。

Result: LAION-2B CLIP下NAES在COCO-MCQ达82.4%、VOC2007-MCQ达93.0%，分别超越KTN 2.9与0.9个百分点；OpenAI CLIP下较PeakPatch分别提升4.4与26.0个百分点。同时在COCO与MSR-VTT的否定描述检索上取得提升，并在SigLIP、SigLIP 2及更大CLIP编码器上持续有效；检索提升不依赖竞争描述条件化。

Conclusion: 显式地将肯定匹配与被否定对象的证据分离，可实质性改善否定感知排序，说明利用肯定-否定能力不对称是提升冻结VLM否定理解的有效途径。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language models often match concepts mentioned in a caption without respecting their negation: a query "a kitchen with no toaster" favors an image containing a toaster. Yet these models are substantially stronger at matching affirmative descriptions. We exploit this asymmetry with Negation-Aware Evidence Scoring (NAES), which reformulates negated descriptions into affirmative queries and combines their evidence by caption structure. A lightweight text decomposer, NegSplit, identifies this structure by extracting negated spans and the remaining affirmative context. For captions with an affirmative remainder, NAES matches that content and applies a presence-based correction for the denial. When no affirmative content remains, it queries the denied object's presence and reflects the evidence around a reference to score the negated description. With LAION-2B CLIP, NAES achieves 82.4% accuracy on COCO-MCQ and 93.0% on VOC2007-MCQ, surpassing KTN by 2.9 and 0.9 percentage points; with OpenAI CLIP, it exceeds PeakPatch accuracy by 4.4 and 26.0 points, respectively. NAES also improves negated-description retrieval on COCO and MSR-VTT, with gains extending to SigLIP, SigLIP 2, and larger CLIP encoders. The strongest multiple-choice results use competing descriptions to center and align evidence; retrieval improvements persist without this conditioning. These findings show that explicitly separating affirmative matching from denied-object evidence can substantially improve negation-aware ranking.

</details>


### [46] [Where to Generate Touch: Do Image Latents Preserve Material Recognition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15834)
*Wanghao Ye,Aarosh Das,Sihan Chen,Yiting Wang,Bowei Tian,Guoheng Sun,Shwai He,Zheyu Shen,Ziyao Wang,Yexiao He,Zhaoyi Liu,Meng Liu,Yuning Zhang,Meng Feng,Ziyi Wang,Yilong Dai,Yifei Dong,Siyuan Peng,Zhenle Duan,Joshua Liu,Lang Xiong,Daeun Jung,Aditya Sarkar,Parastoo Pilevar,Xiaochuan Gong,Yancheng Zhu,Yingying Fan,Qiong Wang,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文比较了离散tokenizer与SD-VAE潜在空间在触觉生成中的表现，发现SD-VAE重建更能保留触觉语义，据此选用SD-VAE潜变量并构建照片到触觉的生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式触觉有望突破真实采集接触数据的限制，但触觉生成器需要合适的潜在空间，而如何选择该潜在空间缺乏依据。

Method: 以在真实GelSight按压数据上训练的材料分类器作为探针，检验其在预训练图像模型编码-解码重建上的性能保持情况；在ObjectFolder Real、Touch and Go及DIGIT物体识别（YCB-Slide）上对比三种16×离散tokenizer与Stable Diffusion VAE（SD-VAE）。

Result: ObjectFolder Real上分类器在三种16×离散tokenizer重建上下降6.5–9.7个点，在SD-VAE重建上仅降0.3个点；离散tokenizer在Touch and Go与YCB-Slide上损失更大；仅用重建数据（无新标签）重训练可缩小离散差距；SD-VAE重建保持物体身份，物体匹配准确率76.7%，接近真实触觉的78.9%，高于MAGVIT-v2的67.0%。

Conclusion: SD-VAE潜变量更能保留触觉语义，故被选为触觉生成器的潜在空间，并在其中训练了照片到触觉的生成器；代码将在论文录用后发布。

Abstract: Generated touch could extend robot learning beyond recorded contacts. A tactile generator needs a latent space, and we choose one by testing whether a material classifier trained on real GelSight presses still works on encode-decode reconstructions from pretrained image models. On ObjectFolder Real, the classifier loses 6.5 to 9.7 points on reconstructions from three 16x discrete tokenizers and 0.3 points on those from the Stable Diffusion VAE (SD-VAE). The 16x tokenizers again lose far more on Touch and Go and on DIGIT object recognition (YCB-Slide). Retraining the classifier on reconstructions, with no new labels, narrows the discrete gap. SD-VAE reconstructions also keep object identity, with 76.7% object-matching accuracy against 78.9% for real touch and 67.0% for MAGVIT-v2. We therefore select SD-VAE latents and show a photo-to-touch generator trained in them. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [47] [Image Deblending with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15760)
*Dan Botchan,Ohad Fried,Yacov Hel-Or*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将图像去混合（deblending）建模为联合逆问题，用扩散后验采样同时重建两幅源图像，并提出免训练、文本提示引导的两阶段框架（潜空间语义分离+像素空间细化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去混合是病态问题：单张混合图可被分解为多个合理源层，解空间巨大。扩散模型虽常用于逆问题，但多针对单输出重建；人类能借语义理解消解视觉歧义，本文受此启发，用文本提示约束解空间。

Method: 将deblending表述为联合逆问题，用扩散后验采样一次性重建两幅源图，证明单一扩散先验足以解耦两幅完整图像；提出免训练框架，两阶段架构：文本引导的潜扩散以提示作为高层语义线索做语义分离，随后像素空间细化恢复细节；随机采样生成多个可行分解并可无真值排序；支持盲去混合与空间变化alpha图。

Result: 大量实验表明：在未见域上达到SOTA；可扩展到空间变化alpha map；在标准标量混合基准上与监督基线相当。

Conclusion: 语义级文本引导+联合扩散后验采样可有效约束去混合的病态解空间，无需训练即可实现多解生成与排序，兼具跨域泛化与对现有监督方法的竞争力。

Abstract: Image deblending is the task of decomposing a composite image back into its source images, which is an ill-posed problem, as a single blended image can be decomposed into multiple plausible source layers. While diffusion models are often used to solve inverse problems, they typically address single-output reconstruction problems. In this work, we formulate deblending as a joint inverse problem, employing diffusion posterior sampling to simultaneously reconstruct two source images, demonstrating that a single diffusion prior is expressive enough to disentangle two full images at once rather than just one. Drawing inspiration from human perception, where semantic understanding helps disentangle visual inconsistencies, we propose a training-free framework that leverages text prompts to constrain this solution space. We address the blind deblending task through a two-stage architecture: text-guided latent diffusion performs semantic separation using prompts as high-level cues, followed by a pixel-space refinement to recover fine details. Stochastic sampling produces multiple plausible decompositions that can be ranked without ground truth. Extensive experiments show that our training-free method achieves state-of-the-art results on unseen domains and extends to spatially-varying alpha maps, while remaining competitive with supervised baselines on standard scalar blending benchmarks.

</details>


### [48] [Multi-View 3D Reconstruction Using V-JEPA Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15143)
*Anwar Shamim,Jeffrin Sam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究将预测式视频编码器特征用于RGB多视角3D重建，比较全局/空间相机回归与联合相对目标，并训练独立TUM RGB-D模型。结果显示空间相机配置降低ATE约29.3%，但轨迹误差劣于恒等位姿、深度误差劣于全局配置；联合相对目标改善归一化内参与对齐Chamfer距离，却增大原始Chamfer距离。室内场景相机误差0.3063 m优于恒等位姿0.3694 m，但深度误差0.4279劣于常数中值深度0.2516。结论强调需分离比较各组件与编码器，才能归因于视频预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测式视频编码器可提供稠密视觉表征，但迁移至3D重建需准确的深度、相机位姿与跨视角几何；现有工作易将重建增益笼统归因于视频预训练，缺乏对表征适配、对齐形状与原始重建的隔离分析。

Method: 构建融合视频预训练特征、相机估计、场景聚合、对极传输与残差pointmap的架构，进行监督式纯RGB多视角重建；在CO3D上对比全局与空间相机回归及联合相对目标，并在TUM RGB-D上独立训练模型，辅以refinement与消融实验。

Result: CO3D 168个五视图窗口：空间相机配置ATE较全局配置低29.3%，但轨迹误差劣于恒等位姿、深度误差劣于全局配置；联合相对目标将归一化内参误差与相似变换对齐Chamfer距离由0.6447降至0.5573，但原始Chamfer距离由3.5535升至4.4374。TUM 163个室内窗口：相机误差0.3063 m对恒等位姿0.3694 m；深度误差0.4279对常数中值深度0.2516；refinement使室内相机误差恶化。

Conclusion: 各配置在轨迹、位姿与深度指标上存在竞争性影响，指标间结论不一致；应将表征适配、相似对齐形状与原始重建区分评估，并在归因于预测式视频预训练前开展隔离的组件与编码器对比。

Abstract: Predictive video encoders provide dense visual representations, but adapting them to 3D reconstruction requires accurate depth, camera poses, and cross-view geometry. We study supervised RGB-only multi-view reconstruction using an architecture that combines video-pretrained features, camera estimation, scene aggregation, epipolar transport, and residual pointmaps. Experiments compare global and spatial camera regression with joint relative objectives on CO3D, alongside an independently trained TUM RGB-D model. On 168 CO3D five-view windows, the spatial-camera configuration achieves 29.3% lower absolute trajectory error than the global-camera configuration, but performs worse than identity poses in trajectory error and worse than the global configuration in depth error. The joint relative-objective configuration improves normalized intrinsics error and similarity-aligned Chamfer distance from 0.6447 to 0.5573, but increases raw Chamfer distance from 3.5535 to 4.4374. On 163 indoor windows, camera error is 0.3063 m versus 0.3694 m for identity poses, while depth error is 0.4279 versus 0.2516 for constant median depth. Refinement worsens indoor camera error, and additional experiments show competing depth and pose effects. Overall, the results distinguish representation adaptation, aligned shape, and raw reconstruction, highlighting the need for isolated component and encoder comparisons before attributing reconstruction gains to predictive video pretraining.

</details>


### [49] [SCR-Mamba: Skeleton-conditioned Mamba with cross-scale incremental refinement for efficient 3D human pose estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15526)
*Jie Wang,Xuejing Gu,Congzhe Zhang,Zibo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SCR-Mamba，通过骨骼条件状态建模（SCSM）与跨尺度增量精化（CSIR）提升视频 3D 人体姿态估计的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Mamba 的视频 3D 人体姿态估计方法虽以线性复杂度建模长序列，但其选择性状态更新未直接利用骨骼关系；同时在全时间分辨率上堆叠建模模块计算开销大，而压缩时间序列又难以保留逐帧细节。

Method: 核心为两个模块：SCSM 由骨骼连接与左右对称关系生成骨骼上下文，并以其为条件生成空间与时间分支的选择性参数，使结构关系参与状态信息的写入、传播与读取；CSIR 通过内容自适应聚合构建多尺度运动表示，并用粗尺度建模产生的增量逐步精化保留的逐帧特征，从而在整合长程运动上下文的同时降低全分辨率计算量。

Result: Human3.6M（SH 2D 检测、243 帧输入）：SCR-Mamba-L 达到 36.8 mm MPJPE (P1)，7.72 M 参数、15.10 G MACs，计算量不足 StructMamPose-L 的 1/7 且精度相当；SCR-Mamba-X 达到 36.6 mm P1、30.9 mm P-MPJPE (P2)，为当前最优。MPI-INF-3DHP：PCK 99.3%、AUC 88.1%、MPJPE 14.2 mm。

Conclusion: 将骨骼结构先验显式注入 Mamba 的选择性状态更新，并结合跨尺度增量精化，可在显著降低计算成本的同时实现 SOTA 精度，验证了结构条件化与多尺度精化对视频 3D 人体姿态估计的有效性。

Abstract: Mamba-based methods for video-based 3D human pose estimation can model long sequences with linear complexity, but their selective state updates usually lack direct exploitation of skeletal relations. Meanwhile, stacking modeling blocks at the full temporal resolution leads to high computational overhead, while compressing the temporal sequence makes it difficult to retain per-frame details. To address these issues, we propose SCR-Mamba, whose core consists of Skeleton-Conditioned State Modeling (SCSM) and Cross-Scale Incremental Refinement (CSIR). SCSM generates a skeleton context from bone connections and left-right symmetry relations and generates the selective parameters of the spatial and temporal branches conditioned on it, so that structural relations are involved in the writing, propagation, and reading of state information. CSIR constructs multi-scale motion representations through content-adaptive aggregation and progressively refines the retained per-frame features with the increments produced by coarse-scale modeling, integrating long-range motion context while reducing full-resolution computation. On Human3.6M with Stacked Hourglass (SH) 2D detections and 243-frame inputs, SCR-Mamba-L achieves 36.8 mm MPJPE (P1) with 7.72 M parameters and 15.10 G MACs, less than 1/7 of the computational cost of StructMamPose-L with similar accuracy; SCR-Mamba-X further reaches 36.6 mm P1 and 30.9 mm P-MPJPE (P2), achieving state-of-the-art performance. On MPI-INF-3DHP, SCR-Mamba-X achieves a percentage of correct keypoints (PCK) of 99.3%, an area under the curve (AUC) of 88.1%, and an MPJPE of 14.2 mm.

</details>


### [50] [Fear of Memorizing Objects: End-to-End Zero-Shot Model-Based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15459)
*Leon Greiner,Wout Boerdijk,Junwen Huang,Benjamin Busam,Rudolph Triebel,Maximilian Durner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端可训练的Transformer架构FOMO，将CAD模板信息早期注入检测流程，并构建大规模杂乱场景合成数据集Clutter6D用于零样本多实例目标检测训练，在BOP-Classic-Core上以0.23s/图达到54.2 mAP（排名第二，落后首位3.8pp，但快7倍以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本基于模型的目标检测方法采用"先提议再匹配"（proposal-and-match）流程：先生成与目标无关的掩码提议，再在多个松耦合阶段与模板特征匹配。该范式在杂乱场景中易产生碎片化提议，在遮挡下匹配性能退化。此外，现有训练数据集物体多样性有限，缺乏真实密集杂乱场景。

Method: 1) FOMO：可学习隐变量token、多视角CAD模板与查询图像特征之间进行双向注意力，早期注入物体模板信息，产生物体感知的图像特征与条件化查询，再由解码器输出边界框与置信度，端到端训练；2) Clutter6D：超50万张图像、逾1万个多样物体模型，渲染于多实例、不同遮挡程度的高度杂乱场景。

Result: 在BOP-Classic-Core上零样本取得54.2 mAP，每图0.23 s，总体排名第二，与第一名相差3.8 pp，速度快7倍以上；通过广泛消融实验验证了架构设计选择的有效性。

Conclusion: FOMO通过将模板信息前置融合，规避了提议-匹配范式的碎片化与遮挡匹配退化问题，在精度与速度间取得良好折衷，并证明大规模杂乱合成数据对零样本多实例检测训练的关键作用。

Abstract: This paper presents FOMO (Fear of Memorizing Objects), an end-to-end trainable transformer-based architecture for zero-shot model-based object detection. Existing approaches typically follow a proposal-and-match pipeline: target-agnostic mask proposals are generated and then matched to template features in a set of loosely coupled stages, making them susceptible to fragmented proposals in clutter and degraded matching under occlusions. FOMO elegantly replaces this paradigm by injecting object template information early in the detection pipeline: Bidirectional attention between learnable latent tokens, multi-view CAD templates, and query image features produces object-aware image features and conditioned queries which are subsequently decoded into bounding-box and confidence predictions. We train FOMO on Clutter6D, a novel large-scale synthetic dataset with over 500k images featuring more than 10k diverse object models rendered in highly cluttered scenes with multiple instances and varying occlusion levels, which addresses the limited object diversity and lack of realistic dense clutter in existing datasets for training zero-shot multi-instance algorithms. In true zero-shot fashion, FOMO achieves 54.2 mAP on BOP-Classic-Core at 0.23 s per image, ranking second overall with 3.8 pp difference to the top method while being more than 7x faster. Throughout comprehensive ablation studies we investigate and verify architectural design choices.

</details>


### [51] [WaveFlow-UIE: A Single-Pipeline Wavelet-Domain Flow Model for Efficient Underwater Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15457)
*Abdulaziz Alfaraj,Mohammed Al Naser,Hassan Al Nasser,Muzammil Behzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WaveFlow-UIE，一种单流程小波域流匹配模型，直接学习将退化水下小波表示变换为清晰表示，在保持或提升复原质量的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像复原受颜色失真、散射与细节丢失影响，且这些退化对低频与高频成分作用不同；现有扩散类方法质量好但依赖多阶段流程与昂贵的迭代精化。

Method: 采用离散小波变换，估计物理启发的轻量退化线索（透射率与环境光），以其为条件在小波空间中训练基于流匹配的速度网络；损失由流匹配、频率加权重建、感知、颜色一致性与物理一致性组成。

Result: 在 UIEB、LSUI、UFO-120、EUVP 上取得较优的质量—效率权衡，感知指标具竞争力；相比强基线小波扩散模型显著降低推理开销，且多个配对基准上质量持平或更优；在 UIEB 256×256 下将五步减为单步仍保留几乎全部质量，并额外约 4× 加速。

Conclusion: 单步单通道小波域模型可作为更重的扩散式迭代精化的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Underwater image restoration remains challenging because color distortion, scattering, and detail loss affect low- and high-frequency image components differently. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown strong restoration quality, but often rely on multi-stage pipelines and costly iterative refinement. We present WaveFlow-UIE, a single-pipeline wavelet-domain flow model that directly learns to transform degraded underwater wavelet representations into clean ones. Our method applies a discrete wavelet transform, estimates lightweight physics-inspired degradation cues including transmission and ambient light, and conditions a velocity network trained with flow matching in wavelet space. The training objective combines flow-matching, frequency-weighted reconstruction, perceptual, color-consistency, and physics-consistency losses. Across UIEB, LSUI, UFO-120, and EUVP, WaveFlow-UIE achieves a strong quality-efficiency tradeoff, with especially competitive results on perceptual metrics. Relative to a strong wavelet-based diffusion baseline, the proposed model substantially reduces inference cost while maintaining or improving restoration quality on several paired benchmarks. We show that reducing the inference process from five flow steps to a single step preserves nearly all of the restoration quality while providing an additional speedup of about $4\times$ on UIEB at $256\times256$ resolution. This single-step result suggests that a single-pass wavelet-domain model is an effective alternative to heavier diffusion-style refinement.

</details>


### [52] [SteinPnP: Non-Parametric Uncertainty Quantification in the Perspective-N-Point Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15437)
*Baptiste Lardinoit,Jongseok Lee,Andres Yubero,Seong Hun Lee,Javier Civera,Rudolph Triebel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SteinPnP将Stein变分推断与粒子表示结合，构建完整6-DoF位姿后验的概率PnP求解器，并针对SVI在PnP中的失效提出实用改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PnP求解器通常只输出单一最优位姿，难以刻画多模态不确定性；而直接套用SVI在PnP上易漏掉备选位姿假设或为其分配错误概率权重。

Method: 以非参数位姿粒子集建模6-DoF位姿后验，利用Stein变分推断兼具粒子表达力与优化求解效率；并提出三项改进：发现新的潜在位姿、识别不同假设簇、平衡簇间相对概率权重。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上，SteinPnP能准确刻画复杂的多模态不确定性，同时保持有竞争力的位姿精度。

Conclusion: SteinPnP表明概率化粒子推断可有效替代单点PnP解，为多假设位姿估计提供可行方案。

Abstract: We propose SteinPnP, a probabilistic Perspective- N-Point (PnP) solver that models the full 6-DoF camera pose posterior using a non-parametric set of pose particles. By lever- aging Stein Variational Inference (SVI), SteinPnP combines the expressivity of particle-based representations with the efficiency of optimization-based solvers. However, a naive application of SVI struggles in the PnP problem, often missing alternative pose hypotheses or assigning incorrect probability weights to them. To address these limitations, we propose practical remedies that discover new potential poses, identify distinct hypothesis clusters, and balance their relative probability weights to capture the true underlying distribution. Our experiments across synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SteinPnP accurately captures complex multimodal uncertainties while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [53] [ThermalSplat: Generalizable Pose-Free Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Thermal 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15435)
*Prasanna S,Gopi Raju Matta,Divya Madhuri Vemunuri,Kaushik Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThermalSplat 是首个免位姿、免RGB引导、免逐场景优化的前馈高斯泼溅框架，可直接从稀疏热红外图像重建3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外成像在搜救与自主导航中很重要，但转化为3D表示存在瓶颈：热图像低纹理、发射率随视角变化，导致标准位姿估计器失效，稀疏视角下3DGS管线崩溃；现有热重建方法依赖稠密采集与昂贵的逐场景优化，且多需对齐的RGB-热红外数据来获取位姿与初始点云，无法用于纯热红外的无位姿环境。

Method: 提出前馈式、可泛化的高斯泼溅框架：用软 census 损失实现亮度不变的结构匹配，用边界结构损失显式监督温度不连续性；在多源聚合数据集上训练，单次前向即可重建未见场景。

Result: 稀疏视角条件下严格优于场景特定的热泼溅方法；即使对比这些基线在其理想稠密采集设置下的表现，仍取得更优的感知保真度。

Conclusion: 去除昂贵的逐场景优化，为热红外3D重建提供了高效且可泛化的方案。

Abstract: Thermal infrared imaging is vital for applications like search-and-rescue and autonomous navigation, yet trans- lating it into 3D scene representations remains a critical bottleneck. The low-texture nature and view-dependent emissivity of thermal images cause standard pose estima- tors to fail catastrophically, collapsing standard 3D Gaus- sian Splatting pipelines under sparse-view constraints. Fur- thermore, existing thermal reconstruction methods rely heavily on dense capture and computationally expensive per-scene optimization. Critically, many of these ap- proaches depend on aligned RGB-thermal datasets to ob- tain reliable camera poses and initial point clouds, ren- dering them unusable in purely thermal, unposed envi- ronments. To break this bottleneck, we present Thermal- Splat, the first generalizable, feed-forward Gaussian splat- ting framework that reconstructs 3D scenes directly from sparse thermal images without requiring known camera poses, RGB guidance, or per-scene training. We over- come thermal-specific challenges using a soft census loss for brightness-invariant structural matching and a bound- ary structure loss to explicitly supervise temperature discon- tinuities. Trained on an aggregated multi-source dataset, ThermalSplat synthesizes sharp reconstructions of unseen scenes in a single feed-forward pass. Extensive experi- ments demonstrate that ThermalSplat holds a strict advan- tage over scene-specific thermal splatting methods in the sparse regime. Furthermore, even compared against these baselines operating under their ideal dense capture set- tings, ThermalSplat achieves superior perceptual fidelity. By eliminating the need for computationally expensive per scene optimization, ThermalSplat offers an efficient and generalizable approach to thermal 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [54] [Verifier Choice is a Benchmark Design Variable: Auditing Structural Counting Evaluation in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15433)
*Shurun Li,Michael Hua Wang,Haibo Zeng,Long Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将自动评测中的"验证器"视为基准分数不可忽视的组成部分，在结构化计数任务上系统审计验证器可靠性，发现分数匹配可与图像级分歧共存，且验证协议与生成器分布会扭曲生成器间比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像基准已高度依赖自动评测，但把图像转成分数的验证器常被视为实现细节；作者认为这不安全，因为基准分数实为生成器、提示分布、验证器、验证器使用协议与评分规则的联合属性，而非生成器单独的属性。

Method: 选择可人工审计的结构计数任务，构建以验证器为中心的审计套件，覆盖7类计数对象（手机、椅子、交通灯；三叶草小叶、抽屉面板、插排插孔、涡轮叶片）。基于Qwen-Image-2512与FLUX.2-dev生成的6,240张图像，比较开放MLLM验证器在count-only、reject-aware、zero-shot、few-shot协议下的表现，并加入TIFA式目标查询、检测器基线与两个困难对象上的前沿闭源模型检查；在2,280张平衡评测集上分析。

Result: 总体分数匹配可与图像级分歧并存；验证协议与生成器分布改变所测得的可靠性。所有验证器—协议配置都将两个生成器之间的人工分差缩小7.5–25.0个百分点；加入第三个生成器Z-Image后，四种协议全部反转了人工对Z-Image与FLUX.2-dev的排序。GPT-5.5在三叶草有效计数保真度仅59.92%，Claude Opus 4.7同切片一致性较低（κ=0.432），闭源前沿模型未能消除失效模式。

Conclusion: 自动基准分数须与"因子化验证器可靠性档案"一并报告，包含：总体分差、图像级一致性、生成器比较失真度、有效图像计数保真度、拒绝行为、输出合规性与方向性偏差。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation is now central to text-to-image benchmarking, but the verifier that converts generated images into scores is often treated as an implementation detail. We argue that this is unsafe: an automatic benchmark score is a property of the image generator, prompt distribution, verifier, verifier-use protocol, and scoring rule, not of the generator alone. We study this issue in structural counting, a controlled setting where human-visible counts can be audited. We construct a verifier-centric audit suite over seven counting objects: cell phones, chairs, and traffic lights for object counting, and clover leaflets, dresser drawer fronts, power-strip sockets, and turbine blades for substructure counting. Across 6,240 generated images from Qwen-Image-2512 and FLUX.2-dev, we compare open MLLM verifiers under count-only, reject-aware, zero-shot and few-shot protocols, plus TIFA-like target queries, a detector baseline, and frontier closed-model checks on two hard objects. On a balanced 2,280-image evaluation split, aggregate score matching can coexist with image-level disagreement, and verifier-use protocols and generator distributions change the measured reliability. Every verifier-protocol configuration also shrinks the human score difference between the two generators, by 7.5-25.0 pp, and with a third generator, Z-Image, all four tested protocols reverse the human ordering of Z-Image and FLUX.2-dev on our fixed prompt suite. GPT-5.5 and Claude Opus 4.7 checks do not remove the failure modes: GPT-5.5 still has only 59.92% clover valid-count fidelity, while Claude Opus 4.7 has lower same-slice agreement ($κ=0.432$). We propose reporting automatic benchmark scores with a factorized verifier reliability profile: aggregate score gap, image-level agreement, generator-comparison distortion, valid-image count fidelity, rejection behavior, output compliance, and directional bias.

</details>


### [55] [Learning Motion-Appearance Coupling Priors for Solving Video Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15420)
*Anselm Krainovic,Reinhard Heckel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出学习"运动-外观耦合"作为生成先验：用条件扩散模型建模运动诱导的外观残差分布，并将其作为即插即用正则项用于扩散式视频逆问题重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将预训练图像扩散先验与运动引导结合，以时间一致性约束（光度损失、warping）隐式假设运动可解释帧间或潜空间变化；该假设在遮挡、光照变化、非刚性形变下失效。

Method: 将运动诱导的外观残差（motion-induced appearance residual）的分布用条件扩散模型建模，学习其作为生成先验；该先验作为plug-in正则项嵌入扩散式视频重建流程，可与现有外观正则技术兼容。

Result: 在动态视频逆问题实验中，基于习得运动-外观耦合先验的重建优于光度一致性（photometric-consistency）与noise-warping基线；并成功迁移至心脏MRI重建。

Conclusion: 显式学习运动与外观的耦合关系作为生成先验，比隐式时间一致性约束更鲁棒，既能保留图像扩散模型的优势，又具备跨域（如医学影像）泛化能力。

Abstract: Reconstructing video from degraded measurements requires priors that capture both frame appearance and temporal dynamics. Many recent approaches combine pretrained image diffusion priors with motion guidance, sidestepping the costs of training large video models. Temporal consistency is often enforced via photometric losses or warping-based constraints, which implicitly assume that motion explains frame-to-frame or latent-to-latent changes up to small deviations, an assumption that fails under occlusions, lighting changes, and non-rigid deformation. In this paper, we propose to learn the coupling between motion and frame appearance as a generative prior. We learn the distribution of motion-induced appearance residuals with a conditional diffusion model and use it to regularize reconstruction in video inverse problems. Our motion-appearance prior acts as a plug-in regularizer for diffusion-based video reconstruction, preserving the benefits of image diffusion models while remaining compatible with existing appearance regularization techniques. Experiments on dynamic video inverse problems show that reconstruction with a learnt motion-appearance coupling prior outperforms photometric-consistency and noise-warping baselines, and that the approach also transfers to cardiac MRI reconstruction.

</details>


### [56] [ChartGalaxy++: A Richly Annotated Dataset for Chart Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15418)
*Duan Li,Zheng Wang,Jiangning Zhu,Yuhui Yuan,Shixia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChartGalaxy++，一个包含 217,195 张信息图图表、以场景图（scene graph）为核心标注形式的数据集，用节点表示视觉元素与语义分组、用边编码层次与空间关系，并在三个应用中验证其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息图图表数据集主要对单个元素做边界框与类别标注，属于元素级标注，无法刻画元素间的层次与空间关系，因而限制了信息图组织方式的理解以及结构化设计生成。

Method: 构建以场景图为表示的标注体系：节点对应视觉元素与语义分组，边编码层次与空间关系。真实信息图通过检测视觉元素并逐步分组来构造场景图；合成信息图则直接从生成过程中导出场景图。数据集规模为 217,195 张信息图图表。

Result: 通过三类应用验证实用性：1) 图像到场景图预测；2) 场景图增强的信息图问答；3) 图像生成中场景图保持性的评估。结果显示该数据集对图表理解与生成具有促进作用。

Conclusion: ChartGalaxy++ 以场景图标注弥补了元素级标注的不足，能够支撑信息图的理解、问答与结构化生成评估，推动图表理解与生成研究。

Abstract: Infographic charts communicate data through cohesive compositions of visual elements such as charts, text, and images. Existing infographic chart datasets primarily annotate individual elements with bounding boxes and category labels. Such element-level annotations do not capture hierarchical and spatial relationships, limiting their use for understanding how elements are organized and related within an infographic and for generating well-organized designs. To address this limitation, we introduce ChartGalaxy++, a richly annotated dataset of 217,195 infographic charts. At the core of this dataset is a scene graph representation, in which nodes represent visual elements and semantic groups, and edges encode their hierarchical and spatial relationships. For real infographics, we construct scene graphs by detecting and progressively grouping visual elements; for synthetic ones, we derive them directly from the generation process. We demonstrate the utility of ChartGalaxy++ through three applications: 1) image-to-scene-graph prediction, 2) scene-graph-augmented infographic question answering, and 3) evaluation of scene graph preservation in image generation. Together, these applications highlight the value of ChartGalaxy++ for advancing chart understanding and generation.

</details>


### [57] [CaseScope: A Multi-Field Benchmark for Case-Level Pathology Diagnosis with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15325)
*Wenchuan Zhang,Chengyang Zhang,Fengling Li,Ting Yin,Chen Shu,Jie Chen,Shuwan Zhang,Xiaoyan Wu,Zhang Zhang,Yuhao Yi,Jiancheng Lv,Hong Bu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaseScope：一个要求MLLM从同一病灶的多个视野进行诊断的病理基准，含教学库436例与88本图谱800例，覆盖18个器官系统843个实体、6项任务共4679题；28个开源与4个闭源模型诊断均不稳定，且对多视野信息利用不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理诊断本质上是多视野、多尺度整合的过程，而现有多模态大模型基准多只给单张图像或要求全切片流程，少数提供多视野的基准也仅取自单一公开队列的癌症切片嵌套区域，缺乏真实临床推理场景的覆盖。

Method: 构建CaseScope：(1) CaseScope-Teaching，来自住院医师培训图像库的436例，经重建、去标识化、按WHO本体协调，并对自动预审存疑的病例由病理学家复审；(2) CaseScope-Atlas，来自88本已发表图谱的800例（其中387例为单一疾病的图组），由两个LLM从图注标注且须一致。共覆盖18个器官系统843个实体，设计6项任务共4679题，包括开放式诊断、带本体引导难负样本的十选一诊断（循环评估）、形态学发现的验证与定位。评测28个开源与4个闭源MLLM。

Result: 诊断不稳定：在全部十种选项轮换下均正确，最佳开源与闭源模型分别仅17.9%与61.7%（教学病例）；错误答案偏向经典相似病变。开源模型对教学病例附加视野利用甚少，一行标本部位信息影响更大；在图谱病例提供≥3个视野时，视野数量的作用与部位相当。多数开源模型验证形态学发现仅略高于随机（中位62.0%，最佳81.5%），图像带来中位+3.4个百分点增益；闭源模型达83.2–93.2%。两数据源间排名大体一致。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM尚不具备稳健的多视野病理诊断能力，对跨视野证据整合与形态学发现验证明显不足；CaseScope可作为揭示该差距的诊断性基准，评测代码已开源。

Abstract: Pathologists rarely diagnose from a single image: they scan a section at low power for architecture, zoom into selected fields for cytology, and integrate the evidence across views and scales. Pathology benchmarks for multimodal large language models (MLLMs), however, mostly ask about one image or require whole-slide pipelines, and the few that show several fields draw nested regions from cancer slides of one public cohort. We introduce CaseScope, a benchmark of diagnosis from several curated fields of one lesion: CaseScope-Teaching, 436 cases from a residency-training image library, reconstructed, de-identified, harmonised against a WHO-based ontology and re-examined by a pathologist wherever an automated pre-review raised a doubt, and CaseScope-Atlas, 800 cases from 88 published atlases (387 of them figure series of one disease), labelled from the captions by two LLMs that must agree. They cover 843 entities across 18 organ systems and pose 4,679 items in six tasks, from open-ended and ten-way diagnosis with ontology-guided hard negatives under circular evaluation to the verification and location of morphological findings. Across twenty-eight open and four proprietary MLLMs, diagnoses are unstable -- the best open and proprietary models are correct under all ten option rotations on 17.9% and 61.7% of the teaching cases -- and wrong answers favour the classic look-alike; open models make little use of the additional fields on teaching cases, where the one-line specimen site matters more, although on atlas cases with three or more fields the fields count about as much as the site; and most open models verify findings barely above chance (median 62.0% vs. 50%, best 81.5%), gaining a median +3.4 points from images; proprietary models reach 83.2-93.2%. Rankings broadly replicate between the sources. The evaluation code is available at https://github.com/Wenchuan-Zhang/CaseScope.

</details>


### [58] [Beyond the Explicit: Benchmarking and Detecting Implicit Harm in Short Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15306)
*Chenyang Li,Qifan Yu,Saihui Hou,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式有害短视频数据集DeepHarm-7K（7,110条、细粒度分类、多阶段质控）与多模态检测框架DeepHarm-VL，通过视觉-音频-跨模态两轮推理先识别表面对证再推断潜在有害含义，在多个开源视频语言模型上优于强基线与SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频成为主流媒介，有害内容日益以隐式、碎片化方式传播；既有工作聚焦暴力、仇恨言论等显式类别，难以识别通过叙事语境、情绪框架或跨模态线索隐性传递误导性价值观的视频，其重要原因之一是缺乏专门的隐式有害内容基准。

Method: 构建DeepHarm-7K：按细粒度有害内容分类体系标注，覆盖多类真实场景中的隐式有害视频，并经过严格的多阶段质量控制。在此基础上提出DeepHarm-VL多模态检测框架，融合视觉、音频与跨模态推理，采用两轮推理策略，逐步先识别表层证据、再推断潜在有害语义。

Result: 在多个开源视频-语言模型上，方法相对强基线与现有最优方法均取得一致性提升，在显式与隐式有害短视频检测上均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 细粒度基准与分阶段跨模态推理相结合，可有效缓解隐式有害短视频检测的基准缺失与识别困难问题，为该方向提供了数据与方法基础。

Abstract: Short videos have become a dominant online medium, where harmful content is increasingly conveyed in implicit and fragmented forms. Prior work largely focuses on explicit categories, such as violence and hate speech, and often fails to detect videos that subtly promote misleading values through narrative context, emotional framing, or cross-modal cues, partly due to the lack of dedicated benchmarks for implicit harm. To bridge this gap, we construct DeepHarm-7K, a large-scale harmful short-video dataset comprising 7,110 samples annotated according to a fine-grained harmful-content taxonomy. The dataset systematically covers implicitly harmful videos across diverse real-world scenarios and is constructed through a rigorous multi-stage quality-control process. Building on this dataset, we propose DeepHarm-VL, a multimodal detection framework that integrates visual, audio, and cross-modal reasoning. The framework employs a two-round reasoning strategy to progressively identify surface-level evidence and infer latent harmful implications. Experimental results show consistent improvements over strong baselines and state-of-the-art methods across multiple open-source video-language models, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting both explicit and implicit harmful short videos.

</details>


### [59] [Driving World Models Move the Camera, Not the Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15260)
*Yuqiao Feng,Xiaoyu Ao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有驾驶世界模型虽然会随规划指令移动相机，却几乎不改变场景，其场景响应最多只有真实视频的三分之一；ReTime-Compose 通过"以几何形式注入规划"的免训练方法修复了这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划条件驾驶世界模型被用于比较候选方案，因此其预测应随规划改变而像真实场景那样变化；但广泛使用的控制指标只检验相机是否跟随指令，无法揭示"相机动了、场景没动"的失效模式。

Method: 利用已记录驾驶数据构造反事实参考：对日志做时间重采样可得其他车速下位姿匹配的图像与 LiDAR，双目相机可记录横向平移视角。以"场景响应"（预测复现真实距离变化的比例）为指标评估模型；并通过生成器内部干预定位失败源于规划通道。据此提出 ReTime-Compose：将某记录速度 rollout 的帧传输到规划相机位姿，冻结模型只补全无帧覆盖区域，无需训练。

Result: 已发布世界模型的场景响应至多约为真实视频的三分之一，轨迹分数无法区分被执行与被执行忽略的速度指令；预测几何主要跟随上下文帧，一旦上下文携带规划几何，规划 token 增益很小。该方法使冻结生成器在 KITTI、nuScenes、Argoverse 2 上对约 0.54 m 横向平移的响应接近真实场景，并在三个生成器上对减速规划、两个生成器上对加速规划响应强于重采样。

Conclusion: 规划条件应作为几何而非 token 传递；ReTime-Compose 以免训练方式显著提升世界模型对规划的场景级响应，并揭示现有控制指标不足以评估规划条件建模。

Abstract: Plan-conditioned driving world models are used to compare candidate plans, so their forecast should change with the plan as the real scene would, yet widely used control metrics check only whether the camera follows the command. Recorded drives already contain real outcomes of plans that were never driven: retiming a log gives pose-matched images and LiDAR for other speeds, and a stereo rig records a laterally shifted view. Against these references, released world models move their camera but barely move the scene: their scene response, the share of the real change in distance that the forecast reproduces, reaches at most about a third of real video's, and the trajectory score cannot tell an executed speed command from an ignored one. Interventions inside the generator trace the failure to the plan's channel. The forecast geometry follows the context frames; once they carry the planned geometry, plan tokens add little. ReTime-Compose therefore delivers the plan as geometry, without training. It transports the frames of one logged-speed rollout to the planned cameras, and the frozen model completes only what no frame covers. Frozen generators then follow a 0.54 m lateral shift nearly as the real scene does on KITTI, nuScenes and Argoverse 2, and respond more than re-sampling to slow-down plans in three generators and to speed-up plans in two.

</details>


### [60] [MAST: Medoid-Anchored Subspace Steering for Visual Grounding in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15168)
*Zhitao Chen,Zhenxin Cai,Jingyuan Li,Jin Sha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、输入自适应的激活引导框架MAST，用多中心聚类与medoid锚定的低秩子空间方向替代全局均值引导，并给出探针加权的MAST-PR变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM激活引导以全局激活均值作引导向量，因任务激活呈多中心几何分布，全局均值落在簇间空区、不忠实于任何单一任务，导致跨任务干扰与性能不稳；且各任务纯簇内引导信号低秩，不同查询任务需强调不同的局部基方向族。

Method: 按任务分组样本，在组内做多中心聚类并为每簇选代表medoid；推理时每个查询路由到多个代表medoid，对每个匹配簇提取低秩局部引导方向并跨簇聚合得到最终引导向量；MAST-PR在此基础上用轻量任务感知探针重加权任务特化的局部方向族。

Result: MAST-PR相比MAST平均提升+8.09分。

Conclusion: 激活引导应尊重任务激活的多中心、低秩局部结构，基于medoid锚定子空间与查询路由的免训练框架可缓解跨任务干扰并提升稳定性，探针重加权可进一步增益。

Abstract: Existing activation steering methods for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) use the global activation mean as the steering vector, which tends to cause cross-task interference and unstable performance. We trace this failure to the multi-centered geometry of task activations. The global mean lands in the empty region between clusters and is faithful to no single task. Moreover, the steering signal in each task-pure local cluster is low-rank, and different query tasks benefit from emphasizing different families of local-basis directions under the shared routing protocol. To address this, we propose MAST (Medoid-Anchored Subspace Steering), a training-free, input-adaptive activation steering framework. MAST groups samples by task, performs multi-center clustering within each group, and selects one representative medoid per cluster. At inference time, each query is routed to multiple representative medoids. For each matched cluster, a low-rank local steering direction is extracted and aggregated across clusters to produce the final steering vector. Building on MAST, MAST-PR (Probe-Reweighted) uses a lightweight task-aware probe to reweight task-specialized local-direction families, yielding a further average improvement of +8.09 points over MAST.

</details>


### [61] [From Concepts to Interactions: Dynamic Graph Semantic Recomposition for Safe Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15149)
*Qinghui Gong,Jinshan Lai,Xunlei Chen,Hua Meng,Zhengchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态图语义重组（DGSR），在采样过程中构建并更新文本-视觉状态语义交互图，结合安全/不安全成对监督，实现上下文感知的选择性干预，既抑制不安全内容又保留良性生成，适用于T2I与T2V。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配Transformer在T2I/T2V上进步显著，但无约束生成存在违规与伦理风险，需安全对齐。概念安全性依赖上下文（如用于烹饪的刀是安全的，用于暴力则有害），而现有概念擦除方法不加区分地压制静态孤立表征，忽视语义交互的动态演化，导致含潜在敏感词的安全场景被误抑制，损害生成质量。

Method: DGSR：在采样过程中依据演化的文本-视觉状态动态构建并更新语义交互图；通过上下文感知的图推理捕捉语义依赖；利用成对的安全/不安全监督区分有害与合法交互，从而精确定位并对高风险语义关系进行选择性干预，同时保留良性语义。方法冻结预训练骨干，兼容图像与视频生成。

Result: 在12项T2I评测中有10项取得最低攻击成功率（ASR）；相较最强基线，T2V平均不安全帧率降低41.2%；同时保持较高的生成保真度与时间一致性。

Conclusion: 将安全对齐从静态概念压制转向上下文相关的语义关系干预是可行且有效的，DGSR在显著降低不安全输出的同时维持生成质量，并统一支持图像与视频生成。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow-matching Transformers have greatly improved Text-to-Image (T2I) and Text-to-Video (T2V) generation, but unconstrained generation risks producing illegal or unethical content. Safety alignment must therefore suppress unsafe content while preserving legitimate generation. A concept's safety depends on context: a knife used for cooking is safe, whereas its use in violence is harmful. Yet most concept erasure methods indiscriminately suppress static, isolated representations, overlooking evolving semantic interactions. Consequently, safe scenes containing potentially sensitive terms may be unintentionally suppressed, compromising generation quality. To address this, we propose Dynamic Graph Semantic Recomposition (DGSR), which dynamically constructs and updates a semantic interaction graph from evolving text visual states during sampling. Context aware graph reasoning captures semantic dependencies, while paired safe/unsafe supervision distinguishes harmful from legitimate interactions, enabling precise identification of and selective intervention on high risk semantic relationships while preserving benign semantics. DGSR freezes the pretrained backbone and supports both image and video generation. Experiments show that DGSR achieves the lowest Attack Success Rate (ASR) in 10 of 12 T2I evaluations and reduces the average T2V unsafe frame rate by 41.2\% relative to the strongest baseline, while maintaining high generation fidelity and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [62] [Toward Cross-Rubric Surgical Skill Assessment via Joint Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15125)
*Jiajun Cheng,Haidar M. Abdul-Muhsin,Juha Choi,Shan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Predictive Rubric Alignment (PRA)，用自然语言技能等级描述对手术视频打分，实现跨评分标准的可迁移技能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家审阅手术视频对培训至关重要但耗时费力。现有自动评估方法多预测固定评分标准下的预定技能等级或数值分数，难以迁移到语义等价但表述不同的评估标准。

Method: PRA为联合嵌入预测方法：不匹配等级概率，而是预测评分者加权的技能等级描述嵌入组合；相邻描述特征构成分数参数化路径，将预测嵌入投影到该路径上读取分数。含两变体：PRA-item用可学习查询对应预定义评估条目；PRA-cond由文本条目名称生成查询，支持无需重训练评估未见条目。

Result: 在AIxSuture专家评分缝合视频数据集上，PRA在技能分类上优于已发表基线；仅在两个AIxSuture条目上训练后，PRA-cond零样本评估JIGSAWS未见条目，获得正分数相关性，且Grad-CAM与器械和缝合线的重叠高于描述匹配基线。

Conclusion: 预测式评分标准对齐是实现灵活、可解释自动手术技能评估的有前景方向。

Abstract: Expert review of surgical videos is essential for surgical training but labor-intensive, motivating AI-assisted skill assessment to provide feedback at scale. Although surgical assessment rubrics may use different language to describe similar skills, existing automated methods commonly predict predefined skill levels or numerical scores tied to fixed rubrics, limiting their ability to transfer across semantically equivalent assessment criteria. We propose Predictive Rubric Alignment (PRA), a joint embedding prediction approach that scores surgical videos using natural-language descriptions of skill levels. Rather than matching level probabilities, PRA predicts a rater-weighted combination of skill-level description embeddings. Adjacent description features define a score-parameterized path, and the predicted embedding is projected onto this path to read the score. We introduce two variants: \textit{PRA-item}, which uses learned queries for predefined assessment items, and \textit{PRA-cond}, which generates queries from textual item names to enable assessment of unseen items without retraining. On AIxSuture, a dataset of expert-rated suturing videos, PRA outperforms published baselines in skill classification. We further evaluate zero-shot transfer to the smaller surgical skill assessment benchmark JIGSAWS. Trained on only two AIxSuture rubric items, PRA-cond assesses unseen JIGSAWS items without further training, yielding positive score correlations and greater Grad-CAM overlap with instruments and suture than a description-matching baseline. These results support predictive rubric alignment as a promising direction for flexible and explainable automated surgical skill assessment.

</details>


### [63] [From Seeing to Remembering: Brain-Informed Agentic Text-to-Video Generation for Enhancing Memorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15107)
*Shengqi Dang,Yi He,Jiaying Lei,Feilin Han,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MemCrafter——一种脑启发、迭代式智能体提示精炼框架，通过皮层区域神经记忆性信号与敏感性感知反馈模块提升文本到视频生成的记忆性，同时保持语义对齐与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告、教育、叙事等应用需要既忠实表达语义又令观众记忆深刻的视频；但面向记忆性的文本到视频生成研究不足：人类记忆涉及复杂神经过程，难以建模并转化为生成引导。

Method: 智能体框架含两部分：(1) 基于皮层区域的神经记忆性信号，依据估计的神经响应预测视频记忆性，同时提供优化目标与可解释的区域级信息；(2) 敏感性感知反馈模块，基于对优化目标的概念性敏感性分析导出可操作的提示精炼指导，并保持语义保真。

Result: 大量实验表明MemCrafter持续提升预测的视频记忆性，同时维持语义对齐与感知质量。

Conclusion: 脑信号驱动的智能体提示精炼可有效实现记忆性导向的文本到视频生成，并在记忆性、语义一致性和视觉质量间取得平衡。

Abstract: Generating videos that faithfully convey intended semantics while remaining memorable to viewers is valuable for applications such as advertising, education, and storytelling. However, memorability-oriented text-to-video generation remains underexplored, as human memory involves complex neural processes that are difficult to model and translate into generation guidance. In this paper, we propose MemCrafter, brain-informed approach that enhances video memorability through iteratively agentic prompt refinement. The agentic framework contains two key components. First, we construct a cortical region-based neuro-memorability signal that predicts video memorability from estimated neural responses, providing both an optimization objective and interpretable region-level information. Second, we develop a sensitivity-aware feedback module, motivated by a conceptual sensitivity analysis of the optimization objective, to derive actionable guidance for prompt refinement while preserving semantic fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MemCrafter consistently improves predicted video memorability while maintaining semantic alignment and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [64] [Prediction or Persistence? What Future-Prediction Scores Miss About World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15082)
*Animesh Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出用配对"持续性基线"（persistence control）把视频世界模型的未来预测分数拆解为编码器质量、近期相似性与预测器自身贡献；在27,000次检索试验中发现分数与预测器贡献显著解耦，建议报告分数与其持续性基线之差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法主要依靠未来预测分数来比较和选择预训练视频世界模型作为机器人学习组件，但该分数本身混杂了编码器质量、近期未来与近期过去的高度相似性，以及预测器自身的真实贡献，因此不能单独作为"具备预测能力"的证据。

Method: 构建匹配对照实验：在家庭自我中心视频上做27,000次未来检索试验，模型需从同一视频的11个候选片段中选出真实后续（随机基线0.091），并按各自训练准则零样本排序。每个模型配对一个"持续性对照"——结构完全相同但从不调用预测器，配对差值即隔离出预测器的贡献。在V-JEPA家族及五个模型上对比，其中包含两个动作条件模型（在其训练分布之外评估）。

Result: 分数与贡献解耦：在原生视频的V-JEPA家族内部，预测器为V-JEPA 2.1带来+0.291的top-1提升，而对V-JEPA 2在相同试验上仅+0.03~+0.04。五个模型的贡献范围从+0.291到负值，且仅在两个动作条件模型上为负。仅靠持续性基线即可复现由预训练预期的编码器排序，说明该探针确实捕到真实信号。

Conclusion: 应同时报告未来预测分数及其持续性基线，二者之差（而非分数本身）才使该指标可解释；这是一个仅需一次前向传播的必要检查。下游实用性仍需通过独立的规划或控制评测来建立。

Abstract: Pretrained video world models are compared and chosen as components for robot learning largely by future-prediction scores. On its own, such a score cannot be read as evidence of prediction, because it mixes encoder quality, the near future's resemblance to the recent past, and the predictor's own contribution. We separate the three with a matched control. On 27,000 future-retrieval trials from household egocentric video, a model must pick the true continuation of a clip among eleven candidates from the same video (chance 0.091), ranked zero-shot by its own training criterion. Each model is paired with a persistence control, an identical copy whose predictor is never invoked, so the paired difference isolates the predictor. Score and contribution dissociate within the native-video V-JEPA family alone: the predictor adds +0.291 top-1 for V-JEPA 2.1 but +0.03 to +0.04 for V-JEPA 2 on the identical trials. Across five models the contribution ranges from +0.291 to negative, and it is negative only for the two action-conditioned models, which we evaluate outside their training regime. Persistence alone recovers the encoder ordering expected from pretraining, a sign that the probe tracks real signal. We suggest reporting each future-prediction score together with its persistence baseline. The gap between them, not the score, is what makes the number interpretable: a necessary check costing one forward pass. Establishing downstream usefulness is a separate matter, for a planning or control evaluation.

</details>


### [65] [Test-Time Selective Adaptation for Cross-Modal UAV Object Tracking with Token Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15059)
*Yun Xiao,Hailong Ye,Zhicheng Zhao,Lei Liu,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TSAT（Token Shift测试时选择性自适应），从无标签测试序列中在线提取空间与时间自适应信号，解决跨模态UAV跟踪中的模态外观差异与目标位置突变问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态UAV目标跟踪面临两类挑战：模态切换、UAV运动与传感器间偏移导致的模态诱导外观差异和目标位置突变。现有方法虽建模模态与空间变化，但主要依赖离线表示学习，测试阶段缺乏面向定位的在线自适应能力。

Method: 1）可控Token Shift辅助任务：在预定义token平移与定位偏移之间建立显式空间对应，生成稳定且无需标注的空间自适应信号；2）时间反向预测辅助任务：利用帧间时序依赖，缓解连续跟踪中的累积定位误差；3）可靠性感知更新门控策略：筛选有效自适应信号，抑制不可靠跟踪样本干扰，稳定在线参数更新。

Result: 在CM-UOT数据集上，TSAT相较现有测试时自适应方法达到SOTA性能，在频繁模态切换与严重空间位移下表现出更优鲁棒性；跨跟踪框架与多数据集的实验进一步验证了其有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以无标注测试序列驱动的选择性测试时自适应可有效弥补离线表示学习的定位自适应不足，Token Shift、反向预测与可靠性门控三者协同显著提升跨模态UAV跟踪的鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Cross-modal unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) object tracking faces modality-induced appearance discrepancies and abrupt target-position shifts that can arise from modality switching, UAV motion, and inter-sensor offsets. Although existing methods model modality and spatial variations, they primarily rely on offline representation learning and offer limited localization-oriented adaptation during testing. To address these challenges, we propose Test-Time Selective Adaptation with Token Shift (TSAT), which derives spatial and temporal adaptation signals from unlabeled test sequences for online optimization. Specifically, we design a controllable token shift auxiliary task, which imposes explicit spatial correspondence between predefined token translations and resultant localization offsets, generating stable annotation-free spatial adaptation signals. Furthermore, we introduce a temporal backward prediction auxiliary task to exploit frame-wise temporal dependencies, effectively mitigating cumulative localization errors in continuous tracking. To suppress interference from unreliable tracking samples, we develop a reliability-aware update gating strategy to screen valid adaptation signals and stabilize online parameter updates. Experiments on CM-UOT demonstrate that TSAT achieves state-of-the-art performance against existing test-time adaptation methods, exhibiting superior robustness under frequent modality switching and severe spatial displacement. Experiments across tracking frameworks and datasets further validate the effectiveness and generalization capability of TSAT.

</details>


### [66] [HeadIO: Body-Frame Heading Decomposition for Neural Inertial Odometry on Head-Mounted Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14895)
*Taeyoung Yeon,Yiquan Li,Chenfeng Gao,Vasco Xu,Karan Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadIO 提出一种体坐标系航向分解与双向 Mamba 主干组成的头部惯性里程计方法，利用设备姿态的倾斜分量将重力对齐分量与水平分量分离以获得倾斜不变性，在四个头戴数据集上以近 3× 更低 FLOPs 和 8× 更少参数取得最优或领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有惯性里程计在世界坐标系下运行，不具偏航等变性；偏航增广不能保证等变性，学习式等变层虽可实现但计算代价高（EqNIO 约为 3× FLOPs）。原始体坐标 IMU 输入本身偏航不变，但头部倾斜会使重力与运动信号在设备轴上重新分配，导致表示具有倾斜变异性，因此需要一种兼具倾斜不变性与轻量性的表示。

Method: 将体坐标系航向分解与双向 Mamba 主干结合：仅利用设备姿态中的倾斜分量，把输入分解为重力对齐分量与水平分量，从而实现倾斜不变性；分解模块轻量、可与任意主干组合，并适配实时部署。

Result: 在 Nymeria（最大基准数据集）上，RTE-5s 相对采用学习式等变层的世界坐标方法提升 8.1%（0.260 m→0.239 m），FLOPs 约低 3×、参数少 8×；在所有 Aria-glasses 数据集上 RTE-5s 达 SOTA；IMU-only 部署场景下在 Nymeria 仍为 SOTA。消融显示方法主要依赖倾斜精度（可由重力信号获得）；航向方面，单系数陀螺仪校正消除 11.6% 航向误差并使 RTE-5s 提升 2.5%，稀疏外部航向校正（如每 30 s 一次）可提升 RTE-5s 12.0%。

Conclusion: 基于体坐标航向分解的表示能同时获得偏航等变性与倾斜不变性，且计算高效；方法对倾斜精度依赖强，航向误差可通过轻量校正显著缓解，具备在头戴设备上实时部署的实用价值。

Abstract: Inertial odometry (IO) using only Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) enables camera-free head tracking in head-mounted devices such as VR headsets, AR glasses, and earbuds. Prior IO methods operate in a gravity-aligned world frame, which is not yaw-equivariant. Yaw augmentation improves robustness but does not guarantee equivariance; learned equivariant layers achieve it, but EqNIO's full pipeline costs nearly 3$\times$ the FLOPs of ours. While raw body-frame IMU inputs are inherently yaw-invariant, head tilt redistributes both gravity and motion signals across device axes, making the representation tilt-variant. We propose HeadIO, which combines a body-frame heading decomposition with a bidirectional Mamba backbone for head-mounted inertial odometry. Our decomposition achieves tilt invariance by separating gravity-aligned and horizontal components using only the tilt component of device orientation. We evaluate on four head-mounted datasets and find that HeadIO improves RTE-5s by 8.1% (0.260 m $\to$ 0.239 m) over world-frame methods with learned equivariant layers on Nymeria, the largest benchmark dataset, at nearly 3$\times$ lower FLOPs and 8$\times$ fewer parameters. HeadIO achieves state-of-the-art performance on our primary metric, RTE-5s, on all Aria-glasses datasets, which natively provide body-frame semantics for our decomposition method. We further evaluate the IMU-only deployment scenario on Nymeria, where HeadIO remains state-of-the-art. We show that our method relies mainly on tilt accuracy, which can be obtained from the gravity signal; for yaw, a single-coefficient gyroscope correction removes 11.6% of the yaw error and improves RTE-5s by 2.5%, and sparse external yaw corrections (for example, one every 30 s) can improve RTE-5s by 12.0%. Our decomposition is lightweight, composes with any backbone, and is suitable for real-time deployment on head-mounted devices.

</details>


### [67] [BirdDreams: Joint Perspective and BEV Video Generation for a Plausible, Reactive Driving World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14815)
*Junyoung Seo,Jaewoo Seo,Seungryong Kim,Laura Leal-Taixé,Xiuming Zhang,Seung Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BirdDreams：自回归驾驶世界模型，联合生成透视视频与鸟瞰(BEV)视频，将场景布局纳入预测目标，实现精确自车控制与合理周围车辆运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从透视视频学习周围车辆运动困难：运动与外观纠缠且存在遮挡。现有方法需以指定车辆运动的布局（未来轨迹）为条件，缺乏未来轨迹输入时控制受限。

Method: 联合自回归生成透视视频与由场景标注渲染的示意性BEV视频（固定度量尺度、简单形状、遮挡少）；两路视频扩散Transformer通过跨流联合注意力互相提供信息；将BEV最新帧沿输入自车轨迹平移旋转生成软条件，作为几何接口实现自车控制；推理时仅需单张相机图像，无需HD地图或车辆状态输入。

Result: 在PhysicalAI-AV与nuScenes上，比先前驾驶世界模型更忠实地遵循输入自车轨迹；在无自车动作条件时生成更合理的自车运动；人工评估显示在生成真实周围车辆行为方面优于仅透视基线。

Conclusion: 将BEV布局作为预测内容可缓解遮挡与外观-运动纠缠，为驾驶世界模型提供简洁的几何控制接口，实现无需地图与轨迹输入的联合视频生成与自车控制。

Abstract: Driving world models aim to simulate how a scene unfolds under different ego actions. This requires plausible motion of surrounding vehicles as well as precise ego-trajectory control. Learning vehicle motion from perspective video alone is challenging because motion is entangled with appearance and vehicles can be occluded. Prior work therefore conditions video generation on layouts that specify vehicle motion, providing control over surrounding vehicles but requiring their future trajectories as inputs. We introduce BirdDreams, an autoregressive driving world model that jointly generates perspective video and a schematic bird's-eye-view (BEV) video, making scene layout part of the predicted future. The BEV, rendered from scene annotations for training, depicts roads and vehicles as simple shapes at a fixed metric scale, providing a top-down view of their positions and motion with reduced occlusion between vehicles. Perspective video, by contrast, retains scene details that the BEV omits, such as traffic signals and object appearance.
