Joint attention between two video diffusion transformers, one per stream, lets each stream inform the other during generation. The predicted BEV also provides a geometric interface for ego control: we translate and rotate its latest frame along an input ego trajectory and use the resulting frames as a soft condition that shows how the scene would appear to move if only the ego vehicle moved. At inference, BirdDreams generates both videos from a single camera image without requiring an HD map or vehicle-state inputs. On PhysicalAI-AV and nuScenes, BirdDreams follows input ego trajectories more faithfully than prior driving world models and generates more plausible ego motion without ego-action conditioning. Human evaluation shows that BirdDreams outperforms a perspective-only baseline in generating realistic surrounding-vehicle behavior.

</details>


### [68] [WaveletLoRA: Frequency-Aware Content-Style Decomposition for Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15617)
*Peiyao Wang,Jiahui Sun,Weining Wang,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaveletLoRA，一种基于小波域监督的频域感知框架，通过为DWT各子带分配独立LoRA分支实现单图内容-风格显式解耦，并构建WaveBench及VLM评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图内容-风格解耦是个性化图像生成的核心难题，需将主体身份与视觉风格分离以支持灵活重组。现有方法主要在空间域操作，未显式利用内容与风格天然对应的不同频率特性。

Method: 1) 对参考图做多级离散小波变换(DWT)，分解为低频与高频子带；2) 为每个子带配置专属LoRA分支：低频分支建模全局外观以获取风格，高频分支捕捉结构细节以保留内容；3) 引入稠密Mixture-of-Experts聚合模块与频域正则化目标，抑制风格泄漏、增强内容保真；4) 构建WaveBench基准，并提出基于VLM的评估协议，显式度量属性相似度与属性分离度。

Result: 大量实验表明WaveletLoRA一致优于现有方法，生成结果更符合人类偏好。

Conclusion: 频域显式监督为内容-风格解耦提供了有效且可解释的路径，配合专门的基准与评估协议可系统衡量解耦质量。

Abstract: Content-style decomposition from a single image is a fundamental challenge in personalized image generation, aiming to separate subject identity from visual style for flexible recomposition. Existing approaches primarily operate in the spatial domain, without explicitly exploiting the distinct frequency characteristics naturally associated with content and style. In this paper, we propose WaveletLoRA, a novel frequency-aware framework for explicit content-style decomposition through wavelet-domain supervision. Specifically, we first apply a multi-level discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to decompose the reference image into low-frequency and high-frequency subbands. We then assign a dedicated LoRA branch to each subband, where the low-frequency branch models global appearance for style acquisition, while the high-frequency branches capture structural details for content preservation. To further improve disentanglement quality, we introduce a dense Mixture-of-Experts aggregation module together with a frequency-domain regularization objective, which reduces style leakage and enhance content fidelity. In addition, we establish WaveBench and develop a VLM-based evaluation protocol that explicitly measures both attribute similarity and attribute separation for disentanglement assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WaveletLoRA consistently outperforms existing methods and produces results that better align with human preference.

</details>


### [69] [Learning-Based Interpolation of Detector Gaps in Photon-Counting X-ray Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15614)
*Johannes B. Thalhammer,Patrick Ilg,Tina Dorosti,Sebastian Peterhansl,Theresa Hiu,Daniel Frey,Zijin Huang,Florian Schaff,Daniela Pfeiffer,Franz Pfeiffer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对拼接式探测器视场物理间隙的插补任务，作者将WGAN-GP与条件Pix2Pix适配于nanoCT投影，通过输出掩码与强度统计约束实现仅修改间隙像素的填充，二者在LPIPS与直方图距离上最优，Pix2Pix训练成本低约5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拼接模块化探测器的视场存在物理间隙，必须在相位恢复、断层重建等下游处理前填补。与自然图像修复不同，探测器间隙插补有两个硬约束：非间隙像素必须保持完全不变；填充区域的强度统计（均值与分布）必须保持，因为均值强度即使微小偏移也会在重建后引发环形伪影。

Method: 将WGAN-GP（带梯度惩罚）与条件Pix2Pix两种生成模型适配到该任务；采用掩码策略将全部改动限制在间隙像素内；设计约束填充区域均值强度与强度分布的损失项。在含模拟间隙的nanoCT投影上训练，并与线性插值、Navier–Stokes修复、深度修复网络MADF对比：模拟间隙上做定量评估，真实探测器间隙上做视觉评估；并进行消融实验。

Result: WGAN-GP与Pix2Pix取得最佳感知相似度（LPIPS）与直方图距离，推理时间仅次于线性插值；Pix2Pix训练所需GPU小时约为WGAN-GP的1/5。消融表明对网络输出施加掩码对生成合理修复结果贡献最大。应用于正弦图时，Pix2Pix填充最自然，但相较线性插值其重建质量仅略有提升。

Conclusion: 带掩码输出与强度统计约束的生成式模型可有效完成探测器间隙插补，其中Pix2Pix在质量与训练成本间取得最佳折中；但对正弦图重建的最终增益有限，说明该环节仍是下游重建质量的次要瓶颈。代码与最终Pix2Pix权重已开源。

Abstract: Image detectors assembled from tiled modules have physical gaps in the field of view that must be filled before downstream processing such as phase retrieval or tomographic reconstruction. Unlike natural-image inpainting, detector-gap interpolation must leave all other pixels unmodified and preserve the intensity statistics of the filled region, since even a small shift in mean intensity can cause ring artifacts after reconstruction. We adapted two generative models, a Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty (WGAN-GP) and a conditional Pix2Pix model, to this task. We implemented a masking strategy that restricts all changes to the gap pixels and loss terms that constrain the mean intensity and the intensity distribution of the filled region. Both models were trained on nanoCT projections with simulated gaps and compared with linear interpolation, Navier--Stokes inpainting, and the deep image-inpainting network MADF, quantitatively on simulated gaps and visually on real detector gaps. WGAN-GP and Pix2Pix achieved the best perceptual similarity (LPIPS) and histogram distance and, apart from linear interpolation, the shortest inference time. Pix2Pix required about five times fewer training GPU-hours than WGAN-GP. Ablation studies showed that masking the network output contributes by far the most to plausible inpainting results. Applied to sinograms, Pix2Pix produced the most natural gap filling but improved the reconstruction only slightly compared with linear interpolation. The source code and the weights of the final Pix2Pix model are available at https://github.com/J-3TO/detectorGapRemoval_Pix2Pix.

</details>


### [70] [Taming Neural Fields with a Frequency-Lipschitz Curriculum for Sparse-View CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15652)
*Songtao Hu,Jingrui Wu,Hong Ding,Fanhao Zhu,Yunhao Liu,Suyang Xi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLIC框架，用频率-Lipschitz联合课程显式调度INR表示容量，实现稀疏视角CT的单阶段自先验重建，达SOTA无监督质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVCT投影不足导致不适定逆问题，易拟合条纹伪影与虚构结构；现有INR依赖隐式谱偏置或迭代外部去噪，对优化过程中高频容量何时释放缺乏显式控制。

Method: 核心为频率-Lipschitz联合课程：随机傅里叶特征编码与非均匀Lipschitz预算下的谱归一化MLP解码耦合，引导由粗到细重建；终端容量由FBP梯度统计自动校准，实现扫描自适应、免逐扫描调参；并引入先验引导自适应射线采样，在高梯度解剖边界集中积分采样以提升前向模型保真度。全程单阶段训练，无需迭代去噪。

Result: 在四个公开CT数据集、无噪与低剂量设置下，取得SOTA无监督重建质量，并在多样解剖结构上表现一致稳定。

Conclusion: 将INR表示容量显式化、可调度化，配合自动容量校准与自适应采样，为SVCT提供高效且鲁棒的单阶段自监督重建方案。

Abstract: Sparse-view computed tomography (SVCT) reduces radiation dose by acquiring fewer projection views, but the resulting under-determined inverse problem requires careful regularization to prevent reconstruction algorithms from fitting streak artifacts and hallucinated structures. Existing implicit neural representation (INR) methods rely on implicit spectral bias or iterative external denoising, yet they provide limited control over when high-frequency capacity is exposed during optimization. To address this limitation, we propose FLIC (Frequency-Lipschitz INR Curriculum), a capacity-controlled self-prior INR framework that makes the network's representational capacity explicit and schedulable. The core of our method is a joint frequency-Lipschitz curriculum that couples random Fourier feature encoding with spectrally normalized MLP decoding under non-uniform Lipschitz budgets, guiding reconstruction along a coarse-to-fine trajectory from stable global anatomy to fine details. The terminal capacity is automatically calibrated from FBP gradient statistics, enabling scan-adaptive regularization without per-scan manual tuning of the terminal Lipschitz budget. We further introduce a prior-guided adaptive ray sampling strategy that concentrates integration samples near high-gradient anatomical boundaries, improving forward-model fidelity. The entire framework operates in a single training stage without iterative denoising, enabling efficient per-image reconstruction. Extensive experiments on four public CT datasets under noise-free and low-dose settings show that our method achieves state-of-the-art unsupervised reconstruction quality with consistent performance across diverse anatomical structures.

</details>


### [71] [TRACE: Trajectory Synchronization for Training-Free Resolution Extension in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15983)
*Libo Zhu,Fan Li,Zhixin Wang,Binglu Wang,Weisheng Dong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练方法TRACE，通过同步低/高分辨率生成轨迹实现高分辨率视频生成，兼顾细节、结构与效率，较FreeSwim提升2.0-2.9倍速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型多受限于低分辨率，直接高分辨率训练成本高昂；现有免训练方法在细节保真、场景结构和效率上仍不足。

Method: TRACE在早期去噪阶段按对应噪声水平对齐低/高分辨率预测的场景内容，由低分辨率预测与当前高分辨率状态计算高分辨率更新，无需高分辨率模型推理；高分辨率轨迹保留细粒度变化以生成新细节；再用局部窗口注意力在共享高分辨率隐状态中精修细节；并检测疑似重复主体，对照低分辨率布局校正选定区域及其求解器历史，其它区域保持细节。

Result: 在多种视频生成骨干与分辨率上，感知质量与文本-视频对齐优于SOTA免训练方法FreeSwim，端到端加速2.0-2.9倍。

Conclusion: TRACE以轨迹同步与局部精修实现免训练高分辨率视频生成，在质量与效率上均优于现有免训练方案，代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: More text-to-video models are available, but many remain limited to low resolutions. Training them for higher resolutions is costly, while existing training-free methods still struggle with fine detail, scene structure, and efficiency. We propose TRACE, a training-free method that synchronizes low- and high-resolution generation trajectories. During early denoising, TRACE aligns predicted scene content at corresponding noise levels. It computes high-resolution updates from low-resolution predictions and the current high-resolution state, without high-resolution model inference. The high-resolution trajectory retains fine-grained variation for generating new details. Local window attention then refines fine details in a shared high-resolution latent state. TRACE checks suspected duplicate subjects against the corresponding low-resolution layout and corrects selected regions and their solver history while preserving detail elsewhere. Experiments across all kinds of video generation backbones and resolutions show improved perceptual quality and text-video alignment over FreeSwim, a state-of-the-art training-free method, with 2.0-2.9x end-to-end speedups. Our code and models will be released at https://github.com/libozhu03/TRACE.

</details>


### [72] [Lang-SVG: Hierarchical Image Vectorization with Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15941)
*Xi Liu,Chaoyi Zhou,Run Wang,Jiaang Li,Feng Luo,Junxiang Huang,Siyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Lang-SVG，用带语义标签与父子关系的显式树结构组织SVG图元，结合可控粒度开放词表分割先验、MLLM语义标注、深度自适应简化与树引导剪枝合并、L1-SAM-Prompting补全，并给出衡量结构质量与语义部件对齐的新评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像矢量化方法以像素级渲染保真度为目标，生成的SVG缺乏与人类感知的对象-部件层级对齐，难以编辑和操作。

Method: 1) 引入显式树结构组织SVG图元，带语义标签与父子关系；2) 用粒度可控、开放词表的分割先验初始化对象-部件层级；3) 利用带SVG树上下文的多模态大语言模型为未标注节点赋语义标签；4) 深度自适应简化降低形状复杂度；5) 树引导剪枝与合并消除冗余节点；6) L1-SAM-Prompting恢复缺失区域。

Result: 在渲染保真度与结构质量两方面均达到SOTA；并提出新的评测协议，量化结构质量与语义部件对齐程度。

Conclusion: 显式语义树结构与MLLM语义标注结合，可在保持高重建保真度的同时显著提升SVG的结构可编辑性与感知一致性，为矢量化提供了结构与语义层面的新评价范式。

Abstract: Image vectorization reconstructs raster images as compact scalable vector graph- ics (SVG), providing a structured and editable code representation of images. However, most existing vectorization methods optimize for pixel-level render- ing fidelity, often producing SVGs that lack alignment with human-perceived object-part hierarchies, making them difficult to manipulate. To address this, we propose Lang-SVG, which introduces an explicit tree structure to organize SVG primitives with semantic labels and parent-child relationships. Lang-SVG incorporates granularity-controllable and open-vocabulary segmentation priors to initialize object-part hierarchies and leverages multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with SVG tree context to assign semantic labels to unlabeled nodes. To reduce redundancy, we introduce depth-adaptive simplification to reduce shape complexity and Tree-Guided Pruning and Merging to eliminate redundant SVG nodes. L1-SAM-Prompting further recovers missing regions to improve recon- struction fidelity and structural completeness. Beyond reconstruction fidelity, we introduce a new evaluation protocol that measures structural quality and semantic part alignment. Experiments demonstrate that Lang-SVG achieves state-of-the-art performance in both rendering fidelity and structural quality. Project website: https://xiliu8006.github.io/Lang-SVG/.

</details>


### [73] [Reconstruct What You Can: Distractor-free 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16259)
*Joel Julin,Liuyue Xie,Ananya Bal,Mose Sakashita,Koichiro Niinuma,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCan-GS 提出基于形变场雅可比灵敏度分数的 4D 高斯泼溅框架，将一致场景内容（Core Gaussians）与不一致观测（Distractor Gaussians）分离，无需掩码或预训练分割器即可抑制遮挡物、阴影与烟雾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从随意拍摄的视频中做动态重建时，需要保留真实主体运动、抑制瞬态遮挡物、阴影和体积效应。现有方法要么以静态场景假设牺牲真实运动，要么对所有观测联合建模，导致鬼影与几何不稳定。

Method: 由学习到的形变场推导基于雅可比的形变灵敏度分数，作为重建歧义的经验代理；以该分数引导分离动态 Core Gaussians 与逐视角 Distractor Gaussians，或对高灵敏度 Core Gaussians 做事后剪枝。同一准则统一处理空间遮挡物、光度伪影与演化烟雾/雾，无需掩码、预训练分割器或介质专用监督。

Result: 在合成与真实动态场景及合成烟雾/雾场景上的实验表明，该方法在所评估的各类干扰情形下均有有效性。

Conclusion: 形变灵敏度可作为区分一致内容与不一致观测的有效判据，为无监督、通用化的动态场景干扰抑制提供统一框架。

Abstract: Dynamic reconstruction from casual video must preserve valid subject motion while suppressing transient occluders, shadows, and volumetric effects. Existing methods either suppress valid motion through static-scene assumptions or model all observations jointly, producing ghosting and unstable geometry. We introduce ReCan-GS, a 4D Gaussian Splatting framework that separates coherent scene content from inconsistent observations. It derives a Jacobian-based deformation-sensitivity score from the learned deformation field, using it as an empirical proxy for reconstruction ambiguity. The score guides separation into dynamic Core Gaussians and per-view Distractor Gaussians, or post-hoc pruning of sensitive Core Gaussians. The same criterion supports removal of spatial occluders, photometric artifacts, and evolving smoke or fog without masks, pretrained segmenters, or medium-specific supervision. Experiments on synthetic and real dynamic scenes and synthetic smoke/fog scenes demonstrate its usefulness across the evaluated distractor regimes.

</details>


### [74] [GOOSELoc: A Multimodal Benchmark for Ground-to-Aerial Localization in Unstructured Off-Road Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16162)
*Fabian Deuser,Peter Mortimer,Robin-Nico Kampa,Anna Bößendörfer,Mirko Mählisch,Martin Werner,Norbert Oswald*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GOOSELoc——面向非结构化（森林、田野、乡村道路）地面-航空定位的多模态基准（9,356个场景，含四季环绕RGB、3D LiDAR点云与正射影像），并给出融合图像与LiDAR几何的多模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量式跨视角基准（VIGOR、cross-view KITTI）以城市与道路场景为主，充满人造结构，缺乏越野地形；在GNSS不可靠（林冠下、城市峡谷）时的非结构化环境定位需求未被覆盖。

Method: 构建GOOSELoc基准：慕尼黑周边全四季录制，环绕视RGB图像+3D LiDAR点云与地理参考航空正射影像配对，并提供铺装/非铺装标签与季节差异分析；在此基础上适配并微调四个SOTA跨视角定位基线，另提出结合环绕影像与LiDAR几何的多模态基线。

Result: 即便经过任务特定微调，现有方法在越野环境中性能显著下降；所提多模态基线（影像+LiDAR几何）提升了定位性能，为后续工作提供更强参考基准。

Conclusion: 非结构化环境的地面-航空定位是现有跨视角方法的薄弱环节，多模态（视觉+几何）融合是有效方向；数据集与基线已开源。

Abstract: Cross-view pose estimation estimates a ground camera's geographic pose by matching onboard sensor data with geo-referenced overhead imagery. This is particularly relevant when GNSS is unreliable, for example under forest canopy or in urban canyons. Existing metric cross-view benchmarks, such as VIGOR and cross-view KITTI, are dominated by urban and road scenes with strong human-made structure without off-road terrain. We introduce GOOSELoc, a multimodal benchmark for ground-to-aerial localization in unstructured environments, including forests, fields, and rural tracks. GOOSELoc comprises 9,356 scenes recorded across all four seasons around Munich, Germany, pairing geo-referenced aerial orthophotos with surround-view RGB imagery and 3D LiDAR point clouds. Paved/unpaved labels enable analyzing the impact of terrain structure, while the seasonal recordings allow studying the appearance gap to mostly summer-captured orthophotos. To assess the transferability of existing methods, we adapt and fine-tune four state-of-the-art cross-view localization baselines on GOOSELoc. Despite task-specific fine-tuning, the performance degrades substantially in off-road environments. We therefore propose a multimodal baseline combining surround-view imagery and LiDAR geometry, improving localization performance and providing a stronger reference for future work. Data and baselines are released at https://gooseloc.github.io.

</details>


### [75] [OT-BLS: Optimal-Transport-based Backbone Layer Selection for Frozen DINOv3 in Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15187)
*Natsuo Okamoto,Kazuki Kobayashi,Yoshio Kato,Shuhei Tarashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于最优传输的骨干层选择准则（OT-BLS），用于无训练跨域小样本分割中选择最佳DINOv3层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无训练CD-FSS依赖DINOv3等视觉基础模型，但最优层因episode而异；现有层选择准则未显式评估查询前景/背景预测与支持集标签的一致性。

Method: 利用最优传输算法估计查询与支持块令牌特征间的传输方案，按各候选查询掩码定义的前景-背景划分聚合传输质量，得到反映查询与支持前景/背景对应程度的层选择分数。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上，16个数据集/样本设置中12个优于末层基线；平均mIoU由55.72提升至57.62，较最佳现有准则高2.99点，速度快3.7–14.6倍。

Conclusion: OT-BLS为无训练CD-FSS提供有效且高效的层选择方案，隐式对齐查询与支持的前景/背景语义，显著提升精度与效率。

Abstract: Cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to segment a target class in images from a previously unseen domain using only a few annotated support examples. Training-free CD-FSS methods, which typically leverage vision foundation models such as DINOv3, achieve competitive performance without parameter updates. However, the optimal DINOv3 layer for mask prediction varies across support--query episodes. Although several layer-selection criteria have been proposed, they do not explicitly evaluate whether the predicted query foreground and background labels agree with the corresponding support labels. To address this limitation, we propose a novel layer-selection criterion, Optimal-Transport-Based Backbone Layer Selection (OT-BLS). Our key insight is that an effective layer-selection criterion can be derived from the optimal-transport correspondence between query and support patch-token features. Based on this insight, we first estimate the transport plan using a well-established optimal transport algorithm and then compute a layer-selection score by aggregating the transported mass according to the foreground--background partition defined by each candidate query mask. The resulting score reflects how well the predicted foreground and background of the query correspond to those of the support. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of OT-BLS. It outperforms the conventional last-layer baseline in 12 of 16 dataset/shot settings. Furthermore, OT-BLS improves the average mIoU from 55.72 to 57.62 and achieves a 2.99-point improvement over the best-performing existing layer-selection criterion while running 3.7--14.6$\times$ faster.

</details>


### [76] [Lines Are Ideas: Visual Creativity Assessment in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14907)
*Baoliang Chen,Zihao Lin,Hao Liu,Xiangjie Sui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将绘画创造力评估重构为配对绘画间的结构化语义偏离：新颖性用双曲空间中的测地距离量化，适当性用学得的决策边界条件评估，并以抽象度自适应约束正则化，在AuDrA-Drawings上达到与专家判断的最优对齐并具强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从绘画评估人类创造力的方法要么依赖昂贵的专家标注，要么将创造力当作单一回归目标，忽略了其内在结构。心理学理论长期强调创造力源于新颖性与适当性的平衡，本文据此重新建模。

Method: 将创造力表述为配对绘画间的结构化语义偏离：新颖性度量对原始基础草图的偏离程度，适当性判断该偏离是否有意义。该形式自然导出双曲表示——新颖性由测地距离量化，适当性通过学得的决策边界条件评估，并由抽象度条件的自适应约束进一步调节。

Result: 在AuDrA-Drawings数据集上与专家判断的对齐达到SOTA，并在评分者与任务偏移下表现出强鲁棒性；此外揭示了创造力变化具有可解释的径向组织结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为自动创造力评估提供了有原理依据的几何基础，兼顾可解释性与性能。

Abstract: Assessing human creativity from drawings provides a scalable way to evaluate human creative ability. However, existing approaches either rely on costly expert annotation or treat creativity as a single regression target, overlooking its underlying structure. Psychological theories have long emphasized that creativity emerges from balancing novelty and appropriateness. Motivated by this principle, we recast creativity as structured semantic deviation between paired drawings, where novelty measures the extent of departure from the original base sketch, and appropriateness determines whether such departures remain meaningful. This formulation naturally leads to a hyperbolic representation, in which novelty is quantified by geodesic distance and appropriateness is evaluated conditionally via a learned decision boundary, further regulated by an abstraction-conditioned adaptive constraint. Experiments on AuDrA-Drawings demonstrate state-of-the-art alignment with expert judgments and strong robustness under rater and task shifts. Beyond performance gains, our framework reveals an interpretable radial organization of creative variation, offering a principled geometric foundation for automatic creativity assessment.

</details>


### [77] [Neural Compression for Scientific Particle Data: Perspectives on How Learning Can Help](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14832)
*Tian Qiu,Carolina Cuesta-Lazaro,Sheng Di,Haewon Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出科学粒子数据与CV点云在分形维数上的本质差异导致现有神经点云压缩方法失效，在宇宙学、分子动力学与等离子体物理数据上神经编解码器仅比传统G-PCC基线带来微弱码率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子模拟规模持续增长，科学数据压缩需求迫切。神经点云压缩（PCC）在计算机视觉基准上表现优异，但其能否迁移到科学粒子数据尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 通过分形维数刻画两类数据差异：CV点云局部呈平面性，科学粒子则不具备该性质，从而削弱了多数PCC方法依赖的八叉树结构的有效性；并在宇宙学、分子动力学和等离子体物理三类数据域上开展评估。

Result: 神经编解码器在科学数据上相较传统G-PCC基线仅有边际码率降低，揭示出显著的性能差距；八叉树类结构在该数据上效率受限。

Conclusion: 科学数据结构特性限制了当前神经压缩方法的适用性，论文指出失效所在，并展望学习型方法在未来的潜在改进方向。

Abstract: As particle-based simulations grow in scale, efficient compression of scientific data becomes critical. Neural point cloud compression (PCC) methods excel on computer vision (CV) benchmarks, but do they transfer to scientific particle data? We highlight that scientific particles differ from CV point clouds in fractal dimension: CV point clouds are locally planar, but particles are not. This key difference limits the effectiveness of the octree data structure that underpins most PCC methods. Neural codecs achieve only marginal bitrate reductions over the traditional G-PCC baseline on scientific data, revealing a significant performance gap. Supported by an evaluation spanning domains of cosmology, molecular dynamics, and plasma physics, we outline where the structure of scientific data limits current neural approaches and where learning can help in the future.

</details>


### [78] [A General Concept-based Decomposition for Vision-Language Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15195)
*Simone Alberto Peirone,Ortal Senouf,Francesca Pistilli,Giuseppe Averta,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GRACE，在特征空间层面学习概念级结构，实现与人类感知对齐、跨任务跨域忠实的语义/通用概念分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将视觉嵌入分解为概念级贡献，但实例级优化本质上是局部的，无法刻画模型的全局语义结构，而这对跨输入、跨任务、跨域的组合泛化至关重要。

Method: GRACE 在特征空间层面学习概念结构，生成语义概念与通用概念两种分解；核心是新的训练目标，在概念空间中保持不同样本之间的几何关系，从而刻画概念如何组合与相互关联。

Result: 在多个图像与视频基准上验证，概念分解与人类感知对齐，并在不同任务与域上实现忠实分解；开销极小，在保留原有大部分性能的同时提升可解释性。

Conclusion: 以全局特征空间的结构化概念学习替代局部实例级优化，可在低开销下为现有视觉-语言模型提供更可解释、可泛化的概念分解。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are designed to capture the compositional structure of the world through multi-modal alignment; however, it remains unclear whether their representations truly align with the way a human would naturally describe the same visual input. Prior works attempt to decompose visual embeddings into their concept-level contributions, but their instance-level optimization is inherently local and does not capture the model's global semantic structure, which is essential for compositional generalization across different inputs, tasks, and domains. We introduce GRACE, a method that learns this concept-based structure at the feature space level, producing semantic and general concept decompositions. To achieve this, we propose a novel training objective that preserves the geometric relations of different samples in the concept space, enabling a better understanding of how concepts compose and relate to each other. We validate GRACE on several image and video benchmarks, showing that its concept decomposition is aligned with human perception and achieves faithful decompositions across different tasks and domains. With minimal overhead, GRACE makes existing Vision-Language Models more interpretable, while preserving most of their original performance.

</details>


### [79] [CineMME: From Dialogue Grounding to Narrative Reasoning in Multimodal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15161)
*Jin Liu,Dawei Du,Yexiang Liu,Sijie Zhu,Fan Chen,Junxian Duan,Huaibo Huang,Ran He,Longyin Wen,Zhenfang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CineMME，一个经人工核验、约投入1600专家工时构建的叙事视频基准，包含975个短叙事片段与"接地（Grounding）"和"推理（Reasoning）"两条关联赛道，用于诊断模型能否构建从对话到情节的连贯证据链。评估20个MLLM与9个全模态模型后发现显著的"谁-什么-何时"解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准分别考察视频问答、视听推理、时序定位等单项能力，但很少检验这些能力是否在同一片段内形成连贯的"对话→情节"证据链。叙事视频理解要求模型从对话中抽取证据、将其绑定到说话人、并据此推断叙事意义，缺乏针对性的诊断工具。

Method: 构建 CineMME 基准：975个精选短叙事视频片段，两条互关联赛道——CineMME-Grounding 含13,554条带时间戳的语音片段与33,083个说话人脸框（用于时序对话定位、转写与主动说话人接地）；CineMME-Reasoning 含1,600道选择题与316道开放题，覆盖感知、音频、叙事、社会与场景呈现理解。评测20个MLLM的推理能力与9个全模态模型的接地能力，并进行模态消融实验。

Result: Gemini-3 Pro 在对话时序定位上达68.34% tIoU、转写WER 28.99%，但主动说话人接地仅7.74% vIoU；对有可抽取答案的问题选择题准确率达67.66%。模态消融显示视觉并非总是有益：视觉信息提升感知类任务表现，却可能损害以对话为中心的推理。

Conclusion: CineMME 提供了一个紧凑的诊断性测试平台，用于评估多模态视频模型如何将对话、说话人、视觉证据与叙事意义相连接，并揭示当前模型在"谁-什么-何时"能力上存在明显解耦。

Abstract: Narrative video understanding requires more than recognizing visible events: models must extract evidence from dialogue, bind it to speakers, and use it to infer narrative meaning. Existing video benchmarks evaluate aspects of this problem, including video question answering, audio-visual reasoning, and temporal grounding, but rarely test whether these abilities form a coherent dialogue-to-plot evidence chain within the same clips. We introduce \textbf{CineMME}, a human-verified benchmark designed to diagnose dialogue-grounded plot understanding, supported by approximately $1,600$ expert person-hours of annotation. CineMME contains $975$ curated short narrative video clips and two linked tracks: \textit{CineMME-Grounding}, with $13,554$ timestamped speech segments and $33,083$ speaker face boxes for temporal dialogue localization, transcription, and active-speaker grounding; and \textit{CineMME-Reasoning}, with $1,600$ multiple-choice and $316$ open-ended questions spanning perception, audio, narrative, social, and scene-presentation understanding. Our main evaluation compares $20$ MLLMs on reasoning and $9$ omni-modal models on grounding. The results reveal a pronounced who-what-when decoupling: Gemini-3 Pro localizes dialogue in time ($68.34\%$ tIoU) and transcribes it with a WER of $28.99\%$, yet achieves only $7.74\%$ vIoU for active-speaker grounding while achieving $67.66\%$ MCQ accuracy on questions with extractable answers. Modality ablations further show that vision is not uniformly beneficial: it improves performance on perception-heavy questions but can hurt dialogue-centric reasoning. CineMME provides a compact diagnostic testbed for evaluating how multimodal video models connect dialogue, speakers, visual evidence, and narrative meaning.

</details>


### [80] [Duo: A Reciprocal World Model for Full-Duplex Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15131)
*Hengyuan Cao,Biao Gong,Guoshan Lu,Qiyuan Zhang,Lin Liu,Yiding Tian,Yilei Jiang,Yuxin Ma,Chengzhang Wu,Kun Chen,Yiqian Xie,Ting Liu,Xin Zheng,Xijun Gu,Tianren Ma,Jiancheng Pan,Chi Zhang,Xuzhe Zheng,Siyi Li,Zhenzhong Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出互惠世界模型（RWM）框架及首个全双工世界模型 Duo v1，将"意图"（Intent）与"实现"（Realization）视为世界的两大基本能力，实现实时音视频感知与自然对话/打断的全双工交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要模拟物理现实，而将推理与决策置于世界之外作为外部控制，未把智能与现实同等地纳入"世界"的构成。作者主张智能与物理现实同等根本。

Method: 三项关键设计：(1) 用自回归建模承担连续交互推理与参与决策，而非仅作扩散模型的指令理解；(2) 构建分块（chunk）特定条件，并在统一时间线上对齐 Intent 与 Realization 的 token，通过模块专属注意力掩码强制块内与跨块的因果依赖，屏蔽未来输入；(3) 采用双 rollout 蒸馏，在自回归生成的 Intent token 与 Realization 合成的音视频历史上训练，缓解两模块的分布偏移。

Result: 两模块持续感知实时音频、视频与文本，支持含自发对话与打断的全双工交互；用户还可共享真实物体、重塑虚拟场景、引导助手或在无鼠标键盘条件下进行音视频游戏。

Conclusion: Duo v1 是迈向该方向的第一步，后续版本即将发布，作者期待该新兴领域获得更广泛探索。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce the Reciprocal World Model (RWM) and present Duo v1, a full-duplex world model that continuously perceives audiovisual inputs while responding to users in real time. The RWM treats intelligence and physical reality as equally fundamental to what constitutes a world. Existing world models largely focus on simulating the latter, leaving reasoning and decision-making as external controls rather than intrinsic parts of the world itself. In RWM, we identify two equally fundamental capabilities: Intent, which provides the cognition underlying world interactions, and Realization, which generates coherent audio-visual behavior. To realize this formulation, Duo v1 introduces three key technical designs: (1) Leveraging autoregressive modeling for continuous interaction reasoning and participation decisions, rather than restricting it to instruction understanding for diffusion models. (2) Constructing chunk-specific conditions and synchronize tokens across Intent and Realization on a unified timeline. Module-specific attention masks enforce causal dependencies both within and across chunks, preventing access to future inputs. (3) Mitigating distribution shift in both modules through dual rollout distillation, training on autoregressively generated Intent tokens and synthesized audiovisual histories from Realization. Both modules continuously perceive real-time audio, video, and text, enabling full-duplex interaction with spontaneous conversations and interruptions. Beyond conversation, users can share real objects, reshape virtual scenes, guide assistants, or play audiovisual games without mouse or keyboard controls. The release of Duo v1 makes our first step, with the next version coming soon. We look forward to broader exploration of this emerging field.

</details>


### [81] [TraceKV: Policy-Preserving Low-Rank KV Cache Compression for Reasoning Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15569)
*Ke Li,Zishan Shao,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraceKV是一种免训练的低秩KV压缩框架，通过在线策略校准、敏感度感知SVD与全局秩分配，在保持推理策略的前提下实现近无损的推理精度与5.64倍KV缓存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型VLM因大规模多模态上下文和不断增长的推理轨迹而承担巨大的KV缓存开销。低秩KV压缩是有前景的方案，但现有方法在压缩推理KV状态时会导致相对稠密模型的预测策略漂移不断累积，引发推理失败。作者将其归因于两点：静态上下文与在线策略推理状态之间的校准状态失配，以及各KV状态误差敏感度不均。

Method: 提出TraceKV，一个免训练的低秩KV压缩框架，在统一的"策略保持"原则下整合三项技术：在线策略（on-policy）校准、敏感度感知SVD、全局秩分配，并可结合量化使用；同时优化低秩注意力kernel。

Result: 在多种VLM与视觉推理基准上取得更优的精度—内存权衡，结合量化实现5.64倍KV缓存压缩且推理精度近无损；优化后的低秩注意力kernel带来最高1.83倍端到端解码加速。

Conclusion: 通过显式降低推理过程中的策略-KL散度，TraceKV缓解了低秩压缩导致的策略漂移问题，为推理型VLM提供了高效、免训练的KV缓存压缩方案。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) impose substantial KV cache demands from both large multimodal contexts and growing reasoning traces. Low-rank KV compression has emerged as a promising paradigm that stores low-dimensional latents of KV states and can be combined with quantization. However, existing low-rank methods often induce growing predictive policy drift from the dense model when compressing reasoning KV states, resulting in reasoning failures. We identify two key causes of this degradation: calibration-state mismatch between static contexts and on-policy reasoning states, and unequal error sensitivity across KV states. To address these challenges, we propose TraceKV, a training-free low-rank KV compression framework that unifies on-policy calibration, sensitivity-aware SVD, and global rank allocation under a single policy-preserving principle, substantially reducing policy-KL divergence during reasoning. Experiments across multiple VLMs and visual reasoning benchmarks demonstrate superior accuracy--memory trade-offs, achieving near-lossless reasoning accuracy with 5.64$\times$ KV cache compression when combined with quantization. Optimized low-rank attention kernels further deliver up to 1.83$\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/TraceKV.

</details>


### [82] [IncR-3DSGG: Incremental Posed RGB Image Based 3D Scene Graph Generation via Privileged Depth Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15429)
*Mrunmai Phatak,Daniel Kienzle,Roman Küble,Jörg Hähner,Rainer Lienhart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IncR-3DSGG，仅用带位姿RGB图像增量构建3D场景图，无需推理期深度传感器；通过参数化关系预测器（PRP）从2D特征与2D几何线索学习物体间距离与相对角度，并用位姿图优化（PGO）跨视角融合，恢复度量一致的三维物体位置。在3RScan上取得52.2平均物体召回与30.8平均谓词召回（MonoSSG为27.0/17.6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图生成方法在推理时依赖深度传感：或用于跨视角点云重叠的实例关联，或用于从几何线索计算物体间关系，导致只能部署在带深度传感器的平台上。作者希望摆脱推理期对深度与重建几何的依赖，仅凭带位姿RGB图像实现度量接地的3D场景图增量构建。

Method: 输入为带位姿RGB图像（位姿由现成视觉SLAM提供外参）。核心组件为参数化关系预测器（PRP），从2D视觉特征与2D几何线索回归物体对之间的成对距离与相对角度，仅在训练阶段使用3D几何监督。随后将各视角学习到的成对测量通过位姿图优化（PGO）融合，恢复度量接地的3D物体位置，并增量式构建全局一致的3D场景图。整个推理流程不使用深度或重建几何。

Result: 在真实数据集3RScan上、按3DSSG评测协议：平均物体召回52.2、平均谓词召回30.8；对比MonoSSG的27.0与17.6，且推理阶段不使用深度或重建几何，性能显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 仅凭带位姿RGB图像即可预测物体间关系并获得度量接地的3D物体表示，从而在无深度传感器条件下增量构建3D场景图，拓展了场景图生成方法的部署平台范围。

Abstract: Scene graphs provide a compact, structured representation of environments by capturing objects and their spatial or semantic relationships in a form suitable for embodied agents. Existing 3D scene graph generation methods often rely on depth sensing at inference, either to associate object instances across views via point cloud overlap or to compute inter-object relations from geometric cues, limiting deployment to platforms with depth sensors. We propose IncR-3DSGG, which builds 3D scene graphs incrementally from posed\footnote{RGB images with camera extrinsics estimated by an off-the-shelf visual SLAM system.} RGB images, eliminating the use of depth sensors at inference. Our method leverages a Parametric Relation Predictor (PRP) that learns to estimate pairwise distances and relative angles between objects from 2D visual features and 2D geometric cues, using 3D geometric supervision available only during training. These learned pairwise measurements are fused across views through Pose Graph Optimization (PGO)~\cite{grisetti2010tutorial} to recover metrically grounded 3D object positions and build a globally consistent 3D scene graph incrementally. On the real-world 3RScan~\cite{wald2019rio} dataset, our method attains $52.2$ mean object recall and $30.8$ mean predicate recall under the 3DSSG~\cite{wald2020learning} evaluation protocol, against $27.0$ and $17.6$ for MonoSSG~\cite{wu2023incremental}, while using no depth or reconstructed geometry at inference. This shows that relations and metrically grounded 3D object representations can be predicted from posed RGB images alone.

</details>


### [83] [RECALL: Real-Time Recalibration of Multi-Camera Rigs via Learned OEM-Anchored Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14763)
*Mario Alberto Tapia de la Cruz,Charalambos Poullis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RECALL：面向可移动多相机机架的前馈式单次重标定方法，以OEM标定为锚、借已知目标的短观测预测各相机内参与外参，并用学到的、跨相机耦合的循环算子与网内学习高斯-牛顿步解算共享旋转gauge，无需采集数据集，速度约0.12 ms/机架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相机机架出厂即预标定，但重新布置、磨损、热循环与振动会导致标定持续漂移。经典捆集调整（BA）需要新的多视角采集与全局优化，代价随机架规模增长，无法实时使用，因此需要能在线、按需完成的快速重标定。

Method: 前馈单次重标定：以出厂（OEM）标定为先验锚点，结合对已知物体的短时观测，预测各相机当前内参与外参。标定参数分解为机架共享全局旋转与小的逐相机漂移。不一次性回归，而用学习到的、机架耦合的循环算子迭代精化：可微投影重投影 + 每一步跨相机注意力；共享旋转gauge以学习的高斯-牛顿步在网内求解而非直接回归。真值可合成，故完全在线生成数据训练。

Result: 合成10相机基准上优于逐相机与机架耦合联合BA（旋转0.26° vs 0.47°，焦距5.4 vs 8.9 px），速度约快10^5倍（约0.12 ms/机架）。真实数据（TotalCapture、CMU Panoptic）在绝对/相对旋转上匹配或超越离线机架耦合BA，速度快4–5个数量级；Panoptic环绕机架上相对位姿与最强纯图像基础模型（VGGT、VGGT-Ω、π^3）相当，同时保持度量性且焦距精度约高15倍。

Conclusion: RECALL证明可移动多相机机架的在线重标定可由前馈+学习迭代算子高效实现：以OEM标定为锚、机架耦合循环精化与网内高斯-牛顿gauge求解，可在保持度量标定的同时获得远超BA的实时性与更优精度，且依赖合成训练数据即可泛化到真实场景。

Abstract: Multi-camera rigs ship pre-calibrated from the manufacturer, but their calibration goes stale each time the rig is repositioned, and drifts continuously through wear, thermal cycling, and vibration. Recovering it on-the-fly is hard: classical bundle adjustment needs a fresh multi-view capture and a global optimization whose cost grows with the rig, ruling out real-time use. In this paper we present RECALL, a feed-forward method that recalibrates a movable multi-camera rig in a single pass. From the nominal OEM calibration as an anchor and a brief observation of a known object, it predicts each camera's current intrinsics and extrinsics, written as a shared global rotation of the rig composed with a small per-camera drift. Instead of regressing the calibration in one shot, RECALL refines its estimate with a learned, rig-coupled recurrent operator that reprojects through a differentiable projection and applies attention across the cameras at each step, and solves for the shared rotational gauge in-network with a learned Gauss-Newton step rather than regressing it. Because the ground truth is synthesizable, we train entirely on data generated on-the-fly and need no captured dataset. On a synthetic 10-camera benchmark, RECALL is more accurate than per-camera and rig-coupled joint bundle adjustment ($0.26^\circ$ vs $0.47^\circ$ rotation, $5.4$ vs $8.9$ px focal) and runs about $10^5\times$ faster at about $0.12$ ms per rig. On real data (TotalCapture, CMU Panoptic) it matches or beats offline rig-coupled bundle adjustment on absolute and relative rotation while running four-to-five orders of magnitude faster, and on the Panoptic surround rig it also matches the strongest image-only foundation models (VGGT, VGGT-$Ω$, $π^3$) on relative pose while staying metric and about $15\times$ more accurate in focal length. Code: https://github.com/theICTlab/RECALL-multicam

</details>


### [84] [What Entropy-Based View Selection Cannot See: An Audit of Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14686)
*Sivasuthan Sukumar,Uthayasanker Thayasivam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统检验了视觉语言模型测试时增强（TTA）中视角选择所依赖的信号。研究发现：准确率提升几乎全部来自视角选择而非梯度更新；熵在预测错误时对正确视角的排序接近随机，因其只度量置信度而不度量类别倾向；而增强流程中原本被忽视的信号——裁剪保留原图的比例——能更有效地排序视角，在15个基准数据集中的14个上优于熵、11个上优于最强评分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉语言模型在测试图像与训练数据存在风格或来源差异时准确率下降。常见补救方法是对每张测试图像生成随机裁剪和翻转的多个视角并整合预测，但部分视角会丢失目标，因此需判断应信任哪些视角。主流方法依赖预测熵等评分函数，然而这些函数所依据的信息——模型对每个视角的自身响应——长期缺乏检验。本文旨在固定其他流程环节，专门审视这一信号选择问题。

Method: 控制其他流程阶段不变，仅改变视角选择信号进行对照实验：(1) 分解准确率来源，比较视角选择与梯度更新的贡献；(2) 在预测错误但至少一个视角预测正确的样本上，评估熵对正确/错误视角的排序能力，并与oracle对比；(3) 在两个图像编码器上复现，并设计控制实验排除明显的统计伪影；(4) 给出精确的理论分析，说明熵只度量置信度而不度量视角倾向的类别；(5) 提出使用增强流程中已有但未被利用的信号——每个裁剪保留原图的比例——作为排序依据，并验证其先验可预测哪些数据集会受益。

Result: (1) 视角选择而非梯度更新贡献了几乎全部的准确率增益；(2) 在预测错误的情形下，熵对正确视角的排序能力不优于随机，仅恢复了oracle在同一视角集上所恢复准确率的约二十分之一；(3) 该结论在两个图像编码器上成立，并通过控制实验排除了明显的统计伪影；(4) 理论分析解释了这一局限性的来源；(5) 按裁剪保留原图比例排序，在15个基准数据集中的14个上优于熵，在11个上优于所测试的最强评分函数；(6) 预先测量可预测哪些数据集会受益。

Conclusion: 视角选择是TTA准确率增益的主要来源，而主流基于熵的置信度评分在关键的错误纠正场景中近乎无效，根源在于熵无法反映视角对具体类别的偏好。增强流程中已存在但被忽视的信号（裁剪保留原图比例）是一种廉价且更有效的视角排序依据，且其收益可通过事先测量预测。这提示后续TTA方法应重新审视评分信号的信息内容，而非仅反复精炼基于模型响应的评分函数。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP can classify images into categories they were never explicitly trained to recognize, but accuracy degrades when test images differ in style or source from their training data. A common remedy generates randomly cropped and flipped copies of each test image, called views, and combines their predictions. Since some views miss the object, methods must identify which views to trust. The dominant approach uses prediction entropy to measure confidence and retains the most certain views. Other methods use signals such as embedding typicality. Despite repeated refinements of these scoring functions, the information they rely on, the model's own response to each view, remains largely unexamined. We investigate this choice while holding other pipeline stages fixed. First, we show that view selection, rather than the gradient update, accounts for almost all accuracy gains. We then examine whether entropy identifies useful views when predictions are wrong, despite at least one view predicting the correct class. In these cases, entropy ranks correct views above incorrect ones no better than chance, recovering only about one-twentieth of the accuracy recovered by an oracle over the same views. This finding holds across two image encoders and under a control ruling out an obvious statistical artifact. An exact analysis explains this limitation: entropy measures confidence but not which class a view favors. We identify an unused signal already available in the augmentation pipeline: the fraction of the original image retained by each crop. Ranking views by this quantity improves accuracy on 14 of 15 benchmark datasets over entropy and on 11 of 15 over the strongest scoring function tested. A pre-method measurement also predicts which datasets benefit.

</details>


### [85] [Ultrasound Elastography: Excitation Methods and Low-Cost Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15645)
*Corin F. Otesteanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用普通智能手机作为机械激励源的低成本超声弹性成像方案，结合有限元法求解弹性逆问题，在仿体上获得的弹性重建结果与专用激励器相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超声弹性成像需借助额外昂贵装置产生组织激励，并通过附加软硬件集成到成像流程中，成本高、集成复杂。作者希望寻找更简单、易集成且低成本的激励替代方案。

Method: 以现成智能手机作为机械激励源在组织中诱导位移，获取组织响应后，采用有限元法（FEM）求解弹性逆问题（IP）来重建组织力学属性。

Result: 对模拟组织的仿体进行初步弹性重建，其结果与使用专用驱动器诱导谐波位移时的重建结果相似。

Conclusion: 智能手机可作为可行且低成本的机械激励源用于超声弹性成像，验证了该低成本方案的可行性。

Abstract: Ultrasound elastography (or elasticity imaging) is a method that characterizes the tissue mechanical properties with the purpose of aiding the diagnosis of certain pathologies. Many novel clinical applications use the assumption that these tissue properties are changed in the presence of pathological processes. Characterization of tissue mechanical properties is achieved by imaging the response of the tissue to an induced stress or excitation. A plethora of approaches have been used in the scientific community to induce excitation in tissue, which usually require additional tools or devices than are costly. Furthermore these are usually integrated in the imaging pipeline using additional software or hardware for control. An alternative that is simpler, faster to integrate (and low-cost) is proposed in this work, by using readily available smartphones as a source of mechanical excitation. The induced displacements are used to solve the elasticity inverse problem (IP) using a finite element method (FEM) approach. Preliminary elasticity reconstructions of a phantom that mimics tissue are similar to when using a dedicated actuator as means of inducing harmonic displacements, illustrating the feasibility of our low-cost ultrasound elasography approach.

</details>


### [86] [Sub-actions in Action: Text-Guided Hand-Role Alignment for Sub-Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16056)
*Sadegh Rahmaniboldaji,Filip Rybansky,Quoc Vuong,Frank Guerin,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出子动作检测任务与BiScopeNet框架，在不改动视频-LLM视觉编码器/语言模型的前提下，增强第一视角视频中左右手角色及其操作对象的识别能力，并构建BiScopeCap-100K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在第一视角（egocentric）视频中难以区分左手与右手在动作中的角色差异，缺少显式建模左右手分工及其所操作对象的监督信号与任务形式。

Method: (1) 引入子动作检测任务，要求模型显式识别高层动作进行中的左手/右手角色及被操作对象；(2) 提出BiScopeNet，以插件方式增强现有视频-LLM，不修改视觉编码器与语言模型；(3) 包含FocusAlign——门控、文本引导的注意力模块，选择性对齐视频特征与文本上下文以突出动作相关线索；(4) 包含MetaLoss——掩码化、对象加权损失，强化视觉可落地的词项；(5) 构建BiScopeCap-100K：由现有第一视角数据集整理的大规模视频级数据集，提供联合描述高层动作、左右手角色与操作对象的稠密字幕，并附带空间元数据用于定位。

Result: 在所评估的多个Video-LLM骨干上，BiScopeNet在动作、手部角色与对象识别三方面均优于对应的监督基线，并在BiScopeCap上取得所评估配置中的最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: 通过子动作检测任务与BiScopeNet的轻量增强模块，可有效提升视频-LLM对第一视角手部角色与操作对象的辨识能力；配套数据集为后续研究提供密集、带空间元数据的监督资源。

Abstract: Our objective is to enhance VLMs ability to distinguish the roles of the left and right hands in egocentric actions. We introduce sub-action detection, a task that requires models to explicitly recognise the left- and right-hand roles and their manipulated objects within an ongoing high-level action. To address this challenge, we propose BiScopeNet, a framework that augments existing video-LLMs without modifying their vision encoders or language models. BiScopeNet incorporates two components: FocusAlign, a gated text-guided attention-based module that selectively aligns video features with textual context to emphasise action-relevant cues, and MetaLoss, a masked, object-weighted loss that prioritises visually grounded terms. To supervise sub-action detection, we introduce BiScopeCap-100K, a large-scale video-level dataset curated from existing egocentric datasets, providing dense captions that jointly describe high-level actions, left/right-hand roles, and manipulated objects, enriched with spatial metadata for grounding. Across the evaluated Video-LLM backbones, BiScopeNet improves action, hand-role, and object recognition over the corresponding supervised baselines, achieving the strongest aggregate performance among the evaluated configurations on BiScopeCap.

</details>


### [87] [Iris-Z: Scaling One Generative Model Past Specialist Geometry Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16010)
*Alejandro Cañada,Chema Garabito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一图像生成器 Iris-Z，用单一骨干网络同时生成外观与几何（深度），在规模化后超越专用深度估计模型，验证"痛苦教训"适用于视觉智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP 已从众多任务专用模型走向单一统一生成模型，并在数据、参数与算力扩展中持续提升。作者主张这一"痛苦教训"同样适用于计算机视觉、3D 与空间理解：应投入通用架构与规模化，而非任务专用设计。单目几何长期被专用架构占据，是检验该主张的理想对象。

Method: Iris-Z 为通用图像生成器：单一骨干同时去噪、推理并理解图像与其深度；一套权重支持三种能力——生成带深度的图像、从照片估计深度、在给定深度上绘制图像。不使用任何专用层。

Result: 在足够规模下，Iris-Z 在既有深度基准上超越最先进的专用模型，同时保留原始骨干的生成能力。

Conclusion: 统一生成架构加规模化可取代任务专用设计，单目几何亦然；作者向社区发布两个模型。

Abstract: Language moved from many task-specific models to one unified model: a single generative model with a single generative training task that handles all of them within the same backbone, and keeps improving with data, parameters and compute. We believe the bitter lesson applies just as much to computer vision, 3D and spatial understanding, in the end to visual intelligence: the effort should go into general architectures that solve all these tasks in a unified way, and into scaling them, rather than into task-specific designs. We test this on monocular geometry, a task that has belonged to specialised architectures for years. Iris-Z is a general image generator that produces appearance and geometry together. A single backbone denoises, reasons about and understands the image and its depth at the same time, and one set of weights generates images with their depth, estimates depth from photographs and paints images onto a given depth. At enough scale it surpasses state-of-the-art specialists on established depth benchmarks while keeping the generative abilities of the backbone it started from without specialized layers. We release two models for the community.

</details>


### [88] [Predicting the Needle in a Petabyte Scale Haystack: Open-Vocabulary Event Anticipation in Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16000)
*Shreelekha Revankar,Mikhail Klassen,Creon Levit,Ash Hoover,Kavita Bala,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文用视觉-语言模型嵌入的向量差表示地表变化，构建自动化全球事件发现引擎与迄今最大全球卫星事件数据集，并训练可预测变化位置、结果类型与完成时间的全球尺度事件预判模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星星座可实现日级对地成像，能在事件（如森林砍伐、城市建设）完成前进行预判以支持及时干预；但现有系统无法在开放词表下同时回答"变化发生在哪里、将变成什么、何时完成"三个问题。构建此类模型需要多样的事件数据，而事件如"大海捞针"，全球尺度人工标注不可行。

Method: 将变化表征为不同时间点视觉-语言模型嵌入之间的向量差，在潜空间中以这种"算术"方式直接检索语义变换，从而：(1) 构建无需人工标注的自动化全球事件发现数据引擎；(2) 构建全球卫星事件数据集（21,000+ 地点、64+ 事件类型、约 200 万张 PlanetScope 与 Sentinel-2 影像，覆盖六大洲）；(3) 训练全球尺度事件预判模型。

Result: 模型变化检测最大 F1 达 93.5%；预测事件检索性能比基线高 3.6 倍；可预测完成日期，中位误差仅 3 天，且在完成前九个月即可给出预测。

Conclusion: 以嵌入向量差表示变化的算术思路，为开放词表下的大规模事件发现与预判提供了可扩展方案，验证了自动化数据引擎与全球尺度预判模型的可行性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Satellite constellations now image the Earth daily, capturing the lifecycle of events from deforestation to urban construction. Anticipating these events before completion enables timely intervention, yet existing systems cannot jointly identify where a change occurs, what it will become, and when it will finish across an open vocabulary. Building such a model requires diverse event data, but events are like needles in a haystack, making manual annotation at global scale infeasible. We address this problem by representing change as the vector difference between vision-language model embeddings at distinct time points. This arithmetic approach allows us to search for semantic transformations directly in the latent space, enabling (1) an automated data engine for global event discovery without manual annotation and (2) the largest global satellite event dataset to our knowledge, comprising 21,000+ locations across 64+ event types with ~2M images from PlanetScope and Sentinel-2 spanning six continents, to train (3) a novel global-scale event anticipation model. Our model detects change with a 93.5% max F1 score, outperforms baselines 3.6x on predicted event retrieval, and forecasts completion dates with a 3-day median error, even nine months before completion. Code: https://github.com/ShreelekhaR/predicting-the-needle

</details>


### [89] [CoCode-World: Shared 3D Proxy Representation for Multi-Player World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15602)
*Qirui Li,Xin Zhang,Yabo Chen,Jinkun Hao,Paul L Rosin,Yu-Kun Lai,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Ran Yi,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoCode-World 提出一种共享 3D 代理（proxy）框架，将结构控制与视觉合成解耦，用预训练视频模型在单一生成分支中联合生成双玩家视图，实现跨视角一致的多玩家生成世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多玩家生成式世界模型需在不同相机运动与遮挡条件下对共享实体给出一致观测。显式世界表示可提供空间控制，但精确建模单个游戏地图限制场景多样性；而仅靠共享几何又无法保证跨视角外观一致。

Method: 将成对代理视频、外观参考与目标视角按一致空间布局拼接，用预训练视频模型联合生成两路视图，借助模型原生注意力在单分支内实现跨视角通信；采用 LoRA 适配学习代理遵循与外观对应关系。构建双人 GTA V 数据集，将同步 RGB 观测与基于共享地图几何和游戏引擎状态的代理视频配对。

Result: 实验显示可将外观迁移到训练中未见过的角色与场景风格，视频质量与跨视角玩家身份一致性均优于所评估基线；消融实验考察了代理构建方式与跨视角通信的影响。

Conclusion: 将结构控制（可编程 3D 代理）与外观合成分离，并利用视频模型原生注意力进行跨视角协调，是构建多玩家一致生成世界模型的有效方案。

Abstract: Multiplayer generative world models must produce observations that agree on shared entities despite different camera motions and occlusions. Explicit world representations provide spatial control, but precise modeling of individual game maps limits scene diversity, while shared geometry alone cannot ensure consistent appearance across views. We present CoCode-World, a shared 3D proxy framework that separates structural control from visual synthesis for two-player video generation. Programmable proxies specify scene layout, motion, and occlusion, while RGB references guide character and scene appearance. To coordinate visual details across players, we concatenate paired proxy videos, appearance references, and target views in a consistent spatial layout and generate both views jointly with a pretrained video model. This formulation uses the model's native attention for cross-view communication within a single generation branch, with LoRA adaptation learning proxy adherence and appearance correspondence from paired observations. We construct a two-player GTA~V dataset that pairs synchronized RGB observations with proxy videos derived from shared map geometry and game-engine state. Experiments demonstrate appearance transfer to characters and scene styles unseen during training, with improvements in video quality and cross-view player identity over the evaluated baseline. Ablations further examine how proxy construction and cross-view communication affect generation. Project page: https://iapple233.github.io/CCW/.

</details>


### [90] [Open Vision Banana: A Fully Open Recipe for Generative Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15844)
*Arpit Jadon,Valentinos Pariza,Jona Ruthardt,Ryousuke Yamada,A. Sophia Koepke,Andrei Bursuc,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVB 提供完全开源可复现的配方，将预训练图像生成器转为统一多任务感知系统，并揭示颜色编码、提示设计、参数适配与任务平衡等关键设计选择及其交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Banana 表明预训练生成器可将视觉任务表述为条件图像编辑，但其数据、权重、代码与实现细节均未公开，缺乏可复现性，因此需要一套完全开放的配方。

Method: 基于 4B 与 9B 开源权重基座模型，使用公开数据集构建的 2.1M–3.8M 训练对；系统分析颜色编码、提示设计、参数适配与任务平衡等设计选择；并提出 legend ensembling 测试时缩放方法——在多种类-颜色图例下分割图像并以多数投票融合结果。

Result: Cityscapes 零样本 mIoU 达 73.9，超过 Vision Banana 报告的 69.9；可比肩 SOTA 仿射不变深度估计器；非零样本变体在指代与推理分割上落后 Vision Banana 不足 5 点，但实例分割差距较大；模型基本保留基座的图像编辑能力；legend ensembling 无需重训即在 Cityscapes 提升最高 8.7 点、COCO-Object 提升最高 15.0 点 mIoU。

Conclusion: 生成器式感知可通过公开数据与开放权重复现并超越原工作，其性能高度依赖相互作用的超参选择；测试时图例集成能有效纠正不稳定的颜色-类别绑定。作者开源了数据处理流程、训练与评测代码及全部检查点。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that pretrained image generators encode real-world understanding beyond image synthesis. Vision Banana exploits this by framing vision tasks as conditional image editing. However, its data, weights, code, and implementation details are not public. We present Open Vision Banana (OVB), a fully open and reproducible recipe for converting pretrained generators into unified multi-task perception systems. Our analyses identify the design choices that matter most for generalization, including color encoding, prompt design, parameter adaptation, and task balancing. These choices interact, so individually helpful choices can hurt when combined with others. Starting from 4B and 9B parameter open-weight base models and using 2.1M-3.8M training pairs from public datasets, OVB surpasses Vision Banana's reported Cityscapes zero-shot performance (73.9 vs. 69.9 mIoU) and rivals state-of-the-art affine-invariant depth estimators. Its non-zero-shot variants come within five points of Vision Banana on referring and reasoning segmentation but remain further behind on instance segmentation. All models largely retain the base model's image-editing abilities. With legend ensembling, we introduce a test-time scaling method for generator-based perception. It segments an image under several class-color legends and fuses the results by majority vote, correcting unstable color-class bindings and improving mIoU by up to 8.7 points on Cityscapes and 15.0 points on COCO-Object without retraining. We release the dataset construction and processing pipeline, the training and evaluation code, as well as the final, intermediate, and ablation checkpoints. Project website: https://openvisionbanana.github.io

</details>


### [91] [Back to the Question: Tracing Visual Distraction Through Text Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15039)
*Marie Amale Huynh,Regev Cohen,Serena Yeung-Levy,Sivan Doveh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过分析发现视觉干扰图即便在模型正确选中相关图像时仍会降低VLM准确率，并将中间层"干扰图→文本"注意力连接识别为干扰通路，进而提出答案一致性正则化，无需直接约束注意力即可削弱该通路，在五个基准上平均提升16个百分点且不损失单图性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态视觉语言模型面临视觉场景杂乱问题：当目标图像伴随干扰图像出现时，模型准确率显著下降。现有缓解方法局限于"选择相关图像"或"限制图像间信息交互"，但作者发现即使模型已正确识别相关图像，干扰仍造成精度损失，说明仅靠选择不够，需要揭示干扰的内部机制并针对性地抑制。

Method: 在三个7-8B开源VLM、五个经改造加入干扰图的感知基准上做系统性分析；结合注意力分析与Qwen2.5-VL中的定向"敲除"（targeted knockouts），定位出中间层的干扰图到文本的连接通路；提出答案一致性正则化（answer consistency regularization），鼓励有干扰与无干扰条件下答案分布一致，间接减少问题token对干扰图的中间层注意力。

Result: 干扰图即使在被正确识别相关图像的情况下也会降低准确率；中间层distractor-to-text连接是干扰影响推理的关键通路；答案一致性正则化虽未直接约束注意力，但确实降低了中间层问题token对干扰图的注意力；在五个干扰图设置下循环准确率平均比Qwen2.5-VL基线提升16个百分点，同时保持单图性能。

Conclusion: 干扰并非仅源于图像选择失败，而是经由中间层跨模态注意力通路渗入推理过程；通过输出层面的一致性正则化即可隐式抑制该通路，是一种简洁有效的鲁棒性提升方案。

Abstract: The visual world is cluttered: robust vision-language reasoning requires ignoring irrelevant information. Yet vision-language models (VLMs) can suffer substantial accuracy losses when answering questions about a target image with distractors present. Existing mitigations focus on selecting relevant images or limiting information exchange between images. Through a systematic analysis of three open 7-8B VLMs on five perception benchmarks adapted to include distractor images, we show that distractors reduce accuracy even when models correctly identify the relevant image, suggesting that selection alone is not enough. To understand the mechanism behind this interference, we combine attention analysis with targeted knockouts in Qwen2.5-VL and identify middle-layer distractor-to-text connections as a pathway through which irrelevant visual information affects reasoning. We introduce answer consistency regularization, which encourages consistent answer distributions with and without distractors. Without directly constraining attention, this objective reduces middle-layer attention from question tokens to distractor images, consistent with the pathway our analysis implicates. With five distractors, our approach improves circular accuracy by an average of 16 percentage points over the Qwen2.5-VL baseline across the five benchmarks while preserving single-image performance.

</details>


### [92] [Reality Perception Gap: Are AI-Manipulated Video Detectors Ready for the Real World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14941)
*Xiangrui Liu,Xinmiao Luan,Xinyuan Wang,Zhiyuan Fang,Chi-Yao Huang,Douglas C. Montgomery,Yanjie Fu,Felix Juefei-Xu,YeZhou Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Reality Perception Gap (RPG)基准，用1万条经跨平台转发压缩、含相机端光学伪影的真实素材及社会敏感内容视频，检验AI伪造视频检测器的部署鲁棒性。18个检测器在1%假阳性率下对伪造视频的召回均不超过15%；失败源于生成器/内容分布偏移与转发压缩抹除低层痕迹两方面，表明被动检测或已触及极限，需在部署条件下训练并融合低层、时序与语义证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伪造视频检测器多在干净、精选数据集上评测，而实际部署中视频常经历社交媒体跨平台转发压缩，且真实视频存在硬件采集造成的相机侧光学伪影；此外犯罪、战时等社会敏感内容的伪造视频危害巨大。因此需要衡量检测器在上述真实条件下的表现。

Method: 构建RPG基准：10,000条视频，含前沿生成器生成与部分合成编辑的内容，施加跨平台转发压缩，并与携带相机端光学伪影的真实素材配对；纳入社会脆弱性内容。评测18个检测器，并设置阳性对照与重编码扫描实验，以分解失败来源。

Result: 在1%假阳性率下，无一检测器对伪造视频的召回率超过15%。阳性对照与重编码扫描显示两类互补失败：生成器与内容偏移在压缩前即导致性能下降；转发压缩抹除基于伪影的检测器所依赖的低层痕迹。

Conclusion: 现有检测器在部署条件剥离其依赖的生成伪影后表现不佳；鲁棒检测需在部署条件下训练，并融合低层、时序与语义证据，被动检测可能已到达其能力上限。

Abstract: AI-manipulated video detectors are typically evaluated on clean, curated datasets. In deployment, however, videos have usually passed through repost compression on social-media platforms and sometimes were captured by imperfect hardware. To measure detection under these conditions, we introduce Reality Perception Gap (RPG), a benchmark of 10,000 videos from frontier generators and partially synthetic edits, processed with cross-platform repost compression and paired with real footage that carries camera-side optical artifacts. RPG also includes socially fragile content, such as crime and wartime footage, where fabricated videos can cause severe social harm. Across 18 detectors, none catches more than 15% of fakes at a 1% false-positive rate. A positive control and a re-encoding sweep separate two sources of failure: generator and content shift degrades detectors before any compression, and repost compression erases the low-level traces that the artifact-based detectors relies on. Existing detectors therefore struggle when deployment conditions strip away the generation artifacts they rely on. These complementary failures suggest that robust detection requires training under deployment conditions and combining low-level, temporal, and semantic evidence, where passive detection may have reached its limits.

</details>


### [93] [From Pixels to Prompts: Physics-Guided Language Enhancement for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14898)
*Mei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种物理特征引导的多模态框架，通过筛选稳定的手工物理描述符构造结构化文本，结合LoRA微调的CLIP实现无需外部提示、跨生成器泛化的AIGC图像检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC检测器依赖特定生成器伪影，对未见模型泛化能力有限；作者希望找到能稳定区分真实与AI生成图像的内在物理图像特征。

Method: 在20余个生成模型图像上分析15种手工物理描述符，提出特征稳定性-判别性验证评估（FSDVA）筛选出5个核心特征（如Laplacian方差、Sobel统计、残差噪声方差），构建结构化文本描述，与图像表示通过原生物理引导多模态框架融合，并以LoRA适配CLIP ViT-L/14；推理时仅需输入图像。

Result: GenImage上SDv1.4达99.0%、Wukong达96.7%，八个生成模型平均97.23%（同设置下C2pCLIP为95.8%）；UniversalFakeDetect上对多样及未见生成设置保持稳定泛化。

Conclusion: 精选物理图像统计量为预训练视觉-语言表示提供互补取证证据，实现可解释、更生成器无关的AIGC检测。

Abstract: AI-generated content (AIGC) has substantially narrowed the visual gap between natural and synthetic images, while existing detectors often rely on generator-specific artifacts and exhibit limited generalization to unseen models. This motivates a fundamental question: which intrinsic physical image characteristics provide stable, discriminative cues for distinguishing natural from AI-generated images? We investigate this by systematically identifying robust physical features and incorporating them into a vision-language representation without external textual prompts at inference. We first analyze 15 handcrafted physical descriptors across images from over 20 generative models. Based on cross-generator stability and real-fake discriminability, we propose a Feature Stability-Discriminability Validation Assessment (FSDVA) to select a compact set of reliable features. Five core features, including Laplacian variance, Sobel statistics, and residual-noise variance, are then used to construct structured textual descriptions encoding physical evidence for CLIP-based learning. We integrate these physics-enriched descriptions with image representations via a native physics-guided multimodal framework, adapting CLIP ViT-L 14 with LoRA. At inference, the model requires only the input image, with no external captions or manual prompts. On GenImage, our method achieves 99.0 percent accuracy on SDv1.4 and 96.7 percent on Wukong, with a mean of 97.23 percent across eight generative models versus 95.8 percent for C2pCLIP under the same setting. On UniversalFakeDetect, it maintains strong, stable generalization across diverse and unseen generation settings. These results show that carefully selected physical image statistics provide complementary forensic evidence to pretrained vision-language representations, enabling interpretable, more generator-agnostic AIGC detection.

</details>


### [94] [MAGIC: Modality-Aware Gini Inspection for Clinical Hallucination Detection in LMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15530)
*Sachin Acharya,Niyoj Oli,Shishir Adhikari,Aminu Lawal,Yash Raj Shrestha,Prashnna K Gyawali,Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以注意力权重在视觉/文本模态间分布失衡作为幻觉信号，用Gini系数量化跨层跨头的模态注意力集中度，训练逻辑回归分类器检测医学LMM幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学大型多模态模型（LMM）在高风险临床决策支持中面临幻觉威胁；现有幻觉检测方法多为单模态设计，无法刻画医学影像场景中的视觉-语言交互动态。

Method: 将LMM幻觉检测形式化为答案token生成过程中注意力权重跨模态的不均衡分布问题；用Gini系数量化各层、各注意力头的模态专属注意力集中度，提取视觉token与文本token间注意力质量重分配特征；基于这些特征训练逻辑回归分类器。

Result: 统计分析显示幻觉回答中视觉与文本token之间的注意力质量存在显著重分配；在两个医学LMM、三种医学影像模态上评估，性能与现有幻觉检测基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 模态注意力失衡是可解释、轻量且有效的医学LMM幻觉检测信号，跨模型与跨影像模态具备一定泛化性。

Abstract: Recent advances in medical large multimodal models (LMMs) have brought promising progress to clinical decision support, yet hallucination, the generation of confident but clinically unverifiable outputs, poses a critical threat to the reliability demanded in high-stakes medical settings. Existing hallucination detection methods, predominantly designed for unimodal settings, fail to capture the visual-linguistic dynamics inherent to medical imaging. We formulate the problem of hallucination detection in LMMs as disproportionate distribution of attention weights across the modalities during answer token generation. We quantify modality-specific attention concentration across layers and heads using the Gini coefficient and demonstrate statistically significant redistribution of attention mass between visual and textual tokens in hallucinated responses. We leverage these features to train a logistic regression-based classifier and evaluate it on two medical LMMs across three medical imaging modalities, demonstrating that our method achieves competitive or superior performance against contemporary hallucination detection baselines.

</details>


### [95] [SyntheIce: A Synthetic Radargram Annotation Benchmark for Englacial Layer Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14728)
*Muhammad Behroze Hassan,Bayu Adhi Tama,Sanjay Purushotham,Vandana P. Janeja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出斜率感知贪心片段连接算法与四瓦片XCheck验证框架，并构建带精确真值的SyntheIce合成数据集，实现冰内雷达图像完整层位重建与跨瓦片连续性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰内层位蕴含冰盖气候与流动历史信息，但大规模提取受限于人工标注耗时、半自动检测器产生碎片化而非连续层位；同时缺少跨雷达剖面、跨瓦片边界的连续性验证手段与带真值的评测数据。

Method: (1) 斜率感知贪心片段连接：基于行相似度门控与指数加权移动平均(EWMA)斜率预测器重建全幅层位，并为每条重建赋予内容哈希ID以实现跨雷达图的稳定识别；(2) 构建SyntheIce合成数据集：600幅雷达图，覆盖多复杂度与真实噪声条件，其中150幅宽幅图切分为4个相邻瓦片，均含精确真值；(3) 将XCheck一致性验证框架从双图扩展到4个连续瓦片，在每个边界处强制连续性约束。

Result: SyntheIce能够支撑真实标注算法的开发与评测；在专家标注的真实雷达图对比中，方法取得高精确率、召回率、F1与平均IoU，验证了向真实数据的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 当空间特征碎片化但保留一致的局部几何结构时，基于轨迹的连接比基于外观的匹配更可靠；该结论可推广至冰科学之外的领域。所提算法与验证框架为冰内层位的大规模自动提取提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Englacial layers in radio-echo-sounding radargrams (subsurface ice images) encode the climate and flow history of ice sheets and glaciers. However, large-scale extraction is hindered by time-consuming manual annotation and semi-automated detectors that produce fragmented rather than continuous horizons. We address two challenges: reconstructing complete layers from partial detections across radargram profiles and validating layer continuity across adjacent tile boundaries. We introduce a slope-aware greedy fragment-linking algorithm that reconstructs full-width layers using row-similarity gating and an exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) slope predictor. Each reconstruction receives a content-based hash ID for stable cross-radargram identification. For quantitative evaluation, we generate SyntheIce, a synthetic dataset of 600 radargrams spanning multiple complexity levels and realistic noise conditions, including 150 wide radargrams divided into four adjacent tiles. All samples include exact ground-truth annotations. We evaluate the dataset using XCheck, a consistency-based validation framework, extending its original two-image setting to four consecutive tiles and enforcing continuity at every boundary. This demonstrates the dataset's utility for developing and evaluating real-world annotation algorithms. Our findings extend beyond ice science: when spatial features are fragmented but retain consistent local geometry, trajectory-based linking is more reliable than appearance-based matching. Evaluation against expert-labeled real radargrams demonstrates high precision, recall, F1-score, and mean IoU, supporting the method's transfer to real-world data.

</details>


### [96] [AcrossPlace: Do Vision--Language Models Recognize Places Beyond Their Source Views? A Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15314)
*Zhenyu Li,Tianyi Shang,Shuhan Dong,Ruotong Xu,Qingyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 AcrossPlace，一个具有来源感知（provenance-aware）的双向语言-视觉地点识别基准，用于分离观测迁移、图库竞争与语言证据对检索分数的影响，并建立可复现的评测标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的地点识别需要在描述来源图像之外的视觉观测上恢复地理身份，但聚合检索分数常混杂观测迁移、图库竞争与语言证据，掩盖了真实识别能力；现有评测缺乏区分这些因素的系统标准。

Method: 基于 GSV-Cities 构建 AcrossPlace：56,726 个地点、23 个城市、156,800 条自动生成且规则过滤的描述、113,404 张目标图像；每条描述保留来源图像溯源，检索在不相交的目标观测上进行。T2I 与 I2T 共享统一的地点身份相关性准则，允许多个有效匹配。设两种地理协议（P0：已覆盖城市内未见地点；P1：留出城市泛化），并采用配对观测比较、图库受控评测、固定查询扩展与盲法标注审计。

Result: 三个训练种子下，地点监督的 SigLIP2 在 P0 上 T2I Recall@1 由源图像的 19.69% 降至独立目标的 12.31%；固定 P1 查询时，图库从 500 扩至 8,425 个地点使 Recall@1 由 14.67% 降至 2.87%；240 条描述审计显示自动接受与事实证据间存在偏差。

Conclusion: AcrossPlace 揭示了现有方法在观测迁移与图库扩展下的性能衰减及自动标注的可靠性问题，为评估跨观测、跨地理环境的可迁移地点身份提供了可复现的评测标准。

Abstract: Language-guided place recognition requires recovering geographic identity across visual observations beyond the image from which a description originates. However, aggregate retrieval scores often entangle observation transfer, gallery competition, and linguistic evidence, obscuring the underlying recognition capability. We introduce AcrossPlace, a provenance-aware benchmark establishing a systematic evaluation standard for bidirectional language--vision place recognition. Built upon GSV-Cities, AcrossPlace comprises 56,726 places across 23 cities, 156,800 automatically generated, rule-filtered descriptions, and 113,404 target images. Each description retains its source-image provenance, while retrieval operates on disjoint target observations. Text-to-image (T2I) and image-to-text (I2T) retrieval share a unified place-identity relevance criterion supporting multiple valid matches. Two geographic protocols evaluate unseen places within represented cities (P0) and generalization to held-out cities (P1). Paired observation comparisons, gallery-controlled evaluations, fixed-query scaling, and blinded annotation auditing systematically characterize retrieval performance. Across three training seeds, place-supervised SigLIP2 T2I Recall@1 decreases from 19.69% on source images to 12.31% on independent targets under P0. For fixed P1 queries, expanding the gallery from 500 to 8,425 places reduces Recall@1 from 14.67% to 2.87%. A 240-description audit further reveals discrepancies between automated acceptance and factual evidence. These findings establish AcrossPlace as a reproducible evaluation standard for assessing transferable place identity across visual observations and geographic environments.

</details>


### [97] [Arbor: When Labels Are Not Coarsened at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15311)
*Faisal Mohammad,Mohamed Younis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 合并不同层级标注的数据集会使部分图像只有粗标签、部分类无细标签。作者指出标准训练把粗标签分数记为子类概率之和，仅当同一超类内各类等可能时精确；对从未细标注的"novel class"会误分给已知兄弟类。提出 Arbor，用虚拟 novel 类与 logistic gate 建模标签粗化过程，以 coarsening likelihood 训练，理论上其总体最优等于 Elkan-Noto 正无标签修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 把不同分类层级标注的数据合并训练时，粗标签把整个超类的概率质量合并，隐含"超类内各类等概率"的假设。当超类中存在从未被细粒度标注的 novel class 时，该假设被违反，分类器会把 novel 类图像错误地分配给已知兄弟类。作者希望显式建模标签如何被粗化，从而在统一框架下同时处理 known 与 novel 类。

Method: 在含 novel 类的每个超类中引入一个虚拟 novel 类，并用 logistic gate 在虚拟 novel 类与已知类之间分配该超类的概率质量；用观测标签在该粗化模型下的似然（coarsening likelihood）训练 gate 与分类器。作者证明总体最优处 gate 等于正无标签学习的 Elkan-Noto 修正，并表明在训练中内嵌该修正优于训练后再做后处理。

Result: 在 Cars、CUB、Aircraft（90% 粗标签）上，Arbor 将 76–85% 的 novel 类图像正确归入其超类，优于层级分类器、层级新颖性检测与 Elkan-Noto 后处理；known 类图像上的叶类 top-1 准确率与最佳基线相差不超过 1.6 个点，尽管其最终预测更常返回超类。在更难 iNaturalist-2019 上，novel 类准确率由 46%（uniform target，最强基线）提升至 68%。

Conclusion: 显式建模标签粗化机制并把正无标签修正式 gate 训练进分类器，可有效缓解超类内 novel 类的概率质量被已知类吞并的问题，在粗标签占主导的层级数据集上同时兼顾 novel 类归入超类与已知类的细粒度识别。

Abstract: Merging datasets labelled at different levels of a taxonomy leaves some images with only a coarse label, and some classes with no fine-grained label at all. Standard training scores a coarse label by the total probability of the classes under it. Coarse labels name superclasses, and the standard objective is exact only when every class within a superclass is equally likely to receive a coarse label. We call a class novel if it is never labelled finely, and known otherwise. A novel class violates the assumption, and the classifier assigns its images to a known sibling. We propose Arbor, which models how labels become coarse. In each superclass with a novel class, Arbor adds a virtual novel class, and a logistic gate divides the superclass's probability between it and the known classes. The gate is trained with the likelihood of the observed labels under this model, the coarsening likelihood. We prove that at its population optimum the gate equals the Elkan-Noto correction of positive-unlabelled learning, and show that training the correction into the classifier improves on applying it after training. Across Cars, CUB and Aircraft with 90% coarse labels, Arbor correctly assigns 76-85% of novel-class images to their superclass, more than hierarchical classifiers, hierarchical novelty detection and the Elkan-Noto correction. Its top-1 accuracy over the leaf classes on known-class images is within 1.6 points of the best baseline, although its final predictions return the superclass more often. On the more challenging iNaturalist-2019 benchmark, Arbor improves novel-class accuracy from 46% (uniform target, the strongest baseline) to 68%.

</details>


### [98] [Track4D: Representing Dense 3D Tracking for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15287)
*Yushi Lan,Zeren Jiang,Kelvin Li,Xingang Pan,Chuanxia Zheng,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将预训练视频扩散模型（VDM）改造用于密集3D点跟踪，系统比较了RTV与NCMV两种视频化表示，并以LoRA微调冻结的Wan骨干，提出Track4D，在四个基准上取得有竞争力的零样本跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集3D点跟踪的训练数据稀缺，而视频扩散模型在大规模视频上预训练、蕴含丰富的运动先验，因此希望复用其先验；但难点在于3D轨迹需表示成类视频信号，这类信号可能落在VDM训练所用的自然视频分布之外，因此需要系统评估不同表示形式的有效性。

Method: 提出两种视频表示：1）Residual 3D Tracking Video（RTV），编码每像素相对查询帧的3D偏移，可直接从生成视频中恢复3D轨迹；2）Normalized Coordinate Map Video（NCMV），为每个像素标注随时间不变的规范坐标，更接近自然视频、更易学习，但需借助现成2D跟踪器后处理恢复3D轨迹。实现上对冻结的Wan骨干做LoRA适配，并以现成3D重建器输出的点图作为几何条件（scaffold），仅在单一动态3D跟踪数据集上训练。此外引入绕过视频VAE的回归读出（regression readout）并使用额外训练数据进一步提升性能。

Result: RTV的跟踪精度更高，NCMV则证明VDM能够以视频形式生成密集对应关系。Track4D在四个真实与合成基准上取得有竞争力的零样本跟踪结果；绕过VAE的回归读出配合额外训练数据带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明VDM先验可有效迁移至密集3D跟踪，两种表示各有取舍（精度 vs. 分布一致性与可学习性），为后续利用VDM先验开展密集3D跟踪与4D重建的工作提供了方向性启示。

Abstract: We study how to repurpose video diffusion models (VDMs), pretrained on millions of videos, for dense 3D point tracking, where training data is scarce. Doing so is non-trivial, as it requires representing 3D tracks as video-like signals that may lie outside the natural-video distribution on which the VDM was trained. We thus systematically assess the effectiveness of two such video representations. The first, Residual 3D Tracking Video (RTV), encodes per-pixel 3D offsets relative to a query frame and allows recovery of 3D trajectories directly from the generated video. The second, Normalized Coordinate Map Video (NCMV), labels each pixel with a canonical coordinate invariant through time. This is closer to a natural video and easier for the VDM to learn, but requires additional post-processing with an off-the-shelf 2D tracker to recover 3D tracks. RTV tracks more accurately, while NCMV shows that a VDM can generate dense correspondence as a video. We instantiate both representations with LoRA adaptation of a frozen Wan backbone, conditioned on point maps from an off-the-shelf 3D reconstructor as a geometric scaffold. Trained with a single dynamic 3D tracking dataset, Track4D achieves competitive zero-shot tracking on four real and synthetic benchmarks. A regression readout that bypasses the video VAE and uses additional training data brings further improvement, demonstrating the potential of this direction for dense 3D tracking. We hope our analysis will inspire future work that leverages VDM priors for dense 3D tracking and 4D reconstruction. Project page: https://nirvanalan.github.io/projects/track4d

</details>


### [99] [GingivitisSeg: A Public Dataset and Comparative Benchmark for Gingivitis Segmentation in Intraoral Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15184)
*Zeynep Ozdemir,Zehra Hasgul,Irem Ulku,Omer Ozgur Tanriover,Fatma Karacaoglu,Murat Akkaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GingivitisSeg公开基准（961张口内照片，像素级牙龈炎标注，含672/144/145划分），比较七种分割模型-骨干配置，UPerNet-ResNet50取得最高mIoU 76.49%、牙龈炎IoU 54.91%，但与DeepLabV3+/SegFormer差异不显著；组合优化组件可提升性能，性能与病灶区域大小相关且随图像模糊而下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口内照片的牙龈炎像素级分割可支撑计算机辅助牙周评估，但面临严重的前景-背景类别不平衡、低对比度边界以及光照和解剖结构变化等挑战，且缺乏公开基准与标准化评测。

Method: 构建GingivitisSeg基准数据集（961张图像，像素级炎症标注，预设训练/验证/测试划分）；在统一划分、输入分辨率和评价指标下比较七种语义分割模型-骨干组合；开展控制消融实验考察类别加权交叉熵损失、Dice损失与辅助监督。

Result: UPerNet+ResNet50取得最高mIoU 76.49%与牙龈炎IoU 54.91%；经Holm校正后，与DeepLabV3+/ResNet50及SegFormer/MiT-B2的差异不具统计显著性；优化组件在UPerNet-ResNet50配置下组合使用时带来性能提升；性能与牙龈炎区域大小相关，并在更强图像模糊下下降。

Conclusion: 建立了可复现的牙龈炎分割公开基准与标准化评测流程，表明主流分割架构在该任务上性能接近、优化组件组合有效，并揭示病灶大小与图像质量对性能的影响；数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Gingivitis segmentation from intraoral photographs can support computer-assisted periodontal assessment, but pixel-level delineation remains challenging due to severe foreground-background class imbalance, low-contrast boundaries, and variations in illumination and anatomy. We introduce GingivitisSeg, a public benchmark comprising 961 intraoral images with pixel-level annotations of gingival inflammation and predefined training (672), validation (144), and test (145) partitions. Seven semantic segmentation model-backbone configurations were compared using standardized partitions, input resolution, and evaluation metrics. Controlled ablation experiments examined class-weighted cross-entropy loss, Dice loss, and auxiliary supervision. UPerNet with ResNet50 achieved the highest mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 76.49% and gingivitis IoU of 54.91%. However, differences from DeepLabV3+ with ResNet50 and SegFormer with MiT-B2 were not statistically significant after Holm correction. The evaluated optimization components improved performance when combined in the UPerNet-ResNet50 configuration. Further analyses showed that performance was associated with gingivitis region size and decreased under stronger image blur. The dataset, benchmark splits, and source code are available at https://github.com/zeynepozdemir/GingivitisSeg.

</details>


### [100] [MatrixSplat: Matrix-Parallel and Feature-Scalable 3D Gaussian Splatting Rendering on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15153)
*Yangming Zhang,Siyi Wu,Jian Wang,Bingzhe Li,Wei Niu,Sheng Di,Ming Li,Miao Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatrixSplat 提出一种矩阵并行的 3DGS 渲染框架，通过将 alpha 合成的顺序依赖替换为顺序无关的加权累积，把高维特征渲染映射到 Tensor Core 上的矩阵乘累加，实现特征可扩展的高效渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 渲染管线与当代 GPU 架构不匹配：深度排序后的 alpha 合成带来强逐像素顺序依赖，限制并行度，导致以标量 FMA 为主的执行方式，无法利用 Tensor Core 等高吞吐矩阵单元。对于多模态、语义增强的 3DGS，需从数百万高斯中累积高维特征，计算与显存开销急剧上升，该失配更加严重。

Method: 识别 alpha 合成的顺序依赖为高维特征渲染的核心瓶颈，采用顺序无关的加权累积公式，将特征渲染转化为矩阵导向计算。框架包含三项设计：(i) 基于 tile 的 splatting 管线，将特征累积映射为 warp 级 Tensor Core 矩阵乘累加；(ii) warp 粒度的高斯打包策略，缓解可见性不规则性并提升特征复用；(iii) 惰性通道分批特征取数管线，降低共享内存压力、保持高 warp 占用率，并将访存与矩阵执行重叠以处理高维特征。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360、LERF 与 3D-OVS 上，相较 3DGS 最高加速 3.8×、相较 TC-GS 加速 2×，同时保持渲染质量与语义精度；高维特征下渲染 kernel 时间最高降低 36.7×；集成到现有快速渲染器后可额外获得 1.69–2.84× 加速。

Conclusion: 通过将 3DGS 特征渲染重构为矩阵并行计算，MatrixSplat 有效解决了顺序合成瓶颈，在保持质量的前提下显著提升高维特征渲染效率，并可与现有快速渲染器互补集成。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a mainstream representation for real-time novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction. However, existing 3DGS rendering pipelines are poorly aligned with modern GPU architectures: depth-sorted alpha compositing introduces strong per-pixel sequential dependencies, limiting parallelism and resulting in scalar FMA-dominated execution that underutilizes high-throughput matrix units such as Tensor Cores. This mismatch becomes more severe for multimodal and semantically enriched 3DGS, where high-dimensional features must be accumulated from millions of Gaussians, leading to rapidly increasing computation and memory costs. We propose MatrixSplat, a matrix-parallel rendering framework for feature-scalable 3DGS on modern GPUs. We first identify the sequential dependency of alpha compositing as a key obstacle to efficient high-dimensional feature rendering, and adopt an order-independent weighted accumulation formulation that transforms feature rendering into matrix-oriented computation. Our framework features three key designs: (i) a tile-based splatting pipeline that maps feature accumulation to warp-level matrix-multiply-accumulate execution on Tensor Cores; (ii) a warp-granularity Gaussian packing strategy that mitigates visibility irregularity and improves feature reuse; and (iii) a lazy channel-batched feature-fetching pipeline that reduces shared-memory pressure, maintains high warp occupancy, and overlaps memory access with matrix execution for high-dimensional features. On Mip-NeRF 360, LERF, and 3D-OVS, MatrixSplat achieves up to 3.8$\times$ speedup over 3DGS and 2$\times$ over TC-GS while maintaining rendering quality and semantic accuracy, and further reduces render-kernel time by up to 36.7$\times$ for high-dimensional features. Integrated into existing fast renderers, it provides an additional 1.69-2.84$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [101] [Prior-as-Skill: Remember Outside and Read Inside with On-Policy Self-Distillation for Self-Evolving VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15766)
*Yan Lin,Jingyu Sun,Yifei Qian,Zhengtao Yao,Yuxia Wang,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau,Zhongliang Guo,Stephen McGough*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PriorSplit，将步骤顺序先验存放在模型外部，用训练集中相邻步骤先后计数来填补缺失帧，仅需 OPSD 同等训练成本，在帧缺失场景下优于所有对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化视觉智能体把学到的技能存为关键帧加步骤笔记（含顺序）。现有 on-policy 自蒸馏（OPSD）类内部化方法会把顺序信息固化进"读帧"权重：用完整帧训练时模型在帧缺失时靠猜，用缺帧训练时模型倾向记忆顺序，导致记忆覆盖所见。部署中帧常缺失或顺序异常（如人或网站重排步骤），因此需要既不牺牲对帧的依赖、又能应对缺帧的方案。

Method: PriorSplit 不做 OPSD 之外的额外训练。它把顺序先验保存在模型外部：统计训练回合中两个相邻步骤谁先出现，形成计数。推理时模型先判断所问的两个步骤是否都在视野中：若都在，则依据帧回答；否则由外部计数给出答案。即"有帧看帧、无帧查表"的分流机制。
