Result: 在合成技能上（五个模型）以及新回合中带步骤标注的顺序问题上（涵盖网页、烹饪、自我中心任务），帧缺失时 PriorSplit 在 OPSD 同等训练成本下优于所有对比方法。用 Qwen3-VL-4B 在合成技能上，在异常顺序场景下也接近 OPSD，而那些靠存储顺序获益最多的方法则明显落后。

Conclusion: 将顺序先验外置并以"帧可见性"分流，可在不增加训练成本的前提下同时改善缺帧鲁棒性与异常顺序表现，避免顺序记忆对视觉证据的覆盖。

Abstract: Self-evolving visual agents keep what they learn as skills: key frames with step notes on a task's steps and their order. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is now a popular way to train such skills into a vision-language model that matches, on its own answers, a teacher that also reads the written order. At deployment, frames often go missing or show an unusual order, as when a person or a website reorders the steps. Existing internalization methods keep any order they store in the frame-reading weights: trained on full frames, the model guesses when they are missing, and trained with frames withheld, it stores the order, so memory can override what it sees. We propose PriorSplit, which fills in missing frames from an order kept outside the model, with no training beyond OPSD. It counts which of two neighboring steps comes first in training episodes, and the model checks if both asked steps are in view: if so, it answers from the frames, and otherwise the counts answer. Extensive experiments on synthetic skills with five models and on step-tagged order questions in new episodes of seen web, cooking and egocentric tasks demonstrate that, with frames missing, PriorSplit outperforms every compared method at OPSD's training cost. With Qwen3-VL-4B on synthetic skills, it also stays close to OPSD on unusual orders, where methods that gain most by storing the order fall behind.

</details>


### [102] [How Far Can Frozen Vision-Language Models Go on Event Data? A Training-Free Study on EventBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15756)
*Md Kamrujjaman Mobin,Md Salman Shamil,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个无需训练的EBMV @ ECCV 2026挑战赛（General Understanding赛道）基线系统：将事件bin渲染为红/蓝ON/OFF帧后拼成网格图，用冻结的Qwen3.5 9B视觉语言模型以类别特定提示完成四类任务，动作识别则交由官方EventGPT-Plus-2B，在2,084道测试题上取得62.28%总体准确率（9/27名）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者刻意不使用任何挑战赛训练数据，目的是量化现成RGB预训练模型仅凭表示转换与提示工程在事件相机数据上能达到的上限，从而为后续微调方法提供一个纯净的"零训练"参照点。

Method: 对五类任务中的四类（手势识别、细粒度理解、因果推理、事件OCR）：将事件bin渲染为红/ON、蓝/OFF图像，按类别选取bin子集并拼接为单张网格图，对同一冻结Qwen3.5 9B VLM使用类别特定提示进行查询；第五类动作识别则把每个bin直接输入组织方提供的EventGPT-Plus-2B。此外对每个类别可视化测试样本及模型实际接收的图像，向评测服务器提交六种替代表示进行打分，并对完整测试集做事后分析。

Result: 总体准确率62.28%（2,084题，排名9/27）。动作识别80.27%、因果推理77.22%表现最佳；手势识别54.70%、事件OCR 34.57%最弱。事后分析发现：边界框裁剪几乎从未真正贴合内容，导致OCR文本以约其水平分辨率五分之一的分辨率进入模型；细粒度理解预测明显偏向前两个答案选项。

Conclusion: 现成RGB预训练VLM配合事件表示转换与提示工程即可在事件流理解上取得中游水平（9/27），但性能受限于表示分辨率损失与低分辨率渲染（尤其OCR）以及答案选项偏置。作者据此指出裁剪/分辨率管线与去偏是提升零训练基线的关键方向。

Abstract: We present a training-free system for the EBMV @ ECCV 2026 challenge "Event Stream Understanding with EventBench" (General Understanding track), which evaluates vision-language models on five event-camera understanding tasks. We use none of the challenge's training data: our goal is to measure how far off-the-shelf, RGB-pretrained models can go on event data through representation and prompting alone. For four of the five categories (gesture recognition, detailed understanding, causal reasoning, and event OCR) we render event bins as red/ON, blue/OFF frames, select a category-specific subset of bins, stitch them into a single grid image, and query the same frozen Qwen3.5 9B vision-language model with a category-specific prompt for all four tasks. The fifth category, action recognition, feeds every bin directly into the organizers' EventGPT-Plus-2B. The system reaches 62.28% overall accuracy across 2,084 test questions (rank 9/27), led by action recognition and causal reasoning (80.27%, 77.22%) and limited most by gesture recognition and event OCR (54.70%, 34.57%). We visualize test samples from every category together with the exact images the model receives, score six alternative representations against the evaluation server, and analyze the full test set post hoc. That analysis shows that our bounding-box crop almost never tightened around content, so event OCR text reached the model at roughly one fifth of its horizontal resolution, and that detailed-understanding predictions are skewed toward the first two answer options.

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Distance Detection and Adaptive Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15991)
*Kai Yi Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在FPS游戏《反恐精英：条件零》中对比目标检测与语义分割模型的敌人检测性能，并提出融合两类算法的自适应距离检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比YOLOv5s目标检测与定制语义分割在游戏敌人检测中的推理速度与精度差异，并解决模型复杂度提升时二者推理时间差距可能呈指数级扩大的问题。

Method: 定制语义分割网络架构实现逐像素检测；使用YOLOv5s训练目标检测模型；训练后比较两者推理时间与形状检测精度；提出自适应距离检测（Adaptive distance detection）融合两种算法，并提出自适应实例分割以结合语义分割与全景分割。

Result: 目标检测推理略快，语义分割较慢但对目标精确形状的检测效果更好；二者推理差异在模型复杂度增加时可能指数级扩大。

Conclusion: 两种方法在速度与形状精度上各有取舍，单一算法难以兼顾；通过自适应距离检测与自适应实例分割的组合策略可在速度与精度间取得平衡。

Abstract: This paper is about object detection model and semantic segmentation model in an FPS game Counter Strike: Condition Zero to detect enemies. Semantic segmentation is pixel-wise detection algorithm. We customized the architecture for semantic segmentation. We used YOLOv5s to train object detection model. After training, the inference time of semantic segmentation was compared to object detection. It was observed that object detection was slightly faster and semantic segmentation is slower but better at detecting the exact shape of the object. However, we found out that this slight difference of inference can grow exponentially if the both models get more complex. To solve this, we propose a novel approach called Adaptive distance detection which combines both algorithms. We will also introduce Adaptive instance segmentation to combine semantic segmentation and panoptic segmentation.

</details>


### [104] [SPEM: Scan-Prior Entropy Modeling for LiDAR Point Cloud Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16212)
*Bojun Liu,Yangzhi Ma,Jie Li,Dong Liu,Li Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPEM：一种不改变坐标表示、仅在熵模型中利用LiDAR扫描先验的几何压缩框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云存在扫描引起的空间规律性，对几何压缩有信息价值。现有扫描感知方法通过将几何变换到球面或波束对齐坐标系来利用这些规律，但会把采集先验与几何表示耦合，改变空间划分与量化方式，可能无法精确恢复原始笛卡尔几何。作者主张采集先验应指导概率建模，而非支配编码表示。

Method: 提出SPEM扫描先验熵建模框架，无需坐标变换即在熵模型内利用扫描规律。引入多频球面旋转位置编码（multi-frequency spherical rotary positional encoding），在骨干自注意力与解码上下文检索中刻画扫描对齐的空间关系；基于该编码设计解码记忆自回归预测器，通过可复用key/value缓存高效利用已解码占用信息。

Result: 在有损与无损LiDAR几何压缩任务上均取得state-of-the-art性能，表明扫描先验建模在不同坐标表示下均有效。

Conclusion: 将采集先验从表示层解耦、转而用于熵建模，可在保持原始笛卡尔几何可精确恢复的同时获得压缩增益，为扫描感知点云压缩提供了新范式。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds exhibit scan-induced spatial regularities that are informative for geometry compression. Existing scan-aware approaches often exploit these regularities by transforming coded geometry into spherical or beam-aligned coordinate systems. However, this couples acquisition priors with geometry representation, altering spatial partitioning and quantization, and potentially preventing exact recovery of the original Cartesian geometry. We argue that acquisition priors should guide probability modeling rather than dictate the coding representation. We propose SPEM, a Scan-Prior Entropy Modeling framework that exploits LiDAR scanning regularities within the entropy model without requiring coordinate transformation. We introduce a multi-frequency spherical rotary positional encoding that captures scan-aligned spatial relationships in both backbone self-attention and decoded-context retrieval. Building upon this encoding, we develop a decoded-memory autoregressive predictor that efficiently exploits previously decoded occupancy information through reusable key/value caches. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both lossy and lossless LiDAR geometry compression, highlighting the effectiveness of scan-prior modeling across different coordinate representations.

</details>


### [105] [From Foundation VLM to Versatile Driving VLM: A Triplet Incremental Training Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16727)
*Shengtao Wu,Zichao Dong,Weixuan Qiao,Xiaolu Liu,Xin Zhan,Junbo Chen,Jianke Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Triplet Incremental Training框架，通过(图像,深度,QA对)三元组与三流训练，将3D几何感知非侵入式注入通用VLM，部署时丢弃深度分支，实现零推理开销，并在四类驾驶基准上超越SOTA驾驶VLM且保留通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普遍认为将通用VLM适配自动驾驶需重型3D空间先验或侵入式架构改造以弥补空间接地缺失；作者指出通用VLM本身已具备跨驾驶任务的可迁移性，真正缺失的只是精确的度量深度，而该瓶颈可通过车载传感器与深度基础模型低成本解决。

Method: Triplet Incremental Training：自动构建(image, depth, QA pair)三元组，采用统一三流训练流水线——(1) 非冻结主VLM流用于适配驾驶任务；(2) 辅助深度流通过显式几何约束引导视觉骨干隐式"想象"3D场景几何；(3) 冻结孪生VLM流以logit级KL蒸馏防止语义漂移。部署时丢弃辅助深度分支，保持零推理开销。

Result: 在覆盖3D空间感知、驾驶导向语言推理、端到端规划与通用多模态理解四大能力的四个主要基准上评测：性能超越SOTA驾驶VLM，在3D感知上可与领域专用专家模型媲美，并成功保持原有通用推理能力。

Conclusion: Triplet Incremental Training提供了一种统一、可直接部署的范式，可将通用基础模型转化为鲁棒的驾驶智能，且无需架构大改与额外推理成本。

Abstract: Adapting general Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to Autonomous Driving (AD) is thought to require heavy 3D spatial priors or intrusive architectural overhauls to compensate for a lack of spatial grounding. In this work, we present a counterintuitive pilot study: off-the-shelf general VLMs already exhibit remarkable adaptability across diverse driving tasks, while precise metric depth remains the primary missing link, a bottleneck readily addressable via onboard sensors and depth foundation models. To this end, we introduce the Triplet Incremental Training Framework, a non-intrusive paradigm that seamlessly instills 3D geometric awareness into a general VLM backbone while fully preserving its open-world capabilities. Driven by automatically constructed (image, depth, QA pair) triplets, our framework operates via a unified tri-stream training pipeline: an unfrozen primary VLM stream to adapt to driving tasks, an auxiliary depth stream that guides the visual backbone to implicitly imagine 3D scene geometry via explicit geometric constraints, and a frozen twin VLM stream employing logit-level KL distillation to prevent semantic drift. Crucially, the auxiliary depth branch is discarded during deployment, ensuring the enhanced geometric awareness comes with zero inference overhead. We evaluate our framework across four major benchmarks targeting four diverse capabilities spanning 3D spatial perception, driving-oriented language reasoning, end-to-end planning, and general multimodal understanding. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art driving VLMs, rivals domain-specific expert models in 3D perception, and successfully retains its original general reasoning capabilities. This demonstrates that Triplet Incremental Training offers a unified, deployment-ready paradigm for transforming general foundation models into robust driving intelligences.

</details>


### [106] [SALT: State-Aligned Low-Rank Transfer for Single-Temporal Cross-Dataset Structural Localization in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17077)
*Aggelos Psiris,Georgios Angelidis,Giorgos Sfikas,Maria Vakalopoulou,Thomas Lagkas,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SALT（State-Aligned Low-Rank Transfer）框架：在冻结DINOv3骨干下学习正/负结构状态，通过状态对齐低秩适配仅在秩-2子空间修正patch特征，实现单时相跨数据集变化检测的高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥感变化检测依赖空间对齐的双时相影像，实际场景中常不可得；单时相方法虽可从带标注源域学习建筑区域等结构证据并迁移到目标影像，但特征适配易过度特化预训练特征、削弱跨数据集泛化能力。作者进一步指出，当特征适配的参数化与用于预测的状态相互独立时，这种风险更高。

Method: SALT保持DINOv3骨干冻结，学习负、正两种结构状态，二者既对每个patch打分，又通过State-Aligned Low-Rank Adaptation（SALA）张成用于修正patch特征的秩-2子空间；Directional State Alignment监督修正方向，提升正patch证据、压低负patch证据；Geometry Preservation约束预训练patch特征间成对关系的变化幅度。

Result: 在LEVIR-CD与WHU航拍建筑数据集、双向迁移、统一source-only协议下，SALT优于四个复现基线：像素级AP分别提升9.45与5.79个点，IoU分别提升27.69与14.25个点（LEVIR-CD→WHU、WHU→LEVIR-CD），仅0.068M可训练参数、每图40.2 ms。

Conclusion: 将特征适配参数化与预测状态对齐的低秩迁移能有效缓解源域过特化、提升跨数据集泛化，在极低参数量与实时性条件下显著超越基线；代码已开源以支持复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Change detection in remote sensing conventionally relies on spatially aligned bi-temporal imagery, which may be unavailable or unreliable in practical scenarios. Recent single-temporal approaches address this limitation by learning structural evidence, such as building regions, from a labeled source dataset and transferring it to unseen target imagery using only the current image. However, adaptation to the source dataset can over-specialize pretrained features and reduce their cross-dataset generalization, a risk that this paper argues is higher when the feature adaptation is parameterized independently of the states used for prediction. To address this issue, the current study introduces SALT, a State-Aligned Low-Rank Transfer framework. SALT keeps a DINOv3 backbone frozen and learns negative and positive structural states that both score each patch and, through State-Aligned Low-Rank Adaptation (SALA), span the rank-2 subspace in which patch features are corrected. Directional State Alignment supervises these corrections to raise the evidence of positive patches and lower that of negative ones, while Geometry Preservation limits changes to the pairwise relations among pretrained patch features. Experiments on LEVIR-CD and the WHU aerial building dataset show that, at the pixel level and in both transfer directions, SALT outperforms four baselines reproduced under a common source-only protocol, improving over the strongest reproduced baselines by 9.45 and 5.79 points in pixel-level AP and by 27.69 and 14.25 points in IoU (LEVIR-CD to WHU and WHU to LEVIR-CD, respectively), with only 0.068M trainable parameters and 40.2 ms per image. In order to facilitate reproducibility and further research, the source code of SALT is available at https://github.com/gthpapadopoulos/SALT

</details>


### [107] [Report of the 8th LSVOS Challenge: Mobile Unified Multimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16925)
*Chang Liu,Henghui Ding,Tong Yang,Dayoung Kil,Seong-heum Kim,Qilin Xiang,Ronghao Zheng,Ziyang Zhang,Shanlin Jiang,Ruohan Shi,Qilin Fan,Chengfeng Qiu,Kaifeng Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本报告总结了ECCV 2026第8届LSVOS挑战赛中的MUMU赛道：要求在0.5B参数与8GB峰值推理内存预算内，用单一统一模型完成多概念图像标注（质量/场景/事件词表）、开放词表目标检测与简洁图像描述三项任务，并按效率因子加权的任务得分排名。三支优胜队伍均基于Florence-2-base，但分别通过轻量标注头与跨任务推理精化、共享低秩适配器与任务相关解码、免训练流程（描述→标签映射+检测TTA）进行扩展，结果显示紧凑提示式VLM可胜任三任务，而新类别开放词表检测仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型通常以大参数量、多模型分治的方式处理标注、检测与描述任务，难以在移动端等资源受限场景部署。MUMU赛道旨在检验单一统一模型在严格参数（0.5B）与内存（8GB）预算下同时完成多概念图像标注、开放词表检测和图像描述的能力，并推动效率与精度兼顾的方案设计。

Method: 赛道设定多概念图像标注（质量、场景、事件三套词表）、开放词表目标检测、简洁图像描述三项任务，提交按效率因子缩放的加权任务得分排序。三支优胜方案均以Florence-2-base为基座：方案一增加轻量标注头并引入跨任务推理精化；方案二采用共享低秩适配器，在覆盖度感知的多源数据上训练并按任务进行差异化解码；方案三为免训练流水线，将描述映射为标签并对检测应用测试时增强。

Result: 三支队伍均成功在预算约束内以单一模型同时输出标注、检测框与描述，且各自扩展策略在不同任务上取得领先成绩；同时评测显示新类别上的开放词表检测性能显著落后，构成整体得分的主要短板。

Conclusion: 紧凑的提示式视觉语言模型（如Florence-2-base级）经针对性设计监督信号、解码策略与跨任务线索后，可统一支撑多概念标注、开放词表检测与图像描述三项输出；其中开放词表检测对新类别的泛化仍是最主要瓶颈，是后续优化的关键方向。

Abstract: This report summarizes the Mobile Unified Multimodal Understanding (MUMU) track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge, held in conjunction with ECCV 2026. MUMU asks for a single unified model that jointly performs multi-concept image tagging over quality, scene, and event vocabularies, open-vocabulary object detection, and concise image captioning, within a budget of 0.5B parameters and 8 GB of peak inference memory. Submissions are ranked by a weighted task score scaled by an efficiency factor. We describe the tasks, rules, and evaluation protocol, report the final results, and review the methods of the top three teams. All three leading solutions build on the compact Florence-2-base model, but extend it in different ways: lightweight tagging heads with cross-task inference refinement, a shared low-rank adapter trained on coverage-aware multi-source data with task-dependent decoding, and a training-free pipeline that maps captions to tags and applies detection test-time augmentation. The results show that a compact prompt-based vision-language model can serve all three outputs when supervision, decoding, and cross-task cues are designed for each task, while open-vocabulary detection on novel categories remains the main bottleneck.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Democratising Low-Data Medical Image Classification: CPU-Trainable ZACH-ViT and Its Geometric Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16608)
*Athanasios Angelakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在严格资源受限条件（<260k参数、仅CPU）下，对四种ZACH-ViT变体与两种紧凑MIL基线在MedMNIST+及三个外部基准上进行系统评测，量化小样本/全量与不同训练轮数的性能差异及CPU端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类实际部署面临标注稀缺与算力受限的双重约束，需要在极少参数与纯CPU环境下评估不同分类头与MIL结构的可行性与适用边界。

Method: 采用<260,000参数、从头训练的紧凑模型：四种ZACH-ViT头部（线性、欧氏原型、Poincaré、球面）与两种紧凑MIL基线；在7个MedMNIST+数据集与PCam、ISIC2017、深圳结核胸片三个外部基准上评测。低数据协议为每类50张训练图、5个种子，在23与47轮以验证集选检查点；全数据参考仅用1个种子；另在4线程CPU上完成40次训练运行并测单图推理延迟。

Result: 23轮时Compact-TransMIL取得十数据集平均测试Macro-F1最高（53.34%），全数据为73.93%；47轮时ZACH-Linear以57.76%微弱领先（Compact-TransMIL 57.54%）。延长训练在300组配对比较中有239组提升Macro-F1。ISIC2017低数据47轮由hZACH-Spherical最高（44.09%，SD 4.09%），全数据下ZACH-EuclideanProto最佳（51.27%）。CPU端模型单图推理约4–9 ms，但图像解码与预处理显著抬高端到端延迟。

Conclusion: 紧凑医学影像模型在给定平台上可纯CPU训练与部署；其性能优势高度依赖任务与数据制度，短轮次、低数据下MIL基线更稳，长轮次与特定数据集上特定几何头部更优，故不存在普适最优结构；同时需关注预处理主导的端到端延迟。

Abstract: Medical image classification in resource-constrained settings must contend with limited annotations and computational capacity. We evaluate four ZACH-ViT variants with linear, Euclidean prototype, Poincare and spherical heads alongside two compact multiple-instance-learning baselines. All models are trained from scratch with fewer than 260,000 parameters across seven MedMNIST+ datasets and three external benchmarks (PCam, ISIC2017 and Shenzhen tuberculosis radiographs). The low-data protocol uses 50 training images per class and five seeds, with validation-selected checkpoints at 23 and 47 epochs; a full-data reference uses one seed. Compact-TransMIL achieves the highest ten-dataset mean test Macro-F1 at 23 epochs (53.34%) and with full data (73.93%). At 47 epochs, ZACH-Linear narrowly leads (57.76% versus 57.54% for Compact-TransMIL). Extending training improves Macro-F1 in 239 of 300 paired comparisons. On ISIC2017, hZACH-Spherical has the highest 47-epoch low-data Macro-F1 (44.09% with SD 4.09%), whereas ZACH-EuclideanProto leads with full data (51.27%). We additionally conduct 40 CPU-only training runs on ISIC2017 and Shenzhen using four CPU threads; model-only single-image inference requires approximately 4-9 ms, while image decoding and preprocessing substantially increase end-to-end latency. The results demonstrate the feasibility of CPU-only training on the tested platform and the task- and regime-dependence of compact medical imaging under strict resource constraints.

</details>


### [109] [ReFineR: Single image to dense point cloud registration at pixel level with point splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16583)
*Ugo Insalaco,Jean-Emmanuel Deschaud,Pierre Dellenbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFineR：基于可微点泼溅渲染与平滑梯度方向测度的离线框架，用单张图像与粗略位姿精细化配准到预重建点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机与LiDAR观测互补，但在机器人平台上，由于时间不同步、机械振动或点云重建误差，即使标定后仍存在数像素的残余重投影误差，在中距离可造成分米级几何偏差，影响稠密跨模态监督、纹理映射、语义标签迁移等下游任务。

Method: 提出离线框架ReFineR：（1）基于可微点泼溅（point splatting）栅格化实现点云的图像化渲染；（2）采用平滑的梯度方向测度（GOM）作为分析-合成优化目标，迭代优化相机位姿；（3）优化前自动估计点云尺度因子，形成尺度不变的统一公式，适用于多场景。

Result: 在由异构数据源采集、人工标定图像构成的精选数据集上进行了评估；实验表明预先估计尺度因子后再优化，可在广泛场景下实现稳健的位姿精细化。

Conclusion: ReFineR 能有效消除残余重投影误差，实现单图像到预重建点云的高精度离线配准，且通过尺度不变建模提升跨场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDARs (Light Detection and Ranging) provide complementary observations of the same scene: cameras capture rich appearance cues while LiDAR sensors provide accurate geometric measurements. In this work, we address the problem of fine image-to-point cloud registration from a single image and a roughly estimated camera pose. On robotic platforms, residual reprojection errors of several pixels are commonly observed even after calibration due to temporal desynchronization, mechanical vibrations, or inaccuracies in point cloud reconstruction. Such errors can lead to decimeter-level geometric discrepancies at moderate distances and significantly affect multiple downstream tasks, such as dense cross-modal supervision, texture mapping, and semantic label transfer. To address this challenge, we introduce ReFineR: an offline framework to finely register individual images to pre-reconstructed point clouds. ReFineR builds upon differentiable point splatting rasterization and a smooth Gradient Orientation Measure (GOM) to iteratively refine the camera pose through analysis-by-synthesis optimization. We evaluated the method on a curated dataset of manually calibrated images collected from heterogeneous data sources. Furthermore, we show that an automatic point cloud rescale factor can be estimated prior to optimization, enabling robust refinement across a wide range of scenarios using a single scale-invariant formulation. The project page can be found at https://refiner-project.github.io/

</details>


### [110] [ActWorld-JEPA: Action-Conditioned JEPA World Models Meet Latent Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16579)
*Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Caiyan Jia,Lei Yang,Lin Liu,Yuchen Liu,Ziyang Liu,Zirui Li,Gang Wang,Peiliang Wu,Li Wang,Yadan Luo,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActWorld-JEPA 将联合嵌入预测架构用于候选轨迹规划的驾驶决策：PC-JEPA 用轨迹意图引导潜在预测并做门控交叉注意力融合，R³ 在规划监督下以有界门控残差校正基础规划分数，在 NAVSIM 与 nuScenes 上取得竞争力指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 能在潜空间做未来预测而无需像素重建，但仅凭潜预测一致性无法判定候选规划的相对驾驶质量，缺少从预测表征到候选选择的桥接机制。

Method: 提出 ActWorld-JEPA 联合嵌入框架，含两模块：(1) Plan-Conditioned JEPA (PC-JEPA)：以全局与逐步轨迹意图引导循环潜在预测，再用门控交叉注意力将预测未来与当前场景融合；(2) Relational Residual Reranking (R³)：在规划监督下建模规划表征与未来表征间的一致性与差异，生成有界门控残差以修正基础规划分数。推理仅用观测上下文与候选轨迹。

Result: NAVSIM v1 navtest 94.1 PDMS（最终选择器用 NAVTEST 聚合反馈校准）；NAVSIM v2 navtest 91.6 EPDMS、navhard 42.7 EPDMS；nuScenes 1–6 s 平均 L2 误差 1.20 m，预测碰撞率 0.76%。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了从 JEPA 预测表征通向基于预期后果的驾驶决策的有效路径。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) enable future prediction in latent space without pixel reconstruction. However, latent predictive consistency alone does not establish the relative driving quality of candidate plans. We present ActWorld-JEPA, a joint-embedding formulation for candidate-based driving. Where Plan-Conditioned JEPA (PC-JEPA) uses global and step-wise trajectory intentions to guide recurrent latent prediction, then combines the predicted future with the current scene through gated cross-attention; Relational Residual Reranking ($R^3$) models agreement and discrepancy between plan and future representations under planning supervision, yielding bounded, gated residual corrections to the base planning scores. This formulation connects latent prediction to candidate selection using only observed context and proposed trajectories at inference. Benchmark-specific implementations report 94.1 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest, with the final selector calibrated using aggregate NAVTEST feedback, and 91.6 and 42.7 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest and navhard, respectively. The nuScenes evaluation reports a mean $L_2$ error of 1.20 m and a predicted collision rate of 0.76% over 1--6 s. This work provides an effective path from JEPA's predictive representations to driving decisions informed by anticipated consequences.

</details>


### [111] [Can an LVLM Perceive Event Boundaries Like a Human?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16452)
*Hyolim Kang,Jungsoo Lee,Yeji Song,Whie Jung,Janghoon Cho,Juntae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）是否能以与人类一致的方式感知视频事件边界，提出"发现-精炼"框架，在Kinetics-GEBD上取得最优无监督性能，并扩展到小时级第一人称视频，构建了EgoBoundary基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs能有效推理视频内容，但其对事件边界的感知是否与人类一致尚不清楚；同时现有方法难以应对长时程第一人称视频中的边界检测。

Method: 以通用事件边界检测（GEBD）为切入点：先用无监督方法发现候选边界，再由LVLM进行精炼（accept/reject判断）；针对小时级第一人称视频，采用由粗到细的时间发现与局部化LVLM判断。

Result: Kinetics-GEBD上达到无监督SOTA；人类评估显示LVLM与群体共识的一致性处于人类标注者之间的范围内，分歧集中于低共识样本；所构建的EgoBoundary上，代表性监督与无监督方法性能显著下降。

Conclusion: LVLM更擅长精炼而非直接预测事件边界，与人类感知高度一致；长时程第一人称事件边界检测仍是开放挑战，EgoBoundary提供了相应基准。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) reason effectively about video content, but whether they perceive its event boundaries in a manner consistent with humans remains unclear. We first investigate this through Generic Event Boundary Detection (GEBD), which identifies event boundaries naturally perceived by humans in continuous video, without relying on predefined event categories. On Kinetics-GEBD dataset, LVLMs miss many boundaries when predicting directly from frames, but effectively refine candidates proposed by an unsupervised discovery method, achieving state-of-the-art unsupervised GEBD performance. We extend this discovery-refinement approach to hour-long egocentric video, where boundary discovery requires a broader semantic scale and whole-video LVLM refinement becomes impractical. We address these challenges through coarse-to-fine temporal discovery and localized LVLM accept/reject judgments. Human evaluation shows that the LVLM's agreement with group consensus falls within the range observed among human annotators, with disagreements concentrated on low-consensus cases. Using this pipeline, we construct EgoBoundary, a benchmark for event boundary detection in hour-scale egocentric video. Representative supervised and unsupervised methods degrade substantially on EgoBoundary, highlighting the challenge of long-horizon event boundary detection.

</details>


### [112] [HIFC-IQA: Train-Free Cross-Domain Image Quality Assessment via Dual-Process Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16451)
*Yu Li,Zhengran Shen,Puchao Zhou,Yukang Ding,Yachun Mi,Boyuan Yang,Xi Zhai,Shaohui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出完全免训练的跨域无参考图像质量评价框架HIFC，通过非对称感知分解解耦语义内容与失真敏感表示，并融合记忆共识分支与直觉分支获得质量分数，在多个基准上取得UDA方法中最优平均SRCC/PLCC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成失真与真实复杂退化之间存在域偏移，是NR-IQA的核心挑战；现有无监督域适应方法依赖大量重训练，且迭代伪标签带来误差累积。

Method: 受人类认知双过程理论启发，设计免训练跨域IQA框架：1）非对称感知分解，将语义内容与失真敏感表示解耦；2）记忆共识分支，从合成参考图库中检索稳定的"模糊质量质心"；3）直觉分支，通过零样本视觉-语言对齐捕捉美学极化；两分支协同融合得到最终质量分数。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA，在对比的UDA方法中取得最高平均SRCC与PLCC；在合成到真实的跨域设定下以显著幅度领先，体现较强鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 免训练的双分支共识范式可有效缓解域偏移与伪标签误差累积，为跨域NR-IQA提供高泛化、低开销的新思路。

Abstract: The gap between controlled synthetic distortions and complex real-world degradations introduces a critical domain shift challenge for No-Reference Image Quality Assessment. Although Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) approaches attempt to address this issue, they remain constrained by substantial retraining overhead and cumulative error propagation inherent in iterative pseudo-labeling. Inspired by the dual-process theory of human cognition for remarkable generalization ability, we propose Hierarchical Intuitive and Fuzzy Consensus (HIFC), a completely train-free Cross-Domain IQA framework. HIFC employs asymmetric perceptual decomposition to disentangle semantic content from distortion-sensitive representations. The final quality score is fused through the synergy of two complementary branches: a memory consensus branch that retrieves a stable Fuzzy Quality Centroid from a synthetic reference gallery, and an intuitive branch that captures aesthetic polarization via zero-shot vision-language alignment. Extensive experiments show that HIFC achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, attaining the highest average SRCC and PLCC among competing UDA methods. Most notably, in the challenging synthetic-to-authentic cross-domain setting, HIFC achieves a substantial performance margin over competing methods, demonstrating remarkable robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [113] [Model Distribution-Aware Multimodal Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16418)
*Jiajun Shen,Yonglin Wu,Ruonan Yu,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MDA-DD：用多阶段模型的动态交错队列提供监督，结合非对称双投影与非中心二阶矩目标做多模态数据集蒸馏，100 对合成数据即在 Flickr30K 上大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态数据集蒸馏（MDD）旨在合成紧凑图文数据集以降低视觉-语言预训练成本。现有特征匹配方法虽提升效率，但合成数据易过拟合优化过程中所见的模型表征，泛化到未见模型状态的能力受限。

Method: 提出模型分布感知的多模态数据集蒸馏（MDA-DD）：(1) 利用不同训练阶段模型构成的动态交错队列（staggered queue）提供监督，使合成数据覆盖更广的模型分布；(2) 引入非对称双投影，在视觉与语言空间中实现互补对齐；(3) 设计非中心二阶矩目标，保留与均值相关的跨模态信息。

Result: 在 Flickr30K 与 MS-COCO 上，各合成数据预算下检索性能一致优于现有方法。仅用 100 对合成数据，Flickr30K 的 IR@1 与 TR@1 分别比同预算最强基线提升 12.9 与 18.2 个百分点，并在两数据集全部六项检索指标上超过使用 500 对数据的所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 以模型分布感知监督缓解表征过拟合、辅以非对称双投影与非中心二阶矩约束，可显著提升合成图文数据的泛化性与数据效率，在极少数据预算下实现跨数据集、全指标的检索优势。

Abstract: Multimodal dataset distillation (MDD) aims to reduce the cost of vision-language pre-training by synthesizing compact image-text datasets. Although recent feature-matching methods have improved distillation efficiency, synthetic data may become specialized to the model representations encountered during optimization, limiting generalization to unseen model states. We propose Model Distribution-Aware Multimodal Dataset Distillation (MDA-DD), which leverages supervision from a dynamic staggered queue of models at different training stages. We further introduce asymmetric dual projection for complementary alignment in visual and linguistic spaces and an uncentered second-order moment objective that retains mean-related cross-modal information. Experiments on Flickr30K and MS-COCO demonstrate consistent retrieval improvements over existing methods across synthetic-data budgets. With only 100 synthetic pairs, MDA-DD improves IR@1 and TR@1 on Flickr30K by 12.9 and 18.2 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline at the same budget, outperforming all compared methods using 500 pairs on both datasets across all six retrieval metrics.

</details>


### [114] [Back to Pixels: Lifting Latent Video Diffusion into Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17336)
*Yanke Song,Shuo Chen,Chris Rockwell,Guande He,Nan Liu,Desai Xie,Tianpei Gu,Yong-Hyun Park,Aryan V S,Dennis Yuren Menn,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Xun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixVid将预训练VAE潜空间视频扩散模型提升为像素空间直接生成每一帧：保留扩散Transformer、以卷积头替代VAE解码器，并以预测干净视频（而非速度）配合更大的噪声偏移保证质量，从而突破潜空间压缩带来的分辨率、条件控制与交互性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿视频生成模型在时空双重压缩的VAE潜空间中训练，虽使训练可行，却限制了高分辨率、可控与交互式生成：分辨率翻倍使token数增至四倍；条件作用于帧组而非单帧；每次响应需等待整个多帧块完成。

Method: 提出PixVid，将预训练潜空间视频模型提升到像素空间逐帧生成。保留原扩散Transformer，用卷积头替换VAE解码器将输出渲染为像素；关键设计为预测干净视频而非速度，并采用更大的噪声偏移（noise shift）。基于Wan2.1-14B微调。

Result: 在约1.3M训练片段内即与潜空间基线具竞争力；逐帧像素生成下，在相同每帧token数下实现2倍分辨率；深度条件视频编辑在快速运动下保持对齐；可对每个动作在下一帧即时响应。

Conclusion: PixVid以极简改动将潜空间视频扩散模型迁移至像素空间，验证了预测干净视频与大噪声偏移的有效性，并在分辨率、条件对齐与交互时延三方面同时受益，为高分辨率、可控、交互式视频生成提供可行路径。

Abstract: Frontier video generation models work in a VAE latent space that compresses video in both space and time. This compression made training tractable, but now limits high-resolution, controllable, and interactive generation -- doubling resolution quadruples token count, conditions apply to groups of frames rather than individual ones, and each response must wait for the rest of its multi-frame block. We present PixVid, a simple yet effective method that lifts a pretrained latent video model to directly generate every frame in pixel space. PixVid keeps the diffusion transformer and replaces the VAE decoder with a convolutional head that renders its outputs into pixels. We find that predicting clean video rather than velocity, together with a larger noise shift, is key to quality. Finetuned from Wan2.1-14B, PixVid is competitive with the latent baseline within $\sim 1.3$M training clips. Working framewise in pixels, it generates at $2\times$ higher resolution with the same per-frame token count, keeps depth-conditioned video editing aligned under fast motion, and responds to each action at the next frame. Project page: https://chenshuo20.github.io/PixVid/

</details>


### [115] [FLEX-LBP: Learning Local Binary Patterns with Exact Binary Inference for Texture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17302)
*Mohamed Alimoussa,Amal El Kaid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLEX-LBP通过反向传播端到端学习LBP的半径、采样角、颜色变换与阈值，前向保持精确二值编码与硬直方图，仅用90个可学习参数即提升纹理分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LBP的半径、采样角、颜色变换与阈值需针对每个数据集手工设定，缺乏可学习性且难以最优适配；作者希望在不改变推理成本、保持可解释硬二值描述符的前提下自动化这些超参数选择。

Method: 提出FLEX-LBP：将LBP参数（半径、采样角、颜色变换、阈值）设为可学习变量，前向通路仍计算精确二值码和硬直方图计数，反向传播使用直通估计器（straight-through estimator），使部署描述符为可解释的硬二值LBP，仅90个可学习参数且推理成本不变；在6个彩色纹理基准上做受控消融，并比较经典LBP族、PCANet、神经LBP、冻结ImageNet ResNet-50特征及Kather结直肠癌组织学基准。

Result: 值得学习的部件依数据集而定；统一配置（冻结颜色变换、学习几何与阈值）在5个随机种子下平均提升最近邻精度3.55个百分点，6个基准中5个显著提升且从不显著变差；线性判别读出下提升0.9点，全学习描述符提升2.7点并具最高均值；在6个基准中的5个优于5种经典LBP族、PCANet与神经LBP；平均最近邻精度超过冻结ResNet-50（2350万参数）；Kather基准上全学习描述符达93.8%，显著高于最佳经典Completed LBP（92.8%）；学习到的半径方向跨种子可复现；噪声会淹没单像素比较、光源变化翻转通道间比较，中值采样或灰度世界归一化可恢复96–97%。

Conclusion: FLEX-LBP证明LBP关键参数可由梯度学习且不牺牲硬二值可解释性与推理效率；数据集依赖的部件选择提示应按需学习，配合稳健采样/归一化可抵抗噪声与光照变化；代码以即插即用PyTorch层开放。

Abstract: Local Binary Patterns (LBP) remain among the most widely used texture descriptors, from industrial inspection to medical imaging. Yet their radius, sampling angles, color transform and thresholds are fixed by hand for each dataset. We present FLEX-LBP, which learns these parameters by backpropagation while every forward pass computes exact binary codes and hard histogram counts. A straight-through estimator acts only in the backward pass, so the deployed descriptor is an interpretable hard-binary LBP with 90 learnable parameters and unchanged inference cost. In a controlled ablation on six color texture benchmarks, the component worth learning is dataset-dependent. One configuration, applied unchanged everywhere, learns the geometry and thresholds with the color transform frozen. It improves the nearest-neighbor accuracy of the identical fixed descriptor by 3.55 points on average over five seeds, significantly on five of six benchmarks and never significantly worse. With a linear discriminant readout, it gains 0.9 points; the fully learned descriptor gains 2.7 and has the highest mean. FLEX-LBP is more accurate than five classical LBP-family descriptors, PCANet and a neural LBP on five of six benchmarks. Its mean nearest-neighbor accuracy exceeds that of frozen ImageNet ResNet-50 features (23.5 million parameters). On the Kather colorectal cancer histology benchmark, the fully learned descriptor reaches 93.8%, significantly above the best classical descriptor (Completed LBP, 92.8%). The learned radii adapt in directions that recur across seeds. Noise drowns single-pixel comparisons and illuminant change flips inter-channel ones; median sampling or gray-world normalization restores 96-97%. The code is available as a drop-in PyTorch layer at https://github.com/medalimoussa/FLEX-LBP.

</details>


### [116] [SpotDiff: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Zero-Shot Change Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17172)
*Anit Upadhyaya,Ata Akbari Asanjan,Chaitanya Jalluri,Subash Yeggina,Jeremy Kravitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpotDiff 是一个免训练、即插即用的零样本遥感变化检测框架，通过组合 DINOv3、AnyUp 与 SAM 3 三个视觉基础模型实现无标签、跨分辨率的变化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥感变化检测依赖大量时间对齐的像素级标注，难以跨区域、跨平台和跨领域扩展；现有零样本方法虽减少监督需求，但因特征粗糙或跨时相不一致，对细粒度变化定位差。

Method: 免训练组合式框架：用 DINOv3 提取语义稳健特征，AnyUp 将特征重建到像素分辨率，SAM 3 提供文本引导的感兴趣区域提案，并通过推理时多尺度策略（SWIMS）提升小目标可靠性；最后在提案区域内比较两时相重建特征来度量变化，全程无可学习组件、无微调、无变化标签。

Result: 首次将通用特征上采样器用于变化检测，仅此一项较双线性上采样提升 13.8 F1；LEVIR-CD 上达 82.46 F1，超最强先验免训练方法 2.06 点；SECOND 上建筑变化检测提升 10.3 IoU，六类平均提升 2.2 IoU；PlanetScope（3 m）与 Sentinel-2（10 m）定性结果证明无需修改即可跨分辨率迁移。

Conclusion: 组合现代基础模型是实现可扩展、无标签变化检测的有效路径。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection usually relies on large collections of temporally aligned, pixel-level annotations, which limits its scalability across regions, imaging platforms and application domains. Recent zero-shot approaches reduce the need for supervision, but they often localize fine-grained changes poorly because their features are coarse or inconsistent across time. We present SpotDiff, a training-free, plug-and-play framework for zero-shot change detection built by composing complementary vision foundation models. SpotDiff uses DINOv3 for semantically robust features, AnyUp to reconstruct them at pixel resolution, and SAM 3 for text-guided region-of-interest proposals, which an inference-time multi-scale strategy (SWIMS) makes reliable on small objects. Change is then measured by comparing the reconstructed features of the two dates inside the proposed regions, with no learnable components, fine-tuning or change labels. To our knowledge, SpotDiff is the first change detection method to use a universal feature upsampler, which alone adds 13.8 F1 over bilinear upsampling of the same features. On LEVIR-CD, SpotDiff reaches 82.46 F1, 2.06 points above the strongest prior training-free method, and on the multi-class SECOND benchmark it improves building change detection by 10.3 IoU and the six-class average by 2.2 IoU. Qualitative results on PlanetScope (3 m) and Sentinel-2 (10 m) imagery show that the same pipeline transfers across resolutions without modification. Our results indicate that composing modern foundation models is an effective route to scalable, label-free change detection.

</details>


### [117] [STeP-C: Real-Time Neural Compression for Distributed Fiber-Optic Sensing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17126)
*Tingfeng Li,Ming-Fang Huang,Shaobo Han,Yue Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STeP-C神经压缩框架，解耦空间编码、时间预测与熵优化残差编码，以GPU侧符号计算、缓存概率表、无GIL并行算术编码等工程优化突破熵编码瓶颈，在真实DAS试验台上实现98.85%压缩率、31.04 dB全带PSNR与535 MB/s吞吐的实时流式管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFOS（如DAS）每秒产生数百MB的连续高分辨率时空数据，实时存储与传输压力巨大。已有神经压缩方法或需逐段重训练而不兼容流式，或仅在离线验证，尚无在大规模连续流式DAS采集系统上端到端落地的先例。

Method: STeP-C将空间编码、时间预测与熵优化残差编码解耦，并以基于条件熵缩减的率失真目标端到端训练。针对部署瓶颈，采用GPU侧符号计算、缓存概率表、无GIL并行算术编码、FP16推理与批量化熵编码，构建以GPU环形缓冲区为核心的实时管线。

Result: 在实时DAS试验台上实现98.85%文件体积缩减、31.04 dB全带PSNR，平均吞吐535 MB/s，超过1 kHz、81 km流所需的324 MB/s。在实用流式码率下显著优于传统编解码器；可在同一采集系统内跨未见信道范围免重训练迁移；下游检测器一致性良好，高置信类别一致率96.62%，事件类型Jaccard相似度100%。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在真实流式DAS系统上端到端验证神经压缩的实时可行性，表明熵编码是流式部署的主导瓶颈，并证明压缩后仍可保持下游检测任务的一致性，为DFOS数据实时传输与存储提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Distributed fiber-optic sensing (DFOS) technologies such as Distributed Acoustic Sensing (DAS) generate continuous, high-resolution spatio-temporal data at rates exceeding hundreds of megabytes per second, creating severe challenges for real-time storage and transmission. While neural compression methods can achieve strong rate-distortion performance on sensing data, prior approaches either require per-segment retraining incompatible with streaming, or have only been validated offline. None have been demonstrated end-to-end on a continuously streaming DAS acquisition system at scale. We present STeP-C (Spatio-Temporal Predictive Compression), a neural compression framework that decouples spatial encoding, temporal prediction, and entropy-optimized residual coding, trained end-to-end with a rate-distortion objective motivated by conditional entropy reduction. Critically, we identify entropy coding as the dominant bottleneck for streaming deployment. We address it through GPU-side symbol computation, cached probability tables, and parallel GIL-free arithmetic coding, together with FP16 inference and batched entropy coding, enabling a full real-time pipeline built around a GPU ring buffer. We validate this pipeline on a live DAS testbed, achieving a 98.85% file size reduction at 31.04 dB full-band PSNR and an average throughput of 535 MB/s, exceeding the 324 MB/s requirement for 1 kHz, 81 km DAS streams. We further show that STeP-C substantially outperforms conventional codecs at practical streaming bitrates, can be applied to unseen channel ranges within the same acquisition system without retraining, and largely maintains downstream detector consistency, with 96.62% high-confidence class agreement and 100% Jaccard similarity on detected event types.

</details>


### [118] [Teach with Blinks: Breaking Linguistic Momentum in On-Policy Distillation for Fine-Grained Multimodal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16402)
*Tangzheng Lian,Hang Zhang,Guanyu Hu,Renjiu Hu,Zhensong Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Jifei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BlinkOPD提出让教师在"睁眼"和"闭眼"两种条件下分别读取同一前缀，以缓解on-policy蒸馏中的"语言惯性"问题，仅用500张训练图像即在六个细粒度基准上达到SOTA，并在十个留出基准上展现强零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的细粒度感知可通过on-policy蒸馏（OPD）将昂贵的推理时缩放（zooming）摊销为单次前向过程。但vanilla OPD存在"语言惯性"（linguistic momentum）：教师会沿着学生的错误前缀继续推理，导致纠错性监督被削弱。

Method: 提出BlinkOPD：教师在每个token处"眨眼"，即对同一前缀分别以"睁眼"（可见图像）与"闭眼"（遮蔽视觉输入）两种条件各读取一次，通过两次输出的差异构造对比式监督信号；并引入"第一眼增益"（first-glance gain）机制，对与教师判断冲突的rollout施加更强的纠错权重，从而从每条on-policy轨迹中提取更有效的监督。

Result: 在仅使用500张训练图像（比现有OPD方法少12倍数据）的条件下，于六个细粒度基准上取得SOTA平均性能；并在涵盖通用视觉感知、视觉幻觉与多模态推理的十个留出基准上实现显著的零样本提升。

Conclusion: 通过"眨眼"式双条件教师读取与第一眼增益加权，BlinkOPD有效缓解了on-policy蒸馏中的语言惯性，显著提升监督效率与数据效率，并具备良好的跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective post-training approach to improving fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models by amortizing costly inference-time zooming operations into a single-pass recipe. However, vanilla OPD is inherently susceptible to linguistic momentum: the teacher can follow the student's erroneous prefixes, thus weakening corrective supervision. To address this, we propose Blink On-Policy Distillation (BlinkOPD), in which the teacher "blinks" at every token: it reads the same prefix once with its eyes open and once with its eyes closed. A first-glance gain further strengthens the correction on rollouts that conflict with the teacher. By extracting more effective supervision from each on-policy trajectory, BlinkOPD achieves state-of-the-art average performance across six fine-grained benchmarks using only 500 training images (12x less data) than existing OPD methods. Beyond fine-grained perception, BlinkOPD also demonstrates strong zero-shot improvements across ten held-out benchmarks spanning general visual perception, visual hallucination, and multimodal reasoning abilities. Code: https://github.com/Supltz/BlinkOPD

</details>


### [119] [ReliaGuard: Reconstruction-Aware Watermarking for Source Attribution and Blind Edit Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16368)
*Hailong Wang,Wuzheng Tan,Zixin Zhang,Xujun Fang,Fengjuan Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReliaGuard，一种重构感知水印框架，同时实现来源归属与盲式编辑定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像编辑使来源追溯与编辑验证变难；现有水印虽结合版权保护与篡改定位，但背景重建会削弱空间证据，即使源标识仍可恢复。

Method: 写入端在图像失真与来源保持约束下，联合优化受保护图像及其变分自编码器重构的密钥化特征响应；读取端仅用接收图像与密钥，通过残差证据精化融合水印响应与局部图像上下文，并以原生分辨率引导的多尺度读取预测编辑掩码；训练使用完整编辑输出、局部拼接版本及二者的处理版本。

Result: 在覆盖三个图像域、300 张源图与 900 个编辑案例的独立测试集上，完整输出编辑区域 IoU 达 85.61%，局部拼接输出达 94.90%（阈值按完整输出 1% 背景误报率标定）；完整输出结果超最强基线 45.02 个百分点，两条件下标识比特准确率均达 100%。经七类图像处理后，定位分数分别为 81.01% 与 86.49%，比特准确率为 92.76% 与 92.79%。

Conclusion: 该方法在背景重建及后续图像处理条件下，可同时支持来源恢复与盲式编辑定位。

Abstract: Generative image editing facilitates content creation but complicates source attribution and edit verification. Existing watermarking methods combine copyright protection with tamper localization, yet background reconstruction can weaken spatial evidence even when source identifiers remain recoverable. We propose ReliaGuard, a reconstruction-aware watermarking framework for source attribution and blind edit localization. The writer optimizes keyed feature responses in both the protected image and its variational autoencoder reconstruction under image-distortion and source-preservation constraints. Using only the received image and a secret key, the reader combines watermark responses with local image context through residual evidence refinement and predicts an edit mask through native-resolution-guided multiscale reading. It is trained on complete editing outputs, locally spliced counterparts, and processed versions of both. On an independent test set of 300 source images and 900 editing cases from three image domains, our method achieves edit-region intersection-over-union scores of 85.61% for complete outputs and 94.90% for locally spliced outputs at a threshold calibrated to a nominal 1% background false-positive rate on complete outputs. The complete-output result exceeds the strongest baseline by 45.02 percentage points, while decoded identifier-bit accuracy reaches 100% in both conditions. Across seven families of image processing, the corresponding localization scores are 81.01% and 86.49%, with identifier-bit accuracies of 92.76% and 92.79%, respectively. These results support source recovery and blind edit localization under background reconstruction and subsequent image processing.

</details>


### [120] [Pl@ntNet machine-to-machine identification service with Pl@ntNet API: enabling automated biodiversity monitoring through large-scale plant identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16538)
*Benjamin Bourel,Jean-Christophe Lombardo,Alexis Joly,Mathias Chouet,Thomas Paillot,Antoine Affouard,Vanessa Hequet,Herv{é} Go{ë}au,Pierre Bonnet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pl@ntNet推出机器对机器AI植物鉴定API服务，支持无人工干预的自动化鉴定流程，覆盖8.5万物种，物种预测准确率0.89，已处理超8400万次鉴定请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态监测日益产生来自相机、无人机、机器人和在线平台的海量图像，而现有AI植物鉴定系统多依赖用户手动上传图像，缺乏可直接嵌入自动化数据处理流程的鉴定服务。

Method: 提出Pl@ntNet机器对机器AI植物鉴定服务：通过应用程序编程接口（API）以编程方式访问其植物识别模型及配套的分类学处理，实现无需人工干预的自动化鉴定工作流，将大规模图像集与自动化观测系统直接链接至AI鉴定系统；并以iNaturalist昆虫照片为可复现示例进行验证。

Result: 覆盖全球约8.5万物种；物种预测准确率0.89；处理速度较需人工干预的流程快至多666倍；上线以来处理逾8400万次鉴定请求，其中2025年超4000万次。示例中标准笔记本约30分钟处理2,424张图像，生成708条植物-传粉者互作数据，花朵检测加权精确率0.98，属级鉴定准确率0.88。已应用于昆虫图像重建植物-传粉者互作、社交媒体地理标记图像研究植物分布、路边图像检测入侵植物、农业机器人识别杂草等场景。

Conclusion: 机器对机器物种鉴定已具备相当规模并满足真实需求，为生物多样性监测开辟新范式：自动化传感器与图像集可直接对接AI鉴定服务。

Abstract: 1. Monitoring plant biodiversity requires identifying large numbers of plants across space and time. Although artificial intelligence (AI) can now identify plant species from images with high accuracy, most existing systems rely on users manually submitting images. As ecological monitoring increasingly generates large image collections from cameras, drones, robots and online platforms, there is a growing need for plant identification services that can be directly integrated into automated data-processing workflows. 2. Here, we present Pl@ntNet's machine-to-machine AI plant identification service. This service uses an application programming interface (API) to provide programmatic access to Pl@ntNet's plant recognition models and the associated taxonomic processing. This service enables the implementation of automated identification workflows without human intervention and can thus directly link large image collections and automated observation systems to an AI-based plant identification system. 3. We establish that this service, which covers 85,000 species from around the world, (1) has a species prediction accuracy of 0.89, (2) process images up to 666 times faster than workflows requiring human intervention, (3) and has processed over 84 million identification requests since its launch, including more than 40 million in 2025 alone. We demonstrate how it has already enabled the implementation of a new generation of ecological workflows: identifying plants from images of insects to reconstruct the plant-pollinator interactions, analysing geolocated images from social media to study plant distribution, detecting invasive plants using images taken at the roadside, and identifying weeds using agricultural robots. Finally, we illustrate its application using a reproducible example: identifying plants from insect photos on iNaturalist. In approximately 30 min on a standard laptop, 2,424 images were processed to generate a dataset of 708 plant-pollinator interactions, with a weighted precision of 0.98 for flower detection and an accuracy of 0.88 for genus plant identification. 4. In conclusion, this machine-to-machine species identification is already operating on a considerable scale and meets a genuine need. All of this paves the way for a new paradigm in biodiversity monitoring, in which automated sensors and image collections can be directly connected to AI-based identification services.

</details>


### [121] [K2V: Generative Keyframing For Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17324)
*Felix O'Mahony,Daniel Olmeda Reino,Roberto Cipolla,Ayush Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将物理推理从视频扩散模型中解耦，交由语言模型驱动的关键帧生成模块显式推理，扩散模型仅负责关键帧间插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型外观与纹理质量高，但在经典生成范式下所有物理推理都必须在扩散过程中同时且唯一地完成，导致生成内容存在显著物理错误。

Method: 把物理推理任务重新分配给由语言模型支持的关键帧生成模块：借助语言进行显式推理，并对视频关键时刻的外观进行细粒度控制；随后以这些关键帧作为条件驱动视频生成模型，使扩散模型仅承担在物理正确的中间状态之间插值的较简单任务，而非用扩散推断完整序列。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著优于现有方法，在Physics-IQ Verified基准和PhyGenBench上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过将物理推理外置到语言模型与关键帧层面、降低扩散模型的任务复杂度，可有效缓解视频生成中的物理错误，验证了任务重分配范式的有效性。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are able to generate samples with high-quality appearance and textural qualities. However, content produced by these methods is known to exhibit significant physical errors. This is because, in a classical video generation setting, all physical reasoning must take place simultaneously and exclusively within the diffusion process.We propose to resolve these issues by redistributing the task of physical reasoning onto a language model-backed keyframe generation module, which allows for explicit reasoning in language, and fine-grained control over the appearance of the video at important moments in the video. We then condition a video generation model with these keyframes, reducing the role of the diffusion model to the simpler task of interpolating between physically-correct intermediate states, rather than inferring an entire sequence with diffusion. Results demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance on the Physics-IQ Verified benchmark and PhyGenBench.

</details>


### [122] [BrickFlow: Connectivity-Guided Brick Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17321)
*Peter Kulits,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BrickFlow：在程序化生成的装配数据上训练集合条件SE(3)流模型学习零件拼接先验，再用少量(1%)数据微调注入图像条件，并在测试时加入解析连通性场引导以生成连通的LEGO装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由零件集合与目标物体图像推断各零件位姿以完成装配，与传统3D重建不同，需满足离散物理约束；该约束提供结构信息但使问题呈组合爆炸且对微小位姿误差高度敏感。

Method: 1) 在程序化生成的装配数据上训练以集合为条件的SE(3)流模型，学习零件如何拼合的先验；2) 用约1%数据量微调，注入图像条件；3) 针对学到的流仅捕捉全局结构而不保证连通性的问题，引入解析的连通性场引导，在测试时将预测拉向连通装配。

Result: 装配质量随模型规模、预训练与引导而提升，验证了各组件有效性。

Conclusion: 生成式流先验+少量图像微调+测试时解析物理引导的组合可有效解决组合式装配问题；模型与数据已开放。

Abstract: Given a set of LEGO parts and an image of an assembled object, our task is to infer the pose of each part to assemble the object. In contrast with traditional 3D reconstruction, doing so requires satisfying discrete physical constraints, which provide structure but render the problem combinatorial and highly sensitive to small pose errors. To learn a prior over how parts fit together, we train a set-conditioned SE(3) flow model on procedurally generated assemblies. We then finetune this model to inject image conditioning, using 1% as much data. However, while the learned flow captures global structure, it does not enforce connectivity. To address this, we introduce an analytic connectivity-field guidance to pull predictions toward connected assemblies at test time. Through this, we demonstrate improved build quality with model size, pretraining, and guidance. We make our models and data available for research purposes at https://kulits.github.io/BrickFlow/

</details>


### [123] [MALNISI-Infused Graph Construction and Weighted Contrastive Loss based GAT for Unsupervised Representation Learning in HSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17199)
*Soumi Sarkar,Subhadip Boral,Sarbani Palit,Ashish Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MALNISI邻域光谱相似度指标构建HSI图结构，结合改进图注意力网络与加权对比损失学习判别性低维表示，并用谱聚类完成无监督高光谱聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像蕴含高维判别性光谱信息，现有基于图神经网络的无监督表示学习尚未充分刻画邻域光谱关系，且存在过平滑与细粒度光谱细节丢失问题，缺乏能刻画像素邻域相似性的有效度量与损失设计。

Method: 三项贡献：(1) 提出MALNISI（平均绝对局部邻域逆光谱指数），基于邻域信息度量像素间相似度，构建加权邻接矩阵赋予HSI图结构；(2) 提出改进图注意力网络，将MALNISI权重作为辅助特征融入增强的多头注意力机制以强化邻域注意力，并引入残差连接、跳跃投影与特征归一化缓解过平滑、保留细粒度光谱细节；(3) 提出加权对比损失，利用邻域感知的MALNISI加权，使强连接像素在隐空间保持接近、弱连接像素相互远离，最终对低维嵌入进行谱聚类。

Result: 在六个基准HSI数据集上的实验验证了三项贡献的有效性与必要性，对比研究表明其性能优于十四种最先进方法。

Conclusion: 邻域感知的图构建、注意力增强与权重化对比学习相结合，可有效实现高光谱图像的无监督判别性表示学习与聚类。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSI) possess discriminating spectral characteristics with respect to land utilization due to high-dimensional information. Recently, unsupervised representation learning using graph neural networks has unveiled these utilization-specific complex spectral relations. This article has three main contributions. First, it proposes an index, the Mean Absolute Local Neighborhood Inverse Spectral Index (MALNISI), which measures the similarity between two pixels based on their neighborhood information. The index is used to construct a weighted adjacency matrix, giving the HSI a graph structure. Second, it proposes an upgraded Graph Attention Network that learns compact latent representations of hyperspectral data from the constructed graph. In this network, an enhanced multi-head attention mechanism incorporates the MALNISI weights as an auxiliary feature to emphasize learned attention between neighboring pixels. To alleviate over-smoothing and preserve fine-grained spectral details, the architecture integrates residual connections, skip projections, and feature normalization, which together enhance representation capability, stabilize learning, and accelerate convergence. Third, it proposes a weighted contrastive loss function that incorporates neighborhood-aware MALNISI between connected pixels, encouraging strongly connected pixels in the high-dimensional space to remain close in the latent space, and weakly connected pixels to stay apart. The resulting discriminative low-dimensional embeddings are then clustered using spectral clustering. Experimental analysis on six benchmark HSI datasets demonstrates the efficiency and necessity of the three aforementioned contributions, and the comparative study highlights superior performance over fourteen state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [124] [eDMD: Endpoint Distribution Matching for Few-Step Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17194)
*Tong Zhao,Xianfang Zeng,Yucheng Han,Ruoyu Wang,Mingkun Lei,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端点分布匹配蒸馏(eDMD)：仅在学生最终输出上训练共享fake评分模型以修正每步去噪，并引入精确教师(PT)多步短轨迹监督，使匹配项当且仅当最终分布一致时消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步DMD中，学生只交付最终输出，但现有方法在中间状态或全部去噪步上训练fake模型，导致学生更新信号与中间分布纠缠，匹配方向被扭曲，甚至在最终输出已匹配教师后信号仍非零。

Method: 1) 采用单一共享fake模型，仅以学生的最终输出训练，并用其校正每个去噪步；2) 理论分析证明该端点参考下分布匹配项当且仅当学生最终输出分布等于教师分布时为零，与中间分布无关；3) 引入精确教师(PT)，在最终噪声区间以短多步轨迹替代单步引导预测，并为中间步配校准的查询密度。

Result: 在SD3-Medium、FLUX.2-klein(4B与9B)及Qwen-Image-2.1上，eDMD在4步生成中持续优于现有分布匹配方法。

Conclusion: 将fake参考锚定于学生端点输出可消除多步蒸馏中的信号纠缠与残余匹配偏差，配合更精确的教师监督，可在保持4步采样效率的同时稳定提升少步蒸馏质量。

Abstract: Distribution matching distillation (DMD) has emerged as a leading paradigm for distilling diffusion models into few-step students, where an auxiliary fake score model tracks the student's distribution and the discrepancy between the teacher and fake predictions drives the student toward the teacher. In multi-step distillation, although only the student's final output is delivered, existing methods train the fake model on intermediate states or across all denoising steps. Consequently, the student's update signal becomes entangled with intermediate distributions, which can distort the matching direction and remain nonzero even after the student's final outputs already match the teacher distribution. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Endpoint Distribution Matching Distillation (eDMD), which trains a single shared fake model exclusively on the student's final outputs and uses it to correct every denoising step. Our theoretical analysis proves that this endpoint reference guarantees that the distribution matching term vanishes if and only if the student's final output distribution matches the teacher's, regardless of intermediate distributions. Beyond the fake reference, we further strengthen teacher supervision through a precise teacher (PT) that replaces single-step guided predictions with short multi-step trajectories on the final noise interval, paired with a calibrated query density for intermediate steps. Experiments on SD3-Medium, FLUX.2-klein (4B and 9B), and Qwen-Image-2.1 demonstrate that eDMD consistently outperforms existing distribution matching methods for 4-step generation.

</details>


### [125] [HiVE-VQA: Hierarchical Interaction via Volumetric Embedding for Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17146)
*Robbie Hamilton,Fan Zhang,David Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出HiVE-VQA：首个将JEPA类视频编码器（V-JEPA 2.1）用于无参考视频质量评估的框架，冻结编码器、仅训练5.2M参数的3D卷积头，在多个benchmark上SROCC/PLCC超越17个SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户生成视频激增使准确鲁棒的NR-VQA成为重要挑战；现有方法依赖架构复杂的多分支流水线或对单分支骨干进行昂贵微调。作者希望验证：单个冻结的视频基础模型能否作为有效NR-VQA骨干。

Method: 从预训练V-JEPA 2.1编码器多个深度提取token网格，堆叠为层次化时空体（hierarchical spatio-temporal volume）；由紧凑3D卷积头沿层、空间、时间轴融合，结合浅层低级失真线索与深层的语义及时序上下文。编码器全程冻结，仅训练头部（5.2M参数，占总参数5.7%），无辅助多模态模型，推理代价与视频分辨率无关。

Result: 仅在LSVQ上训练、不做任何微调评估：在intra-dataset（LSVQ-test、LSVQ-1080p）与cross-dataset（KoNViD-1k、LIVE-VQC）上，SROCC与PLCC均优于17个SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 对单一冻结视频基础模型进行层次化、深度融合的特征利用，比任务特定多网络融合更适合作为NR-VQA的高效有效基础。

Abstract: The proliferation of user-generated video content has made accurate and robust no-reference video quality assessment (NR-VQA) a highly important challenge. Existing research increasingly relies on architecturally complex multi-branch pipelines or computationally intensive fine-tuning of single-branch backbones. In this paper, we investigate whether a single frozen video foundation model can serve as an effective NR-VQA backbone, and propose HiVE-VQA, the first framework (to our knowledge) to apply a JEPA-based video encoder to this task. HiVE-VQA extracts token grids from multiple depths of a pre-trained V-JEPA 2.1 encoder and stacks them into a hierarchical spatio-temporal volume, which a compact 3D convolutional head fuses across its layer, spatial, and temporal axes, combining low-level distortion cues from shallow layers with the semantic and temporal context captured at greater depths. The encoder remains frozen throughout, so only the head is trained: 5.2M parameters (5.7\% of the total), with no auxiliary multi-modal models and an inference cost that is invariant to video resolution. Trained solely on LSVQ and evaluated without any fine-tuning, HiVE-VQA outperforms 17 state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in SROCC and PLCC on both intra-dataset (LSVQ-test, LSVQ-1080p) and cross-dataset (KoNViD-1k, LIVE-VQC) benchmarks. These results indicate that hierarchical, depth-wise fusion of features from a single frozen video foundation model provides a more effective and efficient basis for NR-VQA than task-specific multi-network fusion. Code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/RobHam99/HiVE-VQA.

</details>


### [126] [Efficient Periocular Recognition through Model Scaling and Cross Spectral VIS NIR Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16764)
*Fabian Stockhard,Juan E. Tapia,Christoph Busch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaEye 系统性研究了可见光与跨光谱（VIS/NIR）眼周验证中输入分辨率与主干网络的缩放策略，在精度、计算量与稳定性之间权衡，支持按协议与部署约束选择紧凑眼周架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当人脸区域被遮挡或图像分辨率受限时，全脸验证失效，眼周识别是高效替代方案；但眼周输入与主干网络的缩放选择缺乏系统认识。

Method: 基于 AdaFace 风格框架，比较全脸、遮挡人脸与专用 80x40 眼周输入，并变化主干宽度与嵌入维度；在 4 个 VIS/VIS 与 VIS/NIR 协议上评测验证精度、计算成本、推理吞吐、训练显存、模板大小与训练稳定性，采用多随机种子实验。

Result: 直接眼周处理将单图识别成本从 6.30 GMACs 降至 1.64 GMACs，实测吞吐提升约 3 倍；眼周模型在原生 UFPR 眼周图像上更优，而基于人脸的协议更倾向于保留更多输入信息；适度缩减主干宽度在 CASIA 与 ND-NIVL 上可保持平均验证性能并降低计算量；嵌入压缩降低模板存储，主要利于部署；部分配置（尤其同时压缩嵌入与主干）需要数值稳定化。

Conclusion: 应依据目标协议与部署约束选择紧凑眼周架构，在准确率、效率、存储与鲁棒性之间取得平衡；输入与容量的收益具有协议依赖性，并非越小越好。

Abstract: Periocular recognition offers an efficient alternative to full-face verification when facial regions are occluded or image resolution is limited. We present AdaEye, a systematic study of input and backbone scaling for periocular verification in visible-spectrum (VIS) and cross-spectral (VIS/NIR) settings. Using an AdaFace-style framework, we compare full-face, masked-face, and dedicated 80x40 periocular inputs while varying backbone width and embedding dimensionality. Across four VIS/VIS and VIS/NIR protocols, we assess verification accuracy, computational cost, inference throughput, training memory, template size, and training stability. Direct periocular processing reduces recognition cost from 6.30 to 1.64 GMACs per image and increases measured throughput by approximately threefold. Its effectiveness depends on the evaluation protocol: periocular models perform better on native UFPR periocular images, whereas face-based protocols favor retaining more input information. Moderate backbone width reductions maintain comparable mean verification performance on CASIA and ND-NIVL while reducing computation. Embedding compression decreases template-storage requirements, primarily benefiting deployment. Multi-seed experiments reveal that some configurations require numerical stabilization, particularly when embedding and backbone reductions are combined. These findings support selecting compact periocular architectures according to the target protocol and deployment constraints, balancing accuracy, efficiency, storage, and robustness.

</details>


### [127] [What Limits Thermal Infrared Drone Detection? A Cross-Dataset Benchmark Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16724)
*Tamara R. Lenhard,Jan Kels,Tobias Koch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统刻画了公开红外无人机数据集在相机系统、处理流程与标注上的可变性，建立跨采集与处理流程的伪影分类体系，并基于长波红外数据研究训练数据组成与模型特性对YOLO检测性能、泛化与失效模式的影响，发现训练数据组成比YOLO代数或模型容量更具决定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外成像可实现不依赖环境光照的无人机连续监测，但公开红外数据集在相机系统、处理管线与标注实践上的巨大且常未记录的可变性，使检测器性能评估与跨域泛化分析难以可靠进行。

Method: 系统性地对现有红外无人机数据集进行特征刻画；建立伪影分类体系，按采集与处理流程来源划分为光学与传感器效应、色调映射与图像增强、记录相关伪影；聚焦长波红外数据，考察训练数据组成与模型特性对YOLO类检测器的检测性能、泛化能力及条件相关失效模式的影响。

Result: 检测性能主要受训练数据组成支配，而非YOLO代际或模型容量；跨数据集泛化呈现显著不对称性；最优配置仍漏检超过40%的无人机，在极小目标、杂乱城市场景与热交叉情形下漏检率更高。

Conclusion: 现有红外无人机数据集存在明显不足，数据组成与领域差异比模型规模更关键；亟需规范数据采集、处理与标注流程并改进数据集设计，以提升检测可靠性与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Thermal infrared (IR) imaging enables drone detection with reduced dependence on ambient illumination, supporting continuous surveillance beyond the limitations of visible-spectrum sensing. However, substantial and often undocumented variability in camera systems, processing pipelines, and annotation practices across public IR drone datasets complicates the assessment of detector performance and cross-domain generalization. To examine these sources of variability and their implications for drone detection, we systematically characterize existing IR datasets and establish an artifact taxonomy reflecting their origins across the acquisition and processing pipeline, encompassing optical and sensor effects, tone mapping and image enhancement, and recording-related artifacts. Focusing on long-wave IR data, we investigate the impact of training data composition and model characteristics on YOLO-based detection performance, generalization, and condition-dependent failure modes. Our results demonstrate that detection performance is governed more strongly by training data composition than by YOLO generation or model capacity, while cross-dataset generalization exhibits pronounced asymmetries. Even the best-performing configuration fails to detect over 40\% of drones, with elevated miss rates for very small targets, cluttered urban backgrounds, and thermal crossover, highlighting the shortcomings of existing datasets.

</details>


### [128] [Sprint3D: Accelerating End-to-End 3D Asset Generation through Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16701)
*Sho Kuno,Ruihan Yu,Yonghao Yu,Guixu Lin,Lian Fu,Muyao Niu,Yu-Ju Tsai,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yi-Chuan Huang,Siqi Yang,Fengbo Lan,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sprint3D 在公开的 Pixal3D 文生三维管线上做端到端加速：将网格后处理迁移到 GPU 并移出关键路径，同时降低整流流每步开销并从 76 步蒸馏到 8 步，单张 H200 上单资产中位耗时由 133 s 降至 4.05 s（33×），且多数输入视角法向指标优于原管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到三维生成模型已能产出资产级带纹理网格，但每个资产需数分钟，迭代慢、部署成本高。既有工作多只加速网络采样，若同时优化网格处理则往往需自训练生成器；本文希望不重训模型，直接在已公开管线上挖掘端到端可节省的时间。

Method: 对 Pixal3D 做性能剖析，发现瓶颈在网络之后：四个整流流仅占 14% 墙钟时间，网格后处理占 85%，其中主要是基于 CPU 的 UV 展开。据此在 GPU 上重建后处理并将其移出关键路径；再削减整流流每次评估的额外开销，并用蒸馏把网络评估次数从 76 降至 8。

Result: 相同导出设置下，中位单资产耗时 133 s→4.05 s（33×，单卡 H200）。在 2,653 个 held-out Toys4K 对象上，Pixal3D 协议的全部输入视角法向指标均有提升，但平均轮廓 IoU 略低、点云对齐分数偏低、细粒度纹理细节减少。仅重建后处理即可使共用导出代码的 TRELLIS.2 加速 6.7×，且评测指标无可检测变化。

Conclusion: 端到端瓶颈分析表明，三维生成管线的延迟主要来自非网络部分，工程化后处理与采样蒸馏可带来数量级加速；但加速伴随轮廓、几何对齐与纹理细节的轻微质量折衷，需在效率与保真度间权衡。

Abstract: Image-to-3D generative models now produce asset-quality textured meshes, but at minutes per asset they remain slow to iterate with and costly to deploy. Prior work has mostly accelerated the sampling of the networks, and where it also optimizes the mesh processing, it trains its own generators. We instead start from a publicly released pipeline and ask how much of its end-to-end time can be saved. Profiling the public Pixal3D pipeline shows that its latency is dominated by what happens after the networks: the four rectified flows take 14% of the wall-clock time and mesh post-processing takes 85%, most of it in CPU-based UV unwrapping. We therefore first rebuild the mesh post-processing on the GPU and take it off the critical path, then cut the per-evaluation overhead of the flows and distill them from 76 to 8 network evaluations. The resulting pipeline, Sprint3D, reduces the median time per asset from 133 s to 4.05 s on one H200 GPU at the same export settings (33$\times$). At the same time, on 2,653 held-out Toys4K objects it improves every input-view normal metric of the Pixal3D protocol, with a slightly lower mean silhouette IoU, lower point-cloud alignment scores and less fine texture detail. The rebuilt post-processing alone also speeds up TRELLIS.2, which uses the same export code, by 6.7$\times$ without a detectable change in the evaluated metrics.

</details>


### [129] [AURA 2.0: From User-Driven Responses to Model-Initiated Conversations in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17048)
*Shuhan Li,Huankang Guan,Xintong Guo,Yang Bo,Yuyang He,Dunyuan Xu,Jingyan Chen,Fang Liu,Tsz Kin Wan,Jinpeng Chen,Zhenqi He,Yuxuan Hu,Wenbo Li,Han Gao,Qijun Li,Long Chen,Hongsheng Li,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AURA 2.0 是一个流式视频大语言模型，引入模型主动发起对话（MIC）、流感知工具调用（SATC）与交互式视觉定位（IVG）三项升级，并配合长程交互记忆（LHIM）与三阶段训练，在开源流式模型中取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 助手仅在被提示时响应，即使视频流中出现值得讨论的事件也保持沉默，交互完全由用户驱动，缺乏主动性。

Method: 在 AURA 基础上提出 MIC（识别视觉流中值得讨论的话题并在合适时机主动发起对话）、SATC（基于当前视觉与对话上下文调用外部工具并融合返回信息）、IVG（支持用户输入与模型输出双向视觉定位，识别用户标注对象并输出帧对齐边界框）；构建专用流式交互数据集，采用三阶段训练方案将新行为与既有流式交互能力融合，并加入 Long-Horizon Interaction Memory（LHIM）维持长视频流的上下文连续性。

Result: 在公开流式基准上，AURA 2.0 在开源流式模型中达到领先性能；针对性评估进一步验证了其新增的交互能力。

Conclusion: AURA 2.0 实现了从纯用户驱动到模型主动交互的转变，并开源模型权重与推理代码。

Abstract: Most AI assistants respond only when prompted, remaining silent even when events in the video stream offer meaningful opportunities for conversation. To move beyond purely user-driven interaction, we present AURA 2.0, a streaming video large language model that introduces three core upgrades to AURA. The key advance is Model-Initiated Conversation (MIC), which enables the model to identify topics worth discussing in the evolving visual stream and initiate conversations at appropriate moments without a user query. Stream-Aware Tool Calling (SATC) enables the model to invoke external tools using the current visual and conversational context and incorporate returned information into the ongoing interaction. Interactive Visual Grounding (IVG) supports visual grounding in both user inputs and model outputs. The model can identify objects marked by users on video frames and produce frame-aligned bounding boxes to link its responses to specific objects and regions. To develop these capabilities, we construct a dedicated streaming interaction dataset and adopt a three-stage training scheme to integrate the new behaviors with AURA's existing streaming interaction abilities. We further incorporate Long-Horizon Interaction Memory (LHIM) to maintain contextual continuity over extended video streams. Evaluations on public streaming benchmarks show that AURA 2.0 achieves leading performance among open-source streaming models, while targeted evaluations further validate its new interaction capabilities. We open-source the model weights and inference code.

</details>


### [130] [Visual Cipher Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16861)
*Deyao Hong,Jiayi Jiang,Yuchun Chen,Yixuan Shi,Wenyi Li,Zheng Jiang,Chengxing Xie,Mingyu Su,Qingle Liu,Mingju Gao,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Kaisen Yang,Huan-ang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Visual Cipher Bench，包含 40 个仅以黑白线稿呈现的视觉谜题，用于评测智能体从纯图像中自主发现规则并进行长程视觉推理的能力；最强模型 GPT-6 Astra (max) 仅达 26.7%，八个模型中五个低于 1%，而给出单条非剧透机制提示后准确率从 7.8% 升至 55.6%，表明瓶颈在于规则发现而非多步执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在长程编码与终端任务上的成功依赖两个条件：显式文本规格与即时执行反馈。现实中关键信息常仅存在于图像中，规则与线索难以转写为文本且缺少测试用例反馈，因此长程能力能否从文本迁移到视觉推理尚不明确。而现有评测无法刻画该能力：视觉推理基准多为单轮短链问答，智能体基准则聚焦文本环境或允许试错的交互状态。

Method: 构建 Visual Cipher Bench：40 个原创视觉谜题，分为空间操作与模拟、逻辑约束与规划、规则归纳、知识型解码四类；每题仅提供 1–3 张黑白线稿与极简提交说明，要求智能体完全从图像中发现解题机制，需反复检视图像、模拟空间变换并自主交叉验证线索。评测覆盖 8 个前沿模型的 29 种推理强度配置，并设计十题子集上的单条非剧透机制提示对照实验。

Result: 即使最强模型 GPT-6 Astra (max) 准确率也仅 26.7%，八个模型中有五个低于 1%；在十题子集上，仅提供一条非剧透机制提示即可将准确率由 7.8% 提升至 55.6%。

Conclusion: 当前智能体的主要瓶颈不在于执行多步计划，而在于从视觉证据中自主发现规则；Visual Cipher Bench 为衡量智能体的长程视觉推理提供了严格基准。

Abstract: While language-model agents excel at long-horizon coding and terminal tasks, this success relies on two conditions: explicit text specifications and immediate execution feedback. However, neither condition holds in real-world problems where critical information exists only in images, leaving it unclear whether long-horizon capability transfers from text to visual reasoning. In such visual settings, because rules and clues resist text transcription and lack test-case feedback, agents must repeatedly inspect images, simulate spatial transformations, and autonomously cross-check clues. Despite this challenge, existing evaluations fail to capture such capability: visual reasoning benchmarks largely reduce tasks to single-turn, short-chain question answering, whereas agent benchmarks focus on text-based environments or interactive states that permit trial-and-error. To bridge this gap, we introduce Visual Cipher Bench, a collection of 40 original visual puzzles across four categories: spatial manipulation and simulation, logical constraints and planning, rule induction, and knowledge-based decoding. Each puzzle provides only one to three black-and-white line drawings and a minimal submission instruction, requiring agents to discover the underlying solving mechanism entirely from the images. Across 29 reasoning-effort configurations of eight frontier models, even the strongest model, GPT-6 Astra (max), achieves only 26.7% accuracy, while five of the eight models score below 1%. Yet on a ten-task subset, providing a single non-spoiler mechanism hint boosts accuracy from 7.8% to 55.6%. This sharp contrast shows that the primary bottleneck of current agents lies not in executing multi-step plans, but in autonomously discovering rules from visual evidence. Visual Cipher Bench thus provides a rigorous benchmark for measuring long-horizon visual reasoning in agents.

</details>


### [131] [TTB: Test-time Baking for Efficient Rendering of Decoder-only View Synthesis Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17331)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Ryo Hachiuma,Jaesung Choe,Min-Hung Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu,Cheng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Test-Time Baking (TTB)：将跨视角注意力学到的全局场景信息"烘焙"进轻量MLP，实现快速高保真的新视角解码，最高23倍渲染加速且质量与SOTA持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Decoder-only大型视图合成模型（LVSM）配KV-cache虽质量领先，但计算开销大；Test-Time Training（TTT）层虽节省计算，却牺牲渲染质量。需在效率与质量间取得更好平衡。

Method: 两个关键设计：(1)复用TTT的fast-weight MLP，从其输入-输出对中显式学习跨视角注意力映射，而非完全替换注意力，从而保持TTT级效率但质量显著提升；(2)解决context预填充与新视角渲染的模态错位——将context token解耦为仅含相机信息的context rendering token作为TTB输入，含图像内容的context ground-truth token作为TTB映射目标，减轻烘焙MLP的模态泛化负担。

Result: 在多个基准上实现最高23倍渲染加速，渲染质量与当前SOTA相当。

Conclusion: 通过显式学习跨视角注意力映射并对context token做模态解耦，TTB在保持高效率的同时达到SOTA级视图合成质量，为免几何重建的decoder-only视图合成提供了一条高效高保真路径。

Abstract: Decoder-only Large View Synthesis Models (LVSMs) with KV-cache have recently achieved state-of-the-art quality by regressing novel views from neural networks without reconstructing 3D geometry, while Test-Time Training (TTT) layers save computation but sacrifice quality. We propose Test-Time Baking (TTB), a strategy that learns to bake global scene information from cross-view attention into a lightweight MLP, enabling fast and high-fidelity novel-view decoding. The first key is to repurpose the fast-weight MLP of TTT to learn the cross-view attention mapping explicitly from its input-output pairs, rather than replacing attention entirely, achieving the same rendering efficiency as TTT while delivering substantially higher quality. The second key addresses a modality misalignment between context prefilling and novel-view rendering: we disentangle context tokens into camera-only context rendering tokens to serve as TTB input, and context ground-truth tokens with image content to serve as the TTB mapping target, ensuring the baked MLPs are relieved of the modality generalization challenge between context and novel-view processing. Our method achieves up to **23x** rendering speedup with quality on par with the state-of-the-art across several benchmarks. Project page: https://test-time-baking.github.io

</details>


### [132] [Fog-FWL: A Large-Scale Full-Waveform LiDAR Dataset for Recovering Fog-Hidden Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16742)
*Kazuma Ikeda,Kotaro Oishi,Ryosei Hara,Ryo Yoshida,Takahiro Kado,Ibuki Fujioka,Taro Beppu,Mariko Isogawa,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Fog-FWL 数据集体系，包含真实雾天全波形 LiDAR 基准 Fog-FWL-Real（7,527 帧真实数据，规模为现有唯一真实雾天 FWL 数据的 579 倍）与基于物理仿真器生成的大规模合成训练集 Fog-FWL-Sim（12,000 帧），用于从全波形层面恢复被雾遮蔽的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 易受雾影响：雾散射在近端产生强后向散射并衰减远处目标回波，峰值检测常丢弃弱目标回波；全波形 LiDAR（FWL）在峰值检测前保留原始光信号，可保留这些弱回波，但缺乏合适数据阻碍了相关研究。

Method: 构建 Fog-FWL 数据集基础：Fog-FWL-Real 含 8 个场景、58 个序列的 7,527 帧真实雾天 FWL，配直方图级 Object/Fog/Noise 标注、10,719 个 3D 边界框，并按目标回波衰减定义三档难度；Fog-FWL-Sim 由自研物理仿真器 Fog-FWL-Simulator 生成 12,000 帧，集成空间非均匀雾密度场、含多重散射的时间分辨光传输及光子计数 SPAD 接收模型；两子集采用相同传感器格式与标注协议，支持不使用真实波形训练的严格 sim-to-real 评估。

Result: 分析表明，在最难条件下仅 34.9% 的目标光线的目标回波进入前三强峰，凸显波形级恢复的必要性。

Conclusion: Fog-FWL-Real、Fog-FWL-Sim 与 Fog-FWL-Simulator 将公开发布，以推动雾天鲁棒 LiDAR 感知研究。

Abstract: LiDAR is a core sensor for autonomous driving, yet it is highly vulnerable to fog. Fog scattering produces strong backscatter near the sensor and attenuates reflections from objects behind it, so weak object returns are often discarded by the peak detection that converts received signals into point clouds. Full-waveform LiDAR (FWL), which records the received optical signal before peak detection, retains these weak returns, but the lack of suitable data has prevented research on recovering fog-hidden objects from FWL. We present Fog-FWL, a dataset foundation for this task that consists of a real-world benchmark, Fog-FWL-Real, and a large-scale synthetic training set, Fog-FWL-Sim. Fog-FWL-Real contains 7,527 real foggy FWL frames from 8 scenes and 58 sequences, which is 579x larger than the only existing real foggy FWL data, together with histogram-level Object/Fog/Noise labels, 10,719 3D bounding boxes, and three difficulty levels defined by the attenuation of object reflections. Fog-FWL-Sim contains 12,000 frames generated by our physics-based simulator, Fog-FWL-Simulator, which integrates spatially heterogeneous fog density fields, time-resolved light transport including multiple scattering, and a photon-counting SPAD receiver model. The two subsets share the same sensor format and annotation protocol, enabling a strict sim-to-real evaluation in which no real waveforms are used for training. Our analysis shows that, under the hardest condition, object returns are included in the three strongest peaks for only 34.9% of object rays, underscoring the need for waveform-level recovery. Fog-FWL-Real, Fog-FWL-Sim, and the Fog-FWL-Simulator will be publicly released to facilitate research on fog-robust LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [133] [PiLLVD: A Real-World Low-Light Video Dataset for Dynamic Scenes Captured with Off-the-Shelf Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16515)
*Yaser Abu Awwad,Paul L. Rosin,Oktay Karakuş*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PiLLVD——首个基于树莓派多相机系统采集的配对低光视频数据集，面向运动丰富场景，并在零样本设定下基准评测现有低光视频增强方法，揭示其泛化不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光增强方法多在静态图像上开发与评测，低光视频增强进展受限于缺乏真实配对视频数据；采集配对低光/正常光视频需时空对齐，并避免运动模糊、曝光不匹配与动态场景中的对应误差，难度大。

Method: 构建PiLLVD数据集：使用低成本、易获取的树莓派多相机系统与现成相机，同步采集动态室内外场景的配对低光序列与对齐的正常光参考序列，覆盖多样化的人与物体运动及不同光照条件；同时给出完整采集流程，并在零样本设定下对代表性预训练低光视频增强方法进行基准评测。

Result: 现有方法在不同序列上泛化表现不一致，无法统一适用；表明该数据集对现有方法构成显著挑战，兼具基准评测与推动新算法研发的价值。

Conclusion: PiLLVD弥补了真实配对低光视频数据的空白，强调逐帧稳定性与运动相关伪影的评测需求，为低光视频增强研究提供基准平台。

Abstract: Low-light image and video enhancement has received increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing enhancement methods are developed and evaluated on still images, while progress in low-light video enhancement remains limited by the lack of real-world paired video datasets. Acquiring paired low-light and normal-light video sequences is challenging because it requires spatially and temporally aligned captures while avoiding motion blur, exposure mismatch, and correspondence errors in dynamic scenes. To address this gap, we introduce PiLLVD, the Pi-based Low-Light Video Dataset, a paired low-light video dataset captured using an accessible Raspberry Pi-based multi-camera system with off-the-shelf cameras. PiLLVD provides paired low-light and aligned normal-light reference sequences for dynamic indoor and outdoor scenes, including diverse human and object motion under varying illumination conditions. Unlike existing datasets that are often image-based, static, synthetic, or limited to simple motion patterns, PiLLVD focuses on motion-rich scenes where frame-to-frame stability and motion-related artefacts are important for evaluation. In addition to presenting the dataset and acquisition pipeline, we benchmark representative pre-trained low-light video enhancement methods on PiLLVD under a zero-shot setting. The results show that existing methods do not generalise uniformly across the proposed sequences, highlighting the challenges posed by the proposed dataset and its value for both benchmarking existing methods and supporting the development of future low-light video enhancement algorithms.

</details>


### [134] [TokenStream: Multi-Subject Personalized Video Generation with Token-wise LoRA and Guided Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16897)
*Hung Nguyen,Quang Qui-Vinh Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenStream：基于优化的无需参考视频训练数据的文本到视频多主体组合生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张参考图学习导致主体外观与静态图像偏置耦合、难以解耦；独立训练适配器合并又会造成身份混淆。目标是无需联合优化即可组合多个独立学习的主体身份。

Method: 1) Token-wise LoRA（ToRA）：将低秩更新限制在与主体关联文本token的cross-attention value投影上，为每个主体提供独立适配路径；2) T2V引导流：早期去噪阶段从冻结基模型复制query和key，复用其布局与运动先验，同时保留个性化value；3) 提出Multi-DB基准（30主体、122提示，无外观描述提示与多样交互）。

Result: 在1.3B模型上，Multi-DB基准中身份保持度由DreamBooth的0.422提升至0.467，文本对齐由0.652提升至0.688。

Conclusion: token级LoRA隔离与query/key复用相结合，可在不联合优化、无参考视频数据条件下有效实现多主体文本到视频组合生成。

Abstract: We introduce TokenStream, an optimization-based framework that composes independently learned subject identities in text-to-video generation without a reference-video training dataset or joint optimization of each new subject combination. Learning from a single reference image makes subject appearance difficult to separate from static-image bias, while merging independently trained adapters can mix identities.
