Token-wise LoRA (ToRA) restricts low-rank updates to the cross-attention value projections of subject-associated text tokens, providing a separate adaptation path for each subject. A T2V-guided stream copies queries and keys from the frozen base model during early denoising to reuse its layout and motion priors while retaining personalized values. We also introduce Multi-DB, a 30-subject, 122-prompt benchmark with appearance-free prompts and varied interactions. On Multi-DB at 1.3B, TokenStream improves identity preservation from 0.422 for DreamBooth to 0.467 and textual alignment from 0.652 to 0.688.

</details>


### [135] [Gravity and Flight: Learning to Navigate Fine-Grained Remote Sensing Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16408)
*Yuheng Zong,Minghua Wang,Xin Zhao,Xiaobin Liu,Yongjian Dong,Chanho Eom*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Gravity and Flight (G&F) 框架，用于在有限监督下对复杂遥感场景进行细粒度场景专精，将视觉验证与推理验证分离并耦合策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能力不断增强，但在复杂遥感场景中的推理仍较粗糙，且未充分基于任务相关的视觉证据。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) Evidence-of-Thought 监督微调 (EoT-SFT)，通过描述、分析与有依据的结论来教会模型细粒度观察与忠实推理；(2) Relational Transfer Policy Optimization (RTPO)，在同一任务下对具有细粒度视觉差异的场景匹配图像对独立采样回答，并采用基于评分量规的验证——Gravity 检验视觉保真度与描述覆盖度，Flight 以描述为前提评估证据使用及成对判断间的关系，二者得分与任务正确性形成共享的图像对级优势，从而耦合两个回答的策略更新。

Result: 在港口场景专精中，仅用 1,008 条演示和 500 对训练样本，G&F 在六个评估集上平均得分 69.12，分别超过骨干模型与监督初始化 9.76 和 4.56 分；对照实验显示其优于单图像 RL 与联合输入 RL，且移除任一验证视角都会导致性能下降。应用于城市场景专精时，在三个评估子集上同样优于基础模型与监督初始化。

Conclusion: G&F 通过分离视觉与推理验证并耦合策略更新，在有限监督下有效实现遥感场景的细粒度专精，且具备跨场景的可迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly capable, yet their reasoning in complex remote sensing scenes can remain coarse-grained and insufficiently grounded in task-relevant visual evidence. We propose Gravity and Flight (G&F), a framework for scene specialization with limited supervision that separates visual and reasoning verification while coupling policy updates. It combines Evidence-of-Thought supervised fine-tuning (EoT-SFT) and Relational Transfer Policy Optimization (RTPO). EoT-SFT teaches fine-grained observation and faithful reasoning through description, analysis, and supported conclusions. RTPO independently samples responses to scene-matched image pairs with fine-grained visual differences under a shared task. Its rubric-based verification combines Gravity, which checks visual fidelity and description coverage, and Flight, which takes descriptions as premises to assess evidence use and relations between paired judgments. Their scores and task correctness yield a shared pair-level advantage that couples policy updates from both responses. Using 1,008 demonstrations and 500 training pairs for harbor specialization, G&F achieves a mean score of 69.12 across six evaluation sets, exceeding the backbone and supervised initialization by 9.76 and 4.56 points. Matched controls show gains over single-image and joint-input RL, and removing either verification view reduces performance. Applying G&F to urban specialization also improves on the base model and supervised initialization across three evaluation subsets. Project details and updates are available at https://github.com/Z0ngL1ng/Gravity-and-Flight.

</details>


### [136] [Description-Guided CLIP Adaptation with Local Evidence Calibration for Few-Shot Plant Disease Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16373)
*Zhenkun Ye,Qian Zhou,Hua Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出描述引导的两阶段CLIP适配方法，用症状/对比描述增强类语义并聚合细粒度patch证据，兼顾少样本植物病害识别与跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本下标注样本有限，难以覆盖诊断性症状与类内差异；现有CLIP方法依赖粗粒度类语义和全局视觉表征，忽略病害特异诊断信息与局部细粒度线索；过度少样本适配又会导致过拟合并削弱大规模预训练的泛化能力。

Method: 描述引导的两阶段CLIP适配：1）用症状描述与对比描述丰富类语义，引入典型视觉症状及相似病害间判别线索；2）以增强语义引导类相关patch证据聚合，用细粒度线索校准全局图文匹配；3）适配阶段先以轻量参数更新视觉-语言编码器，再冻结后训练线性分类器；4）推理时域内用分类器预测，跨域用适配后文本编码器的类原型预测以保留视觉-语言迁移性。在ViT-B/16与ViT-L/14两个骨干上验证。

Result: 在PlantVillage、Cassava、Pear上多数少样本设置取得最高平均准确率；ViT-L/14、16-shot下分别达93.16%、79.30%、96.56%；在21个共享PlantDoc类的类对齐跨域评测中达56.96%，优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在细粒度少样本植物病害识别与跨域泛化上均有效，说明描述增强语义与细粒度patch证据聚合可缓解粗粒度语义与过适配问题。

Abstract: Accurate plant disease recognition is essential for intelligent agriculture, but few-shot settings provide limited labeled samples that may not capture diagnostic symptoms and intra-class variations. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, offer transferable visual-semantic knowledge. However, existing CLIP-based methods often rely on coarse class semantics and global visual representations, overlooking disease-specific diagnostic information and fine-grained local cues. Excessive few-shot adaptation may also cause over-specialization and weaken the generalization ability of large-scale pre-training. We propose a description-guided, two-stage CLIP adaptation method to address these issues. Symptom and comparative descriptions enrich class semantics with typical visual symptoms and discriminative cues between similar diseases. The enhanced semantics guide the aggregation of class-relevant patch evidence to calibrate global image-text matching with fine-grained cues. For adaptation, we first update the vision-language encoders with lightweight parameters, then freeze them and train a linear classifier. At inference, the classifier is used for in-domain prediction, while class prototypes from the adapted text encoder support cross-domain prediction and retain vision-language transferability. Experiments with two backbones show that our method achieves the highest mean accuracy in most few-shot settings on PlantVillage, Cassava, and Pear, reaching 93.16%, 79.30%, and 96.56%, respectively, with ViT-L/14 under 16-shot. On class-aligned cross-domain evaluation across 21 shared PlantDoc classes, it achieves 56.96% accuracy, outperforming all compared methods. These results demonstrate its effectiveness in fine-grained few-shot plant disease recognition and cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [137] [MuViRL: Multimodal View-Invariant Visual Speech Representation Learning via Calibrated View Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16655)
*Fei Su,Kai Qiu,Juan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MuViRL自监督框架，通过校准视角聚合实现多模态视角不变的视觉语音表征学习，在真实与合成多视角数据上提升表征一致性与跨视角识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表征学习需在视角间保持一致、同时保留时间状态差异；视觉语音中视角变化会改变发音线索的可见性与可靠性，难以仅从同步音视频中学到此类表征。

Method: 结合同步3D高斯视角合成与多模态自监督；用多模态决策模型从教师预测损失学习相对视角效用，并以校准分数引导互补信息聚合；采用Aggregation-to-Single蒸馏将聚合预测迁移至单分支；通过真实-合成对齐匹配跨视角同步窗口并保留时间关系。

Result: 在真实与合成的多视角数据上，MuViRL提升了多模态表征一致性以及跨视角的视觉语音识别性能。

Conclusion: 校准的视角效用驱动的聚合与蒸馏机制，是应对视角变化下视觉语音表征学习的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning combines complementary signals to capture shared semantic information. For dynamic scenes, these representations need to remain consistent across views while preserving distinctions between temporal states. In visual speech representation learning, view variation changes the visibility and reliability of articulation cues, making it difficult to learn such representations from synchronized audio and video. To address this challenge, we propose MuViRL, a self-supervised framework for multimodal view-invariant visual speech representation learning via calibrated view aggregation. The framework combines synchronized 3D Gaussian view synthesis with multimodal self-supervision to learn consistent representations from real and synthesized data. A multimodal decision model learns relative view utility from teacher prediction losses, and its calibrated scores guide the aggregation of complementary information. Aggregation-to-Single Distillation transfers the aggregated predictions to a single branch. Real-synthetic alignment matches synchronized windows across views while preserving their temporal relations. Experiments on real and synthesized multiview data demonstrate that MuViRL improves multimodal representation consistency and visual speech recognition performance across views.

</details>


### [138] [Learning the Missing Derivative: Neural Multiple-Scattering Operators for Inverse Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16650)
*Sipeng Chen,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用神经算子预测多重散射场并在逆向渲染中直接对其求导，配合在反演轨迹上微调，实现光学厚介质的快速、可微重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从照片重建光学厚介质需建模多重散射及其对消光变化的响应。冻结光源层析（frozen-source tomography）丢弃该导数，可微路径追踪虽能估计但采样昂贵且噪声大。

Method: 用神经算子预测多重散射场并端到端求导，透射率与单次散射直接解析计算；针对算子导数在优化器访问的密度状态下失准的问题，在由该算子驱动的反演轨迹所生成的状态上，以蒙特卡洛散射场监督进行微调。

Result: 在留出的光学厚云数据上，等时间预算下重光照优于冻结光源层析；在显著更多时间下优于可微路径追踪；轨迹微调提升了访问状态处的导数对齐度并减少优化漂移。

Conclusion: 神经算子替代昂贵的多重散射采样是可微逆渲染的有效路线，且面向反演状态分布的微调对保证导数质量至关重要。

Abstract: Reconstructing optically thick media from photographs requires modeling multiple scattering and its response to changes in extinction. Frozen-source tomography discards this derivative; differentiable path tracing estimates it through expensive, noisy sampling. We instead predict the multiple-scattering field with a neural operator and differentiate through it during inverse rendering, while computing transmittance and single scattering directly. A further challenge arises during optimization: an operator that predicts the field accurately on held-out media can still produce poor derivatives at the density states visited by the optimizer. We address this by fine-tuning on states generated during inversions driven by the operator, using Monte Carlo scattering-field supervision. On held-out optically thick clouds, our method improves relighting over frozen-source tomography at equal time and outperforms differentiable path tracing given substantially more time. Training on inversion trajectories also improves derivative alignment at visited states and reduces optimization drift.

</details>


### [139] [Context-Predictive Low-Rank Adaptation for Face Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16534)
*Aminu Yusuf,Shakeel Muhammad Ibrahim,Sulaimon Oyeniyi Adebayo,Muhammad Sohail Ibrahim,Muhammad Shahid Jabbar,Shujaat Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CP-JEPA：将预训练CLIP图像编码器的低秩微调与联合嵌入预测目标结合，用于人脸呈现攻击检测；仅训练期使用掩码预测辅助，推理时只保留LoRA-CLIP编码器与二分类器。OCIM上平均HTER 3.74%、AUC 98.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸PAD需要能跨采集设备、受试者与攻击介质区分物理欺骗线索的表征。大视觉编码器提供可迁移表征，但常规二分类微调只监督最终决策，未显式约束局部人脸证据在部分观测下的表征方式。

Method: CP-JEPA = 预训练CLIP视觉编码器的LoRA低秩适配 + 联合嵌入预测（JEPA）目标。学生在掩码空间位置预测完整图像教师特征；保留未掩码分类、掩码分类与预测一致性三项损失以维持活体/欺骗任务关联；教师由EMA更新提供演化潜变量目标。辅助组件仅用于训练，推理只需LoRA-CLIP编码器+二分类器。评估涵盖单源/多源OCIM迁移、加入CelebA-Spoof训练、迁移至CelebA-Spoof、双源训练及SiW-Mv2未见攻击测试，并做损失消融。

Result: OCIM标准基准：平均HTER 3.74%、平均AUC 98.73%；单源评估在12个源-目标组合上平均HTER 11.36%。三源OCIM加入CelebA-Spoof后，平均HTER由3.45%降至2.35%，AUC由98.68%升至99.32%。SiW-Mv2未见攻击类别平均HTER 3.36%。消融实验考察潜变量预测、掩码分类与预测一致性的贡献。

Conclusion: 上下文预测式适配可提升跨数据集PAD性能，但增益随训练源与评估设置而变，并非稳定一致。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection requires representations that distinguish physical spoofing cues across changes in acquisition devices, subjects, and attack media. Large visual encoders offer transferable representations, but conventional binary fine-tuning supervises their final decisions without explicitly constraining how local facial evidence is represented under partial observation. We present CP-JEPA, a context-predictive adaptation framework that combines low-rank tuning of a pretrained CLIP image encoder with a joint-embedding predictive objective. A student predicts complete-image teacher features at masked spatial locations, while unmasked classification, masked classification, and prediction consistency retain the connection to the live/spoof task. An exponential-moving-average teacher supplies evolving latent targets. The auxiliary components are used only during training; inference requires only the LoRA-adapted CLIP visual encoder and binary classifier. {We evaluate CP-JEPA under single-source and multi-source OCIM transfer, additional CelebA-Spoof training, transfer to CelebA-Spoof, two-source training, and unseen-attack evaluation on SiW-Mv2.} On the standard OCIM benchmark, CP-JEPA achieves 3.74\% mean HTER and 98.73\% mean AUC. {Single-source evaluation achieves 11.36\% mean HTER across twelve source--target combinations.} With CelebA-Spoof added to the three OCIM training sources, CP-JEPA reduces mean HTER from 3.45\% to 2.35\% and increases mean AUC from 98.68\% to 99.32\%. On SiW-Mv2, it achieves 3.36\% mean HTER across the held-out attack categories. Controlled loss ablations examine the contributions of latent prediction, masked classification, and prediction consistency. The results show that context-predictive adaptation can improve cross-dataset PAD performance, although the gains vary with the training sources and evaluation setting.

</details>


### [140] [UniSpark: Exploring Hybrid Attention for Multimodal Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16517)
*Jiancheng Pan,Yuzheng Liu,Hengyuan Cao,Qiyuan Zhang,Yangyan Li,Biao Gong,Zheng Zhang,Ka Long Cheng,Xinyang Wang,Yujun Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSpark-9B 提出一种混合注意力（全注意力 + GatedDeltaNet 线性注意力）骨干，统一支撑多模态理解与生成，并在 MME-S、GenEval 上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型需同时兼顾显式 token 交互与长上下文/紧凑状态传播，纯全注意力代价高，纯线性注意力表达力受限；同时交错图文生成中"噪声视觉目标"与"干净视觉上下文"难以区分处理。

Method: 基于 Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts 骨干（每分支约 9B 参数），结合全注意力与 GatedDeltaNet 线性注意力；自回归文本预测与视觉 flow matching 联合训练；采用分块协议，在全注意力层用注意力掩码、在线性注意力层用循环状态保留规则，隔离噪声视觉目标并提供干净上下文；数据含 159.7M 生成、213.4M 理解、624.8K 交错序列；四阶段课程联合训练，图像合成分辨率扩展至 2K。

Result: MME-S 得分 2470.3，GenEval 得分 0.83；支持 VQA、文生图、指令编辑、交错图文预测，定性示例涵盖交错图像生成、视频合成与视频编辑。

Conclusion: 混合注意力可作为统一多模态理解与生成的有效骨干。

Abstract: We present UniSpark-9B, a unified multimodal model that investigates hybrid attention for visual understanding and generation. Built on a Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts backbone with approximately 9B parameters in each branch, UniSpark combines full attention for explicit token interactions with GatedDeltaNet linear attention for compact context propagation. Autoregressive text prediction and visual flow matching jointly support visual question answering, text-to-image generation, instruction-guided editing, and interleaved text-image prediction. In interleaved generation, noisy visual targets are isolated from subsequent predictions, while clean visual representations provide context for later outputs. A blockwise protocol enforces this distinction through attention masks in full-attention layers and recurrent-state retention rules in linear-attention layers. The selected training sources contain 159.7M generation samples, 213.4M understanding samples, and 624.8K interleaved sequences. A four-stage curriculum jointly trains multimodal understanding and visual generation, refining answer accuracy and instruction following while extending image synthesis to 2K. UniSpark scores 2470.3 on MME-S and 0.83 on GenEval. Qualitative examples further illustrate its capabilities in interleaved image generation, video synthesis, and video editing. Together, these results demonstrate that hybrid attention provides an effective backbone for unified multimodal understanding and generation.

</details>


### [141] [Consistent Video-to-Video Translation via Explicit Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17298)
*Gaurav Parmar,Zhengqi Li,Richard Zhang,Jun-Yan Zhu,Srinivasa Narasimhan,Eli Shechtman,Yotam Nitzan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 vid2vid-long：在输入视频中计算 patch 级对应关系，检索历史生成结果中对应区域的 patch，并通过 correspondence cross-attention 注入模型，从而在保持实时性的同时突破固定长度因果窗口，提升长程时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频到视频（vid2vid）应用需实时生成并保持长程时序一致性。现有方法通过将注意力限制在固定长度的因果窗口来提速，但当相关历史落在上下文窗口之外时，长程一致性受限。

Method: 基于一个简单观察——视频到视频翻译提供显式跨帧参考：输入视频中匹配的区域在输出中也应匹配。方法在输入视频中计算 patch 级对应关系，据此检索先前生成输出中对应区域的 patch，并通过 correspondence cross-attention 以其为条件调制模型。

Result: 实验表明，vid2vid-long 在多种近期视频模型上一致地提升长程时序一致性，同时保持生成质量与实时性能。

Conclusion: 利用输入视频的显式对应关系作为检索线索，可在不扩大因果窗口的前提下有效延展上下文，是一种兼顾实时性与长程一致性的通用增强方案。

Abstract: Interactive video-to-video applications require real-time generation while maintaining long-range temporal consistency. However, recent methods achieve speed by restricting attention to a fixed-length causal window. This limits long-range consistency whenever relevant history falls outside the context window. To address this limitation, we introduce vid2vid-long, a correspondence-based approach grounded in a simple observation. Video-to-video translation provides an explicit cross-frame reference: the regions that match in the input video should match in the output video too. Concretely, we compute patch-level correspondences in the input, guiding retrieval of previously generated outputs in the corresponding regions, and condition the model on these patches through correspondence cross-attention. Our experiments demonstrate that vid2vid-long consistently improves long-range temporal consistency across recent video models while preserving generation quality and real-time performance.

</details>


### [142] [Evaluating Video LLMs' Understanding of Corner Cases in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16767)
*Gabriel Manalu,Minh Son Hoang,Hyeontaek Hwang,Jaesung Lee,Hyojin Choi,Tran Tuan Phong,Seon Kwon Kim,Saad Wazir,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DashBench 基准，用 1,709 段精心筛选的行车视频（含碰撞与非碰撞的罕见长尾"corner case"）评估 25 个 Video LLM，通过开放式问答揭示这些模型在安全关键场景下普遍远低于安全应用所需水平，常误判/漏判危险事件并产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统期望借助 Video LLM 的自然语言解释实现场景描述与可解释决策，但现有驾驶场景基准主要针对常规交通条件与通用理解任务，基于既有驾驶数据集，无法评估模型在最罕见、最安全关键的"corner case"上的表现，缺乏对这类场景的全面评测。

Method: 构建 DashBench 基准：精选 1,709 段行车视频，覆盖碰撞相关与非碰撞的罕见 corner case；设计大量提示词与开放式问答任务，对 25 个 Video LLM 进行评测，并配套严格评估工具包与局限性诊断分析。

Result: 所有被评测模型表现均显著低于安全关键驾驶应用所需阈值，频繁误判或漏判前方道路危险事件，并在解释中幻觉出并不存在的交通参与者或行为。

Conclusion: DashBench 以大规模数据集、严谨评估工具和当前模型缺陷的细致诊断，推动更安全、更可解释的自动驾驶系统研究；代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Natural language explanations promised by modern Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) offer autonomous driving systems a path toward detailed scene description and interpretable decision making on the road. While recent benchmarks have begun assessing these models on driving scenes, they mainly target routine traffic conditions and general comprehension tasks based on existing driving datasets and therefore fail to evaluate model performance on the rarest and most safety-critical driving events, also known as "corner cases". To provide a comprehensive evaluation of Video LLMs on precisely these scenarios, we introduce DashBench, a benchmark testing 25 Video LLMs on 1,709 carefully curated driving videos spanning not only collision-related corner cases but also rare non-collision ones. Through extensive prompts and open-ended question answering, we expose a critical capability gap: all evaluated models perform substantially below the threshold required for safety-critical driving applications, frequently misjudging or missing dangerous events on the road ahead and hallucinating non-existent agents or actions in their explanations. We believe DashBench, combining an extensive dataset with a rigorous evaluation toolkit and a detailed diagnosis of current model limitations, drives progress toward safer, more explainable autonomous driving systems. The code and data will be shared at: cornercase-dashbench.github.io

</details>


### [143] [Ropedia Xperience 2: A Gold Standard for Scaling Physical AI Environments and Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17060)
*Hao Li,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Hui En Pang,Kairui Hu,Mingze Sun,Runmao Yao,Xinhao Ji,Xiyuan Zheng,Xuanli Chen,Yalun Dai,Yap Kim Hui,Dingwen Zhang,Yuanyuan Gao,Ziang Cao,Cheng Huang Yean,Jackson Peng,Rui Duan,Yaobo Liu,Zetong Wang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Ropedia Xperience 2 数据集：1,589 个度量尺度 3D 扫描与 4,230 段第一人称人类操作录制（74 项任务、131 小时），并证明每份采集可转化为仿真环境、全身机器人轨迹与额外机器人行为，为建立"环境与行为"双重维度的物理AI扩展律奠定数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大基础模型虽能直接从视觉观测驱动机器人，但在真实世界长时程、富接触任务上仍然脆弱。物理AI的进展依赖两类互补资源：监督任务执行方式的人类行为演示，以及可闭环 rollout、评估与改进策略的交互环境。语言模型已有清晰的扩展律，而物理AI的关键问题是能力如何随"环境"与"行为"规模扩展，这首先要求明确该扩展哪些数据、以及如何持续生产与复用这些数据。

Method: 构建 Ropedia Xperience 2 数据体系：1) 采集 1,589 个度量尺度 3D 扫描（环境侧）；2) 采集 4,230 段第一人称人类录制，覆盖 74 项任务、共 131 小时（行为侧）；3) 数据复用流水线——将场景转换为物理可交互仿真环境，将人体动作重定向为全身机器人轨迹，并通过虚拟遥操作与自动化长时程轨迹生成扩充机器人行为。

Result: 验证了每份采集均可重新用于机器人学习：场景可转为交互式仿真，人类动作可重定向为全身机器人轨迹，并可由此生成额外的机器人行为数据，形成环境与行为联合、可循环扩增的数据来源。

Conclusion: 将"环境与行为"的联合采集确立为物理AI数据采集的黄金标准，为建立跨环境规模与行为规模的扩展律提供数据基础。

Abstract: Large foundation models such as GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.1 have recently been shown to control robots directly from visual observations. They nevertheless remain brittle on long-horizon, contact-rich tasks in real-world environments. Further progress depends on two complementary resources: demonstrations of human behavior that supervise how tasks are performed, and interactive environments in which policies can be rolled out, evaluated, and improved in closed loop. Language models have advanced along well-characterized scaling laws; the corresponding question for physical AI is how capability scales with environments and behaviors, which first requires identifying which data to scale and how to produce and reuse such data continuously. To address this question, we introduce Ropedia Xperience 2, comprising 1,589 released metric-scale 3D scans and a collection of 4,230 egocentric recordings of 74 tasks (131 hours). We further show that each capture can be repurposed for robot learning: scenes are converted into physically interactive simulation environments, human motion is retargeted to whole-body robot trajectories, and additional robot behaviors are obtained through virtual teleoperation and automated long-horizon trajectory generation. We propose this joint capture of environments and behaviors as a gold standard for physical AI data collection, laying a data foundation for establishing scaling laws over environments and behaviors.

</details>


### [144] [EG360: Efficient and Generalizable $360^\circ$ Depth Estimation via Adaptive Panorama-Perspective Distillation and Spherical Cross-Level Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16793)
*Laiyan Ding,Chongxi Meng,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EG360：一种高效且可泛化的全景深度估计模型，仅6.593M参数，在Jetson Orin NX 16GB上达35 FPS，同时保持强零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景深度估计的大型预训练模型零样本泛化强但计算量大，难以在嵌入式平台实时推理；而轻量全景模型多面向单域评测，域偏移下泛化差。本文旨在弥合这两类模型之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于轻量深度模型，提出由场景几何引导的自适应全景—透视蒸馏（APPD）策略，以及用于全局语义感知的球面跨层级特征融合（SCFF）模块；重参数化后仅6.593M参数。

Result: 在真实世界基准上取得强零样本性能；在Jetson Orin NX 16GB MAXN模式下以35 FPS运行，而此前ViT-small模型不足2 FPS。

Conclusion: EG360在极小参数量下兼顾泛化性与实时性，为嵌入式平台全景深度估计提供了有效方案，代码将开源。

Abstract: Generalized panorama depth estimation has witnessed great advances in recent years. These methods typically achieve robust zero-shot generalization performance using large pretrained models, but their high computational cost makes real-time inference on embedded platforms challenging. In contrast, previous lightweight panorama models have mostly been developed for single-domain evaluations and cannot generalize well due to domain shifts. To reduce the gap between these two types of models, we introduce EG360, an efficient and generalizable panorama depth estimation model. Specifically, building on a lightweight depth model, we design an Adaptive Panorama-Perspective Distillation (APPD) strategy guided by scene geometry, along with a Spherical Cross-Level Feature Fusion (SCFF) module for global semantic perception. As a result, with only 6.593M parameters after reparameterization, EG360 achieves strong zero-shot performance on real-world benchmarks and runs at 35 FPS on a Jetson Orin NX 16GB in MAXN mode, whereas a previous ViT-small model runs at less than 2 FPS. Our code will also be made public for future research.

</details>


### [145] [PhysLoop: Harnessing LLMs' Physics Awareness for Self-Improving Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16748)
*Yuxuan Chen,Xiaojie Li,Hao Chen,Juncheng Ma,Kewei Zhang,Zhongjing Du,Guoqiang Wei,Fangfang Wang,Zhen Dong,Zhiyang Dou,Liming Zhao,Yan Zeng,Jianwei Zhang,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysLoop：一个引导LLM将物理知识蒸馏进视频生成模型产物的统一框架，支持可控训练数据构建与推理时视频修复，并发布100K视频数据集及两个评测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在物理动力学与因果一致性上仍不足；从生成视频中学习需要细粒度物理监督，但可靠的质量判断、修正配对与受控变体难以获得；LLM虽具物理知识，却难以可靠地指导视频生成模型产出其训练所需视频。

Method: 提出PhysLoop统一harness，在视频的生成、选择与修正全流程中引导并约束LLM，将其物理知识蒸馏为物理一致的视频；框架支持可控训练数据构建与推理时视频修复，通过识别并修正失败案例实现有限人工干预下的递归自我改进（RSI）。

Result: 在PhysLoop-Gen上评测可控生成，在PhysLoop-Repair上评测局部修复，并在外部Physics-IQ Verified基准上评估物理行为；同时发布覆盖多样真实室内外场景的100K视频、生成工具及两个评测数据集。

Conclusion: PhysLoop为视频生成模型的物理接地自我改进提供了闭环方案，是可递归自我改进方向上的关键一步，并配套开放数据、工具与评测基准。

Abstract: Video generation models produce visually realistic videos but still struggle with physical dynamics and causal consistency. Learning from generated videos requires fine-grained physical supervision, yet reliable quality judgments, correction pairs, and controlled variations are difficult to obtain. Large language models (LLMs) possess physical knowledge, but struggle to reliably guide video generation models in producing their training videos. We present PhysLoop, a unified harness designed to support physically grounded self-improvement in video generation models. The harness guides and constrains LLMs throughout video generation, selection, and correction, distilling their physical knowledge into physically consistent videos. The framework supports controllable training-data construction and inference-time video repair. By identifying and correcting failures, PhysLoop marks a key milestone toward recursive self-improvement (RSI) in video generation models with limited human intervention. We release 100K videos spanning diverse, realistic indoor and outdoor scenes, along with generation tools and two evaluation datasets: PhysLoop-Gen and PhysLoop-Repair. We evaluate controllable generation and localized repair on these datasets, respectively, and assess physical behavior on the external Physics-IQ Verified benchmark.

</details>


### [146] [ElasticDino: Dense Semantic Features Using Elastic Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16984)
*Ulysse Mizrahi,Sean Zaretzky,Dov Danon,Amit H. Bermano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ElasticDino：一种轻量适配器，将ViT特征通过通道一致性的空间形变上采样为像素对齐的高分辨率特征图，在密集预测任务上优于现有方法且计算更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT自注意力的二次复杂度限制特征分辨率并产生空间伪影，影响分割、深度估计等密集预测任务；现有上采样/解码方案存在平滑偏差，丢失细节。

Method: 观察到伪影跨通道具有一致性，可同时消除以匹配输入图像结构；构建基于空间形变的瘦身架构，高效生成像素对齐的高分辨率特征；利用形变严格保留锐利语义、避免平滑偏差；因缺少真值，采用带线性头的像素级语义分割与深度估计作为下游任务监督来引导形变过程，且不使任务特定语义泄漏进模型。

Result: 在多个主流密集任务基准上取得高于现有方法的精度，同时计算性能更优。

Conclusion: ElasticDino以轻量形变适配器实现ViT高分辨率特征的高效、细节保真重建，是一种通用且计算友好的密集预测增强方案。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) serve as the backbone for countless vision tasks, including dense prediction tasks, such as segmentation and depth estimation, even though the quadratic complexity of self-attention severely limits feature resolution and induces spatial artifacts. In this work, we introduce ElasticDino, a lightweight adapter which produces high-resolution feature maps, adhering to the fine details of the input image. As a key insight, we observe the consistency of artifacts across channels, and simultaneously resolve them to match the input image structure. Using a slim architecture based on spatial deformations, ElasticDino produces pixel-aligned, high-resolution feature maps efficiently. Leveraging deformations allows strictly preserving sharp semantics, and thereby avoiding the smoothness biases inherent to alternative approaches. Due to the absence of ground truth, we employ pixel-level semantic segmentation and depth estimation with linear heads to predict a set of downstream dense tasks, guiding the deformation process without leaking task-specific semantics into the model. We demonstrate superior accuracy over current methods on a range of popular dense task benchmarks, with better computational performance.

</details>


### [147] [Automated PD-L1 Tumor Proportion Scoring with Uncertainty-Guided Triage in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16907)
*Kesi Xu,Ayesha Azam,Tanvier Kaur,Monika Kondratowicz,Kinza Asim,Brinder Chohan,Paapa Appiah-Odame,Zhao Chen,David Snead,Nasir Rajpoot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段PD-L1 TPS评估框架（区域识别+双分支细胞计数网络D2Count-Net+不确定性引导分诊TPS-Guard），在154张WSI上MAE 6.96个百分点，处于三位病理医师范围内，不确定性可指示高误差与评分分歧病例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD-L1 TPS决定NSCLC免疫治疗选择，1%与50%截断点附近需重点复核；现有自动评分缺乏对不确定病例的界定与可解释的复核理由，故需在自动评分流程中嵌入不确定性引导的复核机制。

Method: 设计三阶段框架对应TPS评估流程：阶段1识别肿瘤区域并剔除富巨噬细胞斑块；阶段2用双分支细胞计数网络D2Count-Net分别计数PD-L1阳性与阴性肿瘤细胞以计算TPS；阶段3为TPS-Guard，以独立训练模型间的分歧作为模型不确定性，转诊最不确定的切片，并在模型估计跨越1%或50%截断点时标记评分。回顾性单中心研究，154张WSI由三位病理医师评分，模型与每位医师轮流与另两位均值比较。

Result: 114张未参与训练的切片上模型MAE为6.96个百分点，位于三位病理医师区间（4.24–8.29）之内；模型不确定性与TPS绝对误差相关（Spearman rho=0.77），与病理医师间分歧相关（rho=0.71）。转诊最不确定的15%切片后，模型与医师MAE均下降。未重新训练直接用于公开外部队列时，TPS-Guard仍将复核指向高误差病例，增加转诊比例可提升50%截断点的敏感性。

Conclusion: TPS-Guard为个体化自动评分提供复核理由，并能识别同时引起病理医师分歧的切片；其对TPS报告质量的实际影响尚需前瞻性阅片研究验证。

Abstract: Programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) tumor proportion score (TPS) informs immunotherapy selection in non-small cell lung cancer. Near the 1% and 50% cutoffs, the score should also indicate when closer review is warranted. We designed a three-stage framework to follow the sequence of TPS assessment. Stage 1 identifies tumor regions and excludes macrophage-rich patches. Stage 2, a dual-branch cell counting network (D2Count-Net), counts PD-L1-positive and PD-L1-negative tumor cells in these regions to calculate TPS. Stage 3, TPS-Guard, an uncertainty-guided triage mechanism, reports model uncertainty as the disagreement among independently trained models, refers the most uncertain slides, and flags scores near the 1% or 50% cutoff when model estimates disagree across that cutoff. Flags state the reason for referral or alert the pathologist to possible scoring errors on auto-reported slides. In a retrospective, single-center study, 154 whole-slide images were scored by three pathologists, and the model and each pathologist were compared in turn with the mean of the other two. On the 114 slides unseen in training, model mean absolute error (MAE) was 6.96 percentage points, within the range of the three pathologists (4.24 to 8.29), and model uncertainty correlated with absolute TPS error (Spearman rho = 0.77) and inter-pathologist disagreement (rho = 0.71). After referral of the 15% most uncertain slides, model and pathologist MAE both decreased. Applied without retraining to an external, publicly available dataset, TPS-Guard still directed review to higher-error cases, and referring more uncertain cases raised sensitivity at the 50% cutoff. TPS-Guard provides reasons for reviewing individual automated scores and identifies slides that also produce greater disagreement among pathologists. Prospective reader studies should assess TPS-Guard's effect on TPS reporting.

</details>


### [148] [Consensus Capture in Reference-Based UAV Video Stabilization: A Benchmark on Dynamic Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17190)
*Robert Fonod,Nikolas Geroliminis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套两部分基准，量化悬停无人机视频参考帧稳定中"共识捕获"（consensus capture）问题：运动车辆的一致对应会把鲁棒单应估计拉向自身运动，且内点统计无法察觉；同时评估对象掩码的防护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考帧稳定依赖配准流水线，而这些流水线多在仅相机运动的特征匹配基准上选取。真实繁忙路口场景中，多个车辆共同运动会产生互相一致的外点对应，污染鲁棒单应估计，而传统基于内点数/内点比率的诊断无法识别此类失效，现有基准也未覆盖该现象。

Method: 构建静态与动态两种协议。（1）静态协议：在4K航拍帧上以精确已知单应对十个流水线评分，在站点不相交划分、匹配分辨率与特征预算一致条件下调参；（2）动态协议：仅凭视频本身挑选覆盖不同运动前景数量与一致性的悬停片段，加一个静止对照，在离线与实时两种预算下评测；指标包括背景对齐、目标位置误差、估计跟随运动群体位移的比例，参照经人工标注地面控制点审计的伪真值，并对所有候选打分。另测对象掩码的抑制效果与匹配器阈值重调收益。

Result: 静态场景中调优后的AKAZE、RootSIFT、SIFT与BRISK领先；重调匹配器阈值使mAA提升至多0.0062，掩码代价小于0.005。未掩码的ORB在304对实时动态帧对上被捕获28次、离线6次；其被捕获的实时估计中位角点误差达18.8 px，但内点数量与比率仍属典型。掩码可消除所有流水线的捕获，且不改变领先流水线的排序。

Conclusion: 共识捕获是参考帧稳定中真实存在、常规内点统计无法发现的失效模式；对象掩码是有效且代价低的防护手段；静态基准上的排名不足以预测动态场景表现。代码、数据与结果均已公开。

Abstract: Reference-frame stabilization registers every frame of a hovering-drone video onto one reference frame, keeping object positions comparable. The registration pipelines it relies on are selected on feature-matching benchmarks in which only the camera moves. Over a busy intersection, vehicles moving together supply mutually consistent correspondences that can draw the robust homography estimate toward their motion: consensus capture, which inlier statistics cannot flag. A two-part benchmark measures it and the object masking meant to prevent it. A static protocol scores ten pipelines under exactly known homographies on 4K aerial frames, tuned on a site-disjoint split at a matched resolution and feature budget. A dynamic protocol scores them at an offline and a real-time budget on hovers selected from the video alone to span the amount and coherence of moving foreground, plus a stationary control. It measures background alignment, object position error and the share of a moving group's displacement an estimate follows, against a pseudo-ground-truth reference audited with manually annotated ground control points, which also score every candidate. On static scenes, tuned AKAZE, RootSIFT, SIFT and BRISK lead, retuning the matcher thresholds raises the mean average accuracy (mAA) by at most 0.0062, and masking costs less than 0.005. Unmasked ORB is captured on 28 of 304 real-time dynamic pairs and on 6 offline; its real-time captured estimates have a median corner error of 18.8 px yet typical inlier counts and ratios. Masking removes capture for every pipeline and leaves the leading pipelines unchanged. Code, data and results are released.

</details>


### [149] [Validation-Guided Prompt Selection for Differential Morphing Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17176)
*Raghavendra Ramachandra,Hailin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冻结参数视觉语言模型上，通过基于验证集的提示选择（而非微调）可显著提升差异化变形攻击检测性能，Version-1提示将MACER降至19.17%（对照手动提示70.00%），但递归提示修订在仅10名受试者的紧凑验证集上未能超越Generation-0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否利用带标签验证数据、通过提示选择而非模型微调来改进冻结参数的VLM评估器用于差异变形攻击检测（D-MAD），并解决递归提示精炼在紧凑验证条件下的可行性问题。

Method: Version-1生成50个候选提示，使用面向安全的损失（优先漏检变形攻击的MACER，同时计入真实样本分类错误BSCER、响应失败、解析失败与决策不一致）选出最优提示；Version-2利用聚合验证反馈递归修订提示。测试集为160对受试者不相交图像，涵盖LMA、MIPGAN-2与PIPE三类攻击。

Result: Version-1选中提示的测试MACER为19.17%，BSCER为0.00%，对照手动提示MACER为70.00%；配对差异50.83个百分点，95%受试者自助法区间[43.33,58.33]。递归修订未超过Generation-0最佳验证损失，修订后代测试MACER为31.67%。Version-1漏检的23个攻击全部为基于关键点的LMA变形。

Conclusion: 提示表述可显著影响冻结参数VLM的D-MAD性能，提示选择是可行的高效替代方案；但在10名受试者的紧凑验证设置下递归提示精炼仍具挑战，且LMA类变形仍是主要失效模式。

Abstract: Differential morphing attack detection (D-MAD) compares a trusted live capture with a document image that may contain a facial morph. We investigate whether labelled validation data can improve a frozen-parameter vision-language evaluator through prompt selection rather than model fine-tuning. Version~1 generates 50 candidate prompts and selects the top-ranked instruction using a security-oriented loss that prioritises missed morph attacks (MACER) while accounting for bona fide classification errors (BSCER), response failures, parse failures, and decision inconsistency. Version~2 recursively revises prompts using aggregate validation feedback. On a subject-disjoint test set of 160 pairs spanning LMA, MIPGAN-2, and PIPE, the selected Version~1 prompt achieves a (19.17\%) morph attack classification error rate (MACER), with (0.00\%) observed bona fide sample classification error rate (BSCER), compared with (70.00\%) MACER for a controlled manual prompt. The paired (50.83) percentage-point difference has a (95\%) subject-bootstrap interval of ([43.33,58.33]). Recursive revision does not surpass the best Generation~0 validation loss; the reported revised descendant obtains (31.67\%) test MACER. All 23 attacks missed by Version~1 are landmark-based (LMA) morphs. Overall, the results show that prompt formulation can substantially influence frozen-parameter VLM-based D-MAD performance, while also highlighting the challenge of recursive prompt refinement under a compact 10-subject validation setting.

</details>


### [150] [Predicting Ethanol Drinking Status from Nonhuman Primate Liver Histology: A Proof of Concept for Parameter-efficient Fine-tuning in Few-Shot Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17105)
*Amirmohammad Mohammadi,Rupak Khadka,Kathleen A. Grant,Mary Lauren Benton,Joshua Peeples*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 结合视觉语言模型与参数高效微调（PEFT），在少量样本条件下从非人灵长类肝脏组织学切片预测乙醇饮用状态；所有PEFT配置均优于非PEFT基线（ABMIL四样本设置下平衡准确率43.5%→78.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全切片图像（WSI）少样本分类中，VLM+PEFT具有潜力，但尚缺乏在计算病理学具身任务（如从组织学推断饮酒状态）上的实证评估。

Method: 在MATRR数据集子集上，将适配器（adapter）接入VLM，与非PEFT配置对比；采用ABMIL进行WSI级聚合，并进行注意力热图定性分析与病理学家复核。

Result: 所有PEFT配置的平均平衡准确率均高于非PEFT基线；四样本设置下ABMIL由43.5%提升至78.1%；文本通路适配产生更局域的注意力热图，视觉通路适配更弥散；病理学家在不同适配方案中识别出相似的组织学特征。

Conclusion: PEFT适配可显著提升VLM在WSI少样本分类中的性能，且不同适配通路的注意力模式存在差异但组织学依据一致。

Abstract: Combining vision-language models (VLMs) with parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers a promising approach to few-shot whole-slide image (WSI) classification in computational pathology. This study evaluates this approach for predicting ethanol drinking status from nonhuman primate liver histology. Adapters are evaluated against non-PEFT configurations on a subset of the Monkey Alcohol Tissue Research Resource (MATRR). All evaluated PEFT configurations achieve higher mean balanced accuracy than the non-PEFT baselines. For example, in the four-shot setting, attention-based multiple instance learning (ABMIL) alone achieves 43.5% mean balanced accuracy, compared with 78.1% after adding an adapter. Qualitative attention analysis shows that textual pathway adaptations produce localized attention heatmaps, while visual pathway adaptations produce more diffuse ones. A pathologist's review identified similar histological features across adaptations.

</details>


### [151] [Joint Null-Space Projection for Data-Free Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17103)
*Tian Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出联合零空间投影（JNSP），将模型合并重构为子空间干扰问题，通过移除每个任务向量落在其它任务顶部右奇异子空间并集中的分量，实现闭式、无序、免前向的免数据合并，在CLIP ViT-B/16八任务上达89.45%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免数据模型合并方法（平均、幅度剪枝、符号选举）逐元素操作，忽略了每个delta对其作用隐特征诱导的是低维更新，因而任务向量间干扰未被有效处理。

Method: 对每个二维权重张量的各任务delta取前r个右奇异子空间（即该任务最能改变表示的输入方向），从每个任务向量中剔除落在其他任务子空间并集内的分量；该投影为闭式解、与顺序无关、无需前向传播，仅由检查点集合决定。

Result: 在八个CLIP图像分类任务上，仅用检查点即将ViT-B/16专家合并至89.45%平均准确率，比最强逐参数免训练基线TIES-Merging高11.88点、比Task Arithmetic高14.22点、比WUDI免数据共识合并高0.98点；TALL设置三个骨干（ViT-B/16、B/32、L/14）上分别较TIES高+10.8、+12.6、+9.1并在该处复现89.20%；对照实验显示共享单一子空间下降2.9点、串行变体跌至69.6%。

Conclusion: 子空间级干扰建模优于逐元素合并，验证了增益来源于逐任务的子空间划分；但方法在RoBERTa-large上不及普通Task Arithmetic，作者如实报告其骨干依赖性局限。

Abstract: Merging independently fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model requires no data, no labels, and no gradient steps. In practice the task vectors of different tasks interfere, and the standard remedies--averaging, magnitude pruning, sign election--operate entry by entry, ignoring that each delta induces a low-dimensional update on the hidden features it acts upon. We recast merging as a subspace interference problem. For every two-dimensional weight tensor we take the top-r right-singular subspace of each task's delta--the input directions along which that task moves the representation most--and remove from each task vector the component lying in the union of the other tasks' subspaces. The resulting Joint Null-Space Projection (JNSP) is closed-form and order-independent, needs no forward pass, and is defined entirely by the checkpoint set. On eight CLIP image-classification tasks JNSP merges ViT-B/16 experts to 89.45% average accuracy without using a single image, 11.88 points above the strongest parameter-wise training-free baseline (TIES-Merging, 77.57%), 14.22 above Task Arithmetic, and 0.98 above WUDI's data-free consensus merge (88.47%). The gain holds across three backbones on the independently trained TALL release (+10.8, +12.6, +9.1 over TIES for ViT-B/16, ViT-B/32, ViT-L/14) and replicates there at 89.20%; controls confirm the source--a single shared subspace instead of per-task ones loses 2.9 points, and a sequential variant collapses to 69.6%. On RoBERTa-large the same projection underperforms plain task arithmetic, which we report rather than hide. Code is available at https://github.com.

</details>


### [152] [VBot Exo2Ego Policy: Lightweight Data Collection and Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16417)
*Tong Rao,Zhihao Zhang,Yuntian Wang,Siyu Zhou,Hailong Qin,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VBot Exo2Ego Policy——将第三人称视频转换为指定目标actor的第一人称观测的视频生成模型，并配套轻量数据采集管线与移动App，用以从海量真实视频中扩充第一人称操作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称操作数据稀缺，而真实世界中第三人称视频丰富；现有跨视角转换缺乏成对数据、目标相机标定依赖强，且难以处理互联网视频中actor多样性与镜头切换的问题。

Method: 构建支持第一/第三人称及双视角同步采集的轻量管线与App，提供成对RGB与相机几何；在预训练视频扩散Transformer上引入相机几何引导、语义掩码与手部关键点等辅助监督；训练时随机注入相机几何并逐步退火，使推理时无需目标相机标定；结合源视频感知与推理、可选actor参考及多镜头适配，实现actor识别与跨镜头操作跟踪。

Result: 模型能够生成具有较高保真度的手-物交互的第一人称视频，支持无标定推理，并可在镜头切换下持续跟踪目标actor的操作过程（摘要未给出定量指标）。

Conclusion: 所提采集系统与生成模型为跨任务、跨场景、跨参与者地扩充第一人称操作数据提供了基础方案。

Abstract: We present VBot Exo2Ego Policy, a video generation model that turns third-person videos into first-person observations of a selected actor, offering a practical way to expand first-person operation data from abundant real-world footage. To support this goal, we introduce a lightweight data collection pipeline and mobile app that support first-person recording, third-person recording, and synchronized capture of both views. This setup combines accessible single-view collection with paired RGB and camera geometry for learning cross-view correspondence. Building on a pretrained video diffusion transformer, our model incorporates geometric guidance and auxiliary supervision from semantic masks and hand keypoints to facilitate optimization and improve the fidelity of hand-object interactions. Camera geometry is injected stochastically during training and gradually removed, allowing inference without target-camera calibration. To accommodate the diversity of Internet videos, we combine source-video perception and reasoning, optional actor references, and multi-shot adaptation to identify the intended actor and follow an operation across camera cuts. The collection system and generation model provide a foundation for expanding first-person operation data across tasks, scenes, and participants.

</details>


### [153] [RICapBench: A Fine-Grained Visual Understanding Benchmark Dataset for Vision-Language Models in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16413)
*Xue Wang,Yubin Xu,Kun Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出RICapBench数据集与基准，统一遥感图像的实例定位与细粒度描述任务，并构建结合空间关系解析的RelCap-F1及综合指标RICScore，评测15个VLM并指出实例定位是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在遥感图像细粒度理解上存在困难：目标尺度变化大、实例间地理空间关系复杂、视觉细节有限，导致实例定位、可见属性识别与空间关系理解不精确，描述缺乏视觉依据；且现有数据集与基准不足以在无目标特定文本提示条件下评估端到端的实例定位与描述能力。

Method: 构建RICapBench，包含245,466条实例级标注与498,005个空间关系三元组，覆盖通用遥感、无人机遥感与密集小目标场景；每条标注将目标的有向边界框与其可见属性和空间关系（相对周围实例与环境要素）的自然语言描述配对。提出遥感空间关系解析器与RelCap-F1指标，并将RelCap-F1与实例定位、属性描述指标组合为综合指标RICScore。

Result: 对15个先进开源与闭源VLM的评测表明，实例定位是主要瓶颈；LocateAnything取得最高RICScore（31.68），但其空间关系描述得分低于其他任务微调的开源VLM。

Conclusion: RICapBench为遥感细粒度实例定位与描述提供了统一评估框架，揭示现有VLM的核心短板在于实例定位，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have improved remote sensing image analysis and supported geospatial decision-making. However, fine-grained understanding of remote sensing imagery remains challenging for VLMs. Large variations in object scale, complex geospatial relations among instances, and limited visual detail hinder precise instance localization, visible attribute identification, and spatial relation understanding, potentially leading to descriptions that lack visual grounding. Existing datasets and benchmarks insufficiently assess end-to-end instance localization and captioning without target-specific textual cues. To support this evaluation, we introduce RICapBench, a dataset and benchmark that unifies instance localization and fine-grained captioning in remote sensing. RICapBench contains 245,466 instance-level annotations and 498,005 spatial relation tuples spanning general remote sensing, UAV remote sensing, and dense small-object scenarios. Each annotation pairs an object's oriented bounding box with a natural language description of its visible attributes and spatial relations to surrounding instances and environmental elements. To evaluate the accuracy and completeness of spatial relation captioning, we propose a remote sensing spatial relation parser and the RelCap-F1 metric. We combine RelCap-F1 with instance localization and attribute captioning metrics into a composite metric, RICScore. Our evaluation of 15 advanced open-source and closed-source VLMs identifies instance localization as a major bottleneck for these models. LocateAnything achieves the highest RICScore of 31.68, but scores lower on spatial relation captioning than other task-fine-tuned open-source VLMs. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Xldln/RICapBench.

</details>


### [154] [FuGIBench: Evaluating Reliable Preparation in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16400)
*Changheng Cai,Zhichao Huang,Song Ren,Zhichen Zhao,Hongsen Liu,Chaoyang Zheng,Wenxi Wu,Yuan Fang,Wei Wang,Yang Wang,Yuchen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出FuGIBench流式视频基准，首次系统评测"早期证据—响应"之间"准备期"的决策行为，要求模型在等待、可逆准备与响应三种动作间选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助手观察到工人需求后需在工人准备接受帮助前进行准备，并随着需求演变维持或撤回准备；但现有视频预测与交互响应基准基本未测量这一阶段。

Method: 构建FuGIBench：19,893段合成工具辅助视频，覆盖十类工具任务，分为交接（handover）、普通活动、需求取消三条轨道；模型依据因果视频前缀与任务契约输出决策。设计主评分指标，奖励持续准备、纳入响应区间对齐、并对参考标注为等待时出现活动予以惩罚。在固定1,000个episode上开展参考系统对比与取消分析。

Result: 具备更长准备提前量的参考系统响应对齐更差；在匹配场景下，相近的交接提前量对应普通工作期间不同程度的活动；取消分析进一步揭示了准备策略差异。

Conclusion: 评估准备行为必须持续跟踪需求的发展、可行动化与消失过程，仅靠早期预测或响应时点评测不足。

Abstract: An assistant watching a worker can prepare for an emerging need before the worker is ready to receive help.As the need evolves, the assistant must maintain preparation until a response is due, or withdraw it if the need disappears. Video anticipation and interactive response benchmarks leave this interval between early evidence and response largely unmeasured. We introduce FuGIBench (Future-Grounded Interaction), a streaming-video benchmark in which a model chooses among waiting, reversible preparation, and response given a causal video prefix and a task contract. The benchmark contains 19,893 synthetic tool-assistance videos across ten tool tasks, in handover, ordinary-activity, and cancelled-need tracks. One primary score credits sustained preparation, incorporates response-interval alignment, and penalizes activity when the reference prescribes waiting. On a frozen set of 1,000 episodes, a reference system with a longer preparation lead has poorer response alignment, while similar handover leads accompany different levels of activity during ordinary work in matched scenarios. Together with cancellation analyses, these results show why evaluating preparation requires tracking decisions as needs develop, become actionable, or disappear.

</details>


### [155] [RADIANT: Redundancy-Aware Information Allocation for Universal Multi-Modal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16382)
*Shenghai Wu,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RADIANT（冗余感知动态信息分配网络），以"抑制冗余、保留模态独有、构造任务条件协同"三角色组织融合，矩阵指数投影降低共享/独有流干扰，任务记忆硬正更新选择协同交互；六个异构融合任务一致提升，FLIR检测mAP@0.5 从91.2%升至96.8%（+5.6点，相对+6.1%），并支持冻结权重的跨数据集迁移与推理期任务记忆自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为通用多模态融合的失败根源不在模态对齐不足，而在融合过程的信息分配不当：冗余证据被反复放大、模态独有证据在对齐中被过度平滑、任务相关协同证据激活不足。

Method: RADIANT为任务通用框架，围绕三种互补角色组织融合：抑制跨模态冗余重叠、保留模态独有证据、通过跨模态交互构造任务条件协同。具体采用稳定的矩阵指数投影减少共享流与模态独有流之间的干扰；并引入带硬正更新的任务记忆，用于选择当前融合机制下应被激活的协同交互。

Result: 在六个异构融合任务上相较对比方法取得一致提升；FLIR上检测mAP@0.5由91.2%提升至96.8%，较GIF提升5.6个百分点（相对6.1%）。具备较强直接跨数据集迁移能力，且在冻结全部网络权重的情况下，推理期任务记忆自适应可带来进一步增益。消融与敏感性实验支持该分配机制的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多模态融合的关键在于信息分配的合理性而非仅对齐质量；以冗余抑制、独有保留、任务条件协同激活为框架的显式分配机制，可跨任务泛化，并支持无需重训练的推理期自适应。

Abstract: Universal multi-modal fusion often fails not because modalities are insufficiently aligned, but because the fusion process allocates information poorly: redundant evidence is repeatedly amplified, modality-unique evidence is over-smoothed during alignment, and task-dependent synergistic evidence is insufficiently activated. We present RADIANT (Redundancy-Aware Dynamic Information Allocation Network), a task-general framework that organizes fusion around three complementary roles: suppressing redundant cross-modal overlap, preserving modality-unique evidence, and composing task-conditioned synergy through cross-modal interaction. Specifically, stable matrix-exponential projections are used to reduce interference among shared and modality-unique streams, while task memory with hard positive updates selects which synergistic interactions should be activated for the current fusion regime. Extensive experiments across six heterogeneous fusion tasks show consistent improvements over the compared methods. On FLIR, RADIANT improves downstream detection mAP@0.5 from 91.2% to 96.8%, a gain of 5.6 percentage points (6.1% relative) over GIF. RADIANT also exhibits strong direct cross-dataset transfer, with further gains from inference-time task-memory adaptation while keeping all network weights frozen. Additional ablations and sensitivity studies support the robustness of the allocation mechanism.

</details>


### [156] [Hand in Silhouette: Synthetic-to-Real Multi-View Hand Reconstruction via Binary Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16339)
*Leiyao Cui,Lecheng Ruan,Zihang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于二值掩码的多视角标定手部重建框架 SilHand，通过将度量三维假设投影到各视角以查询并融合掩码射线证据，从而缩小仿真到真实的观测差距，并利用大规模合成数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标定多视角手部重建方法高度依赖真实采集数据：采集成本高、手部几何多样性有限、标注常不完美。合成数据虽可扩展且监督精确，但存在显著的仿真-真实外观差距。因此需要一个能降低外观差距、便于利用合成几何数据的观测接口。

Method: 以二值手部掩码（可低成本自动或手工获取）作为输入，不依赖RGB特征进行三维提升；而是将度量三维假设投影至每个视角，查询并融合掩码射线证据完成重建。该以掩码为中心的表述支持在含46.8k抓取姿态与60k自由手部样本的大规模合成数据集上训练，覆盖广泛手部几何，并可直接迁移至真实手部几何。

Result: 在五个真实世界基准上，使用参考手渲染的掩码时，相对重建精度从49.2%提升至63.4%；随合成训练集增大，重建精度持续提升，说明更广几何覆盖可转化为更好的真实性能。此外在掩码损坏情形下仍保持稳健，重建误差最多增加1.63 mm。

Conclusion: 结果支持将二值掩码作为从合成几何学习手部重建的有效接口；该掩码中心表述使真实数据难以获得的手部几何多样性变得可行，并带来更优的真实场景精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Calibrated multi-view hand reconstruction has advanced rapidly, yet the effectiveness of existing methods depends strongly on real captured data, which is costly to acquire, limited in hand-geometry diversity, and often affected by imperfect annotations. Synthetic data offers a scalable alternative with accurate supervision, but a substantial synthetic-to-real appearance gap hinders its use. To address this, we introduce Hand in Silhouette (SilHand), a calibrated multi-view hand reconstruction framework that operates on binary hand masks, obtainable at low cost either automatically or manually, to reduce the synthetic-to-real observation gap. Rather than relying on RGB features for 3D lifting, SilHand projects metric 3D hypotheses into each view to query and fuse mask-ray evidence for hand reconstruction. This mask-centric formulation enables training on a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising 46.8k grasping poses and 60k free-hand samples, providing broad coverage of hand geometry while directly transferring to real captured hand geometry. Across five real-world benchmarks, SilHand improves relative reconstruction accuracy from 49.2% to 63.4% using masks rendered from reference hands. Moreover, reconstruction accuracy improves consistently as the synthetic training set grows, indicating that broader geometric coverage translates into better real-world performance. While achieving such diversity is difficult with real captured data, it becomes practical under our mask-centric formulation. Finally, SilHand remains robust under mask corruption, with reconstruction error increasing by at most 1.63 mm. Together, these results support binary masks as an effective interface for learning hand reconstruction from synthetic geometry.

</details>


### [157] [Selective Generative Restoration for Video Deraining via Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16310)
*Yi'ang Chen,Han Li,Laibin Chang,Hui Liu,Kui Jiang,Junjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性生成式复原范式SGVD：用双一步扩散预测的随机差异估计区域级不确定性，自适应决定保真重建还是生成推断，并以锚点引导轨迹一致性抑制跨帧生成漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频去雨面临雨痕、遮挡与大气效应，且雨退化具有空间异质性：可靠区域需忠实重建，严重遮挡区域内容模糊需更强先验驱动。现有确定性复原易过度平滑，全局生成式复原又会引入不必要幻觉，缺乏按不确定性自适应调节的机制。

Method: 1) 不确定性感知扩散框架：利用双一步扩散预测之间的随机多样性估计区域级复原不确定性，据此划分保真保持区与生成推断区；2) 锚点引导轨迹一致性机制，正则化跨帧随机扩散演化，减少时间维度的生成漂移；3) 采用一步扩散推理以兼顾效率与不确定性感知的生成能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，SGVD在复原性能、感知质量与时间一致性上均优于当前最先进的视频去雨方法。

Conclusion: 将生成先验按复原不确定性进行空间自适应激活，可有效平衡保真与生成，是视频去雨的一条可行且有前景的技术路线。

Abstract: Video deraining is challenging due to rain streaks, occlusions, and atmospheric effects. Existing methods mainly follow a deterministic restoration paradigm, assuming that clean scenes can be uniquely recovered from rainy observations. However, rain degradation is spatially heterogeneous. Reliable regions require faithful reconstruction, whereas severely occluded regions contain ambiguous content that require stronger prior-driven inference. Therefore, conventional deterministic restoration often produces over-smoothed results, while global generative restoration may introduce unnecessary hallucinations. We introduce a Selective Generative Restoration paradigm for video deraining, where generative priors are adaptively activated according to restoration uncertainty. Based on this principle, we propose Selective Generative Video Deraining (SGVD), an uncertainty-aware diffusion framework that enables adaptive fidelity preservation and generative recovery. Specifically, SGVD exploits stochastic diversity between dual one-step diffusion predictions to estimate region-level restoration uncertainty, which determines where observation fidelity should be preserved and where generative inference should be activated. Furthermore, an anchor-guided trajectory consistency mechanism is proposed to regularize stochastic diffusion evolution across frames, reducing temporal generation drift and improving video coherence. To achieve practical efficiency, SGVD adopts one-step diffusion inference while maintaining uncertainty-aware generative capability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGVD achieves superior restoration performance, perceptual quality, and temporal consistency compared with state-of-the-art video deraining methods.

</details>


### [158] [When Chekhov's Gun Fires Anyway: Over-Realization in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16630)
*Jiahao Huang,Kon Woo Kim,Song-Duo Ma,Yuka Kitamura,Felix Wagner,Yunshan Huang,Sunisth Kumar,Xanh Ho,Yun-Ang Wu,Zhijie Chen,Yunjia Xu,Junfeng Jiang,Zefan Yu,Fei Cheng,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"过度实现"(over-realization)失败模式：提示中的目标在否定、角色信念/想象、比喻载体、隐藏视角四种心智空间情境下被不当生成。构建OverReal基准(GEN 698条提示、Det 2841张人工标注图像)量化该现象，并验证自动评判器。评测8个文生图系统发现全部存在过度实现，CLIPScore等常规指标反而奖励该失败，提示扩展仅部分缓解；机制上扩散主干对"被提及目标"的注意力接近"被请求目标"，而应抑制目标的线索词注意力低于普通词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图系统中，提示提及但并未要求呈现的目标仍被生成，此类失败此前仅在否定情境下被深入研究，且现有对齐与偏好指标不惩罚该问题，缺乏系统度量手段。

Method: 基于心智空间理论界定四类情境（否定、信念/想象、比喻载体、隐藏视角），构建OverReal-Gen（698条覆盖四情境的提示，测各系统过度实现率）与OverReal-Det（2841张人工标注生成图像，验证自动评判器）；在此基础上评测8个文生图系统，并分析CLIPScore等指标表现、提示扩展效果以及生成器内部注意力分配。

Result: 8个系统全部出现过度实现；CLIPScore等常规指标奖励而非惩罚该失败；提示扩展仅部分缓解；扩散主干对"提及目标"的注意力几乎等同"请求目标"，而约束性线索词获得的注意力低于普通词。

Conclusion: 过度实现是跨系统的普遍失败，现有指标存在盲区；注意力机制层面的偏差为其内在成因，提示扩展不足以根本解决，需要新的度量与对齐手段。

Abstract: In text-to-image generation, a target that the prompt mentions often appears in the generated image in a way the prompt does not call for. We call this failure over-realization. Mental-spaces theory points to the situations where it can happen: under a negation, inside what a character believes or imagines, as the vehicle of a figurative comparison, or behind a viewpoint that hides the target. Prior work has studied only negation in depth, and the conventional alignment and preference metrics do not penalize the failure. We build OverReal to measure it: OverReal-Gen, 698 prompts covering the four situations, on which we measure each generator's over-realization rate; and OverReal-Det, 2,841 generated images human-labeled for whether and how the target is over-realized, on which we verify the automatic judge that produces this rate. Our evaluation of eight text-to-image systems reveals that all of them over-realize, and that conventional metrics such as CLIPScore reward the failure rather than penalize it. Prompt expansion only partly mitigates the failure. Inside the generator, the diffusion backbone attends to a mentioned target almost as much as to a requested one. The cue words that should hold the target back receive less attention than ordinary words.

</details>


### [159] [Improved CNN-Transformer Network for Neutron Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16620)
*Yuheng Chu,Hongli Li,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合CNN与Transformer的超分辨率模型提升中子图像空间分辨率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子图像受束流品质、闪烁体转换效率与光散射、探测器像元尺寸及噪声水平限制，存在散斑噪声、边缘模糊、细节缺失问题，难以满足高精度缺陷诊断、结构表征与无损检测需求。

Method: 1) 因高质量清晰中子训练图像难以获取，用真实中子图像微调预训练图像复原模型，对现有中子图像增强以构造高质量参考图像；2) 在CRAFT超分网络基础上改进，引入混合先验引导通道融合模块(HPCF)、均值-标准差双统计挤压激励模块(MS-SEBlock)及部分卷积前馈网络(PCFN)，增强纹理与细节信息重建。

Result: 仿真表明，相较双三次插值、SRCNN、SRGAN-VGG等方法，所提网络在全参考指标PSNR、SSIM与无参考指标AGM、SMD上均更优，重建图像的视觉质量与结构保真度显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究为中子图像质量提升提供了一种新思路。

Abstract: The spatial resolution of neutron images is of great importance for material defect diagnosis, structural characterization, and nondestructive testing. However, owing to limitations in neutron beam quality, scintillator conversion efficiency and light scattering, detector pixel size, and noise levels, conventional neutron images often suffer from speckle noise, blurred edges, and missing details, making them inadequate for high-precision analysis. To address these issues, an improved super-resolution model combining convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and Transformers is proposed. First, to overcome the difficulty of obtaining high-quality and clear training images for neutron imaging, a pretrained image restoration model is fine-tuned using real neutron images and then employed to enhance existing neutron images, thereby providing high-quality reference images for subsequent super-resolution model training. Second, an improved CRAFT-based super-resolution network is developed by incorporating a hybrid prior-guided channel fusion module (HPCF), a mean-standard deviation dual-statistics squeeze-and-excitation block (MS-SEBlock), and a partial convolution feed-forward network (PCFN), with the aim of improving the reconstruction of important information, such as textures and fine details, in neutron images. Simulation results demonstrate that, compared with bicubic interpolation, SRCNN, SRGAN-VGG, and other methods, the proposed network achieves superior performance in both full-reference metrics, including PSNR and SSIM, and no-reference metrics, including AGM and SMD. The visual quality and structural fidelity of the reconstructed images are also significantly improved. This study provides a new approach to improving neutron image quality.

</details>


### [160] [ORCA:Orthogonal Residual Consensus Alignment for Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16749)
*Xiaojian Ding,Xian Li,Xiaoying Zhu,Lin Zhao,Kaixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出正交残差共识对齐方法ORCA，通过提取跨视图正交残差抑制融合冗余，并用全局共识校准层级对比对齐，在五个基准上优于十五种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度多视图聚类存在两个问题：融合过程中冗余信息累积，以及不同表征层级上不加区分的对齐，导致视图特有结构与跨视图一致性难以平衡。

Method: 包含两个模块：(1) 语义增量循环融合模块，提取跨视图正交残差作为非冗余增量，并通过散度引导门控对循环状态自适应加权；(2) 共识校准层级对比对齐模块，以全局共识为视图级、成对级与循环级表征的对比对齐加权。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上与十五种对比方法实验，验证了ORCA的有效性，其中在100leaves数据集上较最强基线准确率提升5.25个百分点；消融实验验证了两个模块的作用。

Conclusion: 正交残差融合与共识校准的层级对齐能够协同平衡视图特有结构与跨视图一致性，是一种有效的深度多视图聚类范式。

Abstract: Deep multi-view clustering often suffers from redundant information accumulation during fusion and indiscriminate alignment across representation levels. We propose Orthogonal Residual Consensus Alignment (ORCA) to address these challenges. Its semantic incremental recurrent fusion module extracts cross-view orthogonal residuals as non-redundant increments and adaptively weights recurrent states through divergence-guided gating. Its consensus-calibrated hierarchical contrastive alignment module uses the global consensus to weight contrastive alignment of view-level, pairwise, and recurrent representations. Together, these modules balance view-specific structure with cross-view consistency. Experiments on five benchmarks against fifteen competing methods demonstrate ORCA's effectiveness, including a 5.25 percent accuracy improvement over the strongest baseline on 100leaves. Ablation studies validate both modules. Code is available at https://github.com/XiaojianDing/2026-NeurIPS-ORCA.

</details>


### [161] [A Disease-specific Retinal Aging Biomarker for Predicting Progression to Late Age-related Macular Degeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16955)
*Hexin Dong,Benjamin Hou,Puzhen Wu,Mingquan Lin,Emily Y. Chew,Zhiyong Lu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证"眼底AMD年龄"（FAA），用眼底图像量化AMD特异性视网膜衰老，在AREDS/AREDS2队列中较传统实际年龄显著提升AMD风险预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际年龄（chronological age）虽是AMD主要风险因素，但无法反映个体间视网膜衰老的差异，限制了AMD风险分层与及时干预的精度。

Method: 基于AREDS数据集36,782张眼底图像（2,192名受试者）训练模型，构建FAA以量化AMD相关加速视网膜衰老；在AREDS与AREDS2队列中验证，并将FAA纳入Cox比例风险模型评估预后增益。

Result: AREDS中C-index由0.836提升至0.888，5年Brier分数由0.050降至0.046；AREDS2中C-index由0.715提升至0.756，2年Brier由0.078降至0.076。在两种表型定义（读片中心定义 vs. 专科医师定义）下均保持一致改善。

Conclusion: FAA能有效捕捉AMD相关加速视网膜衰老，将其纳入AMD风险预测框架可提升预后精度，支持进一步评估其用于AMD风险分层的潜力。

Abstract: Age-related macular degeneration (AMD) is a leading cause of irreversible vision loss in older adults. While chronological age is a primary risk factor, it inadequately reflects individual variations in retinal aging, limiting effective AMD risk stratification and timely intervention. Here, we propose and validate fundus-based AMD age (FAA), which quantifies disease-specific retinal aging in AMD and captures accelerated AMD-related retinal aging, thereby improving prognostic precision over chronological age. Trained on 36,782 images from 2,192 participants in the Age-Related Eye Disease Study (AREDS) dataset and validated across AREDS and AREDS2 cohorts, our model demonstrates significant improvements over traditional chronological age-based approaches. Specifically, integrating the proposed FAA into Cox proportional-hazards models significantly enhances prediction performance. FAA improved the C-index from 0.836 to 0.888 and reduced the 5-year Brier score from 0.050 to 0.046 in AREDS, with consistent gains observed in AREDS2 (C-index 0.715 to 0.756; 2-year Brier 0.078 to 0.076). FAA showed improved prognostic performance across the two datasets and under different phenotype definitions (reading center-defined vs. specialist-defined grading), supporting its potential utility for AMD risk stratification. These findings highlight the advantage of incorporating FAA into AMD risk prediction frameworks, supporting further evaluation of FAA for AMD risk stratification.

</details>


### [162] [Raysemble: Metric 3D Bone Reconstruction from a Single X-ray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16869)
*Xiaoyang Li,Xinghao Yan,Yuan Chai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Raysemble 从单张X光片重建八块腰椎与骨盆骨骼，通过分离形状与摆放实现，无需刚性对齐即在15种方法中领先（Dice 65.24%、HD95 9.09 mm、ASSD 3.32 mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张X光片重建多块骨骼的三维结构具有挑战性，现有方法难以在无刚性对齐条件下准确建模形状与空间摆放。

Method: 对每个3D查询点在其图像投影处采样区域与全局特征；共享解码器预测归一化骨骼形状；由预测深度与相机几何确定3D中心与尺度，将形状重采样到毫米级公共网格，从而解耦形状与摆放。

Result: 在23名留出患者、43例CT（各生成一张模拟正位X光片）上，三个随机种子平均取得65.24% Dice、9.09 mm HD95、3.32 mm ASSD，优于15种对比方法；相同输入下Dice较VolumeNeRF提升5.86个百分点。

Conclusion: 形状与摆放分离的建模策略有效支持单X光片多骨骼3D重建，在无需刚性对齐的严格评估下取得领先性能。

Abstract: Raysemble reconstructs eight lumbar and pelvic bones from a single X-ray by separating shape and placement. Each 3D query samples regional and global features at its image projection. A shared decoder predicts normalized bone shapes. Predicted depth and camera geometry determine 3D centers and scales for resampling these shapes onto a common grid in millimeters. Evaluation uses 23 held-out patients with 43 CT scans, each generating one simulated anteroposterior radiograph. Without rigid alignment to CT,Raysemble leads 15 evaluated methods, averaging 65.24% Dice, 9.09 mm HD95, and 3.32 mm ASSD over three random seeds. With identical inputs, it improves Dice over VolumeNeRF by 5.86 percentage points.

</details>


### [163] [Kosmo-GFM: A Production-Ready Geometry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16840)
*Sheng Tang,Shengzhe Wei,Tiancheng Chen,Wenhua Jin,Xinyang Lu,Tinghao Jiang,Shuoxuan Feng,Yupeng Liu,Yu Zhang,Xingshuo Chang,Keren Zhou,Yuhan Xiong,Jiahao Li,Haoming Liu,Jituo Tian,Hui Liang,Chaoqun Tang,Feng Tian,Wentao Ouyang,Kaojin Zhu,Xiaolin Chai,Peihuang Fu,Miao Zheng,Yunbo Hu,Feixue Cai,Zizhen Zou,Weigang Wang,Yexuan Yang,Haoyu Feng,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Kosmo-GFM 场景重建系统，由几何基础模型 GFM、位姿优化引擎 PoseEngine 与重建监督模块 KosmoWorld 组成；在深度、位姿、动态点/场景流误差与运行效率上均优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将学习到的几何先验有效嵌入场景级重建流程，兼顾几何预测精度与位姿估计的效率，并为下游 3D/4D Gaussian Splatting 提供可用位姿与几何。

Method: GFM 从图像/视频预测相机参数、深度、跨视图对应与动态 3D 点；PoseEngine 将预测与几何优化结合，恢复场景级相机位姿与几何；KosmoWorld 由重建的真实场景生成图像与几何监督，反哺提升 GFM 的几何预测；另有 Flash 版采用混合精度。<br>（系统整体为模块化三组件协同设计。）

Result: SpatialBench 的 Sparse 与 Medium 子集上，GFM 取得最低绝对相对深度误差与最高 5° 相对位姿 AUC；五个数据集上动态点与场景流平均端点误差最低。34 个公共场景中，PoseEngine 在 1°/3°/5° 阈值下场景平均位姿 AUC 最高，累计运行 14.07 小时，约为 GLUEMAP（69.67 小时）的 20%；PoseEngine Flash 混合精度运行 2.62 小时，约为其 4%。扩展实验显示数据、模型计算与训练预算增加时性能总体向好。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持在场景级重建管线中引入学习型几何先验的可行性与优势，且可在精度与效率间灵活权衡。

Abstract: We present Kosmo-GFM, a scene reconstruction system that integrates a geometry foundation model (GFM), a pose optimization engine (PoseEngine), and reconstruction-derived supervision from KosmoWorld. GFM predicts camera parameters, depth, cross-view correspondences, and dynamic 3D points from images and videos. PoseEngine combines these predictions with geometric optimization to recover scene-level camera poses and geometry for downstream 3D and 4D Gaussian Splatting. KosmoWorld generates images and geometric supervision from reconstructed real-world scenes to improve GFM's geometric predictions. On the Sparse and Medium subsets of SpatialBench, GFM achieves the lowest absolute relative depth error and the highest relative-pose AUC at 5 degrees among the compared methods. It also achieves the lowest mean endpoint errors for dynamic points and scene flow across the five evaluated datasets. Across 34 scenes completed by all compared reconstruction systems, PoseEngine achieves the highest scene-averaged pose AUC at thresholds of 1, 3, and 5 degrees, with a cumulative runtime of 14.07 hours, approximately 20% of GLUEMAP's 69.67 hours. PoseEngine Flash provides a further accuracy-efficiency trade-off, with a measured mixed-precision runtime of 2.62 hours, approximately 4% of GLUEMAP's runtime. Separate scaling experiments show generally favorable trends with increased data, model computation, and training budgets. These results support the use of learned geometric priors within a scene-level reconstruction pipeline.

</details>


### [164] [DesignTaste: Benchmarking AI-Generator Visual Design Against Award-Wining Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16442)
*Zhou Chen,JiaXiang Li,DanYing Pan,Ye Li,TianRui Yang,EnBao Zhang,YaNan Xiao,LiXin Sun,Anqi Liu,Dong Zhao,JianYuan Li,YuanYuan Ding,Kai Kong,Xinwen Xu,HuiTang Du,Min Zhou,Zhiqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DesignTaste 设计基准，用 403 条设计简报（100 条来自 100 Beste Plakate 2025，303 条来自 ADC 2026）对比六个主流文生图模型与获奖作品的视觉设计能力，发现模型在创意原创性维度普遍落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图基准多评估图文对齐或整体偏好，而对"简报未限定外观时模型能否产出优秀设计方案"这一问题认识不足。

Method: 从获奖设计作品及其公开背景中提炼 403 条设计简报；六个领先商业文生图模型对同一简报各生成一张图像；采用单一模型评审对生成结果与对应获奖作品在五个维度上独立打分。

Result: 在两个数据集上，仅 ArtArk 的总体均分高于获奖作品；六个模型在两个数据集上的创意原创性得分均偏低。

Conclusion: 能生成成品化、视觉悦目的图像并不等同于模型具备良好的设计解题能力；设计导向评估应成为文生图能力评测的重要补充。

Abstract: Text-to-image benchmarks commonly assess text-image alignment or overall preference. Whether models can produce good designs when a brief leaves the appearance open remains less well understood. We introduce DesignTaste, a visual design benchmark that compares generated images with award-winning works. From these works and their publicly available context, we derive 403 design briefs: 100 poster tasks from 100 Beste Plakate 2025 and 303 tasks spanning 20 categories from ADC 2026. Six leading commercial text-to-image models each generate an image from the same brief. A single model judge scores each output and the corresponding award-winning work independently on five evaluation dimensions. On each dataset, only ArtArk achieves a higher mean overall score than the award-winning works. All six models score lower in Creative Originality on both datasets. These results show that producing finished, visually appealing images does not imply that a model can develop good design solutions.

</details>


### [165] [PhysCapBench: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Physically Grounded Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16424)
*Haofeng Wang,Pengcheng Wen,Ruiqi Li,Yuhao Zhang,Tiange Zhang,Boren Zheng,Xuanyi Liu,Siyu Tao,Danni Yu,Gan Lin,Haocheng Tang,Tianzizhe Guo,Mingyuan Su,Boyuan Chen,Zhongyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysCapBench——首个跨第三人称场景、自我中心操作与手持夹爪演示三大领域的物理描述基准，用七个物理维度评估标注质量，并验证物理描述对重建与策略微调的下游价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI需从真实经验中学习可迁移物理知识。视频中蕴含丰富的物理信息，但视频-语言预训练中的语义正确的文字描述常遗漏定义过程的交互与状态转移，导致视频中的物理经验无法自动转化为足够的语言监督。缺乏衡量标注质量、比较描述策略、指导数据精炼的"标尺"。

Method: 构建PhysCapBench：覆盖第三人称场景、自我中心操作、手持夹爪演示三类域；对1,500段视频序列提供人工核验标注；通过七个物理维度评估描述的准确性与覆盖度；以视频重建与操作策略微调检验下游效用。

Result: 相较元数据描述，物理描述使第三人称视频重建LPIPS降低8.6–9.5%，自我中心视频降低26.9–30.9%；训练指令富化同时提升PhysCapBench分数与策略成功率，其中pi0.5在RoboTwin 2.0子任务上提升7.5个百分点。

Conclusion: PhysCapBench使语言监督中的物理内容可被检验与比较，为跨域的物理描述评估与改进提供统一基础，支撑世界模型预训练数据的构建与精炼。

Abstract: Physical AI aims to learn transferable physical knowledge from real-world experience to understand, predict, and act in the physical world. Internet videos and embodied demonstrations provide complementary observations of motion, interaction, and state evolution. A foundational data challenge is to measure and preserve this physical information as training supervision. In video-language pretraining, captions express observed processes in language, yet a semantically correct description can omit the interactions and transitions that define those processes. Rich physical experience in video does not automatically translate into sufficient language supervision. This gap calls for a yardstick to assess annotation quality, compare captioning strategies, and guide training-data refinement. Motivated by our data construction for world-model pretraining, we introduce PhysCapBench, to our knowledge the first unified physical-caption benchmark spanning all three domains: third-person scenes, egocentric manipulation, and handheld-gripper demonstrations. It provides human-verified annotations for 1,500 video sequences and evaluates accuracy and coverage through seven physical dimensions. We examine downstream utility through video reconstruction and manipulation-policy fine-tuning. Physical captions reduce reconstruction LPIPS by 8.6-9.5% on third-person videos and 26.9-30.9% on egocentric videos relative to metadata captions. Training-instruction enrichment improves both PhysCapBench scores and policy success, including a 7.5-percentage-point gain for pi0.5 on a RoboTwin 2.0 subtask. PhysCapBench makes the physical content of language supervision inspectable and comparable, providing a cross-domain basis for its evaluation and improvement.

</details>


### [166] [TransXplainRRG: A Radiologist-Evaluated LVLM for Chest Radiograph Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16393)
*Chayan Mondal,Duc-Son Pham,Tele Tan,Ashu Gupta,Vincent T. Y. Tiong,Shreya Ghosh,Tom Gedeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TransXplainRRG：用 Swin Transformer 图像编码器 + 融合患者病史的文本编码器生成 X 光报告，并结合专家引导的"由内而外"异常发现提取进行报告精修，兼顾性能与可解释性，在 MIMIC-CXR 与 IU X-Ray 上 GREEN 得分优于 MAIRA-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 增强的 X 光报告生成方法忽视临床上下文、未考虑工作流集成、缺乏可解释性，导致临床落地受可靠性与可解释性担忧限制。本文旨在同时解决性能与专家可解释性两大挑战。

Method: 以现成 Swin Transformer 作为视觉编码器，搭配融入患者病史的 Transformer 文本编码器生成放射报告；进一步提出专家引导的"由内而外"（inside-out）策略，仅提取异常发现用于报告精修；整体融合 Transformer 视觉编码器、文本编码器与 LLM。在 MIMIC-CXR 上训练测试，并在未见过的 IU X-Ray 上验证泛化性；引入 GREEN 临床一致性指标及基于放射科医生视角的定性评价指标，并对 100 份生成报告开展初步放射科医生评估。

Result: GREEN 得分 MIMIC-CXR 0.327、IU X-Ray 0.605，均高于同设置下的 MAIRA-2；在标准评测指标上具竞争力，多次持平或超越 SOTA 模型；100 份报告的初步放射科医生评估显示具临床相关性。

Conclusion: 定量与定性分析表明 TransXplainRRG 在高性能自动化与临床所需可解释性之间架起桥梁，具备可靠、可解释的放射报告生成潜力。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLM) enhance X-ray report generation, but often neglect clinical context, overlook workflow integration, and lack explainability, which hinders clinical adoption due to interpretability and reliability concerns. This paper addresses the dual challenges of performance and expert explainability in automated X-ray report generation for clinical AI applications. Our proposed approach, TransXplainRRG, utilises an off-the-shelf Swin Transformer model along with a transformer-based text encoder that incorporates patient medical history to generate a radiology report. Further, we explore an expert-guided `inside-out' approach and extract only abnormal findings for radiology report refinement. Thus, this study bridges the gap between high-performance automation and interpretability critical for clinical practice by combining state-of-the-art transformer-based vision encoders, text encoders, and LLMs. TransXplainRRG is trained and tested on the large-scale MIMIC-CXR dataset and further evaluated on the unseen IU X-Ray dataset to demonstrate its generalizability and robustness. We employed the GREEN metric as a complementary measure of clinical consistency, achieving scores of 0.327 on MIMIC-CXR and 0.605 on IU X-Ray, both higher than those achieved by MAIRA-2 under the same evaluation setting. It also performed competitively across standard evaluation metrics, frequently matching or surpassing state-of-the-art models. Additionally, we introduce qualitative evaluation metrics developed from radiologists' viewpoints and conduct a preliminary radiologist evaluation on 100 generated reports to assess their clinical relevance. Quantitative and qualitative analyses further demonstrate the potential of our TransXplainRRG framework for reliable and explainable radiology report generation.

</details>


### [167] [Unbundling Multimodal Vision Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17334)
*Ellis Brown,Chris Hoang,Shengyi Qian,Shang-Wen Li,Mike Lewis,Marjan Ghazvininejad,Luke Zettlemoyer,Lucas Beyer,Mengye Ren,Jakob Verbeek,Saining Xie,Hu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在完全受控、同等训练算力的条件下拆解视觉编码器的三个设计要素（视觉先验、专用视觉容量、融合前放置），发现预训练视觉先验并非必需，真正关键的是专用视觉容量，且该容量可置于融合内部靠前的骨干层中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态语言模型对视觉输入的编码方式分歧很大：多数使用预训练视觉编码器，部分工作从零与语言模型联合训练编码器，甚至有完全去掉编码器的方案。但已发表模型在数据、架构与算力上均不同，无法隔离"视觉先验、专用视觉容量、融合前放置"这三个被捆绑的设计选择各自的作用。

Method: 构建受控实验框架：所有模型在同一数据上从零预训练，在等生命周期算力（matched lifetime compute）下比较视觉问答与语言基准表现；分别控制视觉先验（预训练 vs 从零联合训练）、专用视觉容量（有无编码器，以及把骨干参数按编码器参数量放大）、放置位置（融合前 vs 融合内早期骨干层）；并扩展到不同骨干规模、将编码器预训练视为零成本、以及从预训练语言模型出发等设定。

Result: 从零联合训练的编码器与预训练编码器表现相当，且无需单独的预训练阶段；无编码器模型即便把骨干按编码器参数量放大，视觉理解仍落后；在融合内部早期骨干层加入纯视觉模块，性能与融合前编码器相当，并可降低首 token 时延（time to first token）；上述结论在不同骨干规模、编码器预训练视为免费、以及从预训练语言模型出发时均成立。

Conclusion: 视觉先验对多模态语言模型并非必要；决定视觉理解能力的是专用视觉容量而非其是否经过上游预训练；该容量也不必置于融合之前，早期融合内的视觉模块同样有效且更利于推理效率。总体上支持"与骨干联合训练、并置于骨干早期的专用视觉容量"这一设计。

Abstract: How multimodal language models should encode visual input remains up for debate. Most models use a pretrained vision encoder, while some recent recipes train the encoder from scratch with the language model or remove it entirely. Vision encoders bundle together three design choices: a visual prior from upstream pretraining, dedicated vision capacity, and pre-fusion placement of that capacity (i.e., before the multimodal backbone). Because published models also differ in data, architecture, and compute, comparing them cannot isolate the effect of each choice. We disentangle these choices via a controlled framework where we pretrain all models from scratch on common data and compare them on visual question answering and language benchmarks at matched lifetime compute. We find that the visual prior is not necessary; an encoder trained jointly from scratch is on par with a pretrained one, without a separate training stage. What matters instead is dedicated vision capacity: an encoder-free model trails on visual understanding even when its backbone is enlarged by the encoder's parameter count. Yet that capacity need not precede multimodal fusion, as vision-only modules added in-fusion to early backbone layers perform comparably to a pre-fusion encoder and can lower time to first token. These results also hold across backbone sizes, when encoder pretraining is treated as free, and when starting from a pretrained language model. Overall, our findings favor dedicated vision capacity trained jointly with the backbone and placed early within it.

</details>


### [168] [Spatial Foundry: A Multimodal Dataset for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17318)
*Ethan Lin,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Spatial Foundry 数据集：5,280 个带可编辑资产的家具化住宅 3D 场景配置，提供共享场景坐标下对齐的视觉、几何与时间多模态数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能需要将视觉观测与环境的几何、物体及持续状态关联；单张图像使这些关系隐式化，而彼此割裂的标注难以在统一参考系下研究外观、相机运动与场景变化。

Method: 构建 5,280 个可编辑住宅 3D 场景，模态包括平面图、透视与全景 RGB、度量深度、点云、网格、实例级 3D 包围盒、与视频配对的相机轨迹；以共享场景坐标对齐跨视角、跨时间观测，并通过可编辑源场景生成额外相机路径与受控物体变化。

Result: 给出数据表示与构建流程，并讨论其在平面图条件 3D 生成、几何重建、实例级感知、相机条件视频生成、共享世界建模与场景编辑中的应用；受控的相机与场景变化支撑空间感知与世界模型研究。

Conclusion: Spatial Foundry 以统一坐标对齐的多模态、可编辑场景数据，为空间感知与世界模型研究提供可控实验平台，项目主页提供视频与交互式彩色点云预览。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires connecting visual observations to the geometry, objects, and persistent state of an environment. Images alone leave these relationships implicit, while disconnected annotations make it difficult to study appearance, camera motion, and scene changes under a common reference. We introduce Spatial Foundry, a collection of 5,280 furnished residential 3D scene configurations with editable assets and aligned visual, geometric, and temporal data. Its modalities include floor plans, perspective and panoramic RGB, metric depth, point clouds, meshes, instance-level 3D bounding boxes, and camera trajectories paired with video. Shared scene coordinates link observations across views and time; editable source scenes enable additional camera paths and controlled object changes. We describe the data representation and construction workflow, and discuss applications in floor-plan-conditioned 3D generation, geometric reconstruction, instance-level perception, camera-conditioned video generation, shared world modeling, and scene editing. Controlled camera and scene variations support spatial perception and world-model research. The public project page provides videos and an interactive colored point-cloud preview.

</details>


### [169] [TuMAP: A Dual-View, Morphotype-Aware Pathomics Pipeline for Quantifying Renal Tubular Remodeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17104)
*Ruijie Wu,Yuechen Yang,Yanfan Zhu,Yu Wang,Shilin Zhao,Ming-Tsun Tsai,Szu-Yuan Li,Shuo-Ming Ou,Tz-Heng Chen,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TuMAP是一个结合双视图基础模型表征与形态型标注的病理组学流程，用于量化肾小管形态异质性并分离形态组成变化与形态内表型差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肾小管外观高度异质，疾病相关的肾小管重塑难以量化，缺乏能区分"形态型组成改变"与"同一形态内部表型差异"的框架。
